תְּפִלּוֹת וּבְרָכוֹתPrayers and Blessings

Nusach Edot HaMizrach, with transliteration in Mizrahi-Sephardic pronunciation. Tap a prayer to open it.

CAPITALS mark the stressed syllablevowels are ah eh ee oh ookh is ח and כtzere is ay before a yod, eh otherwise
Mizrahi-Sephardic Prayers and Blessings — phonetic transliteration

No prayer or psalm matches that search.

Friday Night

לֵיל שַׁבָּת

In order, from candle lighting through Birkat HaMazon

Light before sunset. Common Sephardic practice is to say the blessing first and then light; some families light first and then bless. Follow your family custom.

Hebrew audio

בָּרוּךְ אַתָּה יְהֹוָה אֱלֹהֵינוּ מֶלֶךְ הָעוֹלָם, אֲשֶׁר קִדְּשָׁנוּ בְּמִצְוֹתָיו וְצִוָּנוּ לְהַדְלִיק נֵר שֶׁל שַׁבָּת.

bah-ROOKH ah-TAH ah-doh-NIGH eh-loh-HAY-noo MEH-lekh hah-oh-LAHM, ah-SHER kee-deh-SHAH-noo beh-meetz-voh-TAHV veh-tzee-VAH-noo leh-hahd-LEEK NER shel shah-BAHT.

Blessed are You, Lord our God, King of the universe, who sanctified us with His commandments and commanded us to light the Shabbat light.

Sephardic custom does not add Shehecheyanu at the weekly Shabbat candle lighting.

Recited as part of Arvit, after nightfall. When praying without a minyan, begin with the first line below.

Hebrew audio

אֵל מֶלֶךְ נֶאֱמָן.

ehl MEH-lekh neh-eh-MAHN.

God, faithful King.

Cover the eyes with the right hand:

שְׁמַע יִשְׂרָאֵל, יְהֹוָה אֱלֹהֵינוּ, יְהֹוָה אֶחָד.

sheh-MAH yees-rah-EL, ah-doh-NIGH eh-loh-HAY-noo, ah-doh-NIGH eh-KHAHD.

Hear, O Israel: the Lord is our God, the Lord is One.

In an undertone:

בָּרוּךְ שֵׁם כְּבוֹד מַלְכוּתוֹ לְעוֹלָם וָעֶד.

bah-ROOKH SHEM keh-VOHD mahl-khoo-TOH leh-oh-LAHM vah-ED.

Blessed is the name of His glorious kingdom forever and ever.

First paragraph (Deuteronomy 6:5–9)

וְאָהַבְתָּ אֵת יְהֹוָה אֱלֹהֶיךָ, בְּכָל לְבָבְךָ וּבְכָל נַפְשְׁךָ וּבְכָל מְאֹדֶךָ.

veh-ah-hahv-TAH eht ah-doh-NIGH eh-loh-HEH-khah, beh-KHOHL leh-vahv-KHAH, oov-KHOHL nahf-sheh-KHAH, oov-KHOHL meh-oh-DEH-khah.

You shall love the Lord your God with all your heart, with all your soul, and with all your might.

וְהָיוּ הַדְּבָרִים הָאֵלֶּה, אֲשֶׁר אָנֹכִי מְצַוְּךָ הַיּוֹם, עַל לְבָבֶךָ.

veh-hah-YOO hah-deh-vah-REEM hah-EH-leh, ah-SHER ah-noh-KHEE meh-tzah-veh-KHAH hah-YOHM, ahl leh-vah-VEH-khah.

These words that I command you today shall be upon your heart.

וְשִׁנַּנְתָּם לְבָנֶיךָ וְדִבַּרְתָּ בָּם, בְּשִׁבְתְּךָ בְּבֵיתֶךָ וּבְלֶכְתְּךָ בַדֶּרֶךְ, וּבְשָׁכְבְּךָ וּבְקוּמֶךָ.

veh-shee-nahn-TAHM leh-vah-NEH-khah, veh-dee-bahr-TAH BAHM, beh-sheev-teh-KHAH beh-vay-TEH-khah, oov-lekh-teh-KHAH vah-DEH-rekh, oov-shokh-beh-KHAH oov-koo-MEH-khah.

Teach them diligently to your children and speak of them when you sit in your house and when you walk on the way, when you lie down and when you rise.

וּקְשַׁרְתָּם לְאוֹת עַל יָדֶךָ, וְהָיוּ לְטֹטָפֹת בֵּין עֵינֶיךָ.

ook-shahr-TAHM leh-OHT ahl yah-DEH-khah, veh-hah-YOO leh-toh-tah-FOHT bayn ay-NEH-khah.

Bind them as a sign upon your hand, and let them be frontlets between your eyes.

וּכְתַבְתָּם עַל מְזֻזוֹת בֵּיתֶךָ וּבִשְׁעָרֶיךָ.

ookh-tahv-TAHM ahl meh-zoo-ZOHT bay-TEH-khah oo-veesh-ah-REH-khah.

Write them on the doorposts of your house and on your gates.

Second paragraph (Deuteronomy 11:13–21)

וְהָיָה אִם שָׁמֹעַ תִּשְׁמְעוּ אֶל מִצְוֹתַי, אֲשֶׁר אָנֹכִי מְצַוֶּה אֶתְכֶם הַיּוֹם, לְאַהֲבָה אֶת יְהֹוָה אֱלֹהֵיכֶם וּלְעָבְדוֹ, בְּכָל לְבַבְכֶם וּבְכָל נַפְשְׁכֶם.

veh-hah-YAH eem shah-MOH-ah teesh-meh-OO el meetz-voh-TIGH, ah-SHER ah-noh-KHEE meh-tzah-VEH et-KHEM hah-YOHM, leh-ah-hah-VAH eht ah-doh-NIGH eh-loh-hay-KHEM, ool-ohv-DOH beh-KHOHL leh-vahv-KHEM oov-KHOHL nahf-sheh-KHEM.

If you truly listen to My commandments that I command you today, to love the Lord your God and to serve Him with all your heart and all your soul,

וְנָתַתִּי מְטַר אַרְצְכֶם בְּעִתּוֹ, יוֹרֶה וּמַלְקוֹשׁ, וְאָסַפְתָּ דְגָנֶךָ וְתִירֹשְׁךָ וְיִצְהָרֶךָ.

veh-nah-tah-TEE meh-TAHR ahr-tzeh-KHEM beh-ee-TOH, yoh-REH oo-mahl-KOHSH, veh-ah-sahf-TAH deh-gah-NEH-khah veh-tee-rohsh-KHAH veh-yeetz-hah-REH-khah.

I will give the rain of your land in its season, the early rain and the late rain, and you will gather in your grain, your wine and your oil.

וְנָתַתִּי עֵשֶׂב בְּשָׂדְךָ לִבְהֶמְתֶּךָ, וְאָכַלְתָּ וְשָׂבָעְתָּ.

veh-nah-tah-TEE EH-sev beh-sah-deh-KHAH leev-hem-TEH-khah, veh-ah-khahl-TAH veh-sah-vah-TAH.

I will give grass in your field for your cattle, and you will eat and be satisfied.

הִשָּׁמְרוּ לָכֶם פֶּן יִפְתֶּה לְבַבְכֶם, וְסַרְתֶּם וַעֲבַדְתֶּם אֱלֹהִים אֲחֵרִים, וְהִשְׁתַּחֲוִיתֶם לָהֶם.

hee-shah-meh-ROO lah-KHEM pen yeef-TEH leh-vahv-KHEM, veh-sahr-TEM vah-ah-vahd-TEM eh-loh-HEEM ah-kheh-REEM, veh-heesh-tah-khah-vee-TEM lah-HEM.

Take care lest your heart be led astray, and you turn aside and serve other gods and bow down to them.

וְחָרָה אַף יְהֹוָה בָּכֶם, וְעָצַר אֶת הַשָּׁמַיִם וְלֹא יִהְיֶה מָטָר, וְהָאֲדָמָה לֹא תִתֵּן אֶת יְבוּלָהּ, וַאֲבַדְתֶּם מְהֵרָה מֵעַל הָאָרֶץ הַטֹּבָה אֲשֶׁר יְהֹוָה נֹתֵן לָכֶם.

veh-khah-RAH ahf ah-doh-NIGH bah-KHEM, veh-ah-TZAHR eht hah-shah-MAH-yeem veh-LOH yeeh-YEH mah-TAHR, veh-hah-ah-dah-MAH loh tee-TEN eht yeh-voo-LAH, vah-ah-vahd-TEM meh-heh-RAH meh-AHL hah-AH-retz hah-toh-VAH, ah-SHER ah-doh-NIGH noh-TEN lah-KHEM.

Then the Lord's anger will burn against you; He will shut the heavens so there is no rain and the ground will not yield its produce, and you will quickly perish from the good land the Lord gives you.

וְשַׂמְתֶּם אֶת דְּבָרַי אֵלֶּה עַל לְבַבְכֶם וְעַל נַפְשְׁכֶם, וּקְשַׁרְתֶּם אֹתָם לְאוֹת עַל יֶדְכֶם, וְהָיוּ לְטוֹטָפֹת בֵּין עֵינֵיכֶם.

veh-sahm-TEM eht deh-vah-RIGH EH-leh ahl leh-vahv-KHEM veh-ahl nahf-sheh-KHEM, ook-shahr-TEM oh-TAHM leh-OHT ahl yed-KHEM, veh-hah-YOO leh-toh-tah-FOHT bayn ay-nay-KHEM.

Place these words of Mine upon your heart and your soul; bind them as a sign upon your hand, and let them be frontlets between your eyes.

וְלִמַּדְתֶּם אֹתָם אֶת בְּנֵיכֶם לְדַבֵּר בָּם, בְּשִׁבְתְּךָ בְּבֵיתֶךָ וּבְלֶכְתְּךָ בַדֶּרֶךְ, וּבְשָׁכְבְּךָ וּבְקוּמֶךָ.

veh-lee-mahd-TEM oh-TAHM eht beh-nay-KHEM leh-dah-BER BAHM, beh-sheev-teh-KHAH beh-vay-TEH-khah, oov-lekh-teh-KHAH vah-DEH-rekh, oov-shokh-beh-KHAH oov-koo-MEH-khah.

Teach them to your children, speaking of them when you sit in your house and when you walk on the way, when you lie down and when you rise.

וּכְתַבְתָּם עַל מְזֻזוֹת בֵּיתֶךָ וּבִשְׁעָרֶיךָ.

ookh-tahv-TAHM ahl meh-zoo-ZOHT bay-TEH-khah oo-veesh-ah-REH-khah.

Write them on the doorposts of your house and on your gates.

לְמַעַן יִרְבּוּ יְמֵיכֶם וִימֵי בְנֵיכֶם עַל הָאֲדָמָה, אֲשֶׁר נִשְׁבַּע יְהֹוָה לַאֲבֹתֵיכֶם לָתֵת לָהֶם, כִּימֵי הַשָּׁמַיִם עַל הָאָרֶץ.

leh-MAH-ahn yeer-BOO yeh-may-KHEM vee-MAY veh-nay-KHEM ahl hah-ah-dah-MAH, ah-SHER neesh-BAH ah-doh-NIGH lah-ah-voh-tay-KHEM lah-TET lah-HEM, kee-MAY hah-shah-MAH-yeem ahl hah-AH-retz.

So that your days and your children's days may be multiplied on the land the Lord swore to your fathers to give them, as the days of the heavens above the earth.

Third paragraph (Numbers 15:37–41)

וַיֹּאמֶר יְהֹוָה אֶל מֹשֶׁה לֵּאמֹר.

vah-YOH-mer ah-doh-NIGH el moh-SHEH leh-MOHR.

The Lord spoke to Moses, saying:

דַּבֵּר אֶל בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וְאָמַרְתָּ אֲלֵהֶם, וְעָשׂוּ לָהֶם צִיצִת עַל כַּנְפֵי בִגְדֵיהֶם לְדֹרֹתָם, וְנָתְנוּ עַל צִיצִת הַכָּנָף פְּתִיל תְּכֵלֶת.

dah-BER el beh-NAY yees-rah-EL veh-ah-mahr-TAH ah-leh-HEM, veh-ah-SOO lah-HEM tzee-TZEET ahl kahn-FAY veeg-day-HEM leh-doh-roh-TAHM, veh-nah-teh-NOO ahl tzee-TZEET hah-kah-NAHF peh-TEEL teh-KHEH-let.

Speak to the children of Israel and tell them to make fringes on the corners of their garments throughout their generations, and to put a thread of blue on the fringe of each corner.

וְהָיָה לָכֶם לְצִיצִת, וּרְאִיתֶם אֹתוֹ, וּזְכַרְתֶּם אֶת כָּל מִצְוֹת יְהֹוָה וַעֲשִׂיתֶם אֹתָם, וְלֹא תָתוּרוּ אַחֲרֵי לְבַבְכֶם וְאַחֲרֵי עֵינֵיכֶם, אֲשֶׁר אַתֶּם זֹנִים אַחֲרֵיהֶם.

veh-hah-YAH lah-KHEM leh-tzee-TZEET, oo-reh-ee-TEM oh-TOH, ooz-khahr-TEM eht kohl meetz-VOHT ah-doh-NIGH, vah-ah-see-TEM oh-TAHM, veh-LOH tah-TOO-roo ah-khah-RAY leh-vahv-KHEM veh-ah-khah-RAY ay-nay-KHEM, ah-SHER ah-TEM zoh-NEEM ah-khah-ray-HEM.

It shall be a fringe for you: when you see it you will remember all the Lord's commandments and do them, and not stray after your heart and your eyes, which lead you astray.

לְמַעַן תִּזְכְּרוּ וַעֲשִׂיתֶם אֶת כָּל מִצְוֹתָי, וִהְיִיתֶם קְדֹשִׁים לֵאלֹהֵיכֶם.

leh-MAH-ahn teez-keh-ROO vah-ah-see-TEM eht kohl meetz-voh-TIGH, veeh-yee-TEM keh-doh-SHEEM leh-loh-hay-KHEM.

So that you remember and do all My commandments, and be holy to your God.

אֲנִי יְהֹוָה אֱלֹהֵיכֶם, אֲשֶׁר הוֹצֵאתִי אֶתְכֶם מֵאֶרֶץ מִצְרַיִם, לִהְיוֹת לָכֶם לֵאלֹהִים, אֲנִי יְהֹוָה אֱלֹהֵיכֶם. אֱמֶת.

ah-NEE ah-doh-NIGH eh-loh-hay-KHEM, ah-SHER hoh-tzeh-TEE et-KHEM meh-EH-retz meetz-RAH-yeem, leeh-YOHT lah-KHEM leh-loh-HEEM. ah-NEE ah-doh-NIGH eh-loh-hay-KHEM. eh-MET.

I am the Lord your God, who brought you out of the land of Egypt to be your God; I am the Lord your God. True.

Sung on returning home, before the meal. Each stanza is customarily repeated three times.

Hebrew audio

שָׁלוֹם עֲלֵיכֶם, מַלְאֲכֵי הַשָּׁרֵת, מַלְאֲכֵי עֶלְיוֹן, מִמֶּלֶךְ מַלְכֵי הַמְּלָכִים, הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא.

shah-LOHM ah-lay-KHEM, mahl-ah-KHAY hah-shah-RET, mahl-ah-KHAY el-YOHN, mee-MEH-lekh mahl-KHAY hah-meh-lah-KHEEM, hah-kah-DOHSH bah-ROOKH HOO.

Peace be upon you, ministering angels, angels of the Most High, from the King of kings of kings, the Holy One, blessed be He.

בּוֹאֲכֶם לְשָׁלוֹם, מַלְאֲכֵי הַשָּׁלוֹם, מַלְאֲכֵי עֶלְיוֹן, מִמֶּלֶךְ מַלְכֵי הַמְּלָכִים, הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא.

boh-ah-KHEM leh-shah-LOHM, mahl-ah-KHAY hah-shah-LOHM, mahl-ah-KHAY el-YOHN, mee-MEH-lekh mahl-KHAY hah-meh-lah-KHEEM, hah-kah-DOHSH bah-ROOKH HOO.

Come in peace, angels of peace, angels of the Most High, from the King of kings of kings, the Holy One, blessed be He.

בָּרְכוּנִי לְשָׁלוֹם, מַלְאֲכֵי הַשָּׁלוֹם, מַלְאֲכֵי עֶלְיוֹן, מִמֶּלֶךְ מַלְכֵי הַמְּלָכִים, הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא.

bahr-KHOO-nee leh-shah-LOHM, mahl-ah-KHAY hah-shah-LOHM, mahl-ah-KHAY el-YOHN, mee-MEH-lekh mahl-KHAY hah-meh-lah-KHEEM, hah-kah-DOHSH bah-ROOKH HOO.

Bless me with peace, angels of peace, angels of the Most High, from the King of kings of kings, the Holy One, blessed be He.

בְּשִׁבְתְּכֶם לְשָׁלוֹם, מַלְאֲכֵי הַשָּׁלוֹם, מַלְאֲכֵי עֶלְיוֹן, מִמֶּלֶךְ מַלְכֵי הַמְּלָכִים, הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא.

beh-sheev-teh-KHEM leh-shah-LOHM, mahl-ah-KHAY hah-shah-LOHM, mahl-ah-KHAY el-YOHN, mee-MEH-lekh mahl-KHAY hah-meh-lah-KHEEM, hah-kah-DOHSH bah-ROOKH HOO.

Sit in peace, angels of peace, angels of the Most High, from the King of kings of kings, the Holy One, blessed be He.

צֵאתְכֶם לְשָׁלוֹם, מַלְאֲכֵי הַשָּׁלוֹם, מַלְאֲכֵי עֶלְיוֹן, מִמֶּלֶךְ מַלְכֵי הַמְּלָכִים, הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא.

tzeht-KHEM leh-shah-LOHM, mahl-ah-KHAY hah-shah-LOHM, mahl-ah-KHAY el-YOHN, mee-MEH-lekh mahl-KHAY hah-meh-lah-KHEEM, hah-kah-DOHSH bah-ROOKH HOO.

Go in peace, angels of peace, angels of the Most High, from the King of kings of kings, the Holy One, blessed be He.

Hebrew audio: son, daughter, then Birkat Kohanim

Parents place their hands on each child’s head. Timing varies by family; many Sephardic homes bless the children before Kiddush. For a son:

יְשִׂמְךָ אֱלֹהִים כְּאֶפְרַיִם וְכִמְנַשֶּׁה.

yeh-seem-KHAH eh-loh-HEEM keh-ehf-RAH-yeem veh-kheem-nah-SHEH.

May God make you like Ephraim and Menashe.

For a daughter:

יְשִׂימֵךְ אֱלֹהִים כְּשָׂרָה רִבְקָה רָחֵל וְלֵאָה.

yeh-see-MEKH eh-loh-HEEM keh-sah-RAH reev-KAH rah-KHEL veh-leh-AH.

May God make you like Sarah, Rivkah, Rachel and Leah.

Then, for every child, Birkat Kohanim (Numbers 6:24–26):

יְבָרֶכְךָ יְהֹוָה וְיִשְׁמְרֶךָ. יָאֵר יְהֹוָה פָּנָיו אֵלֶיךָ וִיחֻנֶּךָּ. יִשָּׂא יְהֹוָה פָּנָיו אֵלֶיךָ וְיָשֵׂם לְךָ שָׁלוֹם.

yeh-vah-reh-kheh-KHAH ah-doh-NIGH veh-yeesh-meh-REH-khah. yah-ER ah-doh-NIGH pah-NAHV eh-LEH-khah vee-khoo-NEK-kah. yee-SAH ah-doh-NIGH pah-NAHV eh-LEH-khah veh-yah-SEM leh-KHAH shah-LOHM.

May the Lord bless you and keep you. May the Lord make His face shine upon you and be gracious to you. May the Lord lift up His face toward you and grant you peace.

Said for: Livelihood.

Recited before Kiddush.

Hebrew audio

מִזְמוֹר לְדָוִד, יְהֹוָה רֹעִי לֹא אֶחְסָר.

meez-MOHR leh-dah-VEED, ah-doh-NIGH roh-EE, loh ekh-SAHR.

A psalm of David. The Lord is my shepherd; I shall not lack.

בִּנְאוֹת דֶּשֶׁא יַרְבִּיצֵנִי, עַל מֵי מְנֻחוֹת יְנַהֲלֵנִי.

been-OHT DEH-sheh yahr-bee-TZEH-nee, ahl MAY meh-noo-KHOHT yeh-nah-hah-LEH-nee.

He lays me down in green pastures; He leads me beside still waters.

נַפְשִׁי יְשׁוֹבֵב, יַנְחֵנִי בְמַעְגְּלֵי צֶדֶק לְמַעַן שְׁמוֹ.

nahf-SHEE yeh-shoh-VEV, yahn-KHEH-nee veh-mah-geh-LAY TZEH-dek leh-MAH-ahn sheh-MOH.

He restores my soul; He guides me in paths of righteousness for His name's sake.

גַּם כִּי אֵלֵךְ בְּגֵיא צַלְמָוֶת לֹא אִירָא רָע כִּי אַתָּה עִמָּדִי, שִׁבְטְךָ וּמִשְׁעַנְתֶּךָ הֵמָּה יְנַחֲמֻנִי.

gahm KEE eh-LEKH beh-GAY tzahl-MAH-vet, loh ee-RAH RAH, kee ah-TAH ee-mah-DEE, sheev-teh-KHAH oo-meesh-ahn-TEH-khah, HEH-mah yeh-nah-khah-MOO-nee.

Though I walk through the valley of the shadow of death, I will fear no evil, for You are with me; Your rod and Your staff, they comfort me.

תַּעֲרֹךְ לְפָנַי שֻׁלְחָן נֶגֶד צֹרְרָי, דִּשַּׁנְתָּ בַשֶּׁמֶן רֹאשִׁי, כּוֹסִי רְוָיָה.

tah-ah-ROKH leh-fah-NIGH shool-KHAHN NEH-ged tzoh-reh-RIGH, dee-shahn-TAH vah-SHEH-men roh-SHEE, koh-SEE reh-vah-YAH.

You set a table before me in the presence of my foes; You anoint my head with oil; my cup overflows.

אַךְ טוֹב וָחֶסֶד יִרְדְּפוּנִי כָּל יְמֵי חַיָּי, וְשַׁבְתִּי בְּבֵית יְהֹוָה לְאֹרֶךְ יָמִים.

ahkh TOHV vah-KHEH-sed yeer-deh-FOO-nee kohl yeh-MAY khah-YIGH, veh-shahv-TEE beh-VAYT ah-doh-NIGH leh-OH-rekh yah-MEEM.

Surely goodness and kindness shall follow me all the days of my life, and I shall dwell in the house of the Lord for length of days.

Hebrew audio

Stand, holding the cup of wine. Say quietly:

וַיְהִי עֶרֶב וַיְהִי בֹקֶר

vah-yeh-HEE EH-rev vah-yeh-HEE VOH-ker

And it was evening and it was morning

Then aloud:

יוֹם הַשִּׁשִּׁי. וַיְכֻלּוּ הַשָּׁמַיִם וְהָאָרֶץ וְכָל צְבָאָם.

YOHM hah-shee-SHEE. vah-yeh-khoo-LOO hah-shah-MAH-yeem veh-hah-AH-retz veh-khohl tzeh-vah-AHM.

the sixth day. The heavens and the earth and all their host were completed.

וַיְכַל אֱלֹהִים בַּיּוֹם הַשְּׁבִיעִי מְלַאכְתּוֹ אֲשֶׁר עָשָׂה, וַיִּשְׁבֹּת בַּיּוֹם הַשְּׁבִיעִי מִכָּל מְלַאכְתּוֹ אֲשֶׁר עָשָׂה.

vah-yeh-KHAHL eh-loh-HEEM bah-YOHM hah-sheh-vee-EE meh-lahkh-TOH ah-SHER ah-SAH, vah-yeesh-BOHT bah-YOHM hah-sheh-vee-EE mee-KOHL meh-lahkh-TOH ah-SHER ah-SAH.

On the seventh day God completed His work that He had done, and He rested on the seventh day from all His work that He had done.

וַיְבָרֶךְ אֱלֹהִים אֶת יוֹם הַשְּׁבִיעִי וַיְקַדֵּשׁ אֹתוֹ, כִּי בוֹ שָׁבַת מִכָּל מְלַאכְתּוֹ אֲשֶׁר בָּרָא אֱלֹהִים לַעֲשׂוֹת.

vah-yeh-VAH-rekh eh-loh-HEEM eht YOHM hah-sheh-vee-EE vah-yeh-KAH-desh oh-TOH, kee VOH shah-VAHT mee-KOHL meh-lahkh-TOH, ah-SHER bah-RAH eh-loh-HEEM lah-ah-SOHT.

God blessed the seventh day and made it holy, for on it He rested from all His work that God had created to make.

סַבְרִי מָרָנָן.

sahv-REE mah-rah-NAHN.

With your attention, my masters.

Those present respond:

לְחַיִּים.

leh-khah-YEEM.

To life!

בָּרוּךְ אַתָּה יְהֹוָה אֱלֹהֵינוּ מֶלֶךְ הָעוֹלָם, בּוֹרֵא פְּרִי הַגֶּפֶן.

bah-ROOKH ah-TAH ah-doh-NIGH eh-loh-HAY-noo MEH-lekh hah-oh-LAHM, boh-REH peh-REE hah-GEH-fen.

Blessed are You, Lord our God, King of the universe, who creates the fruit of the vine.

בָּרוּךְ אַתָּה יְהֹוָה אֱלֹהֵינוּ מֶלֶךְ הָעוֹלָם, אֲשֶׁר קִדְּשָׁנוּ בְּמִצְוֹתָיו וְרָצָה בָנוּ, וְשַׁבַּת קָדְשׁוֹ בְּאַהֲבָה וּבְרָצוֹן הִנְחִילָנוּ, זִכָּרוֹן לְמַעֲשֵׂה בְרֵאשִׁית. תְּחִלָּה לְמִקְרָאֵי קֹדֶשׁ, זֵכֶר לִיצִיאַת מִצְרָיִם. כִּי בָנוּ בָחַרְתָּ וְאוֹתָנוּ קִדַּשְׁתָּ מִכָּל הָעַמִּים, וְשַׁבַּת קָדְשְׁךָ בְּאַהֲבָה וּבְרָצוֹן הִנְחַלְתָּנוּ. בָּרוּךְ אַתָּה יְהֹוָה, מְקַדֵּשׁ הַשַּׁבָּת.

bah-ROOKH ah-TAH ah-doh-NIGH eh-loh-HAY-noo MEH-lekh hah-oh-LAHM, ah-SHER kee-deh-SHAH-noo beh-meetz-voh-TAHV veh-rah-TZAH VAH-noo, veh-shah-BAHT kohd-SHOH beh-ah-hah-VAH oov-rah-TZOHN heen-khee-LAH-noo, zee-kah-ROHN leh-mah-ah-SEH veh-reh-SHEET. teh-khee-LAH leh-meek-rah-AY KOH-desh, ZEH-kher lee-tzee-AHT meetz-RAH-yeem. kee VAH-noo vah-khahr-TAH veh-oh-TAH-noo kee-dahsh-TAH mee-KOHL hah-ah-MEEM, veh-shah-BAHT kohd-sheh-KHAH beh-ah-hah-VAH oov-rah-TZOHN heen-khahl-TAH-noo. bah-ROOKH ah-TAH ah-doh-NIGH, meh-kah-DESH hah-shah-BAHT.

Blessed are You, Lord our God, King of the universe, who sanctified us with His commandments and took delight in us, and in love and favor gave us His holy Shabbat as a heritage, a remembrance of the work of creation. It is the first of the holy festivals, a remembrance of the Exodus from Egypt. For You chose us and sanctified us from all the nations, and in love and favor gave us Your holy Shabbat as a heritage. Blessed are You, Lord, who sanctifies the Shabbat.

Mizrahi text: “teh-khee-LAH leh-meek-rah-AY KOH-desh” without the Ashkenazi “kee HOO YOHM,” and “hah-GEH-fen” rather than “hah-GAH-fen.”

After washing, while raising the hands, some families add Psalm 134:2:

שְׂאוּ יְדֵכֶם קֹדֶשׁ וּבָרְכוּ אֶת יְהֹוָה.

seh-OO yeh-deh-KHEM KOH-desh oo-vah-reh-KHOO eht ah-doh-NIGH.

Lift up your hands in holiness and bless the Lord.

Blessing:

בָּרוּךְ אַתָּה יְהֹוָה אֱלֹהֵינוּ מֶלֶךְ הָעוֹלָם, אֲשֶׁר קִדְּשָׁנוּ בְּמִצְוֹתָיו וְצִוָּנוּ עַל נְטִילַת יָדַיִם.

bah-ROOKH ah-TAH ah-doh-NIGH eh-loh-HAY-noo MEH-lekh hah-oh-LAHM, ah-SHER kee-deh-SHAH-noo beh-meetz-voh-TAHV veh-tzee-VAH-noo ahl neh-tee-LAHT yah-DAH-yeem.

Blessed are You, Lord our God, King of the universe, who sanctified us with His commandments and commanded us concerning the washing of the hands.

No speaking between the blessing and HaMotzi.

Hebrew audio

Holding the two loaves (Psalm 145:15–16):

עֵינֵי כֹל אֵלֶיךָ יְשַׂבֵּרוּ, וְאַתָּה נוֹתֵן לָהֶם אֶת אָכְלָם בְּעִתּוֹ. פּוֹתֵחַ אֶת יָדֶךָ, וּמַשְׂבִּיעַ לְכָל חַי רָצוֹן.

ay-NAY khohl eh-LEH-khah yeh-sah-BEH-roo, veh-ah-TAH noh-TEN lah-HEM eht ohkh-LAHM beh-ee-TOH. poh-TEH-ahkh eht yah-DEH-khah, oo-mahs-BEE-ah leh-KHOHL KHIGH rah-TZOHN.

The eyes of all look to You, and You give them their food in its season. You open Your hand and satisfy every living thing with favor.

בָּרוּךְ אַתָּה יְהֹוָה אֱלֹהֵינוּ מֶלֶךְ הָעוֹלָם, הַמּוֹצִיא לֶחֶם מִן הָאָרֶץ.

Blessed are You, Lord our God, King of the universe, who brings forth bread from the earth.

Full Mizrahi (Edot HaMizrach) text. First wash the fingertips (mayim acharonim). Mizrahi siddurim open with a kabbalistic meditation (LeShem Yichud), omitted here. Some communities say Psalm 126 before Birkat HaMazon on Shabbat (see Tehillim).

Hebrew audio: Shabbat version, without the guest’s blessing for the host

Psalm 67 and opening verses

לַמְנַצֵּחַ בִּנְגִינֹת מִזְמוֹר שִׁיר. אֱלֹהִים יְחָנֵּנוּ וִיבָרְכֵנוּ, יָאֵר פָּנָיו אִתָּנוּ סֶלָה. לָדַעַת בָּאָרֶץ דַּרְכֶּךָ, בְּכָל גּוֹיִם יְשׁוּעָתֶךָ. יוֹדוּךָ עַמִּים אֱלֹהִים, יוֹדוּךָ עַמִּים כֻּלָּם. יִשְׂמְחוּ וִירַנְּנוּ לְאֻמִּים, כִּי תִשְׁפֹּט עַמִּים מִישׁוֹר, וּלְאֻמִּים בָּאָרֶץ תַּנְחֵם סֶלָה. יוֹדוּךָ עַמִּים אֱלֹהִים, יוֹדוּךָ עַמִּים כֻּלָּם. אֶרֶץ נָתְנָה יְבוּלָהּ, יְבָרְכֵנוּ אֱלֹהִים אֱלֹהֵינוּ. יְבָרְכֵנוּ אֱלֹהִים, וְיִירְאוּ אֹתוֹ כָּל אַפְסֵי אָרֶץ.

lahm-nah-TZEH-ahkh been-gee-NOHT meez-MOHR SHEER. eh-loh-HEEM yeh-khah-NEH-noo vee-vah-reh-KHEH-noo, yah-ER pah-NAHV ee-TAH-noo SEH-lah. lah-DAH-aht bah-AH-retz dahr-KEH-khah, beh-KHOHL goh-YEEM yeh-shoo-ah-TEH-khah. yoh-DOO-khah ah-MEEM eh-loh-HEEM, yoh-DOO-khah ah-MEEM koo-LAHM. yees-meh-KHOO vee-rah-neh-NOO leh-oo-MEEM, kee teesh-POHT ah-MEEM mee-SHOHR, ool-oo-MEEM bah-AH-retz tahn-KHEM SEH-lah. yoh-DOO-khah ah-MEEM eh-loh-HEEM, yoh-DOO-khah ah-MEEM koo-LAHM. EH-retz nah-teh-NAH yeh-voo-LAH, yeh-vah-reh-KHEH-noo eh-loh-HEEM eh-loh-HAY-noo. yeh-vah-reh-KHEH-noo eh-loh-HEEM, veh-yeer-OO oh-TOH kohl ahf-SAY AH-retz.

For the conductor, with instrumental music, a psalm, a song. May God be gracious to us and bless us; may He make His face shine upon us, Selah. That Your way be known on earth, Your salvation among all nations. The peoples will thank You, God; all the peoples will thank You. Nations will rejoice and sing, for You judge the peoples fairly and guide the nations on earth, Selah. The peoples will thank You, God; all the peoples will thank You. The earth has yielded its produce; may God, our God, bless us. May God bless us, and may all the ends of the earth revere Him.

אֲבָרְכָה אֶת יְהֹוָה בְּכָל עֵת, תָּמִיד תְּהִלָּתוֹ בְּפִי. סוֹף דָּבָר הַכֹּל נִשְׁמָע, אֶת הָאֱלֹהִים יְרָא וְאֶת מִצְוֹתָיו שְׁמוֹר, כִּי זֶה כָּל הָאָדָם. תְּהִלַּת יְהֹוָה יְדַבֶּר פִּי, וִיבָרֵךְ כָּל בָּשָׂר שֵׁם קָדְשׁוֹ לְעוֹלָם וָעֶד. וַאֲנַחְנוּ נְבָרֵךְ יָהּ מֵעַתָּה וְעַד עוֹלָם, הַלְלוּיָהּ. וַיְדַבֵּר אֵלַי, זֶה הַשֻּׁלְחָן אֲשֶׁר לִפְנֵי יְהֹוָה.

ah-vah-reh-KHAH eht ah-doh-NIGH beh-KHOHL ET, tah-MEED teh-hee-lah-TOH beh-FEE. SOHF dah-VAHR hah-KOHL neesh-MAH, eht hah-eh-loh-HEEM yeh-RAH veh-eht meetz-voh-TAHV sheh-MOHR, kee ZEH kohl hah-ah-DAHM. teh-hee-LAHT ah-doh-NIGH yeh-dah-BER PEE, vee-vah-REKH kohl bah-SAHR SHEM kohd-SHOH leh-oh-LAHM vah-ED. vah-ah-NAKH-noo neh-vah-REKH YAH meh-ah-TAH veh-AHD oh-LAHM, hahl-loo-YAH. vah-yeh-dah-BER eh-LIGH, ZEH hah-shool-KHAHN ah-SHER leef-NAY ah-doh-NIGH.

I will bless the Lord at all times; His praise is always in my mouth. The end of the matter, all having been heard: fear God and keep His commandments, for that is the whole of man. My mouth will speak the Lord's praise, and all flesh will bless His holy name forever and ever. And we will bless the Lord from now and forever, Halleluyah. And he said to me: this is the table that is before the Lord.

Zimmun (three or more who ate together)

Leader:

בִּרְשׁוּתְכֶם.

beer-shoot-KHEM.

With your permission.

Others respond:

שָׁמַיִם.

shah-MAH-yeem.

Heaven's permission.

Leader (on Shabbat some add “oo-veer-SHOOT shah-BAHT mahl-keh-TAH”; with ten, say “neh-vah-REKH eh-loh-HAY-noo”):

בִּרְשׁוּת מַלְכָּא עִלָּאָה קַדִּישָׁא, וּבִרְשׁוּתְכֶם, נְבָרֵךְ שֶׁאָכַלְנוּ מִשֶּׁלּוֹ.

beer-SHOOT mahl-KAH ee-lah-AH kah-dee-SHAH, oo-veer-shoot-KHEM, neh-vah-REKH sheh-ah-KHAHL-noo mee-sheh-LOH.

With the permission of the supreme holy King, and with your permission, let us bless the One whose food we have eaten.

Others respond, and the leader repeats:

בָּרוּךְ שֶׁאָכַלְנוּ מִשֶּׁלּוֹ, וּבְטוּבוֹ הַגָּדוֹל תָּמִיד חָיִינוּ.

bah-ROOKH sheh-ah-KHAHL-noo mee-sheh-LOH, oov-too-VOH hah-gah-DOHL tah-MEED khah-YEE-noo.

Blessed is the One whose food we have eaten, and through whose great goodness we always live.

First blessing — HaZan

בָּרוּךְ אַתָּה יְהֹוָה אֱלֹהֵינוּ מֶלֶךְ הָעוֹלָם, הָאֵל הַזָּן אוֹתָנוּ וְאֶת הָעוֹלָם כֻּלּוֹ בְּטוּבוֹ, בְּחֵן בְּחֶסֶד בְּרֶוַח וּבְרַחֲמִים רַבִּים. נֹתֵן לֶחֶם לְכָל בָּשָׂר, כִּי לְעוֹלָם חַסְדּוֹ. וּבְטוּבוֹ הַגָּדוֹל תָּמִיד לֹא חָסַר לָנוּ, וְאַל יֶחְסַר לָנוּ מָזוֹן תָּמִיד לְעוֹלָם וָעֶד. כִּי הוּא אֵל זָן וּמְפַרְנֵס לַכֹּל, וְשֻׁלְחָנוֹ עָרוּךְ לַכֹּל, וְהִתְקִין מִחְיָה וּמָזוֹן לְכָל בְּרִיּוֹתָיו אֲשֶׁר בָּרָא, בְרַחֲמָיו וּבְרֹב חֲסָדָיו, כָּאָמוּר: פּוֹתֵחַ אֶת יָדֶךָ, וּמַשְׂבִּיעַ לְכָל חַי רָצוֹן. בָּרוּךְ אַתָּה יְהֹוָה, הַזָּן אֶת הַכֹּל.

bah-ROOKH ah-TAH ah-doh-NIGH eh-loh-HAY-noo MEH-lekh hah-oh-LAHM, hah-EL hah-ZAHN oh-TAH-noo veh-eht hah-oh-LAHM koo-LOH beh-too-VOH, beh-KHEN beh-KHEH-sed beh-REH-vahkh oov-rah-khah-MEEM rah-BEEM. noh-TEN LEH-khem leh-KHOHL bah-SAHR, kee leh-oh-LAHM khahs-DOH. oov-too-VOH hah-gah-DOHL tah-MEED loh khah-SAHR LAH-noo, veh-AHL yekh-SAHR LAH-noo mah-ZOHN tah-MEED leh-oh-LAHM vah-ED. kee HOO EL zahn oom-fahr-NES lah-KOHL, veh-shool-khah-NOH ah-ROOKH lah-KOHL, veh-heet-KEEN meekh-YAH oo-mah-ZOHN leh-KHOHL beh-ree-yoh-TAHV ah-SHER bah-RAH, veh-rah-khah-MAHV oov-ROHV khah-sah-DAHV, kah-ah-MOOR: poh-TEH-ahkh eht yah-DEH-khah, oo-mahs-BEE-ah leh-KHOHL KHIGH rah-TZOHN. bah-ROOKH ah-TAH ah-doh-NIGH, hah-ZAHN eht hah-KOHL.

Blessed are You, Lord our God, King of the universe, the God who nourishes us and the whole world in His goodness, with grace, kindness, abundance and great compassion. He gives bread to all flesh, for His kindness is forever. Through His great goodness we have never lacked, and may we never lack food, forever and ever. For He is God who feeds and sustains all, His table is set for all, and He prepared sustenance and food for all the creatures He created, in His compassion and abundant kindness, as it is said: You open Your hand and satisfy every living thing with favor. Blessed are You, Lord, who feeds all.

Second blessing — HaAretz

נוֹדֶה לְּךָ יְהֹוָה אֱלֹהֵינוּ, עַל שֶׁהִנְחַלְתָּ לַאֲבוֹתֵינוּ אֶרֶץ חֶמְדָּה טוֹבָה וּרְחָבָה, בְּרִית וְתוֹרָה, חַיִּים וּמָזוֹן. עַל שֶׁהוֹצֵאתָנוּ מֵאֶרֶץ מִצְרַיִם, וּפְדִיתָנוּ מִבֵּית עֲבָדִים. וְעַל בְּרִיתְךָ שֶׁחָתַמְתָּ בִּבְשָׂרֵנוּ, וְעַל תּוֹרָתְךָ שֶׁלִּמַּדְתָּנוּ, וְעַל חֻקֵּי רְצוֹנָךְ שֶׁהוֹדַעְתָּנוּ, וְעַל חַיִּים וּמָזוֹן שֶׁאַתָּה זָן וּמְפַרְנֵס אוֹתָנוּ.

noh-DEH leh-KHAH ah-doh-NIGH eh-loh-HAY-noo, ahl sheh-heen-khahl-TAH lah-ah-voh-TAY-noo EH-retz khem-DAH toh-VAH oor-khah-VAH, beh-REET veh-toh-RAH, khah-YEEM oo-mah-ZOHN. ahl sheh-hoh-tzeh-TAH-noo meh-EH-retz meetz-RAH-yeem, oo-feh-dee-TAH-noo mee-BAYT ah-vah-DEEM. veh-ahl beh-reet-KHAH sheh-khah-tahm-TAH beev-sah-REH-noo, veh-ahl toh-rah-teh-KHAH sheh-lee-mahd-TAH-noo, veh-ahl khoo-KAY reh-tzoh-NAHKH sheh-hoh-dah-TAH-noo, veh-ahl khah-YEEM oo-mah-ZOHN sheh-ah-TAH zahn oom-fahr-NES oh-TAH-noo.

We thank You, Lord our God, for giving our fathers a desirable, good and spacious land as a heritage, covenant and Torah, life and food; for bringing us out of the land of Egypt and redeeming us from the house of bondage; for Your covenant sealed in our flesh, for Your Torah that You taught us, for the laws of Your will that You made known to us, and for the life and food with which You nourish and sustain us.

עַל הַכֹּל יְהֹוָה אֱלֹהֵינוּ אֲנַחְנוּ מוֹדִים לָךְ וּמְבָרְכִים אֶת שְׁמָךְ, כָּאָמוּר: וְאָכַלְתָּ וְשָׂבָעְתָּ וּבֵרַכְתָּ אֶת יְהֹוָה אֱלֹהֶיךָ עַל הָאָרֶץ הַטֹּבָה אֲשֶׁר נָתַן לָךְ. בָּרוּךְ אַתָּה יְהֹוָה, עַל הָאָרֶץ וְעַל הַמָּזוֹן.

ahl hah-KOHL ah-doh-NIGH eh-loh-HAY-noo ah-NAKH-noo moh-DEEM LAHKH oom-vah-reh-KHEEM eht sheh-MAHKH, kah-ah-MOOR: veh-ah-khahl-TAH veh-sah-vah-TAH oo-veh-rahkh-TAH eht ah-doh-NIGH eh-loh-HEH-khah ahl hah-AH-retz hah-toh-VAH ah-SHER NAH-tahn LAHKH. bah-ROOKH ah-TAH ah-doh-NIGH, ahl hah-AH-retz veh-ahl hah-mah-ZOHN.

For all this, Lord our God, we thank You and bless Your name, as it is said: You shall eat and be satisfied, and bless the Lord your God for the good land He has given you. Blessed are You, Lord, for the land and for the food.

Third blessing — Boneh Yerushalayim

רַחֵם יְהֹוָה אֱלֹהֵינוּ עָלֵינוּ וְעַל יִשְׂרָאֵל עַמָּךְ, וְעַל יְרוּשָׁלַיִם עִירָךְ, וְעַל הַר צִיּוֹן מִשְׁכַּן כְּבוֹדָךְ, וְעַל הֵיכָלָךְ, וְעַל מְעוֹנָךְ, וְעַל דְּבִירָךְ, וְעַל הַבַּיִת הַגָּדוֹל וְהַקָּדוֹשׁ שֶׁנִּקְרָא שִׁמְךָ עָלָיו. אָבִינוּ, רְעֵנוּ, זוּנֵנוּ, פַּרְנְסֵנוּ, כַּלְכְּלֵנוּ, הַרְוִיחֵנוּ, הַרְוַח לָנוּ מְהֵרָה מִכָּל צָרוֹתֵינוּ. וְאַל תַּצְרִיכֵנוּ יְהֹוָה אֱלֹהֵינוּ לִידֵי מַתְּנוֹת בָּשָׂר וָדָם וְלֹא לִידֵי הַלְוָאָתָם, אֶלָּא לְיָדְךָ הַמְּלֵאָה וְהָרְחָבָה, הָעֲשִׁירָה וְהַפְּתוּחָה. יְהִי רָצוֹן שֶׁלֹּא נֵבוֹשׁ בָּעוֹלָם הַזֶּה, וְלֹא נִכָּלֵם לָעוֹלָם הַבָּא, וּמַלְכוּת בֵּית דָּוִד מְשִׁיחָךְ תַּחֲזִירֶנָּה לִמְקוֹמָהּ בִּמְהֵרָה בְיָמֵינוּ.

rah-KHEM ah-doh-NIGH eh-loh-HAY-noo ah-LAY-noo veh-ahl yees-rah-EL ah-MAHKH, veh-ahl yeh-roo-shah-LAH-yeem ee-RAHKH, veh-ahl hahr tzee-YOHN meesh-KAHN keh-voh-DAHKH, veh-ahl hay-khah-LAHKH, veh-ahl meh-oh-NAHKH, veh-ahl deh-vee-RAHKH, veh-ahl hah-BAH-yeet hah-gah-DOHL veh-hah-kah-DOHSH sheh-neek-RAH sheem-KHAH ah-LAHV. ah-VEE-noo, reh-EH-noo, zoo-NEH-noo, pahr-neh-SEH-noo, kahl-keh-LEH-noo, hahr-vee-KHEH-noo, hahr-VAKH LAH-noo meh-heh-RAH mee-KOHL tzah-roh-TAY-noo. veh-AHL tahtz-ree-KHEH-noo ah-doh-NIGH eh-loh-HAY-noo lee-DAY mah-teh-NOHT bah-SAHR vah-DAHM veh-LOH lee-DAY hahl-vah-ah-TAHM, eh-LAH leh-yah-deh-KHAH hah-meh-leh-AH veh-hah-reh-khah-VAH, hah-ah-shee-RAH veh-hah-peh-too-KHAH. yeh-HEE rah-TZOHN sheh-LOH neh-VOHSH bah-oh-LAHM hah-ZEH, veh-LOH nee-kah-LEM lah-oh-LAHM hah-BAH, oo-mahl-KHOOT BAYT dah-VEED meh-shee-KHAHKH tah-khah-zee-REH-nah leem-koh-MAH beem-heh-RAH veh-yah-MAY-noo.

Have mercy, Lord our God, on us and on Israel Your people, on Jerusalem Your city, on Mount Zion the dwelling of Your glory, on Your palace, Your abode and Your sanctuary, and on the great and holy House called by Your name. Our Father, tend us, feed us, sustain us, support us, relieve us, and grant us quick relief from all our troubles. Lord our God, do not make us dependent on the gifts or loans of flesh and blood, but only on Your full, generous, rich and open hand. May it be Your will that we not be shamed in this world nor disgraced in the world to come, and restore the kingdom of the house of David Your anointed to its place, speedily in our days.

On Shabbat, add Retzeh:

רְצֵה וְהַחֲלִיצֵנוּ יְהֹוָה אֱלֹהֵינוּ בְּמִצְוֹתֶיךָ, וּבְמִצְוַת יוֹם הַשְּׁבִיעִי, הַשַּׁבָּת הַגָּדוֹל וְהַקָּדוֹשׁ הַזֶּה, כִּי יוֹם גָּדוֹל וְקָדוֹשׁ הוּא מִלְּפָנֶיךָ, נִשְׁבּוֹת בּוֹ וְנָנוּחַ בּוֹ וְנִתְעַנֵּג בּוֹ כְּמִצְוַת חֻקֵּי רְצוֹנָךְ. וְאַל תְּהִי צָרָה וְיָגוֹן בְּיוֹם מְנוּחָתֵנוּ, וְהַרְאֵנוּ בְּנֶחָמַת צִיּוֹן בִּמְהֵרָה בְיָמֵינוּ, כִּי אַתָּה הוּא בַּעַל הַנֶּחָמוֹת. וַהֲגַם שֶׁאָכַלְנוּ וְשָׁתִינוּ, חָרְבַּן בֵּיתְךָ הַגָּדוֹל וְהַקָּדוֹשׁ לֹא שָׁכַחְנוּ, אַל תִּשְׁכָּחֵנוּ לָנֶצַח וְאַל תִּזְנָחֵנוּ לָעַד, כִּי אֵל מֶלֶךְ גָּדוֹל וְקָדוֹשׁ אָתָּה.

reh-TZEH veh-hah-khah-lee-TZEH-noo ah-doh-NIGH eh-loh-HAY-noo beh-meetz-voh-TEH-khah, oov-meetz-VAHT YOHM hah-sheh-vee-EE, hah-shah-BAHT hah-gah-DOHL veh-hah-kah-DOHSH hah-ZEH, kee YOHM gah-DOHL veh-kah-DOHSH HOO mee-leh-fah-NEH-khah, neesh-BOHT BOH veh-nah-NOO-ahkh BOH veh-neet-ah-NEG BOH keh-meetz-VAHT khoo-KAY reh-tzoh-NAHKH. veh-AHL teh-HEE tzah-RAH veh-yah-GOHN beh-YOHM meh-noo-khah-TEH-noo, veh-hahr-EH-noo beh-neh-khah-MAHT tzee-YOHN beem-heh-RAH veh-yah-MAY-noo, kee ah-TAH HOO BAH-ahl hah-neh-khah-MOHT. vah-hah-GAHM sheh-ah-KHAHL-noo veh-shah-TEE-noo, khohr-BAHN bayt-KHAH hah-gah-DOHL veh-hah-kah-DOHSH loh shah-KHAKH-noo, ahl teesh-kah-KHEH-noo lah-NEH-tzahkh veh-AHL teez-nah-KHEH-noo lah-AHD, kee EL MEH-lekh gah-DOHL veh-kah-DOHSH AH-tah.

Be pleased, Lord our God, to strengthen us through Your commandments and through the commandment of the seventh day, this great and holy Shabbat, for it is a great and holy day before You, to cease work and rest on it and delight in it as commanded by the laws of Your will. Let there be no distress or sorrow on our day of rest, and show us the consolation of Zion speedily in our days, for You are the Master of consolations. And though we have eaten and drunk, we have not forgotten the destruction of Your great and holy House; do not forget us forever nor abandon us for all time, for You are God, a great and holy King.

וְתִבְנֶה יְרוּשָׁלַיִם עִירָךְ בִּמְהֵרָה בְיָמֵינוּ. בָּרוּךְ אַתָּה יְהֹוָה, בּוֹנֵה יְרוּשָׁלָיִם.

veh-teev-NEH yeh-roo-shah-LAH-yeem ee-RAHKH beem-heh-RAH veh-yah-MAY-noo. bah-ROOKH ah-TAH ah-doh-NIGH, boh-NEH yeh-roo-shah-LAH-yeem.

Rebuild Jerusalem Your city speedily in our days. Blessed are You, Lord, who builds Jerusalem.

Quietly: ah-MEN.

Fourth blessing — HaTov VeHaMetiv

בָּרוּךְ אַתָּה יְהֹוָה אֱלֹהֵינוּ מֶלֶךְ הָעוֹלָם, לָעַד הָאֵל אָבִינוּ, מַלְכֵּנוּ, אַדִּירֵנוּ, בּוֹרְאֵנוּ, גּוֹאֲלֵנוּ, קְדוֹשֵׁנוּ קְדוֹשׁ יַעֲקֹב, רוֹעֵנוּ רוֹעֵה יִשְׂרָאֵל, הַמֶּלֶךְ הַטּוֹב וְהַמֵּטִיב לַכֹּל, שֶׁבְּכָל יוֹם וָיוֹם הוּא הֵיטִיב לָנוּ, הוּא מֵיטִיב לָנוּ, הוּא יֵיטִיב לָנוּ. הוּא גְמָלָנוּ, הוּא גוֹמְלֵנוּ, הוּא יִגְמְלֵנוּ לָעַד, חֵן וָחֶסֶד וְרַחֲמִים וְרֶוַח וְהַצָּלָה וְכָל טוֹב.

bah-ROOKH ah-TAH ah-doh-NIGH eh-loh-HAY-noo MEH-lekh hah-oh-LAHM, lah-AHD hah-EL ah-VEE-noo, mahl-KEH-noo, ah-dee-REH-noo, boh-reh-EH-noo, goh-ah-LEH-noo, keh-doh-SHEH-noo keh-DOHSH yah-ah-KOHV, roh-EH-noo roh-EH yees-rah-EL, hah-MEH-lekh hah-TOHV veh-hah-meh-TEEV lah-KOHL, sheh-beh-KHOHL yohm vah-YOHM HOO hay-TEEV LAH-noo, HOO may-TEEV LAH-noo, HOO yay-TEEV LAH-noo. HOO geh-mah-LAH-noo, HOO goh-meh-LEH-noo, HOO yeeg-meh-LEH-noo lah-AHD, KHEN vah-KHEH-sed veh-rah-khah-MEEM veh-REH-vahkh veh-hah-tzah-LAH veh-khohl TOHV.

Blessed are You, Lord our God, King of the universe, forever the God who is our Father, our King, our Mighty One, our Creator, our Redeemer, our Holy One, the Holy One of Jacob, our Shepherd, the Shepherd of Israel, the good King who does good to all. Every single day He has done good for us, does good for us and will do good for us. He has bestowed, bestows and will forever bestow on us grace, kindness, compassion, relief, rescue and every good.

HaRachaman

הָרַחֲמָן הוּא יִשְׁתַּבַּח עַל כִּסֵּא כְבוֹדוֹ.

hah-rah-khah-MAHN HOO yeesh-tah-BAKH ahl kee-SEH keh-voh-DOH.

May the Merciful One be praised on His throne of glory.

הָרַחֲמָן הוּא יִשְׁתַּבַּח בַּשָּׁמַיִם וּבָאָרֶץ.

hah-rah-khah-MAHN HOO yeesh-tah-BAKH bah-shah-MAH-yeem oo-vah-AH-retz.

May the Merciful One be praised in heaven and on earth.

הָרַחֲמָן הוּא יִשְׁתַּבַּח בָּנוּ לְדוֹר דּוֹרִים.

hah-rah-khah-MAHN HOO yeesh-tah-BAKH BAH-noo leh-DOHR doh-REEM.

May the Merciful One be praised through us for all generations.

הָרַחֲמָן הוּא קֶרֶן לְעַמּוֹ יָרִים.

hah-rah-khah-MAHN HOO KEH-ren leh-ah-MOH yah-REEM.

May the Merciful One raise the strength of His people.

הָרַחֲמָן הוּא יִתְפָּאַר בָּנוּ לְנֵצַח נְצָחִים.

hah-rah-khah-MAHN HOO yeet-pah-AHR BAH-noo leh-NEH-tzahkh neh-tzah-KHEEM.

May the Merciful One be glorified through us for all eternity.

הָרַחֲמָן הוּא יְפַרְנְסֵנוּ בְּכָבוֹד וְלֹא בְבִזּוּי, בְּהֶתֵּר וְלֹא בְאִסּוּר, בְּנַחַת וְלֹא בְצַעַר.

hah-rah-khah-MAHN HOO yeh-fahr-neh-SEH-noo beh-khah-VOHD veh-LOH veh-vee-ZOOY, beh-heh-TER veh-LOH beh-ee-SOOR, beh-NAH-khaht veh-LOH veh-TZAH-ar.

May the Merciful One sustain us with honor and not disgrace, lawfully and not unlawfully, in ease and not in hardship.

הָרַחֲמָן הוּא יִתֵּן שָׁלוֹם בֵּינֵינוּ.

hah-rah-khah-MAHN HOO yee-TEN shah-LOHM bay-NAY-noo.

May the Merciful One place peace among us.

הָרַחֲמָן הוּא יִשְׁלַח בְּרָכָה רְוָחָה וְהַצְלָחָה בְּכָל מַעֲשֵׂה יָדֵינוּ.

hah-rah-khah-MAHN HOO yeesh-LAKH beh-rah-KHAH reh-vah-KHAH veh-hahtz-lah-KHAH beh-KHOHL mah-ah-SEH yah-DAY-noo.

May the Merciful One send blessing, relief and success to all the work of our hands.

הָרַחֲמָן הוּא יַצְלִיחַ אֶת דְּרָכֵינוּ.

hah-rah-khah-MAHN HOO yahtz-LEE-ahkh eht deh-rah-KHAY-noo.

May the Merciful One make our ways succeed.

הָרַחֲמָן הוּא יִשְׁבֹּר עֹל גָּלוּת מְהֵרָה מֵעַל צַוָּארֵנוּ.

hah-rah-khah-MAHN HOO yeesh-BOHR ohl gah-LOOT meh-heh-RAH meh-AHL tzah-vah-REH-noo.

May the Merciful One soon break the yoke of exile from our necks.

הָרַחֲמָן הוּא יוֹלִיכֵנוּ מְהֵרָה קוֹמְמִיּוּת בְּאַרְצֵנוּ.

hah-rah-khah-MAHN HOO yoh-lee-KHEH-noo meh-heh-RAH koh-meh-mee-YOOT beh-ahr-TZEH-noo.

May the Merciful One soon lead us upright in our land.

הָרַחֲמָן הוּא יִרְפָּאֵנוּ רְפוּאָה שְׁלֵמָה, רְפוּאַת הַנֶּפֶשׁ וּרְפוּאַת הַגּוּף.

hah-rah-khah-MAHN HOO yeer-pah-EH-noo reh-foo-AH sheh-leh-MAH, reh-foo-AHT hah-NEH-fesh oor-foo-AHT hah-GOOF.

May the Merciful One heal us completely, in soul and in body.

הָרַחֲמָן הוּא יִפְתַּח לָנוּ אֶת יָדוֹ הָרְחָבָה.

hah-rah-khah-MAHN HOO yeef-TAKH LAH-noo eht yah-DOH hah-reh-khah-VAH.

May the Merciful One open His generous hand to us.

הָרַחֲמָן הוּא יְבָרֵךְ כָּל אֶחָד וְאֶחָד מִמֶּנּוּ בִּשְׁמוֹ הַגָּדוֹל, כְּמוֹ שֶׁנִּתְבָּרְכוּ אֲבוֹתֵינוּ אַבְרָהָם יִצְחָק וְיַעֲקֹב, בַּכֹּל מִכֹּל כֹּל, כֵּן יְבָרֵךְ אוֹתָנוּ יַחַד בְּרָכָה שְׁלֵמָה, וְכֵן יְהִי רָצוֹן וְנֹאמַר אָמֵן.

hah-rah-khah-MAHN HOO yeh-vah-REKH kohl eh-KHAHD veh-eh-KHAHD mee-MEH-noo beesh-MOH hah-gah-DOHL, keh-MOH sheh-neet-bah-reh-KHOO ah-voh-TAY-noo, ahv-rah-HAHM yeetz-KHAHK veh-yah-ah-KOHV, bah-KOHL mee-KOHL KOHL, KEN yeh-vah-REKH oh-TAH-noo YAH-khahd beh-rah-KHAH sheh-leh-MAH, veh-KHEN yeh-HEE rah-TZOHN veh-noh-MAHR ah-MEN.

May the Merciful One bless each one of us by His great name, as our fathers Abraham, Isaac and Jacob were blessed in all things, from all things, with all things; so may He bless all of us together with a complete blessing. So may it be His will, and let us say Amen.

הָרַחֲמָן הוּא יִפְרֹשׂ עָלֵינוּ סֻכַּת שְׁלוֹמוֹ.

hah-rah-khah-MAHN HOO yeef-ROHS ah-LAY-noo soo-KAHT sheh-loh-MOH.

May the Merciful One spread over us His shelter of peace.

הָרַחֲמָן הוּא יִטַּע תּוֹרָתוֹ וְאַהֲבָתוֹ בְּלִבֵּנוּ, וְתִהְיֶה יִרְאָתוֹ עַל פָּנֵינוּ לְבִלְתִּי נֶחֱטָא, וְיִהְיוּ כָל מַעֲשֵׂינוּ לְשֵׁם שָׁמָיִם.

hah-rah-khah-MAHN HOO yee-TAH toh-rah-TOH veh-ah-hah-vah-TOH beh-lee-BEH-noo, veh-teeh-YEH yeer-ah-TOH ahl pah-NAY-noo leh-veel-TEE neh-kheh-TAH, veh-yeeh-YOO khohl mah-ah-SAY-noo leh-SHEM shah-MAH-yeem.

May the Merciful One plant His Torah and His love in our hearts, and may reverence for Him be before us so that we do not sin, and may all our deeds be for the sake of Heaven.

On Shabbat, add:

הָרַחֲמָן הוּא יַנְחִילֵנוּ עוֹלָם שֶׁכֻּלּוֹ שַׁבָּת וּמְנוּחָה לְחַיֵּי הָעוֹלָמִים.

hah-rah-khah-MAHN HOO yahn-khee-LEH-noo oh-LAHM sheh-koo-LOH shah-BAHT oom-noo-KHAH leh-khah-YAY hah-oh-lah-MEEM.

May the Merciful One grant us a world that is entirely Shabbat and rest, for life everlasting.

A guest adds this blessing for the host:

הָרַחֲמָן הוּא יְבָרֵךְ אֶת הַשֻּׁלְחָן הַזֶּה שֶׁאָכַלְנוּ עָלָיו, וִיסַדֵּר בּוֹ כָּל מַעֲדַנֵּי עוֹלָם, וְיִהְיֶה כְשֻׁלְחָנוֹ שֶׁל אַבְרָהָם אָבִינוּ עָלָיו הַשָּׁלוֹם. כָּל רָעֵב מִמֶּנּוּ יֹאכַל, וְכָל צָמֵא מִמֶּנּוּ יִשְׁתֶּה, וְאַל יֶחְסַר מִמֶּנּוּ כָּל טוֹב לָעַד וּלְעוֹלְמֵי עוֹלָמִים, אָמֵן. הָרַחֲמָן הוּא יְבָרֵךְ אֶת בַּעַל הַבַּיִת הַזֶּה וּבַעַל הַסְּעֻדָּה הַזֹּאת, הוּא וּבָנָיו וְאִשְׁתּוֹ וְכָל אֲשֶׁר לוֹ, בְּבָנִים שֶׁיִּחְיוּ וּבִנְכָסִים שֶׁיִּרְבּוּ. בָּרֵךְ יְהֹוָה חֵילוֹ וּפֹעַל יָדָיו תִּרְצֶה, וְיִהְיוּ נְכָסָיו וּנְכָסֵינוּ מֻצְלָחִים וּקְרוֹבִים לָעִיר, וְאַל יִזְדַּקֵּק לְפָנָיו וְלֹא לְפָנֵינוּ שׁוּם דְּבַר חֵטְא וְהִרְהוּר עָוֹן, שָׂשׂ וְשָׂמֵחַ כָּל הַיָּמִים בְּעֹשֶׁר וְכָבוֹד מֵעַתָּה וְעַד עוֹלָם, לֹא יֵבוֹשׁ בָּעוֹלָם הַזֶּה וְלֹא יִכָּלֵם לָעוֹלָם הַבָּא, אָמֵן כֵּן יְהִי רָצוֹן.

hah-rah-khah-MAHN HOO yeh-vah-REKH eht hah-shool-KHAHN hah-ZEH sheh-ah-KHAHL-noo ah-LAHV, vee-sah-DER BOH kohl mah-ah-dah-NAY oh-LAHM, veh-yeeh-YEH keh-shool-khah-NOH shel ahv-rah-HAHM ah-VEE-noo ah-LAHV hah-shah-LOHM. kohl rah-EV mee-MEH-noo yoh-KHAHL, veh-khohl tzah-MEH mee-MEH-noo yeesh-TEH, veh-AHL yekh-SAHR mee-MEH-noo kohl TOHV lah-AHD ool-oh-leh-MAY oh-lah-MEEM, ah-MEN. hah-rah-khah-MAHN HOO yeh-vah-REKH eht BAH-ahl hah-BAH-yeet hah-ZEH oo-VAH-ahl hah-seh-oo-DAH hah-ZOHT, HOO oo-vah-NAHV veh-eesh-TOH veh-KHOHL ah-SHER LOH, beh-vah-NEEM sheh-yeekh-YOO oo-veen-khah-SEEM sheh-yeer-BOO. bah-REKH ah-doh-NIGH khay-LOH oo-FOH-ahl yah-DAHV teer-TZEH, veh-yeeh-YOO neh-khah-SAHV oon-khah-SAY-noo mootz-lah-KHEEM ookh-roh-VEEM lah-EER, veh-AHL yeez-dah-KEK leh-fah-NAHV veh-LOH leh-fah-NAY-noo SHOOM deh-VAHR KHET veh-heer-HOOR ah-VOHN, SAHS veh-sah-MEH-ahkh kohl hah-yah-MEEM beh-OH-sher veh-khah-VOHD meh-ah-TAH veh-AHD oh-LAHM, loh yeh-VOHSH bah-oh-LAHM hah-ZEH veh-LOH yee-kah-LEM lah-oh-LAHM hah-BAH, ah-MEN KEN yeh-HEE rah-TZOHN.

May the Merciful One bless this table at which we ate, set it with all the world's delicacies, and make it like the table of our father Abraham, peace be upon him: every hungry person will eat from it and every thirsty person drink, and may it never lack any good, forever, Amen. May the Merciful One bless the master of this house and host of this meal, him, his children, his wife and all that is his, with children who live and possessions that grow. Bless, Lord, his strength and favor the work of his hands; may his possessions and ours prosper and be near the city; may no sin or thought of wrongdoing come before him or us; may he rejoice all his days in wealth and honor from now and forever, not shamed in this world nor disgraced in the world to come. Amen, so may it be His will.

Closing

הָרַחֲמָן הוּא יְחַיֵּינוּ וִיזַכֵּנוּ וִיקָרְבֵנוּ לִימוֹת הַמָּשִׁיחַ וּלְבִנְיַן בֵּית הַמִּקְדָּשׁ וּלְחַיֵּי הָעוֹלָם הַבָּא.

hah-rah-khah-MAHN HOO yeh-khah-YAY-noo vee-zah-KEH-noo vee-kah-reh-VEH-noo lee-MOHT hah-mah-SHEE-ahkh ool-veen-YAHN bayt hah-meek-DAHSH ool-khah-YAY hah-oh-LAHM hah-BAH.

May the Merciful One grant us life and merit, and bring us near to the days of the Messiah, the rebuilding of the Holy Temple, and the life of the world to come.

On Shabbat and festivals say “meeg-DOHL”; on weekdays “mahg-DEEL.”

מִגְדּוֹל יְשׁוּעוֹת מַלְכּוֹ, וְעֹשֶׂה חֶסֶד לִמְשִׁיחוֹ, לְדָוִד וּלְזַרְעוֹ עַד עוֹלָם.

meeg-DOHL yeh-shoo-OHT mahl-KOH, veh-OH-seh KHEH-sed leem-shee-KHOH, leh-dah-VEED ool-zahr-OH ahd oh-LAHM.

He is a tower of salvation to His king, and shows kindness to His anointed, to David and his descendants forever.

כְּפִירִים רָשׁוּ וְרָעֵבוּ, וְדֹרְשֵׁי יְהֹוָה לֹא יַחְסְרוּ כָל טוֹב. נַעַר הָיִיתִי גַּם זָקַנְתִּי, וְלֹא רָאִיתִי צַדִּיק נֶעֱזָב, וְזַרְעוֹ מְבַקֶּשׁ לָחֶם. כָּל הַיּוֹם חוֹנֵן וּמַלְוֶה, וְזַרְעוֹ לִבְרָכָה. מַה שֶּׁאָכַלְנוּ יִהְיֶה לְשָׂבְעָה, וּמַה שֶּׁשָּׁתִינוּ יִהְיֶה לִרְפוּאָה, וּמַה שֶּׁהוֹתַרְנוּ יִהְיֶה לִבְרָכָה, כְּדִכְתִיב: וַיִּתֵּן לִפְנֵיהֶם וַיֹּאכְלוּ וַיּוֹתִרוּ כִּדְבַר יְהֹוָה. בְּרוּכִים אַתֶּם לַיהֹוָה, עֹשֵׂה שָׁמַיִם וָאָרֶץ. בָּרוּךְ הַגֶּבֶר אֲשֶׁר יִבְטַח בַּיהֹוָה, וְהָיָה יְהֹוָה מִבְטַחוֹ. יְהֹוָה עֹז לְעַמּוֹ יִתֵּן, יְהֹוָה יְבָרֵךְ אֶת עַמּוֹ בַשָּׁלוֹם.

keh-fee-REEM rah-SHOO veh-rah-EH-voo, veh-dohr-SHAY ah-doh-NIGH loh yahkh-seh-ROO khohl TOHV. NAH-ar hah-YEE-tee gahm zah-KAHN-tee, veh-LOH rah-EE-tee tzah-DEEK neh-eh-ZAHV, veh-zahr-OH meh-vah-KESH LAH-khem. kohl hah-YOHM khoh-NEN oo-mahl-VEH, veh-zahr-OH leev-rah-KHAH. mah sheh-ah-KHAHL-noo yeeh-YEH leh-sohv-AH, oo-MAH sheh-shah-TEE-noo yeeh-YEH lee-reh-foo-AH, oo-MAH sheh-hoh-TAHR-noo yeeh-YEH leev-rah-KHAH, keh-deekh-TEEV: vah-yee-TEN leef-nay-HEM vah-YOH-kheh-loo vah-yoh-TEE-roo keed-VAHR ah-doh-NIGH. beh-roo-KHEEM ah-TEM lah-doh-NIGH, oh-SEH shah-MAH-yeem vah-AH-retz. bah-ROOKH hah-GEH-ver ah-SHER yeev-TAKH bah-doh-NIGH, veh-hah-YAH ah-doh-NIGH meev-tah-KHOH. ah-doh-NIGH ohz leh-ah-MOH yee-TEN, ah-doh-NIGH yeh-vah-REKH eht ah-MOH vah-shah-LOHM.

Young lions may lack and go hungry, but those who seek the Lord will not lack any good. I was young and now I am old, and I have not seen a righteous man forsaken, with his children begging for bread. All day long he is generous and lends, and his children are a blessing. May what we have eaten satisfy us, what we have drunk bring healing, and what we have left over bring blessing, as it is written: He set it before them, and they ate and had some left over, according to the word of the Lord. You are blessed to the Lord, Maker of heaven and earth. Blessed is the man who trusts in the Lord, whose trust the Lord is. The Lord will give strength to His people; the Lord will bless His people with peace.

עֹשֶׂה שָׁלוֹם בִּמְרוֹמָיו, הוּא בְרַחֲמָיו יַעֲשֶׂה שָׁלוֹם עָלֵינוּ וְעַל כָּל עַמּוֹ יִשְׂרָאֵל, וְאִמְרוּ אָמֵן.

oh-SEH shah-LOHM beem-roh-MAHV, HOO veh-rah-khah-MAHV yah-ah-SEH shah-LOHM ah-LAY-noo veh-ahl kohl ah-MOH yees-rah-EL, veh-eem-ROO ah-MEN.

He who makes peace in His heights, in His compassion may He make peace for us and for all His people Israel; and say, Amen.

Shabbat Day

יוֹם שַׁבָּת

Before the daytime meal

Hebrew audio: short form

Said over wine before the Shabbat daytime meal. Some Sephardic communities first add Mizmor LeDavid and Isaiah 58:13–14 (Im Tashiv); this is the short form. Exodus 31:16–17:

וְשָׁמְרוּ בְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל אֶת הַשַּׁבָּת, לַעֲשׂוֹת אֶת הַשַּׁבָּת לְדֹרֹתָם בְּרִית עוֹלָם. בֵּינִי וּבֵין בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל אוֹת הִיא לְעֹלָם, כִּי שֵׁשֶׁת יָמִים עָשָׂה יְהֹוָה אֶת הַשָּׁמַיִם וְאֶת הָאָרֶץ, וּבַיּוֹם הַשְּׁבִיעִי שָׁבַת וַיִּנָּפַשׁ.

veh-shah-meh-ROO veh-NAY yees-rah-EL eht hah-shah-BAHT, lah-ah-SOHT eht hah-shah-BAHT leh-doh-roh-TAHM beh-REET oh-LAHM. bay-NEE oo-VAYN beh-NAY yees-rah-EL OHT HEE leh-oh-LAHM, kee SHEH-shet yah-MEEM ah-SAH ah-doh-NIGH eht hah-shah-MAH-yeem veh-eht hah-AH-retz, oo-vah-YOHM hah-sheh-vee-EE shah-VAHT vah-yee-NAH-fahsh.

The children of Israel shall keep the Shabbat, observing the Shabbat throughout their generations as an eternal covenant. It is a sign between Me and the children of Israel forever, for in six days the Lord made the heavens and the earth, and on the seventh day He ceased and rested.

Exodus 20:11, closing words:

עַל כֵּן בֵּרַךְ יְהֹוָה אֶת יוֹם הַשַּׁבָּת וַיְקַדְּשֵׁהוּ.

ahl KEN beh-RAHKH ah-doh-NIGH eht YOHM hah-shah-BAHT vah-yeh-kahd-SHEH-hoo.

Therefore the Lord blessed the Shabbat day and made it holy.

סַבְרִי מָרָנָן.

sahv-REE mah-rah-NAHN.

With your attention, my masters.

Those present respond:

לְחַיִּים.

leh-khah-YEEM.

To life!

בָּרוּךְ אַתָּה יְהֹוָה אֱלֹהֵינוּ מֶלֶךְ הָעוֹלָם, בּוֹרֵא פְּרִי הַגֶּפֶן.

bah-ROOKH ah-TAH ah-doh-NIGH eh-loh-HAY-noo MEH-lekh hah-oh-LAHM, boh-REH peh-REE hah-GEH-fen.

Blessed are You, Lord our God, King of the universe, who creates the fruit of the vine.

Saturday Night

מוֹצָאֵי שַׁבָּת

Havdalah at the close of Shabbat

Hebrew audio: includes all three spice blessings

Said after nightfall on Saturday night, over a cup of wine, with spices and a multi-wick candle. Full Mizrahi text. Opening verses: Isaiah 41:27, Micah 7:8, Esther 8:16, 1 Samuel 18:14, Genesis 6:8, Isaiah 60:1–2.

רִאשׁוֹן לְצִיּוֹן הִנֵּה הִנָּם, וְלִירוּשָׁלַיִם מְבַשֵּׂר אֶתֵּן. אַל תִּשְׂמְחִי אוֹיַבְתִּי לִי, כִּי נָפַלְתִּי קָמְתִּי, כִּי אֵשֵׁב בַּחֹשֶׁךְ יְהֹוָה אוֹר לִי. לַיְּהוּדִים הָיְתָה אוֹרָה וְשִׂמְחָה, וְשָׂשֹׂן וִיקָר. וַיְהִי דָוִד לְכָל דְּרָכָיו מַשְׂכִּיל, וַיהֹוָה עִמּוֹ. וְנֹחַ מָצָא חֵן בְּעֵינֵי יְהֹוָה, כֵּן נִמְצָא חֵן וְשֵׂכֶל טוֹב בְּעֵינֵי אֱלֹהִים וְאָדָם. קוּמִי אוֹרִי כִּי בָא אוֹרֵךְ, וּכְבוֹד יְהֹוָה עָלַיִךְ זָרָח. כִּי הִנֵּה הַחֹשֶׁךְ יְכַסֶּה אֶרֶץ, וַעֲרָפֶל לְאֻמִּים, וְעָלַיִךְ יִזְרַח יְהֹוָה, וּכְבוֹדוֹ עָלַיִךְ יֵרָאֶה.

ree-SHOHN leh-tzee-YOHN hee-NEH hee-NAHM, veh-lee-roo-shah-LAH-yeem meh-vah-SER eh-TEN. ahl tees-meh-KHEE oh-yahv-TEE LEE, kee nah-FAHL-tee KAHM-tee, kee eh-SHEV bah-KHOH-shekh ah-doh-NIGH OHR lee. lah-yeh-hoo-DEEM high-TAH oh-RAH veh-seem-KHAH, veh-sah-SOHN vee-KAHR. vah-yeh-HEE dah-VEED leh-KHOHL deh-rah-KHAHV mahs-KEEL, vah-doh-NIGH ee-MOH. veh-NOH-ahkh mah-TZAH KHEN beh-ay-NAY ah-doh-NIGH, KEN neem-TZAH KHEN veh-SEH-khel TOHV beh-ay-NAY eh-loh-HEEM veh-ah-DAHM. KOO-mee OH-ree kee VAH oh-REKH, ookh-VOHD ah-doh-NIGH ah-LAH-yeekh zah-RAKH. kee hee-NEH hah-KHOH-shekh yeh-khah-SEH EH-retz, vah-ah-rah-FEL leh-oo-MEEM, veh-ah-LAH-yeekh yeez-RAKH ah-doh-NIGH, ookh-voh-DOH ah-LAH-yeekh yeh-rah-EH.

First to Zion: behold, here they are; and to Jerusalem I give a herald of good news. Do not rejoice over me, my enemy; though I fell, I rose; though I sit in darkness, the Lord is my light. The Jews had light and gladness, joy and honor. David succeeded in all his ways, and the Lord was with him. Noah found favor in the eyes of the Lord; so may we find favor and good understanding in the eyes of God and man. Arise, shine, for your light has come, and the glory of the Lord has risen upon you. For darkness may cover the earth and thick cloud the nations, but the Lord will shine upon you, and His glory will be seen upon you.

Take the cup in the hand and say:

כּוֹס יְשׁוּעוֹת אֶשָּׂא, וּבְשֵׁם יְהֹוָה אֶקְרָא.

KOHS yeh-shoo-OHT eh-SAH, oo-veh-SHEM ah-doh-NIGH ek-RAH.

I will lift the cup of salvation and call upon the name of the Lord.

Each of the next two lines is said twice:

אָנָּא יְהֹוָה הוֹשִׁיעָה נָּא. אָנָּא יְהֹוָה הוֹשִׁיעָה נָּא.

AH-nah ah-doh-NIGH hoh-SHEE-ah NAH. AH-nah ah-doh-NIGH hoh-SHEE-ah NAH.

Please, Lord, save us. Please, Lord, save us.

אָנָּא יְהֹוָה הַצְלִיחָה נָא. אָנָּא יְהֹוָה הַצְלִיחָה נָא.

AH-nah ah-doh-NIGH hahtz-LEE-khah NAH. AH-nah ah-doh-NIGH hahtz-LEE-khah NAH.

Please, Lord, grant us success. Please, Lord, grant us success.

הַצְלִיחֵנוּ, הַצְלַח דְּרָכֵינוּ, הַצְלַח לִמּוּדֵינוּ, וּשְׁלַח בְּרָכָה רְוָחָה וְהַצְלָחָה בְּכָל מַעֲשֵׂה יָדֵינוּ, כְּדִכְתִיב: יִשָּׂא בְרָכָה מֵאֵת יְהֹוָה, וּצְדָקָה מֵאֱלֹהֵי יִשְׁעוֹ. לַיְּהוּדִים הָיְתָה אוֹרָה וְשִׂמְחָה, וְשָׂשֹׂן וִיקָר. וּכְתִיב: וַיְהִי דָוִד לְכָל דְּרָכָיו מַשְׂכִּיל, וַיהֹוָה עִמּוֹ. כֵּן יִהְיֶה עִמָּנוּ תָּמִיד.

hahtz-lee-KHEH-noo, hahtz-LAKH deh-rah-KHAY-noo, hahtz-LAKH lee-moo-DAY-noo, oosh-LAKH beh-rah-KHAH reh-vah-KHAH veh-hahtz-lah-KHAH beh-KHOHL mah-ah-SEH yah-DAY-noo, keh-deekh-TEEV: yee-SAH veh-rah-KHAH meh-ET ah-doh-NIGH, oo-tzeh-dah-KAH meh-eh-loh-HAY yeesh-OH. lah-yeh-hoo-DEEM high-TAH oh-RAH veh-seem-KHAH, veh-sah-SOHN vee-KAHR. ookh-TEEV: vah-yeh-HEE dah-VEED leh-KHOHL deh-rah-KHAHV mahs-KEEL, vah-doh-NIGH ee-MOH. KEN yeeh-YEH ee-MAH-noo tah-MEED.

Grant us success: make our ways succeed and our learning succeed, and send blessing, relief and success to all the work of our hands, as it is written: He will receive a blessing from the Lord and righteousness from the God of his salvation. The Jews had light and gladness, joy and honor. And it is written: David succeeded in all his ways, and the Lord was with him. So may He be with us always.

סַבְרִי מָרָנָן.

sahv-REE mah-rah-NAHN.

With your attention, my masters.

Those present respond:

לְחַיִּים.

leh-khah-YEEM.

To life!

Wine

בָּרוּךְ אַתָּה יְהֹוָה אֱלֹהֵינוּ מֶלֶךְ הָעוֹלָם, בּוֹרֵא פְּרִי הַגֶּפֶן.

bah-ROOKH ah-TAH ah-doh-NIGH eh-loh-HAY-noo MEH-lekh hah-oh-LAHM, boh-REH peh-REE hah-GEH-fen.

Blessed are You, Lord our God, King of the universe, who creates the fruit of the vine.

Spices

Sephardic custom matches the blessing to the spice. Say one of the three.

Mixed spices, or when unsure:

בָּרוּךְ אַתָּה יְהֹוָה אֱלֹהֵינוּ מֶלֶךְ הָעוֹלָם, בּוֹרֵא מִינֵי בְשָׂמִים.

bah-ROOKH ah-TAH ah-doh-NIGH eh-loh-HAY-noo MEH-lekh hah-oh-LAHM, boh-REH mee-NAY veh-sah-MEEM.

Blessed are You, Lord our God, King of the universe, who creates the various kinds of spices.

Fragrant wood or bark (e.g., cloves, cinnamon):

בָּרוּךְ אַתָּה יְהֹוָה אֱלֹהֵינוּ מֶלֶךְ הָעוֹלָם, בּוֹרֵא עֲצֵי בְשָׂמִים.

bah-ROOKH ah-TAH ah-doh-NIGH eh-loh-HAY-noo MEH-lekh hah-oh-LAHM, boh-REH ah-TZAY veh-sah-MEEM.

Blessed are You, Lord our God, King of the universe, who creates fragrant trees.

Fragrant herbs (e.g., mint, rosemary):

בָּרוּךְ אַתָּה יְהֹוָה אֱלֹהֵינוּ מֶלֶךְ הָעוֹלָם, בּוֹרֵא עִשְׂבֵּי בְשָׂמִים.

bah-ROOKH ah-TAH ah-doh-NIGH eh-loh-HAY-noo MEH-lekh hah-oh-LAHM, boh-REH ees-VAY veh-sah-MEEM.

Blessed are You, Lord our God, King of the universe, who creates fragrant herbs.

Fire

בָּרוּךְ אַתָּה יְהֹוָה אֱלֹהֵינוּ מֶלֶךְ הָעוֹלָם, בּוֹרֵא מְאוֹרֵי הָאֵשׁ.

bah-ROOKH ah-TAH ah-doh-NIGH eh-loh-HAY-noo MEH-lekh hah-oh-LAHM, boh-REH meh-oh-RAY hah-ESH.

Blessed are You, Lord our God, King of the universe, who creates the lights of fire.

Havdalah

בָּרוּךְ אַתָּה יְהֹוָה אֱלֹהֵינוּ מֶלֶךְ הָעוֹלָם, הַמַּבְדִּיל בֵּין קֹדֶשׁ לְחוֹל, וּבֵין אוֹר לְחֹשֶׁךְ, וּבֵין יִשְׂרָאֵל לָעַמִּים, וּבֵין יוֹם הַשְּׁבִיעִי לְשֵׁשֶׁת יְמֵי הַמַּעֲשֶׂה. בָּרוּךְ אַתָּה יְהֹוָה, הַמַּבְדִּיל בֵּין קֹדֶשׁ לְחוֹל.

bah-ROOKH ah-TAH ah-doh-NIGH eh-loh-HAY-noo MEH-lekh hah-oh-LAHM, hah-mahv-DEEL bayn KOH-desh leh-KHOHL, oo-VAYN OHR leh-KHOH-shekh, oo-VAYN yees-rah-EL lah-ah-MEEM, oo-VAYN YOHM hah-sheh-vee-EE leh-SHEH-shet yeh-MAY hah-mah-ah-SEH. bah-ROOKH ah-TAH ah-doh-NIGH, hah-mahv-DEEL bayn KOH-desh leh-KHOHL.

Blessed are You, Lord our God, King of the universe, who distinguishes between holy and ordinary, between light and darkness, between Israel and the nations, between the seventh day and the six working days. Blessed are You, Lord, who distinguishes between holy and ordinary.

Drink the wine seated. Extinguish the candle in a little of the remaining wine.

Daily Blessings

בְּרָכוֹת

On waking, at meals, when traveling, and on special occasions

Said immediately on waking, before rising and before washing the hands. A man says “moh-DEH”; a woman says “moh-DAH.”

מוֹדֶה אֲנִי לְפָנֶיךָ, מֶלֶךְ חַי וְקַיָּם, שֶׁהֶחֱזַרְתָּ בִּי נִשְׁמָתִי בְּחֶמְלָה, רַבָּה אֱמוּנָתֶךָ.

Listen

moh-DEH ah-NEE leh-fah-NEH-khah, MEH-lekh KHIGH veh-kah-YAHM, sheh-heh-kheh-zahr-TAH BEE neesh-mah-TEE beh-khem-LAH, rah-BAH eh-moo-nah-TEH-khah.

I thank You, living and eternal King, for You have mercifully restored my soul within me; great is Your faithfulness.

Part one — washing the hands

After leaving the restroom, wash the hands. During the day no blessing is said on this washing. In the morning, on the first washing after waking, the hands are washed three times alternately and this blessing is said:

בָּרוּךְ אַתָּה יְהֹוָה אֱלֹהֵינוּ מֶלֶךְ הָעוֹלָם, אֲשֶׁר קִדְּשָׁנוּ בְּמִצְוֹתָיו וְצִוָּנוּ עַל נְטִילַת יָדַיִם.

bah-ROOKH ah-TAH ah-doh-NIGH eh-loh-HAY-noo MEH-lekh hah-oh-LAHM, ah-SHER kee-deh-SHAH-noo beh-meetz-voh-TAHV veh-tzee-VAH-noo ahl neh-tee-LAHT yah-DAH-yeem.

Blessed are You, Lord our God, King of the universe, who sanctified us with His commandments and commanded us concerning the washing of the hands.

Part two — Asher Yatzar

Hebrew audio

Said every time, after washing:

בָּרוּךְ אַתָּה יְהֹוָה אֱלֹהֵינוּ מֶלֶךְ הָעוֹלָם, אֲשֶׁר יָצַר אֶת הָאָדָם בְּחָכְמָה, וּבָרָא בוֹ נְקָבִים נְקָבִים, חֲלוּלִים חֲלוּלִים. גָּלוּי וְיָדוּעַ לִפְנֵי כִסֵּא כְבוֹדֶךָ, שֶׁאִם יִסָּתֵם אֶחָד מֵהֶם, אוֹ אִם יִפָּתֵחַ אֶחָד מֵהֶם, אִי אֶפְשָׁר לְהִתְקַיֵּם אֲפִלּוּ שָׁעָה אֶחָת. בָּרוּךְ אַתָּה יְהֹוָה, רוֹפֵא כָל בָּשָׂר וּמַפְלִיא לַעֲשׂוֹת.

bah-ROOKH ah-TAH ah-doh-NIGH eh-loh-HAY-noo MEH-lekh hah-oh-LAHM, ah-SHER yah-TZAHR eht hah-ah-DAHM beh-khokh-MAH, oo-vah-RAH VOH neh-kah-VEEM neh-kah-VEEM, khah-loo-LEEM khah-loo-LEEM. gah-LOO-ee veh-yah-DOO-ah leef-NAY khee-SEH keh-voh-DEH-khah, sheh-EEM yee-sah-TEM eh-KHAHD meh-HEM, oh EEM yee-pah-TEH-ahkh eh-KHAHD meh-HEM, ee ef-SHAHR leh-heet-kah-YEM ah-fee-LOO shah-AH eh-KHAHT. bah-ROOKH ah-TAH ah-doh-NIGH, roh-FEH khohl bah-SAHR oo-mahf-LEE lah-ah-SOHT.

Blessed are You, Lord our God, King of the universe, who formed man with wisdom and created in him many openings and many hollows. It is revealed and known before Your throne of glory that if one of them were blocked or one of them opened, it would be impossible to survive even for one hour. Blessed are You, Lord, who heals all flesh and acts wondrously.

Each blessing is written out in full; paste the one that matches the food.

Hebrew audio: HaEtz, HaAdamah, Mezonot, SheHakol

Fruit of the tree — HaEtz

בָּרוּךְ אַתָּה יְהֹוָה אֱלֹהֵינוּ מֶלֶךְ הָעוֹלָם, בּוֹרֵא פְּרִי הָעֵץ.

bah-ROOKH ah-TAH ah-doh-NIGH eh-loh-HAY-noo MEH-lekh hah-oh-LAHM, boh-REH peh-REE hah-ETZ.

Blessed are You, Lord our God, King of the universe, who creates the fruit of the tree.

Produce of the ground — HaAdamah

For vegetables, and for fruit that grows from the ground (e.g., bananas, melons, strawberries).

בָּרוּךְ אַתָּה יְהֹוָה אֱלֹהֵינוּ מֶלֶךְ הָעוֹלָם, בּוֹרֵא פְּרִי הָאֲדָמָה.

bah-ROOKH ah-TAH ah-doh-NIGH eh-loh-HAY-noo MEH-lekh hah-oh-LAHM, boh-REH peh-REE hah-ah-dah-MAH.

Blessed are You, Lord our God, King of the universe, who creates the fruit of the ground.

Baked goods and grain foods — Mezonot

For cake, cookies, crackers, pastries, and cooked dishes of the five grains (wheat, barley, spelt, oats, rye) that are not bread. Bread itself takes HaMotzi.

בָּרוּךְ אַתָּה יְהֹוָה אֱלֹהֵינוּ מֶלֶךְ הָעוֹלָם, בּוֹרֵא מִינֵי מְזוֹנוֹת.

bah-ROOKH ah-TAH ah-doh-NIGH eh-loh-HAY-noo MEH-lekh hah-oh-LAHM, boh-REH mee-NAY meh-zoh-NOHT.

Blessed are You, Lord our God, King of the universe, who creates various kinds of nourishment.

All other foods and drinks — SheHakol

For meat, fish, eggs, dairy, drinks other than wine, and any food not covered above.

בָּרוּךְ אַתָּה יְהֹוָה אֱלֹהֵינוּ מֶלֶךְ הָעוֹלָם, שֶׁהַכֹּל נִהְיָה בִּדְבָרוֹ.

bah-ROOKH ah-TAH ah-doh-NIGH eh-loh-HAY-noo MEH-lekh hah-oh-LAHM, sheh-hah-KOHL neeh-YAH beed-vah-ROH.

Blessed are You, Lord our God, King of the universe, by whose word all things came to be.

For a new seasonal fruit, a new significant garment or item, and the first night of a festival. Sephardic custom: a woman lighting festival candles does not add it at candle lighting.

Hebrew audio

בָּרוּךְ אַתָּה יְהֹוָה אֱלֹהֵינוּ מֶלֶךְ הָעוֹלָם, שֶׁהֶחֱיָנוּ וְקִיְּמָנוּ וְהִגִּיעָנוּ לַזְּמַן הַזֶּה.

bah-ROOKH ah-TAH ah-doh-NIGH eh-loh-HAY-noo MEH-lekh hah-oh-LAHM, sheh-heh-kheh-YAH-noo veh-kee-yeh-MAH-noo veh-hee-gee-AH-noo laz-MAHN hah-ZEH.

Blessed are You, Lord our God, King of the universe, who has given us life, sustained us, and brought us to this time.

Said once per day of travel, after leaving the city’s built-up area, for a trip of at least a few miles (by car, plane, or train). Mizrahi text.

Hebrew audio

יְהִי רָצוֹן מִלְּפָנֶיךָ יְהֹוָה אֱלֹהֵינוּ וֵאלֹהֵי אֲבוֹתֵינוּ, שֶׁתּוֹלִיכֵנוּ לְשָׁלוֹם, וְתַצְעִידֵנוּ לְשָׁלוֹם, וְתַדְרִיכֵנוּ לְשָׁלוֹם, וְתַגִּיעֵנוּ לִמְחוֹז חֶפְצֵנוּ לְחַיִּים וּלְשִׂמְחָה וּלְשָׁלוֹם.

yeh-HEE rah-TZOHN mee-leh-fah-NEH-khah ah-doh-NIGH eh-loh-HAY-noo veh-loh-HAY ah-voh-TAY-noo, sheh-toh-lee-KHEH-noo leh-shah-LOHM, veh-tahtz-ee-DEH-noo leh-shah-LOHM, veh-tahd-ree-KHEH-noo leh-shah-LOHM, veh-tah-gee-EH-noo leem-KHOHZ khef-TZEH-noo leh-khah-YEEM ool-seem-KHAH ool-shah-LOHM.

May it be Your will, Lord our God and God of our fathers, to lead us in peace, direct our steps in peace, guide us in peace, and bring us to our desired destination in life, joy and peace.

If returning the same day, add:

וְתַחְזִירֵנוּ לְשָׁלוֹם.

veh-tahkh-zee-REH-noo leh-shah-LOHM.

And return us in peace.

וְתַצִּילֵנוּ מִכַּף כָּל אוֹיֵב וְאוֹרֵב וְלִסְטִים וְחַיּוֹת רָעוֹת בַּדֶּרֶךְ, וּמִכָּל מִינֵי פֻּרְעָנֻיּוֹת הַמִּתְרַגְּשׁוֹת לָבוֹא לָעוֹלָם, וְתִתְּנֵנוּ לְחֵן וּלְחֶסֶד וּלְרַחֲמִים בְּעֵינֶיךָ וּבְעֵינֵי כָל רֹאֵינוּ, כִּי אֵל שׁוֹמֵעַ תְּפִלָּה וְתַחֲנוּן אַתָּה. בָּרוּךְ אַתָּה יְהֹוָה, שׁוֹמֵעַ תְּפִלָּה.

veh-tah-tzee-LEH-noo mee-KAHF kohl oh-YEV veh-oh-REV veh-lees-TEEM veh-khah-YOHT rah-OHT bah-DEH-rekh, oo-mee-KOHL mee-NAY poor-ah-noo-YOHT hah-meet-rah-geh-SHOHT lah-VOH lah-oh-LAHM, veh-tee-teh-NEH-noo leh-KHEN ool-KHEH-sed ool-rah-khah-MEEM beh-ay-NEH-khah oov-ay-NAY khohl roh-AY-noo, kee EL shoh-MEH-ah teh-fee-LAH veh-tah-khah-NOON AH-tah. bah-ROOKH ah-TAH ah-doh-NIGH, shoh-MEH-ah teh-fee-LAH.

Rescue us from every enemy, ambush, robber and wild beast on the way, and from every kind of calamity that rages upon the world. Grant us grace, kindness and compassion in Your eyes and in the eyes of all who see us, for You are God who hears prayer and supplication. Blessed are You, Lord, who hears prayer.

Many Mizrahi siddurim then add Psalm 91:11, Psalm 121:8, Genesis 32:2–3, and Birkat Kohanim.

Tzitzit, Tefillin and Mikvah

צִיצִית, תְּפִלִּין וּמִקְוֶה

Putting on tzitzit and tefillin, and immersion in the mikveh

Check the strings for damage before putting on a tallit. Hold the tallit and say Psalm 104:1–2, say the blessing, and wrap yourself (with a tallit gadol, the head and body are covered). Then say Mah Yakar. Mizrahi-Sephardic custom.

Hebrew audio

Before wrapping — Psalm 104:1–2

בָּרְכִי נַפְשִׁי אֶת יְהֹוָה, יְהֹוָה אֱלֹהַי גָּדַלְתָּ מְאֹד, הוֹד וְהָדָר לָבָשְׁתָּ. עֹטֶה אוֹר כַּשַּׂלְמָה, נוֹטֶה שָׁמַיִם כַּיְרִיעָה.

bahr-KHEE nahf-SHEE eht ah-doh-NIGH, ah-doh-NIGH eh-loh-HIGH gah-DAHL-tah meh-OHD, hohd veh-hah-DAHR lah-VAHSH-tah. oh-TEH ohr kah-sahl-MAH, noh-TEH shah-MAH-yeem kah-yeh-ree-AH.

Bless the Lord, my soul. Lord my God, You are very great; You are clothed in splendor and majesty. He wraps Himself in light as in a garment; He stretches out the heavens like a curtain.

Blessing — tallit gadol (large tallit)

Say standing, then wrap yourself.

בָּרוּךְ אַתָּה יְהֹוָה אֱלֹהֵינוּ מֶלֶךְ הָעוֹלָם, אֲשֶׁר קִדְּשָׁנוּ בְּמִצְוֹתָיו וְצִוָּנוּ לְהִתְעַטֵּף בַּצִּיצִית.

bah-ROOKH ah-TAH ah-doh-NIGH eh-loh-HAY-noo MEH-lekh hah-oh-LAHM, ah-SHER kee-deh-SHAH-noo beh-meetz-voh-TAHV veh-tzee-VAH-noo leh-heet-ah-TEF bah-tzee-TZEET.

Blessed are You, Lord our God, King of the universe, who sanctified us with His commandments and commanded us to wrap ourselves in the tzitzit.

Blessing — tallit katan (small tallit worn under the clothing)

Say this blessing instead when putting on a tallit katan.

בָּרוּךְ אַתָּה יְהֹוָה אֱלֹהֵינוּ מֶלֶךְ הָעוֹלָם, אֲשֶׁר קִדְּשָׁנוּ בְּמִצְוֹתָיו וְצִוָּנוּ עַל מִצְוַת צִיצִית.

bah-ROOKH ah-TAH ah-doh-NIGH eh-loh-HAY-noo MEH-lekh hah-oh-LAHM, ah-SHER kee-deh-SHAH-noo beh-meetz-voh-TAHV veh-tzee-VAH-noo ahl meetz-VAHT tzee-TZEET.

Blessed are You, Lord our God, King of the universe, who sanctified us with His commandments and commanded us concerning the commandment of tzitzit.

After wrapping — Mah Yakar (Psalm 36:8–11)

מַה יָּקָר חַסְדְּךָ אֱלֹהִים, וּבְנֵי אָדָם בְּצֵל כְּנָפֶיךָ יֶחֱסָיוּן. יִרְוְיֻן מִדֶּשֶׁן בֵּיתֶךָ, וְנַחַל עֲדָנֶיךָ תַשְׁקֵם. כִּי עִמְּךָ מְקוֹר חַיִּים, בְּאוֹרְךָ נִרְאֶה אוֹר. מְשֹׁךְ חַסְדְּךָ לְיֹדְעֶיךָ, וְצִדְקָתְךָ לְיִשְׁרֵי לֵב.

mah yah-KAHR khahs-deh-KHAH eh-loh-HEEM, oov-NAY ah-DAHM beh-TZEL keh-nah-feh-KHAH yeh-kheh-sah-YOON. yeer-veh-YOON mee-DEH-shen bay-teh-KHAH, veh-NAH-khahl ah-dah-neh-KHAH tahsh-KEM. kee ee-meh-KHAH meh-KOHR khah-YEEM, beh-oh-reh-KHAH neer-EH ohr. meh-SHOHKH khahs-deh-KHAH leh-yohd-eh-KHAH, veh-tzeed-kaht-KHAH leh-yeesh-RAY layv.

How precious is Your kindness, God! The children of man take refuge in the shadow of Your wings. They are sated from the abundance of Your House, and You give them to drink from the stream of Your delights. For with You is the source of life; in Your light we see light. Extend Your kindness to those who know You, and Your righteousness to the upright of heart.

Put on the arm tefillin first, then the head tefillin, and do not speak in between. Sephardic custom follows the Shulchan Aruch: one blessing, said on the arm tefillin, covers both. Ashkenazim add a second blessing on the head tefillin and say Barukh Shem; some Sephardic communities also say the second blessing. Follow your rav.

Hebrew audio

Arm tefillin — blessing

Say it as you place the tefillin on the arm, before tightening the strap.

בָּרוּךְ אַתָּה יְהֹוָה אֱלֹהֵינוּ מֶלֶךְ הָעוֹלָם, אֲשֶׁר קִדְּשָׁנוּ בְּמִצְוֹתָיו וְצִוָּנוּ לְהָנִיחַ תְּפִלִּין.

bah-ROOKH ah-TAH ah-doh-NIGH eh-loh-HAY-noo MEH-lekh hah-oh-LAHM, ah-SHER kee-deh-SHAH-noo beh-meetz-voh-TAHV veh-tzee-VAH-noo leh-hah-NEE-ahkh teh-fee-LEEN.

Blessed are You, Lord our God, King of the universe, who sanctified us with His commandments and commanded us to put on tefillin.

Head tefillin — blessing

Say this only if you spoke between the arm and head tefillin, or if your community says it every time.

בָּרוּךְ אַתָּה יְהֹוָה אֱלֹהֵינוּ מֶלֶךְ הָעוֹלָם, אֲשֶׁר קִדְּשָׁנוּ בְּמִצְוֹתָיו וְצִוָּנוּ עַל מִצְוַת תְּפִלִּין.

bah-ROOKH ah-TAH ah-doh-NIGH eh-loh-HAY-noo MEH-lekh hah-oh-LAHM, ah-SHER kee-deh-SHAH-noo beh-meetz-voh-TAHV veh-tzee-VAH-noo ahl meetz-VAHT teh-fee-LEEN.

Blessed are You, Lord our God, King of the universe, who sanctified us with His commandments and commanded us concerning the commandment of tefillin.

Winding the strap around the finger — Hosea 2:21–22

Said while winding the strap three times around the middle finger.

וְאֵרַשְׂתִּיךְ לִי לְעוֹלָם, וְאֵרַשְׂתִּיךְ לִי בְּצֶדֶק וּבְמִשְׁפָּט וּבְחֶסֶד וּבְרַחֲמִים. וְאֵרַשְׂתִּיךְ לִי בֶּאֱמוּנָה, וְיָדַעַתְּ אֶת יְהֹוָה.

veh-eh-rahs-TEEKH lee leh-oh-LAHM, veh-eh-rahs-TEEKH lee beh-TZEH-dek oov-meesh-PAHT oov-KHEH-sed oov-rah-khah-MEEM. veh-eh-rahs-TEEKH lee beh-eh-moo-NAH, veh-yah-DAH-aht eht ah-doh-NIGH.

I will betroth you to Me forever; I will betroth you to Me in righteousness and justice, in kindness and compassion. I will betroth you to Me in faithfulness, and you shall know the Lord.

The blessing is said once. Mizrahi-Sephardic custom has two orders. The Shulchan Aruch and Rav Ovadia Yosef: say it before immersing, outside the water of the mikveh itself. The Ben Ish Chai (Iraqi custom): immerse once, say the blessing, then immerse again. Ask the mikveh attendant or your rav which your community follows.

Hebrew audio

Blessing

בָּרוּךְ אַתָּה יְהֹוָה אֱלֹהֵינוּ מֶלֶךְ הָעוֹלָם, אֲשֶׁר קִדְּשָׁנוּ בְּמִצְוֹתָיו וְצִוָּנוּ עַל הַטְּבִילָה.

bah-ROOKH ah-TAH ah-doh-NIGH eh-loh-HAY-noo MEH-lekh hah-oh-LAHM, ah-SHER kee-deh-SHAH-noo beh-meetz-voh-TAHV veh-tzee-VAH-noo ahl hah-teh-vee-LAH.

Blessed are You, Lord our God, King of the universe, who sanctified us with His commandments and commanded us concerning immersion.

There is no set text for a personal prayer; many women add one in their own words.

Men have no obligation to immerse today. Many immerse by custom before Shabbat and festivals, before Rosh Hashanah and Yom Kippur, and before prayer. The usual practice is that no blessing is said on this immersion, and no set text is required. The verses below are commonly said. A convert is the exception: he or she says the blessing “al hatevilah” (see the women’s entry) after emerging from the water.

Hebrew audio

Verses commonly said

וְזָרַקְתִּי עֲלֵיכֶם מַיִם טְהוֹרִים וּטְהַרְתֶּם, מִכֹּל טֻמְאוֹתֵיכֶם וּמִכָּל גִּלּוּלֵיכֶם אֲטַהֵר אֶתְכֶם.

veh-zah-RAHK-tee ah-lay-KHEM MAH-yeem teh-hoh-REEM oo-teh-hahr-TEM, mee-KOHL toom-oh-tay-KHEM oo-mee-KOHL gee-loo-lay-KHEM ah-tah-HER et-KHEM.

I will sprinkle pure water upon you and you shall be purified; from all your impurities and from all your idols I will purify you. (Ezekiel 36:25)

מִקְוֵה יִשְׂרָאֵל יְהֹוָה.

meek-VEH yees-rah-EL ah-doh-NIGH.

The Lord is the Mikveh (hope) of Israel. (Jeremiah 17:13)

Morning: Pesukei DeZimra

פְּסוּקֵי דְזִמְרָה

Baruch SheAmar, Ashrei, Psalm 150 and Yishtabach. The other psalms of this section are not included.

Say standing, even when praying alone. Do not speak from Baruch SheAmar until after Yishtabach. In the Sephardic order, Hodu (1 Chronicles 16:8–36) is said before this blessing; it is not included here. The text follows the Ari’s version, which the Chida directed Sephardim to say and which later Sephardic siddurim adopted.

Hebrew audio

The opening praises

בָּרוּךְ שֶׁאָמַר וְהָיָה הָעוֹלָם, בָּרוּךְ הוּא. בָּרוּךְ אוֹמֵר וְעוֹשֶׂה, בָּרוּךְ גּוֹזֵר וּמְקַיֵּם, בָּרוּךְ מְרַחֵם עַל הָאָרֶץ, בָּרוּךְ מְרַחֵם עַל הַבְּרִיּוֹת, בָּרוּךְ מְשַׁלֵּם שָׂכָר טוֹב לִירֵאָיו, בָּרוּךְ חַי לָעַד וְקַיָּם לָנֶצַח, בָּרוּךְ פּוֹדֶה וּמַצִּיל, בָּרוּךְ שְׁמוֹ.

bah-ROOKH sheh-AH-mahr veh-hah-YAH hah-oh-LAHM, bah-ROOKH HOO. bah-ROOKH oh-MER veh-oh-SEH, bah-ROOKH goh-ZER oo-meh-kah-YEM, bah-ROOKH meh-rah-KHEM ahl hah-AH-retz, bah-ROOKH meh-rah-KHEM ahl hah-beh-ree-YOHT, bah-ROOKH meh-shah-LEM sah-KHAHR TOHV lee-ray-AHV, bah-ROOKH KHIGH lah-AHD veh-kah-YAHM lah-NEH-tzahkh, bah-ROOKH poh-DEH oo-mah-TZEEL, bah-ROOKH sheh-MOH.

Blessed is the One who spoke and the world came to be; blessed is He. Blessed is He who speaks and acts; blessed is He who decrees and fulfills; blessed is He who has compassion on the earth; blessed is He who has compassion on His creatures; blessed is He who pays a good reward to those who fear Him; blessed is He who lives forever and endures to eternity; blessed is He who redeems and rescues; blessed is His name.

The blessing

בָּרוּךְ אַתָּה יְהֹוָה אֱלֹהֵינוּ מֶלֶךְ הָעוֹלָם, הָאֵל הָאָב הָרַחֲמָן, הַמְהֻלָּל בְּפִי עַמּוֹ, מְשֻׁבָּח וּמְפֹאָר בִּלְשׁוֹן חֲסִידָיו וַעֲבָדָיו. וּבְשִׁירֵי דָוִד עַבְדֶּךָ נְהַלֶּלְךָ יְהֹוָה אֱלֹהֵינוּ, בִּשְׁבָחוֹת וּבִזְמִירוֹת, נְגַדֶּלְךָ וּנְשַׁבֵּחֲךָ וּנְפָאֶרְךָ, וְנַזְכִּיר שִׁמְךָ וְנַמְלִיכְךָ מַלְכֵּנוּ אֱלֹהֵינוּ, יָחִיד חֵי הָעוֹלָמִים, מֶלֶךְ מְשֻׁבָּח וּמְפֹאָר עֲדֵי עַד שְׁמוֹ הַגָּדוֹל. בָּרוּךְ אַתָּה יְהֹוָה, מֶלֶךְ מְהֻלָּל בַּתִּשְׁבָּחוֹת.

bah-ROOKH ah-TAH ah-doh-NIGH eh-loh-HAY-noo MEH-lekh hah-oh-LAHM, hah-EL hah-AHV hah-rah-khah-MAHN, hah-meh-hoo-LAHL beh-FEE ah-MOH, meh-shoo-BAKH oo-meh-foh-AHR beel-SHOHN khah-see-DAHV vah-ah-vah-DAHV. oov-shee-RAY dah-VEED ahv-DEH-khah neh-hah-LEL-khah ah-doh-NIGH eh-loh-HAY-noo, beesh-vah-KHOHT oov-zee-mee-ROHT, neh-gah-DEL-khah oon-shah-BAY-khah oon-fah-ER-khah, veh-nahz-KEER sheem-KHAH veh-nahm-LEEKH-khah mahl-KAY-noo eh-loh-HAY-noo, yah-KHEED KHAY hah-oh-lah-MEEM, MEH-lekh meh-shoo-BAKH oo-meh-foh-AHR ah-DAY AHD sheh-MOH hah-gah-DOHL. bah-ROOKH ah-TAH ah-doh-NIGH, MEH-lekh meh-hoo-LAHL bah-teesh-bah-KHOHT.

Blessed are You, Lord our God, King of the universe, the God, the compassionate Father, praised by the mouth of His people, lauded and glorified by the tongue of His devoted servants. With the songs of David Your servant we will praise You, Lord our God, with praises and psalms; we will magnify You, laud You and glorify You, mention Your name and proclaim You King, our King and our God, the only One, the Life of the worlds, King praised and glorified forever, His great name. Blessed are You, Lord, King praised with praises.

Said for: Livelihood.

Said three times each day: in Pesukei DeZimra, again before Uva LeTziyon after the Amidah, and at Mincha. Say “Poteach et yadekha” (verse 16) with full concentration; if you did not, repeat the verse. Two verses open the prayer (Psalms 84:5 and 144:15) and one closes it (Psalm 115:18). The text follows the Mizrahi siddur.

Hebrew audio

Opening verses

אַשְׁרֵי יוֹשְׁבֵי בֵיתֶךָ, עוֹד יְהַלְלוּךָ סֶּלָה.

ahsh-RAY yohsh-VAY vay-TEH-khah, ohd yeh-hah-leh-LOO-khah SEH-lah.

Happy are those who dwell in Your house; they will praise You forever, Selah. (Psalm 84:5)

אַשְׁרֵי הָעָם שֶׁכָּכָה לּוֹ, אַשְׁרֵי הָעָם שֶׁיְהֹוָה אֱלֹהָיו.

ahsh-RAY hah-AHM sheh-kah-KHAH LOH, ahsh-RAY hah-AHM sheh-ah-doh-NIGH eh-loh-HAHV.

Happy is the people for whom this is so; happy is the people whose God is the Lord. (Psalm 144:15)

Psalm 145 — Tehillah LeDavid

תְּהִלָּה לְדָוִד, אֲרוֹמִמְךָ אֱלוֹהַי הַמֶּלֶךְ, וַאֲבָרְכָה שִׁמְךָ לְעוֹלָם וָעֶד.

teh-hee-LAH leh-dah-VEED, ah-roh-meem-KHAH eh-loh-HIGH hah-MEH-lekh, vah-ah-vahr-KHAH sheem-KHAH leh-oh-LAHM vah-ED.

A psalm of praise, of David. I will exalt You, my God the King, and bless Your name forever and ever.

בְּכָל יוֹם אֲבָרְכֶךָּ, וַאֲהַלְלָה שִׁמְךָ לְעוֹלָם וָעֶד.

beh-KHOHL yohm ah-vahr-KHEH-khah, vah-ah-hahl-LAH sheem-KHAH leh-oh-LAHM vah-ED.

Every day I will bless You, and praise Your name forever and ever.

גָּדוֹל יְהֹוָה וּמְהֻלָּל מְאֹד, וְלִגְדֻלָּתוֹ אֵין חֵקֶר.

gah-DOHL ah-doh-NIGH oo-meh-hoo-LAHL meh-OHD, veh-leeg-doo-LAH-toh AYN KHEH-ker.

Great is the Lord and highly praised; His greatness cannot be fathomed.

דּוֹר לְדוֹר יְשַׁבַּח מַעֲשֶׂיךָ, וּגְבוּרֹתֶיךָ יַגִּידוּ.

dohr leh-DOHR yeh-shah-BAKH mah-ah-SEH-khah, oog-voo-roh-TEH-khah yah-gee-DOO.

One generation will praise Your works to the next, and tell of Your mighty deeds.

הֲדַר כְּבוֹד הוֹדֶךָ, וְדִבְרֵי נִפְלְאֹתֶיךָ אָשִׂיחָה.

hah-DAHR keh-VOHD hoh-DEH-khah, veh-deev-RAY neef-leh-oh-TEH-khah ah-SEE-khah.

The splendor of Your glorious majesty, and the words of Your wonders, I will recount.

וֶעֱזוּז נוֹרְאֹתֶיךָ יֹאמֵרוּ, וּגְדֻלָּתְךָ אֲסַפְּרֶנָּה.

veh-eh-ZOOZ noh-reh-oh-TEH-khah yoh-meh-ROO, oog-doo-laht-KHAH ah-sahp-reh-NAH.

They will speak of the might of Your awesome deeds, and I will tell of Your greatness.

זֵכֶר רַב טוּבְךָ יַבִּיעוּ, וְצִדְקָתְךָ יְרַנֵּנוּ.

ZEH-kher rahv toov-KHAH yah-bee-OO, veh-tzeed-kaht-KHAH yeh-rah-neh-NOO.

They will pour forth the memory of Your abundant goodness, and sing joyfully of Your righteousness.

חַנּוּן וְרַחוּם יְהֹוָה, אֶרֶךְ אַפַּיִם וּגְדָל חָסֶד.

khah-NOON veh-rah-KHOOM ah-doh-NIGH, EH-rekh ah-PAH-yeem oog-dahl KHAH-sed.

The Lord is gracious and compassionate, slow to anger and great in kindness.

טוֹב יְהֹוָה לַכֹּל, וְרַחֲמָיו עַל כָּל מַעֲשָׂיו.

TOHV ah-doh-NIGH lah-KOHL, veh-rah-khah-MAHV ahl kohl mah-ah-SAHV.

The Lord is good to all, and His mercies are upon all His works.

יוֹדוּךָ יְהֹוָה כָּל מַעֲשֶׂיךָ, וַחֲסִידֶיךָ יְבָרְכוּכָה.

yoh-DOO-khah ah-doh-NIGH kohl mah-ah-SEH-khah, vah-khah-see-DEH-khah yeh-vahr-KHOO-khah.

All Your works will thank You, Lord, and Your devoted ones will bless You.

כְּבוֹד מַלְכוּתְךָ יֹאמֵרוּ, וּגְבוּרָתְךָ יְדַבֵּרוּ.

keh-VOHD mahl-khoot-KHAH yoh-meh-ROO, oog-voo-raht-KHAH yeh-dah-beh-ROO.

They will speak of the glory of Your kingdom, and talk of Your might.

לְהוֹדִיעַ לִבְנֵי הָאָדָם גְּבוּרֹתָיו, וּכְבוֹד הֲדַר מַלְכוּתוֹ.

leh-hoh-DEE-ah leev-NAY hah-ah-DAHM geh-voo-roh-TAHV, oo-khvohd hah-DAHR mahl-khoo-TOH.

To make known to mankind His mighty deeds, and the glorious splendor of His kingdom.

מַלְכוּתְךָ מַלְכוּת כָּל עֹלָמִים, וּמֶמְשַׁלְתְּךָ בְּכָל דּוֹר וָדֹר.

mahl-khoot-KHAH mahl-KHOOT kohl oh-lah-MEEM, oo-mehm-shahl-teh-KHAH beh-KHOHL dohr vah-DOHR.

Your kingdom is a kingdom for all time, and Your dominion is in every generation.

סוֹמֵךְ יְהֹוָה לְכָל הַנֹּפְלִים, וְזוֹקֵף לְכָל הַכְּפוּפִים.

soh-MEKH ah-doh-NIGH leh-KHOHL hah-nohf-LEEM, veh-zoh-KEF leh-KHOHL hah-keh-foo-FEEM.

The Lord supports all who fall, and raises up all who are bowed down.

עֵינֵי כֹל אֵלֶיךָ יְשַׂבֵּרוּ, וְאַתָּה נוֹתֵן לָהֶם אֶת אָכְלָם בְּעִתּוֹ.

ay-NAY khohl eh-LEH-khah yeh-sah-BEH-roo, veh-ah-TAH noh-TEN lah-HEM eht ohkh-LAHM beh-ee-TOH.

The eyes of all look hopefully to You, and You give them their food in its season.

פּוֹתֵחַ אֶת יָדֶךָ, וּמַשְׂבִּיעַ לְכָל חַי רָצוֹן.

poh-TEH-ahkh eht yah-DEH-khah, oo-mahs-BEE-ah leh-KHOHL KHIGH rah-TZOHN.

You open Your hand and satisfy the desire of every living thing.

צַדִּיק יְהֹוָה בְּכָל דְּרָכָיו, וְחָסִיד בְּכָל מַעֲשָׂיו.

tzah-DEEK ah-doh-NIGH beh-KHOHL deh-rah-KHAHV, veh-khah-SEED beh-KHOHL mah-ah-SAHV.

The Lord is righteous in all His ways, and kind in all His deeds.

קָרוֹב יְהֹוָה לְכָל קֹרְאָיו, לְכֹל אֲשֶׁר יִקְרָאֻהוּ בֶאֱמֶת.

kah-ROHV ah-doh-NIGH leh-KHOHL koh-reh-AHV, leh-KHOHL ah-SHER yeek-rah-OO-hoo veh-eh-MET.

The Lord is near to all who call upon Him, to all who call upon Him in truth.

רְצוֹן יְרֵאָיו יַעֲשֶׂה, וְאֶת שַׁוְעָתָם יִשְׁמַע וְיוֹשִׁיעֵם.

reh-TZOHN yeh-ray-AHV yah-ah-SEH, veh-eht shahv-ah-TAHM yeesh-MAH veh-yoh-shee-EM.

He fulfills the desire of those who fear Him; He hears their cry and saves them.

שׁוֹמֵר יְהֹוָה אֶת כָּל אֹהֲבָיו, וְאֵת כָּל הָרְשָׁעִים יַשְׁמִיד.

shoh-MER ah-doh-NIGH eht kohl oh-hah-VAHV, veh-ET kohl hah-reh-shah-EEM yahsh-MEED.

The Lord guards all who love Him, and destroys all the wicked.

תְּהִלַּת יְהֹוָה יְדַבֶּר פִּי, וִיבָרֵךְ כָּל בָּשָׂר שֵׁם קָדְשׁוֹ לְעוֹלָם וָעֶד.

teh-hee-LAHT ah-doh-NIGH yeh-dah-BER PEE, vee-vah-REKH kohl bah-SAHR SHEM kohd-SHOH leh-oh-LAHM vah-ED.

My mouth will speak the praise of the Lord, and all flesh will bless His holy name forever and ever.

Closing verse

וַאֲנַחְנוּ נְבָרֵךְ יָהּ מֵעַתָּה וְעַד עוֹלָם, הַלְלוּיָהּ.

vah-ah-NAKH-noo neh-vah-REKH YAH meh-ah-TAH veh-AHD oh-LAHM, hahl-loo-YAH.

We will bless the Lord from now and forever, Halleluyah. (Psalm 115:18)

The final psalm, recited every morning in Pesukei DeZimra. The last verse is customarily said twice.

Hebrew audio

הַלְלוּיָהּ, הַלְלוּ אֵל בְּקָדְשׁוֹ, הַלְלוּהוּ בִּרְקִיעַ עֻזּוֹ.

hahl-loo-YAH, hahl-LOO EL beh-kohd-SHOH, hahl-LOO-hoo beer-KEE-ah oo-ZOH.

Halleluyah. Praise God in His sanctuary; praise Him in the firmament of His strength.

הַלְלוּהוּ בִגְבוּרֹתָיו, הַלְלוּהוּ כְּרֹב גֻּדְלוֹ.

hahl-LOO-hoo veeg-voo-roh-TAHV, hahl-LOO-hoo keh-ROHV good-LOH.

Praise Him for His mighty deeds; praise Him according to His abundant greatness.

הַלְלוּהוּ בְּתֵקַע שׁוֹפָר, הַלְלוּהוּ בְּנֵבֶל וְכִנּוֹר.

hahl-LOO-hoo beh-TEH-kah shoh-FAHR, hahl-LOO-hoo beh-NEH-vel veh-khee-NOHR.

Praise Him with the blast of the shofar; praise Him with lyre and harp.

הַלְלוּהוּ בְּתֹף וּמָחוֹל, הַלְלוּהוּ בְּמִנִּים וְעֻגָב.

hahl-LOO-hoo beh-TOHF oo-mah-KHOHL, hahl-LOO-hoo beh-mee-NEEM veh-oo-GAHV.

Praise Him with drum and dance; praise Him with strings and flute.

הַלְלוּהוּ בְצִלְצְלֵי שָׁמַע, הַלְלוּהוּ בְּצִלְצְלֵי תְרוּעָה.

hahl-LOO-hoo veh-tzeel-tzeh-LAY shah-MAH, hahl-LOO-hoo beh-tzeel-tzeh-LAY teh-roo-AH.

Praise Him with resounding cymbals; praise Him with clanging cymbals.

כֹּל הַנְּשָׁמָה תְּהַלֵּל יָהּ, הַלְלוּיָהּ.

kohl hah-neh-shah-MAH teh-hah-LEL YAH, hahl-loo-YAH.

Let every soul praise the Lord. Halleluyah.

כֹּל הַנְּשָׁמָה תְּהַלֵּל יָהּ, הַלְלוּיָהּ.

kohl hah-neh-shah-MAH teh-hah-LEL YAH, hahl-loo-YAH.

Let every soul praise the Lord. Halleluyah.

Said after the last psalm, and without any speech in between. It does not begin with “Barukh,” because it is joined to Baruch SheAmar. Mizrahi custom: only the chazan stands. On Shabbat and Yom Tov, Mizrahi siddurim add “uvchen” before it. The closing “melekh gadol umehulal” joins the two readings found in earlier sources; Ashkenazim say “gadol batishbachot.”

Hebrew audio

Praise and blessing

יִשְׁתַּבַּח שִׁמְךָ לָעַד מַלְכֵּנוּ, הָאֵל הַמֶּלֶךְ הַגָּדוֹל וְהַקָּדוֹשׁ בַּשָּׁמַיִם וּבָאָרֶץ. כִּי לְךָ נָאֶה, יְהֹוָה אֱלֹהֵינוּ וֵאלֹהֵי אֲבוֹתֵינוּ, שִׁיר וּשְׁבָחָה, הַלֵּל וְזִמְרָה, עֹז וּמֶמְשָׁלָה, נֶצַח, גְּדֻלָּה וּגְבוּרָה, תְּהִלָּה וְתִפְאֶרֶת, קְדֻשָּׁה וּמַלְכוּת, בְּרָכוֹת וְהוֹדָאוֹת מֵעַתָּה וְעַד עוֹלָם.

yeesh-tah-BAKH sheem-KHAH lah-AHD mahl-KAY-noo, hah-EL hah-MEH-lekh hah-gah-DOHL veh-hah-kah-DOHSH bah-shah-MAH-yeem oo-vah-AH-retz. kee leh-KHAH nah-EH, ah-doh-NIGH eh-loh-HAY-noo vay-loh-HAY ah-voh-TAY-noo, SHEER oosh-vah-KHAH, hah-LEL veez-MAH-rah, OHZ oo-mem-shah-LAH, NEH-tzahkh, geh-doo-LAH oog-voo-RAH, teh-hee-LAH veh-teef-EH-ret, keh-doo-SHAH oo-mahl-KHOOT, beh-rah-KHOHT veh-hoh-dah-OHT, meh-ah-TAH veh-AHD oh-LAHM.

May Your name be praised forever, our King, the God, the great and holy King in heaven and on earth. For to You, Lord our God and God of our fathers, belong song and praise, hymn and psalm, strength and dominion, victory, greatness and might, praise and splendor, holiness and kingship, blessings and thanksgiving, from now and forever.

בָּרוּךְ אַתָּה יְהֹוָה, אֵל מֶלֶךְ גָּדוֹל וּמְהֻלָּל בַּתִּשְׁבָּחוֹת, אֵל הַהוֹדָאוֹת, אֲדוֹן הַנִּפְלָאוֹת, הַבּוֹחֵר בְּשִׁירֵי זִמְרָה, מֶלֶךְ אֵל חֵי הָעוֹלָמִים.

bah-ROOKH ah-TAH ah-doh-NIGH, EL MEH-lekh gah-DOHL oo-meh-hoo-LAHL bah-teesh-bah-KHOHT, EL hah-hoh-dah-OHT, ah-DOHN hah-neef-lah-OHT, hah-boh-KHAYR beh-shee-RAY zeem-RAH, MEH-lekh EL KHAY hah-oh-lah-MEEM.

Blessed are You, Lord, God, King great and extolled with praises, God of thanksgiving, Master of wonders, who chooses songs of psalm, King, God, Life of the worlds.

With a Minyan

דְּבָרִים שֶׁבִּקְדֻשָּׁה

Said in the presence of ten men

Said at the end of each service, standing. It may also be said without a minyan; the Kaddish that follows requires one. Bow at “vah-ah-NAKH-noo meesh-tah-khah-VEEM.”

Hebrew audio

עָלֵינוּ לְשַׁבֵּחַ לַאֲדוֹן הַכֹּל, לָתֵת גְּדֻלָּה לְיוֹצֵר בְּרֵאשִׁית, שֶׁלֹּא עָשָׂנוּ כְּגוֹיֵי הָאֲרָצוֹת, וְלֹא שָׂמָנוּ כְּמִשְׁפְּחוֹת הָאֲדָמָה, שֶׁלֹּא שָׂם חֶלְקֵנוּ כָּהֶם, וְגוֹרָלֵנוּ כְּכָל הֲמוֹנָם. שֶׁהֵם מִשְׁתַּחֲוִים לְהֶבֶל וָרִיק, וּמִתְפַּלְלִים אֶל אֵל לֹא יוֹשִׁיעַ. וַאֲנַחְנוּ מִשְׁתַּחֲוִים לִפְנֵי מֶלֶךְ מַלְכֵי הַמְּלָכִים, הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא.

ah-LAY-noo leh-shah-BEH-ahkh lah-ah-DOHN hah-KOHL, lah-TET geh-doo-LAH leh-yoh-TZER beh-reh-SHEET, sheh-LOH ah-SAH-noo keh-goh-YAY hah-ah-rah-TZOHT, veh-LOH sah-MAH-noo keh-meesh-peh-KHOHT hah-ah-dah-MAH, sheh-LOH SAHM khel-KEH-noo kah-HEM, veh-goh-rah-LEH-noo keh-KHOHL hah-moh-NAHM. sheh-HEM meesh-tah-khah-VEEM leh-HEH-vel vah-REEK, oo-meet-pah-leh-LEEM el EL loh yoh-SHEE-ah. vah-ah-NAKH-noo meesh-tah-khah-VEEM leef-NAY MEH-lekh mahl-KHAY hah-meh-lah-KHEEM, hah-kah-DOHSH bah-ROOKH HOO.

It is our duty to praise the Master of all, to ascribe greatness to the Creator of the beginning, who did not make us like the nations of the lands nor place us like the families of the earth, who did not make our portion like theirs nor our lot like all their multitudes. For they bow to vanity and emptiness and pray to a god who cannot save. But we bow before the King of kings of kings, the Holy One, blessed be He.
