שֶׁהוּא נוֹטֶה שָׁמַיִם וְיוֹסֵד אָרֶץ, וּמוֹשַׁב יְקָרוֹ בַּשָּׁמַיִם מִמַּעַל, וּשְׁכִינַת עֻזּוֹ בְּגָבְהֵי מְרוֹמִים. הוּא אֱלֹהֵינוּ וְאֵין עוֹד אַחֵר, אֱמֶת מַלְכֵּנוּ וְאֶפֶס זוּלָתוֹ, כַּכָּתוּב בַּתּוֹרָה: וְיָדַעְתָּ הַיּוֹם וַהֲשֵׁבֹתָ אֶל לְבָבֶךָ, כִּי יְהֹוָה הוּא הָאֱלֹהִים בַּשָּׁמַיִם מִמַּעַל וְעַל הָאָרֶץ מִתָּחַת, אֵין עוֹד.

sheh-HOO noh-TEH shah-MAH-yeem veh-yoh-SED AH-retz, oo-moh-SHAHV yeh-kah-ROH bah-shah-MAH-yeem mee-MAH-ahl, oosh-khee-NAHT oo-ZOH beh-gohv-HAY meh-roh-MEEM. HOO eh-loh-HAY-noo veh-AYN ohd ah-KHER, eh-MET mahl-KEH-noo veh-EH-fes zoo-lah-TOH, kah-kah-TOOV bah-toh-RAH: veh-yah-dah-TAH hah-YOHM vah-hah-sheh-voh-TAH el leh-vah-VEH-khah, kee ah-doh-NIGH HOO hah-eh-loh-HEEM bah-shah-MAH-yeem mee-MAH-ahl veh-ahl hah-AH-retz mee-TAH-khaht, AYN ohd.

He stretches out the heavens and founds the earth; the seat of His glory is in the heavens above and the presence of His might in the loftiest heights. He is our God; there is no other. Our King is true; there is none besides Him, as it is written in the Torah: Know this day and take it to heart that the Lord is God in the heavens above and on the earth below; there is no other.

Second paragraph — Al Ken Nekaveh

עַל כֵּן נְקַוֶּה לְךָ יְהֹוָה אֱלֹהֵינוּ לִרְאוֹת מְהֵרָה בְּתִפְאֶרֶת עֻזָּךְ, לְהַעֲבִיר גִּלּוּלִים מִן הָאָרֶץ, וְהָאֱלִילִים כָּרוֹת יִכָּרֵתוּן, לְתַקֵּן עוֹלָם בְּמַלְכוּת שַׁדַּי, וְכָל בְּנֵי בָשָׂר יִקְרְאוּ בִשְׁמֶךָ, לְהַפְנוֹת אֵלֶיךָ כָּל רִשְׁעֵי אָרֶץ. יַכִּירוּ וְיֵדְעוּ כָּל יוֹשְׁבֵי תֵבֵל, כִּי לְךָ תִּכְרַע כָּל בֶּרֶךְ, תִּשָּׁבַע כָּל לָשׁוֹן.

ahl KEN neh-kah-VEH leh-KHAH ah-doh-NIGH eh-loh-HAY-noo leer-OHT meh-heh-RAH beh-teef-EH-ret oo-ZAKH, leh-hah-ah-VEER gee-loo-LEEM meen hah-AH-retz, veh-hah-eh-lee-LEEM kah-ROHT yee-kah-reh-TOON, leh-tah-KEN oh-LAHM beh-mahl-KHOOT shah-DIGH, veh-khohl beh-NAY vah-SAHR yeek-reh-OO veesh-MEH-khah, leh-hahf-NOHT eh-LEH-khah kohl reesh-AY AH-retz. yah-KEE-roo veh-yed-OO kohl yohsh-VAY teh-VEL, kee leh-KHAH teekh-RAH kohl BEH-rekh, tee-shah-VAH kohl lah-SHOHN.

Therefore we hope in You, Lord our God, soon to see the glory of Your might, removing idols from the earth so that false gods are utterly cut off, perfecting the world under the kingship of the Almighty. All humanity will call upon Your name, and all the wicked of the earth will turn to You. All who dwell on earth will recognize and know that to You every knee must bend and every tongue swear loyalty.

לְפָנֶיךָ יְהֹוָה אֱלֹהֵינוּ יִכְרְעוּ וְיִפֹּלוּ, וְלִכְבוֹד שִׁמְךָ יְקָר יִתֵּנוּ, וִיקַבְּלוּ כֻלָּם אֶת עֹל מַלְכוּתֶךָ, וְתִמְלֹךְ עֲלֵיהֶם מְהֵרָה לְעוֹלָם וָעֶד. כִּי הַמַּלְכוּת שֶׁלְּךָ הִיא, וּלְעוֹלְמֵי עַד תִּמְלֹךְ בְּכָבוֹד, כַּכָּתוּב בְּתוֹרָתֶךָ: יְהֹוָה יִמְלֹךְ לְעֹלָם וָעֶד. וְנֶאֱמַר: וְהָיָה יְהֹוָה לְמֶלֶךְ עַל כָּל הָאָרֶץ, בַּיּוֹם הַהוּא יִהְיֶה יְהֹוָה אֶחָד וּשְׁמוֹ אֶחָד.

leh-fah-NEH-khah ah-doh-NIGH eh-loh-HAY-noo yeekh-reh-OO veh-yee-POH-loo, veh-leekh-VOHD sheem-KHAH yeh-KAHR yee-TEH-noo, vee-kah-beh-LOO khoo-LAHM eht OHL mahl-khoo-TEH-khah, veh-teem-LOHKH ah-lay-HEM meh-heh-RAH leh-oh-LAHM vah-ED. kee hah-mahl-KHOOT sheh-leh-KHAH HEE, ool-oh-leh-MAY ahd teem-LOHKH beh-khah-VOHD, kah-kah-TOOV beh-toh-rah-TEH-khah: ah-doh-NIGH yeem-LOHKH leh-oh-LAHM vah-ED. veh-neh-eh-MAHR: veh-hah-YAH ah-doh-NIGH leh-MEH-lekh ahl kohl hah-AH-retz, bah-YOHM hah-HOO yeeh-YEH ah-doh-NIGH eh-KHAHD oosh-MOH eh-KHAHD.

Before You, Lord our God, they will kneel and fall, and give honor to Your glorious name. They will all accept the yoke of Your kingship, and You will soon reign over them forever and ever. For the kingdom is Yours, and You will reign in glory for all eternity, as it is written in Your Torah: The Lord will reign forever and ever. And it is said: The Lord will be King over all the earth; on that day the Lord will be One and His name One.

Some Sephardic siddurim close with “oov-toh-rah-teh-KHAH kah-TOOV leh-MOHR: sheh-MAH yees-rah-EL, ah-doh-NIGH eh-loh-HAY-noo, ah-doh-NIGH eh-KHAHD.” Follow your siddur.

Recited standing, only in the presence of a minyan of ten adult men. The congregation’s responses are marked; the mourner pauses for each.

Hebrew audio

יִתְגַּדַּל וְיִתְקַדַּשׁ שְׁמֵהּ רַבָּא.

yeet-gah-DAHL veh-yeet-kah-DAHSH sheh-MEH rah-BAH.

Magnified and sanctified be His great name

Congregation: ah-MEN.

בְּעָלְמָא דִּי בְרָא כִרְעוּתֵהּ, וְיַמְלִיךְ מַלְכוּתֵהּ, וְיַצְמַח פֻּרְקָנֵהּ, וִיקָרֵב מְשִׁיחֵהּ.

beh-AHL-mah dee veh-RAH kheer-oo-TEH, veh-yahm-LEEKH mahl-khoo-TEH, veh-yahtz-MAHKH poor-kah-NEH, vee-kah-REV meh-shee-KHEH.

in the world He created according to His will. May He establish His kingdom, cause His redemption to sprout, and bring near His anointed,

Congregation: ah-MEN.

בְּחַיֵּיכוֹן וּבְיוֹמֵיכוֹן וּבְחַיֵּי דְכָל בֵּית יִשְׂרָאֵל, בַּעֲגָלָא וּבִזְמַן קָרִיב, וְאִמְרוּ אָמֵן.

beh-khah-yay-KHOHN oov-yoh-may-KHOHN oov-khah-YAY deh-KHOHL BAYT yees-rah-EL, bah-ah-gah-LAH oo-veez-MAHN kah-REEV, veh-eem-ROO ah-MEN.

in your lifetime and in your days and in the lifetime of all the house of Israel, swiftly and soon; and say, Amen.

Congregation: ah-MEN. yeh-HEH sheh-MEH rah-BAH meh-vah-RAKH leh-ah-LAHM ool-ahl-MAY ahl-mah-YAH yeet-bah-RAKH. By Mizrahi custom the congregation continues saying the next paragraph along with the mourner, through “dah-ah-mee-RAHN beh-AHL-mah.”

יְהֵא שְׁמֵהּ רַבָּא מְבָרַךְ לְעָלַם וּלְעָלְמֵי עָלְמַיָּא יִתְבָּרַךְ, וְיִשְׁתַּבַּח, וְיִתְפָּאַר, וְיִתְרוֹמַם, וְיִתְנַשֵּׂא, וְיִתְהַדָּר, וְיִתְעַלֶּה, וְיִתְהַלָּל שְׁמֵהּ דְּקֻדְשָׁא בְּרִיךְ הוּא.

yeh-HEH sheh-MEH rah-BAH meh-vah-RAKH leh-ah-LAHM ool-ahl-MAY ahl-mah-YAH yeet-bah-RAKH, veh-yeesh-tah-BAKH, veh-yeet-pah-AHR, veh-yeet-roh-MAHM, veh-yeet-nah-SEH, veh-yeet-hah-DAHR, veh-yeet-ah-LEH, veh-yeet-hah-LAHL sheh-MEH deh-kood-SHAH beh-REEKH HOO.

May His great name be blessed forever and for all eternity. Blessed, praised, glorified, exalted, extolled, honored, elevated and lauded be the name of the Holy One, blessed be He,

Congregation: ah-MEN.

לְעֵלָּא מִן כָּל בִּרְכָתָא, שִׁירָתָא, תֻּשְׁבְּחָתָא וְנֶחֱמָתָא, דַּאֲמִירָן בְּעָלְמָא, וְאִמְרוּ אָמֵן.

leh-EL-lah meen kohl beer-khah-TAH, shee-rah-TAH, toosh-beh-khah-TAH veh-neh-kheh-mah-TAH, dah-ah-mee-RAHN beh-AHL-mah, veh-eem-ROO ah-MEN.

beyond all blessings, songs, praises and consolations that are uttered in the world; and say, Amen.

Congregation: ah-MEN.

יְהֵא שְׁלָמָא רַבָּא מִן שְׁמַיָּא, חַיִּים וְשָׂבָע וִישׁוּעָה וְנֶחָמָה וְשֵׁיזָבָא וּרְפוּאָה וּגְאֻלָּה וּסְלִיחָה וְכַפָּרָה וְרֶוַח וְהַצָּלָה, לָנוּ וּלְכָל עַמּוֹ יִשְׂרָאֵל, וְאִמְרוּ אָמֵן.

yeh-HEH sheh-lah-MAH rah-BAH meen sheh-mah-YAH, khah-YEEM veh-sah-VAH vee-shoo-AH veh-neh-khah-MAH veh-shay-zah-VAH oor-foo-AH oog-oo-LAH oos-lee-KHAH veh-khah-pah-RAH veh-REH-vahkh veh-hah-tzah-LAH, LAH-noo ool-KHOHL ah-MOH yees-rah-EL, veh-eem-ROO ah-MEN.

May there be abundant peace from Heaven, with life, plenty, salvation, consolation, deliverance, healing, redemption, forgiveness, atonement, relief and rescue, for us and for all His people Israel; and say, Amen.

Congregation: ah-MEN. Take three steps back, bowing left, right, and forward:

עֹשֶׂה שָׁלוֹם בִּמְרוֹמָיו, הוּא בְּרַחֲמָיו יַעֲשֶׂה שָׁלוֹם עָלֵינוּ וְעַל כָּל עַמּוֹ יִשְׂרָאֵל, וְאִמְרוּ אָמֵן.

oh-SEH shah-LOHM beem-roh-MAHV, HOO beh-rah-khah-MAHV yah-ah-SEH shah-LOHM ah-LAY-noo veh-ahl kohl ah-MOH yees-rah-EL, veh-eem-ROO ah-MEN.

He who makes peace in His heights, in His compassion may He make peace for us and for all His people Israel; and say, Amen.

Congregation: ah-MEN. During the Ten Days of Repentance, say “oh-SEH hah-shah-LOHM” in place of “oh-SEH shah-LOHM.”

Tikkun HaKlali

תִּקּוּן הַכְּלָלִי

Breslov in origin, not Mizrahi: Rebbe Nachman’s ten psalms of repair

About the Tikkun HaKlali

A note on origin. The Tikkun HaKlali comes from Breslov Hasidic theology, not from the Mizrahi-Sephardic tradition. It is included here, with the same Mizrahi pronunciation as the rest of this page, for those who say it.

The Tikkun HaKlali (“the General Remedy”) is a set of ten psalms that Rebbe Nachman of Breslov (1772–1810) revealed in 1810. He taught it as a prescription for repairing the damage caused by the most serious of sins, the sins against the covenant (pegam haBrit), and as a remedy for sin in general.

The ten psalms are said in this order: 16, 32, 41, 42, 59, 77, 90, 105, 137 and 150. Many Breslov Hasidim say them every day. Rebbe Nachman promised that whoever comes to his grave in Uman, says these ten psalms and gives a coin to charity, he will help, however grave the sin.

Primary benefits

Mental & Emotional
Combats Depression and Anxiety: Rebbe Nachman linked spiritual blockages directly to deep sadness and heavy energy. Reciting these songs breaks through mental heaviness, transforming despair into joy.
Spiritual Repair
Complete Atonement: It cleanses the soul of the toxic spiritual “residue” left behind by past mistakes and intentional or accidental sins.
Physical Vitality
Restores the Life Pulse: Kabbalistically, the ten songs correspond to ten physical and metaphysical pulses. Balancing them promotes a holistic sense of physical and mental well-being.
Divine Connection
Pierces “Narrow Places”: When a person feels entirely stuck, distant from God, or unable to find the words to pray, Tikkun HaKlali acts as a crowbar that reopens the gates of personal prayer and faith (Emunah).

Further reading: The Ten Melodies of Awakening (breslov.org, PDF) · The Power of Tikkun HaKlali (kabbalahempowerment.com)

How to recite it

Recite the ten psalms in their set order (16, 32, 41, 42, 59, 77, 90, 105, 137 and 150), from beginning to end and without any interruption whatsoever. Do not stop to speak, to answer, or to attend to anything else until the tenth psalm is complete.

Before you begin, pause for a quiet moment to settle your mind. Bring to heart the one matter for which you are asking Hashem’s help, and hold that intention as you recite.

Hebrew audio

מִכְתָּם לְדָוִד, שָׁמְרֵנִי אֵל כִּי חָסִיתִי בָךְ.

meekh-TAHM leh-dah-VEED, shohm-REH-nee EL kee khah-SEE-tee VAHKH.

A michtam of David. Preserve me, O God, for I have taken refuge in You.

אָמַרְתְּ לַיהֹוָה אֲדֹנָי אָתָּה, טוֹבָתִי בַּל עָלֶיךָ.

ah-MAHRT lah-doh-NIGH ah-doh-NIGH ah-TAH, toh-vah-TEE bahl ah-LEH-khah.

O my soul, you have said to the Lord, “You are my Lord; I have no good besides You.”

לִקְדוֹשִׁים אֲשֶׁר בָּאָרֶץ הֵמָּה, וְאַדִּירֵי כָּל חֶפְצִי בָם.

leek-doh-SHEEM ah-SHER bah-AH-retz HEH-mah, veh-ah-dee-RAY kohl khef-TZEE VAHM.

As for the holy ones who are in the land, and the noble ones, all my delight is in them.

יִרְבּוּ עַצְּבוֹתָם אַחֵר מָהָרוּ, בַּל אַסִּיךְ נִסְכֵּיהֶם מִדָּם, וּבַל אֶשָּׂא אֶת שְׁמוֹתָם עַל שְׂפָתָי.

yeer-BOO ah-tzeh-voh-TAHM ah-KHER mah-hah-ROO, bahl ah-SEEKH nees-kay-HEM mee-DAHM, oo-VAHL eh-SAH eht sheh-moh-TAHM ahl seh-fah-TIGH.

Their sorrows multiply who hasten after another god; I will not pour out their libations of blood, nor take their names upon my lips.

יְהֹוָה מְנָת חֶלְקִי וְכוֹסִי, אַתָּה תּוֹמִיךְ גּוֹרָלִי.

ah-doh-NIGH meh-NAHT khel-KEE veh-khoh-SEE, ah-TAH toh-MEEKH goh-rah-LEE.

The Lord is the portion of my inheritance and of my cup; You maintain my lot.

חֲבָלִים נָפְלוּ לִי בַּנְּעִמִים, אַף נַחֲלָת שָׁפְרָה עָלָי.

khah-vah-LEEM nahf-LOO lee bahn-neh-ee-MEEM, ahf nah-khah-LAHT shahf-RAH ah-LIGH.

The lines have fallen for me in pleasant places; indeed, my inheritance is beautiful to me.

אֲבָרֵךְ אֶת יְהֹוָה אֲשֶׁר יְעָצָנִי, אַף לֵילוֹת יִסְּרוּנִי כִלְיוֹתָי.

ah-vah-REKH eht ah-doh-NIGH ah-SHER yeh-ah-TZAH-nee, ahf lay-LOHT yee-seh-ROO-nee kheel-yoh-TIGH.

I will bless the Lord, who has counseled me; even in the nights my innermost self instructs me.

שִׁוִּיתִי יְהֹוָה לְנֶגְדִּי תָמִיד, כִּי מִימִינִי בַּל אֶמּוֹט.

shee-vee-TEE ah-doh-NIGH leh-neg-DEE tah-MEED, kee mee-mee-NEE bahl eh-MOHT.

I have set the Lord before me always; because He is at my right hand, I shall not be shaken.

לָכֵן שָׂמַח לִבִּי וַיָּגֶל כְּבוֹדִי, אַף בְּשָׂרִי יִשְׁכֹּן לָבֶטַח.

lah-KHEN sah-MAHKH lee-BEE vah-YAH-gel keh-voh-DEE, ahf beh-sah-REE yeesh-KOHN lah-VEH-tahkh.

Therefore my heart rejoices and my glory exults; my flesh too dwells in safety.

כִּי לֹא תַעֲזֹב נַפְשִׁי לִשְׁאוֹל, לֹא תִתֵּן חֲסִידְךָ לִרְאוֹת שָׁחַת.

kee loh tah-ah-ZOHV nahf-SHEE leesh-OHL, loh tee-TEN khah-see-deh-KHAH leer-OHT SHAH-khaht.

For You will not abandon my soul to the grave; You will not let Your devoted one see the pit.

תּוֹדִיעֵנִי אֹרַח חַיִּים, שֹׂבַע שְׂמָחוֹת אֶת פָּנֶיךָ, נְעִמוֹת בִּימִינְךָ נֶצַח.

toh-dee-EH-nee OH-rahkh khah-YEEM, SOH-vah seh-mah-KHOHT eht pah-NEH-khah, neh-ee-MOHT bee-mee-neh-KHAH NEH-tzahkh.

You make known to me the path of life; fullness of joys is in Your presence, delights are at Your right hand forever.

Hebrew audio

לְדָוִד מַשְׂכִּיל, אַשְׁרֵי נְשׂוּי פֶּשַׁע כְּסוּי חֲטָאָה.

leh-dah-VEED mahs-KEEL, ahsh-RAY neh-SOOY PEH-shah keh-SOOY khah-tah-AH.

Of David, a maskil. Happy is the one whose transgression is forgiven, whose sin is covered.

אַשְׁרֵי אָדָם לֹא יַחְשֹׁב יְהֹוָה לוֹ עָוֹן, וְאֵין בְּרוּחוֹ רְמִיָּה.

ahsh-RAY ah-DAHM loh yahkh-SHOHV ah-doh-NIGH LOH ah-VOHN, veh-AYN beh-roo-KHOH reh-mee-YAH.

Happy is the man to whom the Lord counts no iniquity, and in whose spirit there is no deceit.

כִּי הֶחֱרַשְׁתִּי בָּלוּ עֲצָמָי, בְּשַׁאֲגָתִי כָּל הַיּוֹם.

kee heh-kheh-RAHSH-tee BAH-loo ah-tzah-MIGH, beh-shah-ah-gah-TEE kohl hah-YOHM.

When I kept silent, my bones wasted away through my groaning all day long.

כִּי יוֹמָם וָלַיְלָה תִּכְבַּד עָלַי יָדֶךָ, נֶהְפַּךְ לְשַׁדִּי בְּחַרְבֹנֵי קַיִץ סֶלָה.

kee yoh-MAHM vah-LIGH-lah teekh-BAHD ah-LIGH yah-DEH-khah, neh-PAHKH leh-shah-DEE beh-khahr-voh-NAY KAH-yeetz SEH-lah.

For day and night Your hand was heavy upon me; my vigor dried up as in the heat of summer, Selah.

חַטָּאתִי אוֹדִיעֲךָ וַעֲוֹנִי לֹא כִסִּיתִי, אָמַרְתִּי אוֹדֶה עֲלֵי פְשָׁעַי לַיהֹוָה, וְאַתָּה נָשָׂאתָ עֲוֹן חַטָּאתִי סֶלָה.

khah-tah-TEE oh-dee-ah-KHAH vah-ah-voh-NEE loh khee-SEE-tee, ah-MAHR-tee oh-DEH ah-LAY feh-shah-IGH lah-doh-NIGH, veh-ah-TAH nah-SAH-tah ah-VOHN khah-tah-TEE SEH-lah.

I acknowledged my sin to You and did not hide my iniquity; I said, “I will confess my transgressions to the Lord,” and You forgave the guilt of my sin, Selah.

עַל זֹאת יִתְפַּלֵּל כָּל חָסִיד אֵלֶיךָ לְעֵת מְצֹא, רַק לְשֵׁטֶף מַיִם רַבִּים, אֵלָיו לֹא יַגִּיעוּ.

ahl ZOHT yeet-pah-LEL kohl khah-SEED eh-LEH-khah leh-ET meh-TZOH, rahk leh-SHEH-tef MAH-yeem rah-BEEM, eh-LAHV loh yah-GEE-oo.

Therefore let every devoted one pray to You at a time when You may be found; when great waters overflow, they will not reach him.

אַתָּה סֵתֶר לִי מִצַּר תִּצְּרֵנִי, רָנֵּי פַלֵּט תְּסוֹבְבֵנִי סֶלָה.

ah-TAH SEH-ter LEE mee-TZAHR tee-tzeh-REH-nee, rah-NAY fah-LET teh-soh-veh-VEH-nee SEH-lah.

You are my hiding place; You protect me from trouble; You surround me with songs of deliverance, Selah.

אַשְׂכִּילְךָ וְאוֹרְךָ בְּדֶרֶךְ זוּ תֵלֵךְ, אִיעֲצָה עָלֶיךָ עֵינִי.

ahs-keel-KHAH veh-ohr-KHAH beh-DEH-rekh ZOO teh-LEKH, ee-ah-TZAH ah-LEH-khah ay-NEE.

I will instruct you and teach you the way you should go; I will counsel you with My eye upon you.

אַל תִּהְיוּ כְּסוּס כְּפֶרֶד אֵין הָבִין, בְּמֶתֶג וָרֶסֶן עֶדְיוֹ לִבְלוֹם, בַּל קְרֹב אֵלֶיךָ.

ahl teeh-YOO keh-SOOS keh-FEH-red AYN hah-VEEN, beh-MEH-teg vah-REH-sen ed-YOH leev-LOHM, bahl keh-ROHV eh-LEH-khah.

Do not be like a horse or a mule without understanding, whose mouth must be held with bit and bridle, or it will not come near you.

רַבִּים מַכְאוֹבִים לָרָשָׁע, וְהַבּוֹטֵחַ בַּיהֹוָה חֶסֶד יְסוֹבְבֶנּוּ.

rah-BEEM mahkh-oh-VEEM lah-rah-SHAH, veh-hah-boh-TEH-ahkh bah-doh-NIGH KHEH-sed yeh-soh-veh-VEH-noo.

Many are the sorrows of the wicked, but kindness surrounds the one who trusts in the Lord.

שִׂמְחוּ בַיהֹוָה וְגִילוּ צַדִּיקִים, וְהַרְנִינוּ כָּל יִשְׁרֵי לֵב.

seem-KHOO vah-doh-NIGH veh-GEE-loo tzah-dee-KEEM, veh-hahr-NEE-noo kohl yeesh-RAY LEV.

Rejoice in the Lord and be glad, you righteous; sing for joy, all you upright of heart.

Hebrew audio

לַמְנַצֵּחַ מִזְמוֹר לְדָוִד.

lahm-nah-TZEH-ahkh meez-MOHR leh-dah-VEED.

For the conductor; a psalm of David.

אַשְׁרֵי מַשְׂכִּיל אֶל דָּל, בְּיוֹם רָעָה יְמַלְּטֵהוּ יְהֹוָה.

ahsh-RAY mahs-KEEL el DAHL, beh-YOHM rah-AH yeh-mah-leh-TEH-hoo ah-doh-NIGH.

Happy is the one who deals wisely with the poor; on a day of evil the Lord will deliver him.

יְהֹוָה יִשְׁמְרֵהוּ וִיחַיֵּהוּ וְאֻשַּׁר בָּאָרֶץ, וְאַל תִּתְּנֵהוּ בְּנֶפֶשׁ אֹיְבָיו.

ah-doh-NIGH yeesh-meh-REH-hoo vee-khah-YEH-hoo veh-oo-SHAHR bah-AH-retz, veh-AHL tee-teh-NEH-hoo beh-NEH-fesh oy-VAHV.

The Lord will guard him and keep him alive; he will be called fortunate in the land, and may You not deliver him to the will of his enemies.

יְהֹוָה יִסְעָדֶנּוּ עַל עֶרֶשׂ דְּוָי, כָּל מִשְׁכָּבוֹ הָפַכְתָּ בְחָלְיוֹ.

ah-doh-NIGH yees-ah-DEH-noo ahl EH-res deh-VIGH, kohl meesh-kah-VOH hah-FAHKH-tah veh-khohl-YOH.

The Lord will sustain him on his sickbed; You have turned all his lying down in his illness.

אֲנִי אָמַרְתִּי יְהֹוָה חָנֵּנִי, רְפָאָה נַפְשִׁי כִּי חָטָאתִי לָךְ.

ah-NEE ah-MAHR-tee ah-doh-NIGH khah-NEH-nee, reh-fah-AH nahf-SHEE kee khah-TAH-tee LAKH.

I said, “Lord, be gracious to me; heal my soul, for I have sinned against You.”

אוֹיְבַי יֹאמְרוּ רַע לִי, מָתַי יָמוּת וְאָבַד שְׁמוֹ.

oy-VIGH yoh-meh-ROO RAH lee, mah-TIGH yah-MOOT veh-ah-VAHD sheh-MOH.

My enemies speak evil of me: “When will he die, and his name perish?”

וְאִם בָּא לִרְאוֹת שָׁוְא יְדַבֵּר, לִבּוֹ יִקְבָּץ אָוֶן לוֹ, יֵצֵא לַחוּץ יְדַבֵּר.

veh-eem BAH leer-OHT SHAHV yeh-dah-BER, lee-BOH yeek-BOHTZ AH-ven LOH, yeh-TZEH lah-KHOOTZ yeh-dah-BER.

And if one comes to see me, he speaks falsehood; his heart gathers iniquity to itself; he goes out and tells it abroad.

יַחַד עָלַי יִתְלַחֲשׁוּ כָּל שֹׂנְאָי, עָלַי יַחְשְׁבוּ רָעָה לִי.

YAH-khahd ah-LIGH yeet-lah-khah-SHOO kohl soh-neh-IGH, ah-LIGH yahkh-sheh-VOO rah-AH lee.

Together all who hate me whisper against me; against me they devise evil for me.

דְּבַר בְּלִיַּעַל יָצוּק בּוֹ, וַאֲשֶׁר שָׁכַב לֹא יוֹסִיף לָקוּם.

deh-VAHR beh-lee-YAH-ahl yah-TZOOK BOH, vah-ah-SHER shah-KHAHV loh yoh-SEEF lah-KOOM.

“A vile thing has been poured upon him; now that he lies down, he will not rise again.”

גַּם אִישׁ שְׁלוֹמִי אֲשֶׁר בָּטַחְתִּי בוֹ, אוֹכֵל לַחְמִי הִגְדִּיל עָלַי עָקֵב.

gahm EESH sheh-loh-MEE ah-SHER bah-TAHKH-tee VOH, oh-KHEL lahkh-MEE heeg-DEEL ah-LIGH ah-KEV.

Even my close friend in whom I trusted, who ate my bread, has lifted his heel against me.

וְאַתָּה יְהֹוָה חָנֵּנִי וַהֲקִימֵנִי, וַאֲשַׁלְּמָה לָהֶם.

veh-ah-TAH ah-doh-NIGH khah-NEH-nee vah-hah-kee-MEH-nee, vah-ah-shah-leh-MAH lah-HEM.

But You, Lord, be gracious to me and raise me up, that I may repay them.

בְּזֹאת יָדַעְתִּי כִּי חָפַצְתָּ בִּי, כִּי לֹא יָרִיעַ אֹיְבִי עָלָי.

beh-ZOHT yah-DAH-tee kee khah-FAHTZ-tah BEE, kee loh yah-REE-ah oy-VEE ah-LIGH.

By this I know that You favor me: that my enemy does not shout in triumph over me.

וַאֲנִי בְּתֻמִּי תָּמַכְתָּ בִּי, וַתַּצִּיבֵנִי לְפָנֶיךָ לְעוֹלָם.

vah-ah-NEE beh-too-MEE tah-MAHKH-tah BEE, vah-tah-tzee-VEH-nee leh-fah-NEH-khah leh-oh-LAHM.

As for me, You have upheld me in my integrity, and set me before You forever.

בָּרוּךְ יְהֹוָה אֱלֹהֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל מֵהָעוֹלָם וְעַד הָעוֹלָם, אָמֵן וְאָמֵן.

bah-ROOKH ah-doh-NIGH eh-loh-HAY yees-rah-EL meh-hah-oh-LAHM veh-AHD hah-oh-LAHM, ah-MEN veh-ah-MEN.

Blessed is the Lord, God of Israel, from everlasting to everlasting. Amen and Amen.

Hebrew audio

לַמְנַצֵּחַ מַשְׂכִּיל לִבְנֵי קֹרַח.

lahm-nah-TZEH-ahkh mahs-KEEL leev-NAY KOH-rahkh.

For the conductor; a maskil of the sons of Korah.

כְּאַיָּל תַּעֲרֹג עַל אֲפִיקֵי מָיִם, כֵּן נַפְשִׁי תַעֲרֹג אֵלֶיךָ אֱלֹהִים.

keh-ah-YAHL tah-ah-ROHG ahl ah-fee-KAY MAH-yeem, KEN nahf-SHEE tah-ah-ROHG eh-LEH-khah eh-loh-HEEM.

As a deer yearns for streams of water, so my soul yearns for You, O God.

צָמְאָה נַפְשִׁי לֵאלֹהִים לְאֵל חָי, מָתַי אָבוֹא, וְאֵרָאֶה פְּנֵי אֱלֹהִים.

tzah-meh-AH nahf-SHEE leh-loh-HEEM leh-EL KHIGH, mah-TIGH ah-VOH, veh-eh-rah-EH peh-NAY eh-loh-HEEM.

My soul thirsts for God, for the living God; when shall I come and appear before God?

הָיְתָה לִּי דִמְעָתִי לֶחֶם יוֹמָם וָלָיְלָה, בֶּאֱמֹר אֵלַי כָּל הַיּוֹם אַיֵּה אֱלֹהֶיךָ.

hah-yeh-TAH lee deem-ah-TEE LEH-khem yoh-MAHM vah-LAH-yeh-lah, beh-eh-MOHR eh-LIGH kohl hah-YOHM AY-yeh eh-loh-HEH-khah.

My tears have been my bread day and night, as they say to me all day long, “Where is your God?”

אֵלֶּה אֶזְכְּרָה וְאֶשְׁפְּכָה עָלַי נַפְשִׁי, כִּי אֶעֱבֹר בַּסָּךְ אֶדַּדֵּם עַד בֵּית אֱלֹהִים, בְּקוֹל רִנָּה וְתוֹדָה הָמוֹן חוֹגֵג.

EH-leh eh-zeh-KRAH veh-esh-peh-KHAH ah-LIGH nahf-SHEE, kee eh-eh-VOHR bah-SAHKH eh-dah-DEM ahd BAYT eh-loh-HEEM, beh-KOHL ree-NAH veh-toh-DAH hah-MOHN khoh-GEG.

These things I remember, and I pour out my soul within me: how I would pass through the crowd and lead them to the house of God, with the voice of joy and thanksgiving, a festive multitude.

מַה תִּשְׁתּוֹחֲחִי נַפְשִׁי וַתֶּהֱמִי עָלָי, הוֹחִילִי לֵאלֹהִים כִּי עוֹד אוֹדֶנּוּ יְשׁוּעוֹת פָּנָיו.

mah teesh-toh-khah-KHEE nahf-SHEE vah-teh-heh-MEE ah-LIGH, hoh-khee-LEE leh-loh-HEEM kee OHD oh-DEH-noo yeh-shoo-OHT pah-NAHV.

Why are you downcast, my soul, and why are you disquieted within me? Hope in God, for I will yet thank Him for the salvations of His countenance.

אֱלֹהַי עָלַי נַפְשִׁי תִשְׁתּוֹחָח, עַל כֵּן אֶזְכָּרְךָ מֵאֶרֶץ יַרְדֵּן, וְחֶרְמוֹנִים מֵהַר מִצְעָר.

eh-loh-HIGH ah-LIGH nahf-SHEE teesh-toh-KHAHKH, ahl KEN ehz-kah-reh-KHAH meh-EH-retz yahr-DEN, veh-kher-moh-NEEM meh-HAHR meetz-AHR.

My God, my soul is downcast within me; therefore I remember You from the land of the Jordan and the Hermons, from Mount Mizar.

תְּהוֹם אֶל תְּהוֹם קוֹרֵא לְקוֹל צִנּוֹרֶיךָ, כָּל מִשְׁבָּרֶיךָ וְגַלֶּיךָ עָלַי עָבָרוּ.

teh-HOHM el teh-HOHM koh-REH leh-KOHL tzee-noh-REH-khah, kohl meesh-bah-REH-khah veh-gah-LEH-khah ah-LIGH ah-vah-ROO.

Deep calls to deep at the sound of Your channels; all Your breakers and Your waves have passed over me.

יוֹמָם יְצַוֶּה יְהֹוָה חַסְדּוֹ, וּבַלַּיְלָה שִׁירֹה עִמִּי, תְּפִלָּה לְאֵל חַיָּי.

yoh-MAHM yeh-tzah-VEH ah-doh-NIGH khahs-DOH, oo-vah-LIGH-lah shee-ROH ee-MEE, teh-fee-LAH leh-EL khah-YIGH.

By day the Lord commands His kindness, and at night His song is with me, a prayer to the God of my life.

אוֹמְרָה לְאֵל סַלְעִי לָמָה שְׁכַחְתָּנִי, לָמָּה קֹדֵר אֵלֵךְ בְּלַחַץ אוֹיֵב.

oh-meh-RAH leh-EL sahl-EE lah-MAH sheh-khahkh-TAH-nee, lah-MAH koh-DER eh-LEKH beh-LAH-khahtz oh-YEV.

I say to God, my rock, “Why have You forgotten me? Why must I walk in gloom, oppressed by the enemy?”

בְּרֶצַח בְּעַצְמוֹתַי חֵרְפוּנִי צוֹרְרָי, בְּאָמְרָם אֵלַי כָּל הַיּוֹם אַיֵּה אֱלֹהֶיךָ.

beh-REH-tzahkh beh-ahtz-moh-TIGH kheh-reh-FOO-nee tzoh-reh-RIGH, beh-ohm-RAHM eh-LIGH kohl hah-YOHM AY-yeh eh-loh-HEH-khah.

As with a crushing of my bones, my tormentors revile me, as they say to me all day long, “Where is your God?”

מַה תִּשְׁתּוֹחֲחִי נַפְשִׁי וּמַה תֶּהֱמִי עָלָי, הוֹחִילִי לֵאלֹהִים כִּי עוֹד אוֹדֶנּוּ, יְשׁוּעֹת פָּנַי וֵאלֹהָי.

mah teesh-toh-khah-KHEE nahf-SHEE oo-MAH teh-heh-MEE ah-LIGH, hoh-khee-LEE leh-loh-HEEM kee OHD oh-DEH-noo, yeh-shoo-OHT pah-NIGH veh-loh-HIGH.

Why are you downcast, my soul, and why are you disquieted within me? Hope in God, for I will yet thank Him, the salvation of my countenance and my God.

Hebrew audio

לַמְנַצֵּחַ אַל תַּשְׁחֵת לְדָוִד מִכְתָּם בִּשְׁלֹחַ שָׁאוּל, וַיִּשְׁמְרוּ אֶת הַבַּיִת לַהֲמִיתוֹ.

lahm-nah-TZEH-ahkh AHL tahsh-KHET leh-dah-VEED meekh-TAHM beesh-LOH-ahkh shah-OOL, vah-yeesh-meh-ROO eht hah-BAH-yeet lah-hah-mee-TOH.

For the conductor; “Do not destroy”; of David, a michtam; when Saul sent and they watched the house to kill him.

הַצִּילֵנִי מֵאֹיְבַי אֱלֹהָי, מִמִּתְקוֹמְמַי תְּשַׂגְּבֵנִי.

hah-tzee-LEH-nee meh-oy-VIGH eh-loh-HIGH, mee-meet-koh-meh-MIGH teh-sahg-geh-VEH-nee.

Rescue me from my enemies, my God; protect me from those who rise up against me.

הַצִּילֵנִי מִפֹּעֲלֵי אָוֶן, וּמֵאַנְשֵׁי דָמִים הוֹשִׁיעֵנִי.

hah-tzee-LEH-nee mee-poh-ah-LAY AH-ven, oo-meh-ahn-SHAY dah-MEEM hoh-shee-EH-nee.

Rescue me from evildoers, and save me from men of blood.

כִּי הִנֵּה אָרְבוּ לְנַפְשִׁי, יָגוּרוּ עָלַי עַזִּים, לֹא פִשְׁעִי וְלֹא חַטָּאתִי יְהֹוָה.

kee hee-NEH ahr-VOO leh-nahf-SHEE, yah-goo-ROO ah-LIGH ah-ZEEM, loh feesh-EE veh-LOH khah-tah-TEE ah-doh-NIGH.

For behold, they lie in wait for my soul; the fierce gather against me, not for my transgression nor for my sin, Lord.

בְּלִי עָוֹן יְרֻצוּן וְיִכּוֹנָנוּ, עוּרָה לִקְרָאתִי וּרְאֵה.

beh-LEE ah-VOHN yeh-roo-TZOON veh-yee-koh-NAH-noo, oo-RAH leek-rah-TEE oo-reh-EH.

Though I have no iniquity, they run and prepare themselves; awake to meet me, and see.

וְאַתָּה יְהֹוָה אֱלֹהִים צְבָאוֹת אֱלֹהֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל, הָקִיצָה לִפְקֹד כָּל הַגּוֹיִם, אַל תָּחֹן כָּל בֹּגְדֵי אָוֶן סֶלָה.

veh-ah-TAH ah-doh-NIGH eh-loh-HEEM tzeh-vah-OHT eh-loh-HAY yees-rah-EL, hah-KEE-tzah leef-KOHD kohl hah-goh-YEEM, ahl tah-KHOHN kohl boh-geh-DAY AH-ven SEH-lah.

And You, Lord, God of Hosts, God of Israel, arise to punish all the nations; do not be gracious to any treacherous evildoers, Selah.

יָשׁוּבוּ לָעֶרֶב יֶהֱמוּ כַכָּלֶב, וִיסוֹבְבוּ עִיר.

yah-SHOO-voo lah-EH-rev yeh-heh-MOO khah-KAH-lev, vee-soh-veh-VOO EER.

They return at evening, they growl like dogs and prowl about the city.

הִנֵּה יַבִּיעוּן בְּפִיהֶם, חֲרָבוֹת בְּשִׂפְתוֹתֵיהֶם, כִּי מִי שֹׁמֵעַ.

hee-NEH yah-bee-OON beh-fee-HEM, khah-rah-VOHT beh-seef-toh-tay-HEM, kee MEE shoh-MEH-ah.

Behold, they pour forth with their mouths; swords are on their lips, for they say, “Who hears?”

וְאַתָּה יְהֹוָה תִּשְׂחַק לָמוֹ, תִּלְעַג לְכָל גּוֹיִם.

veh-ah-TAH ah-doh-NIGH tees-KHAHK LAH-moh, teel-AHG leh-KHOHL goh-YEEM.

But You, Lord, laugh at them; You mock all the nations.

עֻזּוֹ אֵלֶיךָ אֶשְׁמֹרָה, כִּי אֱלֹהִים מִשְׂגַּבִּי.

oo-ZOH eh-LEH-khah esh-MOH-rah, kee eh-loh-HEEM mees-gah-BEE.

Because of his strength, I will wait for You, for God is my stronghold.

אֱלֹהֵי חַסְדִּי יְקַדְּמֵנִי, אֱלֹהִים יַרְאֵנִי בְשֹׁרְרָי.

eh-loh-HAY khahs-DEE yeh-kah-deh-MEH-nee, eh-loh-HEEM yahr-EH-nee veh-shoh-reh-RIGH.

My God of kindness will come to meet me; God will let me gaze upon my watchful foes.

אַל תַּהַרְגֵם פֶּן יִשְׁכְּחוּ עַמִּי, הֲנִיעֵמוֹ בְחֵילְךָ וְהוֹרִידֵמוֹ, מָגִנֵּנוּ אֲדֹנָי.

ahl tah-hahr-GEM pen yeesh-keh-KHOO ah-MEE, hah-nee-EH-moh veh-khay-leh-KHAH veh-hoh-ree-DEH-moh, mah-gee-NEH-noo ah-doh-NIGH.

Do not slay them, lest my people forget; make them wander by Your power and bring them down, O Lord our shield.

חַטַּאת פִּימוֹ דְּבַר שְׂפָתֵימוֹ וְיִלָּכְדוּ בִגְאוֹנָם, וּמֵאָלָה וּמִכַּחַשׁ יְסַפֵּרוּ.

khah-TAHT PEE-moh deh-VAHR seh-fah-TAY-moh veh-yee-lah-kheh-DOO veeg-oh-NAHM, oo-meh-ah-LAH oo-mee-KAH-khahsh yeh-sah-PEH-roo.

For the sin of their mouth, the word of their lips, let them be trapped in their pride, and for the cursing and lying that they speak.

כַּלֵּה בְחֵמָה כַּלֵּה וְאֵינֵמוֹ, וְיֵדְעוּ כִּי אֱלֹהִים מֹשֵׁל בְּיַעֲקֹב, לְאַפְסֵי הָאָרֶץ סֶלָה.

kah-LEH veh-kheh-MAH kah-LEH veh-ay-NEH-moh, veh-yeh-deh-OO kee eh-loh-HEEM moh-SHEL beh-yah-ah-KOHV, leh-ahf-SAY hah-AH-retz SEH-lah.

Consume them in wrath; consume them till they are no more, and let them know that God rules in Jacob, to the ends of the earth, Selah.

וְיָשׁוּבוּ לָעֶרֶב יֶהֱמוּ כַכָּלֶב, וִיסוֹבְבוּ עִיר.

veh-yah-SHOO-voo lah-EH-rev yeh-heh-MOO khah-KAH-lev, vee-soh-veh-VOO EER.

They return at evening, they growl like dogs and prowl about the city.

הֵמָּה יְנִיעוּן לֶאֱכֹל, אִם לֹא יִשְׂבְּעוּ וַיָּלִינוּ.

HEH-mah yeh-nee-OON leh-eh-KHOHL, eem loh yees-beh-OO vah-yah-LEE-noo.

They wander about to eat, and if they are not satisfied, they spend the night.

וַאֲנִי אָשִׁיר עֻזֶּךָ, וַאֲרַנֵּן לַבֹּקֶר חַסְדֶּךָ, כִּי הָיִיתָ מִשְׂגַּב לִי, וּמָנוֹס בְּיוֹם צַר לִי.

vah-ah-NEE ah-SHEER oo-ZEH-khah, vah-ah-rah-NEN lah-BOH-ker khahs-DEH-khah, kee hah-yee-TAH mees-GAHV lee, oo-mah-NOHS beh-YOHM tzahr LEE.

But I will sing of Your strength and will joyfully proclaim Your kindness in the morning, for You have been a stronghold for me and a refuge in the day of my distress.

עֻזִּי אֵלֶיךָ אֲזַמֵּרָה, כִּי אֱלֹהִים מִשְׂגַּבִּי, אֱלֹהֵי חַסְדִּי.

oo-ZEE eh-LEH-khah ah-zah-MEH-rah, kee eh-loh-HEEM mees-gah-BEE, eh-loh-HAY khahs-DEE.

My strength, to You I will sing praises, for God is my stronghold, the God of my kindness.

Hebrew audio

לַמְנַצֵּחַ עַל יְדוּתוּן לְאָסָף מִזְמוֹר.

lahm-nah-TZEH-ahkh ahl yeh-doo-TOON leh-ah-SAHF meez-MOHR.

For the conductor, on Jeduthun; of Asaph, a psalm.

קוֹלִי אֶל אֱלֹהִים וְאֶצְעָקָה, קוֹלִי אֶל אֱלֹהִים וְהַאֲזִין אֵלָי.

koh-LEE el eh-loh-HEEM veh-etz-ah-KAH, koh-LEE el eh-loh-HEEM veh-hah-ah-ZEEN eh-LIGH.

My voice is to God, and I cry out; my voice is to God, that He may hear me.

בְּיוֹם צָרָתִי אֲדֹנָי דָּרָשְׁתִּי יָדִי לַיְלָה נִגְּרָה וְלֹא תָפוּג, מֵאֲנָה הִנָּחֵם נַפְשִׁי.

beh-YOHM tzah-rah-TEE ah-doh-NIGH dah-RAHSH-tee, yah-DEE LIGH-lah neeg-RAH veh-LOH tah-FOOG, meh-ah-NAH hee-nah-KHEM nahf-SHEE.

On the day of my trouble I seek the Lord; at night my hand is stretched out without rest; my soul refuses to be comforted.

אֶזְכְּרָה אֱלֹהִים וְאֶהֱמָיָה, אָשִׂיחָה וְתִתְעַטֵּף רוּחִי סֶלָה.

ez-keh-RAH eh-loh-HEEM veh-eh-heh-MAH-yah, ah-SEE-khah veh-teet-ah-TEF roo-KHEE SEH-lah.

I remember God and I moan; I meditate, and my spirit grows faint, Selah.

אָחַזְתָּ שְׁמֻרוֹת עֵינָי, נִפְעַמְתִּי וְלֹא אֲדַבֵּר.

ah-KHAHZ-tah sheh-moo-ROHT ay-NIGH, neef-AHM-tee veh-LOH ah-dah-BER.

You hold my eyelids open; I am troubled and cannot speak.

חִשַּׁבְתִּי יָמִים מִקֶּדֶם, שְׁנוֹת עוֹלָמִים.

khee-SHAHV-tee yah-MEEM mee-KEH-dem, sheh-NOHT oh-lah-MEEM.

I consider the days of old, the years long past.

אֶזְכְּרָה נְגִינָתִי בַּלָּיְלָה עִם לְבָבִי אָשִׂיחָה, וַיְחַפֵּשׂ רוּחִי.

ez-keh-RAH neh-gee-nah-TEE bah-LIGH-lah, eem leh-vah-VEE ah-SEE-khah, vah-yeh-khah-PES roo-KHEE.

I remember my song in the night; I meditate with my heart, and my spirit searches:

הַלְעוֹלָמִים יִזְנַח אֲדֹנָי, וְלֹא יֹסִיף לִרְצוֹת עוֹד.

hah-leh-oh-lah-MEEM yeez-NAKH ah-doh-NIGH, veh-LOH yoh-SEEF leer-TZOHT ohd.

Will the Lord reject forever and never again show favor?

הֶאָפֵס לָנֶצַח חַסְדּוֹ, גָּמַר אֹמֶר לְדֹר וָדֹר.

heh-ah-FES lah-NEH-tzahkh khahs-DOH, gah-MAHR OH-mer leh-DOHR vah-DOHR.

Has His kindness ceased forever? Has His promise come to an end for all generations?

הֲשָׁכַח חַנּוֹת אֵל, אִם קָפַץ בְּאַף רַחֲמָיו סֶלָה.

hah-shah-KHAKH khah-NOHT EL, eem kah-FAHTZ beh-AHF rah-khah-MAHV SEH-lah.

Has God forgotten to be gracious? Has He in anger shut off His compassion? Selah.

וָאֹמַר חַלּוֹתִי הִיא, שְׁנוֹת יְמִין עֶלְיוֹן.

vah-oh-MAHR khah-LOH-tee HEE, sheh-NOHT yeh-MEEN el-YOHN.

Then I said, “This is my weakness: the changing of the right hand of the Most High.”

אֶזְכּוֹר מַעַלְלֵי יָהּ, כִּי אֶזְכְּרָה מִקֶּדֶם פִּלְאֶךָ.

ez-KOHR mah-ahl-LAY YAH, kee ez-keh-RAH mee-KEH-dem peel-EH-khah.

I will remember the deeds of the Lord; I will remember Your wonders of old.

וְהָגִיתִי בְכָל פָּעֳלֶךָ, וּבַעֲלִילוֹתֶיךָ אָשִׂיחָה.

veh-hah-GEE-tee veh-KHOHL pah-oh-LEH-khah, oo-vah-ah-lee-loh-TEH-khah ah-SEE-khah.

I will meditate on all Your works and consider all Your deeds.

אֱלֹהִים בַּקֹּדֶשׁ דַּרְכֶּךָ, מִי אֵל גָּדוֹל כֵּאלֹהִים.

eh-loh-HEEM bah-KOH-desh dahr-KEH-khah, mee EL gah-DOHL keh-loh-HEEM.

God, Your way is in holiness; what god is as great as God?

אַתָּה הָאֵל עֹשֵׂה פֶלֶא, הוֹדַעְתָּ בָעַמִּים עֻזֶּךָ.

ah-TAH hah-EL oh-SEH FEH-leh, hoh-DAHT-tah vah-ah-MEEM oo-ZEH-khah.

You are the God who works wonders; You have made Your strength known among the peoples.

גָּאַלְתָּ בִּזְרוֹעַ עַמֶּךָ, בְּנֵי יַעֲקֹב וְיוֹסֵף סֶלָה.

gah-AHL-tah beez-ROH-ah ah-MEH-khah, beh-NAY yah-ah-KOHV veh-yoh-SEF SEH-lah.

With Your arm You redeemed Your people, the children of Jacob and Joseph, Selah.

רָאוּךָ מַּיִם אֱלֹהִים רָאוּךָ מַּיִם יָחִילוּ, אַף יִרְגְּזוּ תְהֹמוֹת.

rah-OO-khah MAH-yeem eh-loh-HEEM rah-OO-khah MAH-yeem yah-KHEE-loo, ahf yeer-geh-ZOO teh-hoh-MOHT.

The waters saw You, God; the waters saw You and trembled; the depths too were shaken.

זֹרְמוּ מַיִם עָבוֹת קוֹל נָתְנוּ שְׁחָקִים, אַף חֲצָצֶיךָ יִתְהַלָּכוּ.

zohr-MOO MAH-yeem ah-VOHT kohl nah-teh-NOO sheh-khah-KEEM, ahf khah-tzah-TZEH-khah yeet-hah-LAH-khoo.

The clouds poured down water, the skies thundered, and Your arrows flashed back and forth.

קוֹל רַעַמְךָ בַּגַּלְגַּל הֵאִירוּ בְרָקִים תֵּבֵל, רָגְזָה וַתִּרְעַשׁ הָאָרֶץ.

kohl rah-ahm-KHAH bah-gahl-GAHL heh-EE-roo veh-rah-KEEM teh-VEL, rahg-ZAH vah-teer-AHSH hah-AH-retz.

The sound of Your thunder was in the whirlwind; lightning lit up the world; the earth trembled and shook.

בַּיָּם דַּרְכֶּךָ וּשְׁבִילְךָ בְּמַיִם רַבִּים, וְעִקְּבוֹתֶיךָ לֹא נֹדָעוּ.

bah-YAHM dahr-KEH-khah oosh-veel-KHAH beh-MAH-yeem rah-BEEM, veh-ee-keh-voh-TEH-khah loh noh-DAH-oo.

Your way was through the sea, Your path through mighty waters, and Your footsteps were not seen.

נָחִיתָ כַצֹּאן עַמֶּךָ, בְּיַד מֹשֶׁה וְאַהֲרֹן.

nah-KHEE-tah khah-TZOHN ah-MEH-khah, beh-YAHD moh-SHEH veh-ah-hah-ROHN.

You led Your people like a flock by the hand of Moses and Aaron.

תְּפִלָּה לְמֹשֶׁה אִישׁ הָאֱלֹהִים, אֲדֹנָי מָעוֹן אַתָּה הָיִיתָ לָּנוּ בְּדֹר וָדֹר.

teh-fee-LAH leh-moh-SHEH eesh hah-eh-loh-HEEM, ah-doh-NIGH mah-OHN ah-TAH hah-YEE-tah LAH-noo beh-DOHR vah-DOHR.

A prayer of Moses, the man of God. Lord, You have been our dwelling place in every generation.

בְּטֶרֶם הָרִים יֻלָּדוּ, וַתְּחוֹלֵל אֶרֶץ וְתֵבֵל, וּמֵעוֹלָם עַד עוֹלָם אַתָּה אֵל.

beh-TEH-rem hah-REEM yoo-LAH-doo, vah-teh-khoh-LEL EH-retz veh-teh-VEL, oo-meh-oh-LAHM ahd oh-LAHM ah-TAH EL.

Before the mountains were born, before You formed the earth and the world, from eternity to eternity You are God.

תָּשֵׁב אֱנוֹשׁ עַד דַּכָּא, וַתֹּאמֶר שׁוּבוּ בְנֵי אָדָם.

tah-SHEV eh-NOHSH ahd dah-KAH, vah-TOH-mer SHOO-voo veh-NAY ah-DAHM.

You return man to dust, and say: Return, children of man.

כִּי אֶלֶף שָׁנִים בְּעֵינֶיךָ כְּיוֹם אֶתְמוֹל כִּי יַעֲבֹר, וְאַשְׁמוּרָה בַלָּיְלָה.

kee EH-lef shah-NEEM beh-ay-NEH-khah keh-YOHM et-MOHL kee yah-ah-VOHR, veh-ahsh-moo-RAH vah-LIGH-lah.

For a thousand years in Your eyes are like yesterday when it has passed, like a watch in the night.

זְרַמְתָּם שֵׁנָה יִהְיוּ, בַּבֹּקֶר כֶּחָצִיר יַחֲלֹף.

zeh-rahm-TAHM sheh-NAH yeeh-YOO, bah-BOH-ker keh-khah-TZEER yah-khah-LOHF.

You sweep them away; they are like a sleep; in the morning they are like grass that passes.

בַּבֹּקֶר יָצִיץ וְחָלָף, לָעֶרֶב יְמוֹלֵל וְיָבֵשׁ.

bah-BOH-ker yah-TZEETZ veh-khah-LAHF, lah-EH-rev yeh-moh-LEL veh-yah-VESH.

In the morning it flourishes and sprouts; by evening it withers and dries.

כִּי כָלִינוּ בְאַפֶּךָ, וּבַחֲמָתְךָ נִבְהָלְנוּ.

kee khah-LEE-noo veh-ah-PEH-khah, oo-vah-khah-mah-teh-KHAH neev-HAHL-noo.

For we are consumed by Your anger, and terrified by Your wrath.

שַׁתָּה עֲוֹנֹתֵינוּ לְנֶגְדֶּךָ, עֲלֻמֵנוּ לִמְאוֹר פָּנֶיךָ.

shah-TAH ah-voh-noh-TAY-noo leh-neg-DEH-khah, ah-loo-MEH-noo leem-OHR pah-NEH-khah.

You have set our iniquities before You, our hidden sins in the light of Your face.

כִּי כָל יָמֵינוּ פָּנוּ בְעֶבְרָתֶךָ, כִּלִּינוּ שָׁנֵינוּ כְמוֹ הֶגֶה.

kee khohl yah-MAY-noo pah-NOO veh-ev-rah-TEH-khah, kee-LEE-noo shah-NAY-noo kheh-MOH HEH-geh.

For all our days pass away in Your fury; we finish our years like a sigh.

יְמֵי שְׁנוֹתֵינוּ בָהֶם שִׁבְעִים שָׁנָה, וְאִם בִּגְבוּרֹת שְׁמוֹנִים שָׁנָה, וְרָהְבָּם עָמָל וָאָוֶן, כִּי גָז חִישׁ וַנָּעֻפָה.

yeh-MAY sheh-noh-TAY-noo vah-HEM sheev-EEM shah-NAH, veh-EEM beeg-voo-ROHT sheh-moh-NEEM shah-NAH, veh-roh-BAHM ah-MAHL vah-AH-ven, kee GAHZ KHEESH vah-nah-OO-fah.

The days of our years are seventy, or with strength eighty; yet their pride is toil and sorrow, for it is soon cut off and we fly away.

מִי יוֹדֵעַ עֹז אַפֶּךָ, וּכְיִרְאָתְךָ עֶבְרָתֶךָ.

mee yoh-DEH-ah ohz ah-PEH-khah, ookh-yeer-ah-teh-KHAH ev-rah-TEH-khah.

Who knows the power of Your anger? Your fury matches the fear due You.

לִמְנוֹת יָמֵינוּ כֵּן הוֹדַע, וְנָבִא לְבַב חָכְמָה.

leem-NOHT yah-MAY-noo KEN hoh-DAH, veh-nah-VEE leh-VAHV khokh-MAH.

Teach us to number our days, that we may gain a heart of wisdom.

שׁוּבָה יְהֹוָה עַד מָתָי, וְהִנָּחֵם עַל עֲבָדֶיךָ.

SHOO-vah ah-doh-NIGH ahd mah-TIGH, veh-hee-nah-KHEM ahl ah-vah-DEH-khah.

Return, Lord; how long? Have compassion on Your servants.

שַׂבְּעֵנוּ בַבֹּקֶר חַסְדֶּךָ, וּנְרַנְּנָה וְנִשְׂמְחָה בְּכָל יָמֵינוּ.

sah-beh-EH-noo vah-BOH-ker khahs-DEH-khah, oo-neh-rah-neh-NAH veh-nees-meh-KHAH beh-KHOHL yah-MAY-noo.

Satisfy us in the morning with Your kindness, that we may sing and rejoice all our days.

שַׂמְּחֵנוּ כִּימוֹת עִנִּיתָנוּ, שְׁנוֹת רָאִינוּ רָעָה.

sah-meh-KHEH-noo kee-MOHT ee-nee-TAH-noo, sheh-NOHT rah-EE-noo rah-AH.

Gladden us for as many days as You afflicted us, for the years we saw trouble.

יֵרָאֶה אֶל עֲבָדֶיךָ פָעֳלֶךָ, וַהֲדָרְךָ עַל בְּנֵיהֶם.

yeh-rah-EH el ah-vah-DEH-khah fah-oh-LEH-khah, vah-hah-dah-reh-KHAH ahl beh-nay-HEM.

Let Your work be seen by Your servants, and Your splendor upon their children.

וִיהִי נֹעַם אֲדֹנָי אֱלֹהֵינוּ עָלֵינוּ, וּמַעֲשֵׂה יָדֵינוּ כּוֹנְנָה עָלֵינוּ, וּמַעֲשֵׂה יָדֵינוּ כּוֹנְנֵהוּ.

vee-HEE NOH-ahm ah-doh-NIGH eh-loh-HAY-noo ah-LAY-noo, oo-mah-ah-SEH yah-DAY-noo koh-neh-NAH ah-LAY-noo, oo-mah-ah-SEH yah-DAY-noo koh-neh-NEH-hoo.

May the pleasantness of the Lord our God be upon us; establish for us the work of our hands; establish the work of our hands.

הוֹדוּ לַיהֹוָה קִרְאוּ בִשְׁמוֹ, הוֹדִיעוּ בָעַמִּים עֲלִילוֹתָיו.

hoh-DOO lah-doh-NIGH keer-OO veesh-MOH, hoh-dee-OO vah-ah-MEEM ah-lee-loh-TAHV.

Give thanks to the Lord, call upon His name; make His deeds known among the peoples.

שִׁירוּ לוֹ זַמְּרוּ לוֹ, שִׂיחוּ בְּכָל נִפְלְאֹתָיו.

SHEE-roo LOH zah-meh-ROO LOH, SEE-khoo beh-KHOHL neef-leh-oh-TAHV.

Sing to Him, make music to Him; speak of all His wonders.

הִתְהַלְלוּ בְּשֵׁם קָדְשׁוֹ, יִשְׂמַח לֵב מְבַקְשֵׁי יְהֹוָה.

heet-hahl-LOO beh-SHEM kohd-SHOH, yees-MAHKH LEV meh-vahk-SHAY ah-doh-NIGH.

Glory in His holy name; let the heart of those who seek the Lord rejoice.

דִּרְשׁוּ יְהֹוָה וְעֻזּוֹ, בַּקְּשׁוּ פָנָיו תָּמִיד.

deer-SHOO ah-doh-NIGH veh-oo-ZOH, bah-keh-SHOO fah-NAHV tah-MEED.

Seek the Lord and His strength; seek His presence always.

זִכְרוּ נִפְלְאוֹתָיו אֲשֶׁר עָשָׂה, מֹפְתָיו וּמִשְׁפְּטֵי פִיו.

zeekh-ROO neef-leh-oh-TAHV ah-SHER ah-SAH, mohf-TAHV oo-meesh-peh-TAY FEEV.

Remember the wonders He has done, His signs and the judgments of His mouth.

זֶרַע אַבְרָהָם עַבְדּוֹ, בְּנֵי יַעֲקֹב בְּחִירָיו.

ZEH-rah ahv-rah-HAHM ahv-DOH, beh-NAY yah-ah-KOHV beh-khee-RAHV.

O seed of Abraham His servant, children of Jacob, His chosen ones.

הוּא יְהֹוָה אֱלֹהֵינוּ, בְּכָל הָאָרֶץ מִשְׁפָּטָיו.

HOO ah-doh-NIGH eh-loh-HAY-noo, beh-KHOHL hah-AH-retz meesh-pah-TAHV.

He is the Lord our God; His judgments are throughout the earth.

זָכַר לְעוֹלָם בְּרִיתוֹ, דָּבָר צִוָּה לְאֶלֶף דּוֹר.

zah-KHAHR leh-oh-LAHM beh-ree-TOH, dah-VAHR tzee-VAH leh-EH-lef DOHR.

He remembers His covenant forever, the word He commanded for a thousand generations.

אֲשֶׁר כָּרַת אֶת אַבְרָהָם, וּשְׁבוּעָתוֹ לְיִשְׂחָק.

ah-SHER kah-RAHT eht ahv-rah-HAHM, oosh-voo-ah-TOH leh-yeetz-KHAHK.

The covenant He made with Abraham, and His oath to Isaac.

וַיַּעֲמִידֶהָ לְיַעֲקֹב לְחֹק, לְיִשְׂרָאֵל בְּרִית עוֹלָם.

vah-yah-ah-mee-DEH-hah leh-yah-ah-KOHV leh-KHOHK, leh-yees-rah-EL beh-REET oh-LAHM.

And He established it for Jacob as a statute, for Israel as an everlasting covenant.

לֵאמֹר לְךָ אֶתֵּן אֶת אֶרֶץ כְּנָעַן, חֶבֶל נַחֲלַתְכֶם.

leh-MOHR leh-KHAH eh-TEN eht EH-retz keh-NAH-ahn, KHEH-vel nah-khah-laht-KHEM.

Saying, “To you I will give the land of Canaan, the portion of your inheritance.”

בִּהְיוֹתָם מְתֵי מִסְפָּר, כִּמְעַט וְגָרִים בָּהּ.

bee-yoh-TAHM meh-TAY mees-PAHR, kee-meh-AHT veh-gah-REEM BAH.

When they were few in number, very few, and sojourners in it.

וַיִּתְהַלְּכוּ מִגּוֹי אֶל גּוֹי, מִמַּמְלָכָה אֶל עַם אַחֵר.

vah-yeet-hahl-leh-KHOO mee-GOY el GOY, mee-mahm-lah-KHAH el AHM ah-KHER.

And they wandered from nation to nation, from one kingdom to another people.

לֹא הִנִּיחַ אָדָם לְעָשְׁקָם, וַיּוֹכַח עֲלֵיהֶם מְלָכִים.

loh hee-NEE-ahkh ah-DAHM leh-ohsh-KAHM, vah-YOH-khahkh ah-lay-HEM meh-lah-KHEEM.

He allowed no man to oppress them, and He rebuked kings on their account.

אַל תִּגְּעוּ בִמְשִׁיחָי, וְלִנְבִיאַי אַל תָּרֵעוּ.

ahl tee-geh-OO veem-shee-KHIGH, veh-leen-vee-IGH ahl tah-reh-OO.

“Do not touch My anointed ones, and do not harm My prophets.”

וַיִּקְרָא רָעָב עַל הָאָרֶץ, כָּל מַטֵּה לֶחֶם שָׁבָר.

vah-yeek-RAH rah-AHV ahl hah-AH-retz, kohl mah-TEH LEH-khem shah-VAHR.

He summoned a famine upon the land; He broke every staff of bread.

שָׁלַח לִפְנֵיהֶם אִישׁ, לְעֶבֶד נִמְכַּר יוֹסֵף.

shah-LAHKH leef-nay-HEM EESH, leh-EH-ved neem-KAHR yoh-SEF.

He sent a man before them; Joseph was sold as a slave.

עִנּוּ בַכֶּבֶל רַגְלוֹ, בַּרְזֶל בָּאָה נַפְשׁוֹ.

ee-NOO vah-KEH-vel rahg-LOH, bahr-ZEL bah-AH nahf-SHOH.

They afflicted his feet with shackles; his soul entered into iron.

עַד עֵת בֹּא דְבָרוֹ, אִמְרַת יְהֹוָה צְרָפָתְהוּ.

ahd ET boh deh-vah-ROH, eem-RAHT ah-doh-NIGH tzeh-rah-FAHT-hoo.

Until the time that his word came true, the word of the Lord refined him.

שָׁלַח מֶלֶךְ וַיַּתִּירֵהוּ, מֹשֵׁל עַמִּים וַיְפַתְּחֵהוּ.

shah-LAHKH MEH-lekh vah-yah-tee-REH-hoo, moh-SHEL ah-MEEM vah-yeh-fah-teh-KHEH-hoo.

The king sent and released him; the ruler of peoples set him free.

שָׂמוֹ אָדוֹן לְבֵיתוֹ, וּמֹשֵׁל בְּכָל קִנְיָנוֹ.

sah-MOH ah-DOHN leh-vay-TOH, oo-moh-SHEL beh-KHOHL keen-yah-NOH.

He made him lord of his house and ruler over all his possessions.

לֶאְסֹר שָׂרָיו בְּנַפְשׁוֹ, וּזְקֵנָיו יְחַכֵּם.

leh-SOHR sah-RAHV beh-nahf-SHOH, oo-zeh-keh-NAHV yeh-khah-KEM.

To bind his princes at his pleasure, and to teach his elders wisdom.

וַיָּבֹא יִשְׂרָאֵל מִצְרָיִם, וְיַעֲקֹב גָּר בְּאֶרֶץ חָם.

vah-yah-VOH yees-rah-EL meetz-RAH-yeem, veh-yah-ah-KOHV GAHR beh-EH-retz KHAHM.

Then Israel came to Egypt, and Jacob sojourned in the land of Ham.

וַיֶּפֶר אֶת עַמּוֹ מְאֹד, וַיַּעֲצִמֵהוּ מִצָּרָיו.

vah-YEH-fer eht ah-MOH meh-OHD, vah-yah-ah-tzee-MEH-hoo mee-tzah-RAHV.

And He made His people very fruitful and made them stronger than their adversaries.

הָפַךְ לִבָּם לִשְׂנֹא עַמּוֹ, לְהִתְנַכֵּל בַּעֲבָדָיו.

hah-FAHKH lee-BAHM lees-NOH ah-MOH, leh-heet-nah-KEL bah-ah-vah-DAHV.

He turned their hearts to hate His people, to deal craftily with His servants.

שָׁלַח מֹשֶׁה עַבְדּוֹ, אַהֲרֹן אֲשֶׁר בָּחַר בּוֹ.

shah-LAHKH moh-SHEH ahv-DOH, ah-hah-ROHN ah-SHER bah-KHAHR BOH.

He sent Moses His servant, and Aaron whom He had chosen.

שָׂמוּ בָם דִּבְרֵי אֹתוֹתָיו, וּמֹפְתִים בְּאֶרֶץ חָם.

sah-MOO VAHM deev-RAY oh-toh-TAHV, oo-moh-feh-TEEM beh-EH-retz KHAHM.

They performed among them His signs and wonders in the land of Ham.

שָׁלַח חֹשֶׁךְ וַיַּחֲשִׁךְ, וְלֹא מָרוּ אֶת דְּבָרוֹ.

shah-LAHKH KHOH-shekh vah-yah-khah-SHEEKH, veh-LOH mah-ROO eht deh-vah-ROH.

He sent darkness and made it dark; and they did not rebel against His word.

הָפַךְ אֶת מֵימֵיהֶם לְדָם, וַיָּמֶת אֶת דְּגָתָם.

hah-FAHKH eht may-may-HEM leh-DAHM, vah-yah-MET eht deh-gah-TAHM.

He turned their waters into blood and caused their fish to die.

שָׁרַץ אַרְצָם צְפַרְדְּעִים, בְּחַדְרֵי מַלְכֵיהֶם.

shah-RAHTZ ahr-TZAHM tzeh-fahr-deh-EEM, beh-khahd-RAY mahl-khay-HEM.

Their land swarmed with frogs, even in the chambers of their kings.

אָמַר וַיָּבֹא עָרֹב, כִּנִּים בְּכָל גְּבוּלָם.

ah-MAHR vah-yah-VOH ah-ROHV, kee-NEEM beh-KHOHL geh-voo-LAHM.

He spoke and swarms of insects came, lice throughout their borders.

נָתַן גִּשְׁמֵיהֶם בָּרָד, אֵשׁ לֶהָבוֹת בְּאַרְצָם.

nah-TAHN geesh-may-HEM bah-RAHD, ESH leh-hah-VOHT beh-ahr-TZAHM.

He gave them hail for rain, flaming fire in their land.

וַיַּךְ גַּפְנָם וּתְאֵנָתָם, וַיְשַׁבֵּר עֵץ גְּבוּלָם.

vah-YAHKH gahf-NAHM oo-teh-eh-nah-TAHM, vah-yeh-shah-BER ETZ geh-voo-LAHM.

He struck their vines and fig trees, and shattered the trees of their territory.

אָמַר וַיָּבֹא אַרְבֶּה, וְיֶלֶק וְאֵין מִסְפָּר.

ah-MAHR vah-yah-VOH ahr-BEH, veh-YEH-lek veh-AYN mees-PAHR.

He spoke and locusts came, and grasshoppers without number.

וַיֹּאכַל כָּל עֵשֶׂב בְּאַרְצָם, וַיֹּאכַל פְּרִי אַדְמָתָם.

vah-YOH-khahl kohl EH-sev beh-ahr-TZAHM, vah-YOH-khahl peh-REE ahd-mah-TAHM.

They consumed all the vegetation in their land and consumed the fruit of their ground.

וַיַּךְ כָּל בְּכוֹר בְּאַרְצָם, רֵאשִׁית לְכָל אוֹנָם.

vah-YAHKH kohl beh-KHOHR beh-ahr-TZAHM, reh-SHEET leh-KHOHL oh-NAHM.

He struck every firstborn in their land, the first of all their vigor.

וַיּוֹצִיאֵם בְּכֶסֶף וְזָהָב, וְאֵין בִּשְׁבָטָיו כּוֹשֵׁל.

vah-yoh-tzee-EM beh-KHEH-sef veh-zah-HAHV, veh-AYN beesh-vah-TAHV koh-SHEL.

He brought them out with silver and gold, and there was none who stumbled among His tribes.

שָׂמַח מִצְרַיִם בְּצֵאתָם, כִּי נָפַל פַּחְדָּם עֲלֵיהֶם.

sah-MAHKH meetz-RAH-yeem beh-tzeh-TAHM, kee nah-FAHL pahkh-DAHM ah-lay-HEM.

Egypt rejoiced when they departed, for the dread of them had fallen upon them.

פָּרַשׂ עָנָן לְמָסָךְ, וְאֵשׁ לְהָאִיר לָיְלָה.

pah-RAHS ah-NAHN leh-mah-SAHKH, veh-ESH leh-hah-EER LAH-yeh-lah.

He spread a cloud as a covering, and fire to light up the night.

שָׁאַל וַיָּבֵא שְׂלָו, וְלֶחֶם שָׁמַיִם יַשְׂבִּיעֵם.

shah-AHL vah-yah-VEH seh-LAHV, veh-LEH-khem shah-MAH-yeem yahs-bee-EM.

They asked and He brought quail, and satisfied them with bread of heaven.

פָּתַח צוּר וַיָּזוּבוּ מָיִם, הָלְכוּ בַּצִּיּוֹת נָהָר.

pah-TAHKH TZOOR vah-yah-ZOO-voo MAH-yeem, hahl-KHOO bah-tzee-YOHT nah-HAHR.

He opened the rock and waters flowed; they ran in dry places like a river.

כִּי זָכַר אֶת דְּבַר קָדְשׁוֹ, אֶת אַבְרָהָם עַבְדּוֹ.

kee zah-KHAHR eht deh-VAHR kohd-SHOH, eht ahv-rah-HAHM ahv-DOH.

For He remembered His holy word to Abraham His servant.

וַיּוֹצִא עַמּוֹ בְשָׂשׂוֹן, בְּרִנָּה אֶת בְּחִירָיו.

vah-yoh-TZEE ah-MOH veh-sah-SOHN, beh-ree-NAH eht beh-khee-RAHV.

He brought out His people with joy, His chosen ones with singing.

וַיִּתֵּן לָהֶם אַרְצוֹת גּוֹיִם, וַעֲמַל לְאֻמִּים יִירָשׁוּ.

vah-yee-TEN lah-HEM ahr-TZOHT goh-YEEM, vah-ah-MAL leh-oom-MEEM yee-rah-SHOO.

He gave them the lands of nations, and they inherited the toil of peoples.

בַּעֲבוּר יִשְׁמְרוּ חֻקָּיו, וְתוֹרֹתָיו יִנְצֹרוּ, הַלְלוּיָהּ.

bah-ah-VOOR yeesh-meh-ROO khook-KAHV, veh-toh-roh-TAHV yeen-TZOH-roo, hahl-loo-YAH.

So that they might keep His statutes and observe His teachings. Halleluyah.

עַל נַהֲרוֹת בָּבֶל שָׁם יָשַׁבְנוּ גַּם בָּכִינוּ, בְּזָכְרֵנוּ אֶת צִיּוֹן.

ahl nah-hah-ROHT bah-VEL, shahm yah-SHAHV-noo gahm bah-KHEE-noo, beh-zohkh-REH-noo eht tzee-YOHN.

By the rivers of Babylon, there we sat and wept as we remembered Zion.

עַל עֲרָבִים בְּתוֹכָהּ, תָּלִינוּ כִּנֹּרוֹתֵינוּ.

ahl ah-rah-VEEM beh-toh-KHAH, tah-LEE-noo kee-noh-roh-TAY-noo.

On the willows in its midst we hung our harps.

כִּי שָׁם שְׁאֵלוּנוּ שׁוֹבֵינוּ דִּבְרֵי שִׁיר, וְתוֹלָלֵינוּ שִׂמְחָה, שִׁירוּ לָנוּ מִשִּׁיר צִיּוֹן.

kee SHAHM sheh-eh-LOO-noo shoh-VAY-noo deev-RAY sheer, veh-toh-lah-LAY-noo seem-KHAH, SHEE-roo LAH-noo mee-SHEER tzee-YOHN.

For there our captors asked us for songs, our tormentors for joy: Sing us one of the songs of Zion.

אֵיךְ נָשִׁיר אֶת שִׁיר יְהֹוָה, עַל אַדְמַת נֵכָר.

AYKH nah-SHEER eht sheer ah-doh-NIGH, ahl ahd-MAHT neh-KHAHR.

How can we sing the Lord's song on foreign soil?

אִם אֶשְׁכָּחֵךְ יְרוּשָׁלָיִם, תִּשְׁכַּח יְמִינִי.

eem esh-kah-KHEKH yeh-roo-shah-LAH-yeem, teesh-KAHKH yeh-mee-NEE.

If I forget you, Jerusalem, let my right hand forget its skill.

תִּדְבַּק לְשׁוֹנִי לְחִכִּי אִם לֹא אֶזְכְּרֵכִי, אִם לֹא אַעֲלֶה אֶת יְרוּשָׁלַיִם עַל רֹאשׁ שִׂמְחָתִי.

teed-BAHK leh-shoh-NEE leh-khee-KEE, eem loh ez-keh-REH-khee, eem loh ah-ah-LEH eht yeh-roo-shah-LAH-yeem ahl rohsh seem-khah-TEE.

Let my tongue cling to my palate if I do not remember you, if I do not set Jerusalem above my highest joy.

זְכֹר יְהֹוָה לִבְנֵי אֱדוֹם אֵת יוֹם יְרוּשָׁלָיִם, הָאֹמְרִים עָרוּ עָרוּ עַד הַיְסוֹד בָּהּ.

zeh-KHOHR ah-doh-NIGH leev-NAY eh-DOHM eht yohm yeh-roo-shah-LAH-yeem, hah-oh-meh-REEM AH-roo AH-roo, ahd hah-yeh-SOHD BAH.

Remember, Lord, against the Edomites the day of Jerusalem, when they said: Tear it down, tear it down to its foundation.

בַּת בָּבֶל הַשְּׁדוּדָה, אַשְׁרֵי שֶׁיְשַׁלֶּם לָךְ, אֶת גְּמוּלֵךְ שֶׁגָּמַלְתְּ לָנוּ.

baht bah-VEL hah-sheh-doo-DAH, ahsh-RAY sheh-yeh-shah-LEM LAHKH, eht geh-moo-LEKH sheh-gah-MAHLT LAH-noo.

Daughter of Babylon, destined for ruin, happy is the one who repays you for what you did to us.

אַשְׁרֵי שֶׁיֹּאחֵז וְנִפֵּץ, אֶת עֹלָלַיִךְ אֶל הַסָּלַע.

ahsh-RAY sheh-yoh-KHEZ veh-nee-PETZ, eht oh-lah-LAH-yeekh el hah-SAH-lah.

Happy is the one who seizes and dashes your infants against the rock.

Hebrew audio

הַלְלוּיָהּ, הַלְלוּ אֵל בְּקָדְשׁוֹ, הַלְלוּהוּ בִּרְקִיעַ עֻזּוֹ.

hahl-loo-YAH, hahl-LOO EL beh-kohd-SHOH, hahl-LOO-hoo beer-KEE-ah oo-ZOH.

Halleluyah. Praise God in His sanctuary; praise Him in the firmament of His strength.

הַלְלוּהוּ בִגְבוּרֹתָיו, הַלְלוּהוּ כְּרֹב גֻּדְלוֹ.

hahl-LOO-hoo veeg-voo-roh-TAHV, hahl-LOO-hoo keh-ROHV good-LOH.

Praise Him for His mighty deeds; praise Him according to His abundant greatness.

הַלְלוּהוּ בְּתֵקַע שׁוֹפָר, הַלְלוּהוּ בְּנֵבֶל וְכִנּוֹר.

hahl-LOO-hoo beh-TEH-kah shoh-FAHR, hahl-LOO-hoo beh-NEH-vel veh-khee-NOHR.

Praise Him with the blast of the shofar; praise Him with lyre and harp.

הַלְלוּהוּ בְּתֹף וּמָחוֹל, הַלְלוּהוּ בְּמִנִּים וְעֻגָב.

hahl-LOO-hoo beh-TOHF oo-mah-KHOHL, hahl-LOO-hoo beh-mee-NEEM veh-oo-GAHV.

Praise Him with drum and dance; praise Him with strings and flute.

הַלְלוּהוּ בְצִלְצְלֵי שָׁמַע, הַלְלוּהוּ בְּצִלְצְלֵי תְרוּעָה.

hahl-LOO-hoo veh-tzeel-tzeh-LAY shah-MAH, hahl-LOO-hoo beh-tzeel-tzeh-LAY teh-roo-AH.

Praise Him with resounding cymbals; praise Him with clanging cymbals.

כֹּל הַנְּשָׁמָה תְּהַלֵּל יָהּ, הַלְלוּיָהּ.

kohl hah-neh-shah-MAH teh-hah-LEL YAH, hahl-loo-YAH.

Let every soul praise the Lord. Halleluyah.

Psalms for Specific Needs

Psalms customarily said for a particular need. Tap a psalm to open it, or print the whole set.

These psalms are said as prayer. The tradition is that the words themselves do not heal or protect; one asks that God answer in the merit of reciting them.

The lists come from Kipa and Hidabroot, and from Shimush Tehillim, a treatise attributed to Rav Hai Gaon of Baghdad and cited by the Rashba and the Chida. Only Shimush Tehillim’s choice of psalm is given here, not the repetitions, amulets or other practices it prescribes.

The Rambam (Laws of Idolatry 11:12) forbids reciting verses to heal the body, but permits reciting psalms so that the merit of reciting them protects a person from trouble and harm.

When praying for someone else, mention the person’s Hebrew name and the mother’s name, for example “Avraham ben Sarah” or “Rivkah bat Leah.”

From Kipa’s list of psalms for finding one’s match. Other published lists add Psalms 121 and 130, and a widespread custom is to recite Shir HaShirim for forty consecutive days.

Hidabroot’s set for peace in the home: Psalm 139, Psalm 128, and the sections of Psalm 119 whose letters spell “Shalom Bayit,” which husband and wife are encouraged to say every day. A rabbi answering on Yeshiva.org.il also recommends all the Songs of Ascent (Psalms 120–134).

Psalm 139Lord, You have searched me and You know me.Psalm 128Happy is everyone who fears the Lord, who walks in His ways.Psalm 119The seven sections whose letters spell “Shalom Bayit”

From Kipa’s list of psalms for recovery, which also includes Psalm 1. When praying for someone who is ill, it is customary to mention the person’s Hebrew name and the mother’s name.

Psalms 3 and 91 are the “Shir shel Pega’im,” the protective psalms named in the Jerusalem Talmud (Shabbat 6:2); Psalm 91 is also said after the bedtime Shema. Psalms 20 and 121 appear in Hidabroot’s lists for protection.

Psalms 121, 130 and 142 are Hidabroot’s set for times of distress. Psalm 20 opens “May the Lord answer you on the day of trouble.”

Psalm 25 is the psalm used in published prayer collections for a child’s return, followed by a personal prayer for the child by name. I found no fuller sourced list for this need.

Psalm 25To You, Lord, I lift up my soul.

Psalms 19, 24, 90 and 101 are Kipa’s list for success; Hidabroot adds Psalm 57.

Lord, You have been…Psalm 101I will sing of kindness and justice; to You, Lord, I will…

Shimush Tehillim has no entry for decrees by name. These are its psalms against calamity (13), every kind of evil (30 and 91), evil tidings (36) and any danger (77).

Shimush Tehillim names Psalm 31 for protection against the evil eye. Psalm 91 is its psalm against every form of evil.

From Shimush Tehillim: Psalm 7 for one pursued by enemies, 9 for deliverance from enemies, 35 against belligerent people, and 70 for victory over enemies. Psalm 83 is in Hidabroot’s set for deliverance from enemies.

Shimush Tehillim’s psalms for a court case: 7 and 20 for success in a court case, 93 for victory over an adversary in court, and 118 for when appearing before a judge.

From Shimush Tehillim: Psalm 100 to gain victory, 70 for victory over enemies, and 60 and 83 for success in battle.

Mizmor LeToda, the psalm of thanksgiving. Mizrahi communities say it every weekday morning in Pesukei DeZimra, though not on Shabbat and festivals.

Psalm 100A psalm of thanksgiving. Shout for joy to the Lord, all…

Tehillim

תְּהִלִּים

Every psalm on this page, in order. Psalms said within another prayer link to their place.

Said for: Safety and Protection.

מִזְמוֹר לְדָוִד, בְּבָרְחוֹ מִפְּנֵי אַבְשָׁלוֹם בְּנוֹ.

meez-MOHR leh-dah-VEED, beh-vohr-KHOH mee-peh-NAY ahv-shah-LOHM beh-NOH.

A psalm of David, when he fled from Absalom his son.

יְהֹוָה מָה רַבּוּ צָרָי, רַבִּים קָמִים עָלָי.

ah-doh-NIGH mah RAH-boo tzah-RIGH, rah-BEEM kah-MEEM ah-LIGH.

Lord, how many are my foes! Many rise up against me.

רַבִּים אֹמְרִים לְנַפְשִׁי, אֵין יְשׁוּעָתָה לּוֹ בֵאלֹהִים סֶלָה.

rah-BEEM oh-meh-REEM leh-nahf-SHEE, AYN yeh-shoo-AH-tah LOH veh-loh-HEEM SEH-lah.

Many say of my soul, “There is no salvation for him in God,” Selah.

וְאַתָּה יְהֹוָה מָגֵן בַּעֲדִי, כְּבוֹדִי וּמֵרִים רֹאשִׁי.

veh-ah-TAH ah-doh-NIGH mah-GEN bah-ah-DEE, keh-voh-DEE oo-meh-REEM roh-SHEE.

But You, Lord, are a shield around me, my glory, and the One who lifts my head.

קוֹלִי אֶל יְהֹוָה אֶקְרָא, וַיַּעֲנֵנִי מֵהַר קָדְשׁוֹ סֶלָה.

koh-LEE el ah-doh-NIGH ek-RAH, vah-yah-ah-NEH-nee meh-HAHR kohd-SHOH SEH-lah.

With my voice I call to the Lord, and He answers me from His holy mountain, Selah.

אֲנִי שָׁכַבְתִּי וָאִישָׁנָה, הֱקִיצוֹתִי כִּי יְהֹוָה יִסְמְכֵנִי.

ah-NEE shah-KHAHV-tee vah-ee-SHAH-nah, heh-kee-TZOH-tee kee ah-doh-NIGH yees-meh-KHEH-nee.

I lay down and slept; I awoke, for the Lord sustains me.

לֹא אִירָא מֵרִבְבוֹת עָם, אֲשֶׁר סָבִיב שָׁתוּ עָלָי.

loh ee-RAH meh-reev-VOHT AHM, ah-SHER sah-VEEV SHAH-too ah-LIGH.

I will not fear the tens of thousands of people who set themselves against me all around.

קוּמָה יְהֹוָה, הוֹשִׁיעֵנִי אֱלֹהַי, כִּי הִכִּיתָ אֶת כָּל אֹיְבַי לֶחִי, שִׁנֵּי רְשָׁעִים שִׁבַּרְתָּ.

KOO-mah ah-doh-NIGH, hoh-shee-EH-nee eh-loh-HIGH, kee hee-KEE-tah eht kohl oy-VIGH LEH-khee, shee-NAY reh-shah-EEM shee-BAHR-tah.

Arise, Lord; save me, my God! For You have struck all my enemies on the cheek; You have broken the teeth of the wicked.

לַיהֹוָה הַיְשׁוּעָה, עַל עַמְּךָ בִרְכָתֶךָ סֶּלָה.

lah-doh-NIGH hah-yeh-shoo-AH, ahl ah-meh-KHAH veer-khah-TEH-khah SEH-lah.

Salvation belongs to the Lord; Your blessing is upon Your people, Selah.

Said for: Health and Healing.

לַמְנַצֵּחַ בִּנְגִינוֹת עַל הַשְּׁמִינִית, מִזְמוֹר לְדָוִד.

lahm-nah-TZEH-ahkh been-gee-NOHT ahl hah-sheh-mee-NEET, meez-MOHR leh-dah-VEED.

For the conductor, with instrumental music, on the eight-stringed lyre; a psalm of David.

יְהֹוָה אַל בְּאַפְּךָ תוֹכִיחֵנִי, וְאַל בַּחֲמָתְךָ תְיַסְּרֵנִי.

ah-doh-NIGH ahl beh-ahp-KHAH toh-khee-KHEH-nee, veh-AHL bah-khah-maht-KHAH teh-yahs-REH-nee.

Lord, do not rebuke me in Your anger, nor chastise me in Your wrath.

חָנֵּנִי יְהֹוָה כִּי אֻמְלַל אָנִי, רְפָאֵנִי יְהֹוָה כִּי נִבְהֲלוּ עֲצָמָי.

khah-NEH-nee ah-doh-NIGH kee oom-LAHL AH-nee, reh-fah-EH-nee ah-doh-NIGH kee neev-hah-LOO ah-tzah-MIGH.

Be gracious to me, Lord, for I am weak; heal me, Lord, for my bones are shaken.

וְנַפְשִׁי נִבְהֲלָה מְאֹד, וְאַתָּה יְהֹוָה עַד מָתָי.

veh-nahf-SHEE neev-hah-LAH meh-OHD, veh-ah-TAH ah-doh-NIGH ahd mah-TIGH.

My soul is deeply shaken; and You, Lord, how long?

שׁוּבָה יְהֹוָה חַלְּצָה נַפְשִׁי, הוֹשִׁיעֵנִי לְמַעַן חַסְדֶּךָ.

SHOO-vah ah-doh-NIGH khahl-TZAH nahf-SHEE, hoh-shee-EH-nee leh-MAH-ahn khahs-DEH-khah.

Return, Lord, rescue my soul; save me for the sake of Your kindness.

כִּי אֵין בַּמָּוֶת זִכְרֶךָ, בִּשְׁאוֹל מִי יוֹדֶה לָּךְ.

kee AYN bah-MAH-vet zeekh-REH-khah, beesh-OHL mee yoh-DEH LAHKH.

For in death there is no remembrance of You; in the grave, who will praise You?

יָגַעְתִּי בְּאַנְחָתִי, אַשְׂחֶה בְכָל לַיְלָה מִטָּתִי, בְּדִמְעָתִי עַרְשִׂי אַמְסֶה.

yah-GAH-tee beh-ahn-khah-TEE, ahs-KHEH veh-KHOHL LIGH-lah mee-tah-TEE, beh-deem-ah-TEE ahr-SEE ahm-SEH.

I am weary with my sighing; every night I flood my bed; I drench my couch with my tears.

עָשְׁשָׁה מִכַּעַס עֵינִי, עָתְקָה בְּכָל צוֹרְרָי.

ah-sheh-SHAH mee-KAH-ahs ay-NEE, ah-teh-KAH beh-KHOHL tzoh-reh-RIGH.

My eye is dimmed by grief; it has grown old because of all my foes.

סוּרוּ מִמֶּנִּי כָּל פֹּעֲלֵי אָוֶן, כִּי שָׁמַע יְהֹוָה קוֹל בִּכְיִי.

SOO-roo mee-MEH-nee kohl poh-ah-LAY AH-ven, kee shah-MAH ah-doh-NIGH kohl beekh-YEE.

Depart from me, all evildoers, for the Lord has heard the sound of my weeping.

שָׁמַע יְהֹוָה תְּחִנָּתִי, יְהֹוָה תְּפִלָּתִי יִקָּח.

shah-MAH ah-doh-NIGH teh-khee-nah-TEE, ah-doh-NIGH teh-fee-lah-TEE yee-KAHKH.

The Lord has heard my plea; the Lord accepts my prayer.

יֵבֹשׁוּ וְיִבָּהֲלוּ מְאֹד כָּל אֹיְבָי, יָשֻׁבוּ יֵבֹשׁוּ רָגַע.

yeh-VOH-shoo veh-yee-bah-hah-LOO meh-OHD kohl oy-VIGH, yah-SHOO-voo yeh-VOH-shoo RAH-gah.

All my enemies will be ashamed and greatly shaken; they will turn back, ashamed in an instant.

Said for: Defeat of Enemies and Adversaries; Success in a Lawsuit.

שִׁגָּיוֹן לְדָוִד אֲשֶׁר שָׁר לַיהֹוָה, עַל דִּבְרֵי כוּשׁ בֶּן יְמִינִי.

shee-gah-YOHN leh-dah-VEED ah-SHER SHAHR lah-doh-NIGH, ahl deev-RAY KOOSH ben yeh-mee-NEE.

A shiggayon of David, which he sang to the Lord concerning Cush the Benjamite.

יְהֹוָה אֱלֹהַי בְּךָ חָסִיתִי, הוֹשִׁיעֵנִי מִכָּל רֹדְפַי וְהַצִּילֵנִי.

ah-doh-NIGH eh-loh-HIGH beh-KHAH khah-SEE-tee, hoh-shee-EH-nee mee-KOHL rohd-FIGH veh-hah-tzee-LEH-nee.

Lord my God, in You I take refuge; save me from all my pursuers and rescue me,

פֶּן יִטְרֹף כְּאַרְיֵה נַפְשִׁי, פֹּרֵק וְאֵין מַצִּיל.

pen yeet-ROHF keh-ahr-YEH nahf-SHEE, poh-REK veh-AYN mah-TZEEL.

lest they tear my soul like a lion, rending it with no one to rescue.

יְהֹוָה אֱלֹהַי אִם עָשִׂיתִי זֹאת, אִם יֶשׁ עָוֶל בְּכַפָּי.

ah-doh-NIGH eh-loh-HIGH eem ah-SEE-tee ZOHT, eem YESH AH-vel beh-khah-PIGH.

Lord my God, if I have done this, if there is wrong in my hands,

אִם גָּמַלְתִּי שׁוֹלְמִי רָע, וָאֲחַלְּצָה צוֹרְרִי רֵיקָם.

eem gah-MAHL-tee shohl-MEE RAH, vah-ah-khahl-TZAH tzoh-reh-REE ray-KAHM.

if I have repaid with evil one who was at peace with me, or plundered my foe without cause,

יִרַדֹּף אוֹיֵב נַפְשִׁי וְיַשֵּׂג וְיִרְמֹס לָאָרֶץ חַיָּי, וּכְבוֹדִי לֶעָפָר יַשְׁכֵּן סֶלָה.

yee-rah-DOHF oh-YEV nahf-SHEE veh-yah-SEG veh-yeer-MOHS lah-AH-retz khah-YIGH, ookh-voh-DEE leh-ah-FAHR yahsh-KEN SEH-lah.

let the enemy pursue my soul and overtake it, trample my life to the ground, and lay my honor in the dust, Selah.

קוּמָה יְהֹוָה בְּאַפֶּךָ הִנָּשֵׂא בְּעַבְרוֹת צוֹרְרָי, וְעוּרָה אֵלַי מִשְׁפָּט צִוִּיתָ.

KOO-mah ah-doh-NIGH beh-ah-PEH-khah hee-nah-SEH beh-ahv-ROHT tzoh-reh-RIGH, veh-OO-rah eh-LIGH meesh-PAHT tzee-VEE-tah.

Arise, Lord, in Your anger; rise up against the fury of my foes; awaken for me the judgment You have commanded.

וַעֲדַת לְאֻמִּים תְּסוֹבְבֶךָּ, וְעָלֶיהָ לַמָּרוֹם שׁוּבָה.

vah-ah-DAHT leh-oo-MEEM teh-soh-veh-VEK-kah, veh-ah-LEH-hah lah-mah-ROHM SHOO-vah.

Let the assembly of the nations surround You, and return on high above it.

יְהֹוָה יָדִין עַמִּים שָׁפְטֵנִי יְהֹוָה, כְּצִדְקִי וּכְתֻמִּי עָלָי.

ah-doh-NIGH yah-DEEN ah-MEEM shohf-TEH-nee ah-doh-NIGH, keh-tzeed-KEE ookh-too-MEE ah-LIGH.

The Lord judges the peoples; judge me, Lord, according to my righteousness and my integrity.

יִגְמָר נָא רַע רְשָׁעִים וּתְכוֹנֵן צַדִּיק וּבֹחֵן לִבּוֹת וּכְלָיוֹת אֱלֹהִים צַדִּיק.

yeeg-MAHR NAH RAH reh-shah-EEM oot-khoh-NEN tzah-DEEK, oo-voh-KHEN lee-BOHT ookh-lah-YOHT eh-loh-HEEM tzah-DEEK.

Let the evil of the wicked come to an end, and establish the righteous; the righteous God tests hearts and minds.

מָגִנִּי עַל אֱלֹהִים, מוֹשִׁיעַ יִשְׁרֵי לֵב.

mah-gee-NEE ahl eh-loh-HEEM, moh-SHEE-ah yeesh-RAY LEV.

My shield is with God, who saves the upright of heart.

אֱלֹהִים שׁוֹפֵט צַדִּיק, וְאֵל זֹעֵם בְּכָל יוֹם.

eh-loh-HEEM shoh-FET tzah-DEEK, veh-EL zoh-EM beh-KHOHL YOHM.

God is a righteous judge, a God who is indignant every day.

אִם לֹא יָשׁוּב חַרְבּוֹ יִלְטוֹשׁ, קַשְׁתּוֹ דָרַךְ וַיְכוֹנְנֶהָ.

eem loh yah-SHOOV khar-BOH yeel-TOHSH, kahsh-TOH dah-RAKH vah-yeh-khoh-neh-NEH-hah.

If one does not repent, He sharpens His sword; He has bent His bow and made it ready.

וְלוֹ הֵכִין כְּלֵי מָוֶת, חִצָּיו לְדֹלְקִים יִפְעָל.

veh-LOH heh-KHEEN keh-LAY MAH-vet, khee-TZAHV leh-dohl-KEEM yeef-AHL.

He has prepared deadly weapons for him; He makes His arrows burning shafts.

הִנֵּה יְחַבֶּל אָוֶן, וְהָרָה עָמָל וְיָלַד שָׁקֶר.

hee-NEH yeh-khah-BEL AH-ven, veh-hah-RAH ah-MAHL veh-yah-LAHD SHAH-ker.

Behold, the wicked one conceives iniquity; he is pregnant with mischief and gives birth to falsehood.

בּוֹר כָּרָה וַיַּחְפְּרֵהוּ, וַיִּפֹּל בְּשַׁחַת יִפְעָל.

bohr kah-RAH vah-yahkh-peh-REH-hoo, vah-yee-POHL beh-SHAH-khaht yeef-AHL.

He dug a pit and hollowed it out, and fell into the trap he made.

יָשׁוּב עֲמָלוֹ בְרֹאשׁוֹ, וְעַל קָדְקֳדוֹ חֲמָסוֹ יֵרֵד.

yah-SHOOV ah-mah-LOH veh-roh-SHOH, veh-AHL kohd-koh-DOH khah-mah-SOH yeh-RED.

His mischief will return upon his own head, and his violence will come down on his own skull.

אוֹדֶה יְהֹוָה כְּצִדְקוֹ, וַאֲזַמְּרָה שֵׁם יְהֹוָה עֶלְיוֹן.

oh-DEH ah-doh-NIGH keh-tzeed-KOH, vah-ah-zahm-RAH SHEM ah-doh-NIGH el-YOHN.

I will thank the Lord according to His righteousness and sing praise to the name of the Lord Most High.

Said for: Defeat of Enemies and Adversaries.

לַמְנַצֵּחַ עַלְמוּת לַבֵּן מִזְמוֹר לְדָוִד.

lahm-nah-TZEH-ahkh ahl-MOOT lah-BEN meez-MOHR leh-dah-VEED.

For the conductor, on the death of Labben; a psalm of David.

אוֹדֶה יְהֹוָה בְּכָל לִבִּי, אֲסַפְּרָה כָּל נִפְלְאוֹתֶיךָ.

oh-DEH ah-doh-NIGH beh-KHOHL lee-BEE, ah-sahp-RAH kohl neef-leh-oh-TEH-khah.

I will thank the Lord with all my heart; I will tell of all Your wonders.

אֶשְׂמְחָה וְאֶעֶלְצָה בָךְ, אֲזַמְּרָה שִׁמְךָ עֶלְיוֹן.

es-meh-KHAH veh-eh-el-TZAH VAHKH, ah-zahm-RAH sheem-KHAH el-YOHN.

I will be glad and rejoice in You; I will sing praise to Your name, Most High.

בְּשׁוּב אוֹיְבַי אָחוֹר, יִכָּשְׁלוּ וְיֹאבְדוּ מִפָּנֶיךָ.

beh-SHOOV oy-VIGH ah-KHOHR, yee-kahsh-LOO veh-yoh-veh-DOO mee-pah-NEH-khah.

When my enemies turn back, they stumble and perish before You.

כִּי עָשִׂיתָ מִשְׁפָּטִי וְדִינִי, יָשַׁבְתָּ לְכִסֵּא שׁוֹפֵט צֶדֶק.

kee ah-SEE-tah meesh-pah-TEE veh-dee-NEE, yah-SHAHV-tah leh-khee-SEH shoh-FET TZEH-dek.

For You have upheld my right and my cause; You sat on the throne as a righteous judge.

גָּעַרְתָּ גוֹיִם אִבַּדְתָּ רָשָׁע, שְׁמָם מָחִיתָ לְעוֹלָם וָעֶד.

gah-AHR-tah goh-YEEM ee-BAHD-tah rah-SHAH, sheh-MAHM mah-KHEE-tah leh-oh-LAHM vah-ED.

You rebuked nations and destroyed the wicked; You blotted out their name forever and ever.

הָאוֹיֵב תַּמּוּ חֳרָבוֹת לָנֶצַח וְעָרִים נָתַשְׁתָּ, אָבַד זִכְרָם הֵמָּה.

hah-oh-YEV TAH-moo khoh-rah-VOHT lah-NEH-tzahkh veh-ah-REEM nah-TAHSH-tah, ah-VAHD zeekh-RAHM HEH-mah.

The enemy is finished, ruined forever; You uprooted their cities, and their very memory has perished.

וַיהֹוָה לְעוֹלָם יֵשֵׁב, כּוֹנֵן לַמִּשְׁפָּט כִּסְאוֹ.

vah-doh-NIGH leh-oh-LAHM yeh-SHEV, koh-NEN lah-meesh-PAHT kees-OH.

But the Lord sits enthroned forever; He has established His throne for judgment.

וְהוּא יִשְׁפֹּט תֵּבֵל בְּצֶדֶק, יָדִין לְאֻמִּים בְּמֵישָׁרִים.

veh-HOO yeesh-POHT teh-VEL beh-TZEH-dek, yah-DEEN leh-oo-MEEM beh-may-shah-REEM.

He will judge the world with righteousness and the peoples with fairness.

וִיהִי יְהֹוָה מִשְׂגָּב לַדָּךְ, מִשְׂגָּב לְעִתּוֹת בַּצָּרָה.

vee-HEE ah-doh-NIGH meez-GAHV lah-DAHKH, meez-GAHV leh-ee-TOHT bah-tzah-RAH.

The Lord is a stronghold for the oppressed, a stronghold in times of trouble.

וְיִבְטְחוּ בְךָ יוֹדְעֵי שְׁמֶךָ, כִּי לֹא עָזַבְתָּ דֹרְשֶׁיךָ יְהֹוָה.

veh-yeev-teh-KHOO veh-KHAH yohd-AY sheh-MEH-khah, kee loh ah-ZAHV-tah dohr-SHEH-khah ah-doh-NIGH.

Those who know Your name trust in You, for You have not forsaken those who seek You, Lord.

זַמְּרוּ לַיהֹוָה יֹשֵׁב צִיּוֹן, הַגִּידוּ בָעַמִּים עֲלִילוֹתָיו.

zahm-ROO lah-doh-NIGH yoh-SHEV tzee-YOHN, hah-GEE-doo vah-ah-MEEM ah-lee-loh-TAHV.

Sing praise to the Lord who dwells in Zion; declare His deeds among the peoples.

כִּי דֹרֵשׁ דָּמִים אוֹתָם זָכָר, לֹא שָׁכַח צַעֲקַת עֲנָוִים.

kee doh-RESH dah-MEEM oh-TAHM zah-KHAHR, loh shah-KHAKH tzah-ah-KAHT ah-nah-VEEM.

For He who avenges blood remembers them; He does not forget the cry of the humble.

חָנְנֵנִי יְהֹוָה רְאֵה עָנְיִי מִשֹּׂנְאָי, מְרוֹמְמִי מִשַּׁעֲרֵי מָוֶת.

khohn-NEH-nee ah-doh-NIGH reh-EH ohn-YEE mee-sohn-IGH, meh-roh-meh-MEE mee-shah-ah-RAY MAH-vet.

Be gracious to me, Lord; see my affliction from those who hate me, You who lift me up from the gates of death,

לְמַעַן אֲסַפְּרָה כָּל תְּהִלָּתֶיךָ בְּשַׁעֲרֵי בַת צִיּוֹן, אָגִילָה בִּישׁוּעָתֶךָ.

leh-MAH-ahn ah-sahp-RAH kohl teh-hee-lah-TEH-khah beh-shah-ah-RAY baht tzee-YOHN, ah-GEE-lah bee-shoo-ah-TEH-khah.

so that I may tell all Your praises in the gates of the daughter of Zion and rejoice in Your salvation.

טָבְעוּ גוֹיִם בְּשַׁחַת עָשׂוּ, בְּרֶשֶׁת זוּ טָמָנוּ נִלְכְּדָה רַגְלָם.

tahv-OO goh-YEEM beh-SHAH-khaht ah-SOO, beh-REH-shet zoo tah-MAH-noo neel-keh-DAH rahg-LAHM.

The nations have sunk into the pit they made; their own foot is caught in the net they hid.

נוֹדַע יְהֹוָה מִשְׁפָּט עָשָׂה בְּפֹעַל כַּפָּיו נוֹקֵשׁ רָשָׁע, הִגָּיוֹן סֶלָה.

noh-DAH ah-doh-NIGH meesh-PAHT ah-SAH, beh-FOH-ahl kah-PAHV noh-KESH rah-SHAH, hee-gah-YOHN SEH-lah.

The Lord has made Himself known; He has executed judgment; the wicked is snared by the work of his own hands. Higgayon, Selah.

יָשׁוּבוּ רְשָׁעִים לִשְׁאוֹלָה, כָּל גּוֹיִם שְׁכֵחֵי אֱלֹהִים.

yah-SHOO-voo reh-shah-EEM leesh-OH-lah, kohl goh-YEEM sheh-kheh-KHAY eh-loh-HEEM.

The wicked will return to the grave, all the nations that forget God.

כִּי לֹא לָנֶצַח יִשָּׁכַח אֶבְיוֹן, תִּקְוַת עֲנִיִּים תֹּאבַד לָעַד.

kee loh lah-NEH-tzahkh yee-shah-KHAKH ev-YOHN, teek-VAHT ah-nee-YEEM toh-VAHD lah-AHD.

For the needy will not always be forgotten, nor the hope of the poor perish forever.

קוּמָה יְהֹוָה אַל יָעֹז אֱנוֹשׁ, יִשָּׁפְטוּ גוֹיִם עַל פָּנֶיךָ.

KOO-mah ah-doh-NIGH ahl yah-OHZ eh-NOHSH, yee-shah-feh-TOO goh-YEEM ahl pah-NEH-khah.

Arise, Lord; let not man prevail; let the nations be judged before You.

שִׁיתָה יְהֹוָה מוֹרָה לָהֶם יֵדְעוּ גוֹיִם, אֱנוֹשׁ הֵמָּה סֶּלָה.

SHEE-tah ah-doh-NIGH moh-RAH lah-HEM, yed-OO goh-YEEM, eh-NOHSH HEH-mah SEH-lah.

Put fear in them, Lord; let the nations know that they are only men, Selah.

Said for: Health and Healing; Annulling Harsh Decrees.

לַמְנַצֵּחַ מִזְמוֹר לְדָוִד.

lahm-nah-TZEH-ahkh meez-MOHR leh-dah-VEED.

For the conductor, a psalm of David.

עַד אָנָה יְהֹוָה תִּשְׁכָּחֵנִי נֶצַח, עַד אָנָה תַּסְתִּיר אֶת פָּנֶיךָ מִמֶּנִּי.

ahd AH-nah ah-doh-NIGH teesh-kah-KHEH-nee NEH-tzahkh, ahd AH-nah tahs-TEER eht pah-NEH-khah mee-MEH-nee.

How long, Lord, will You forget me forever? How long will You hide Your face from me?

עַד אָנָה אָשִׁית עֵצוֹת בְּנַפְשִׁי, יָגוֹן בִּלְבָבִי יוֹמָם, עַד אָנָה יָרוּם אֹיְבִי עָלָי.

ahd AH-nah ah-SHEET eh-TZOHT beh-nahf-SHEE, yah-GOHN beel-vah-VEE yoh-MAHM, ahd AH-nah yah-ROOM oy-VEE ah-LIGH.

How long must I take counsel in my soul, with sorrow in my heart all day? How long will my enemy be exalted over me?

הַבִּיטָה עֲנֵנִי יְהֹוָה אֱלֹהָי, הָאִירָה עֵינַי פֶּן אִישַׁן הַמָּוֶת.

hah-BEE-tah ah-NEH-nee ah-doh-NIGH eh-loh-HIGH, hah-EE-rah ay-NIGH pen ee-SHAHN hah-MAH-vet.

Look, answer me, Lord my God; light up my eyes, lest I sleep the sleep of death.

פֶּן יֹאמַר אֹיְבִי יְכָלְתִּיו, צָרַי יָגִילוּ כִּי אֶמּוֹט.

pen yoh-MAHR oy-VEE yeh-khohl-TEEV, tzah-RIGH yah-GEE-loo kee eh-MOHT.

Lest my enemy say, “I have overcome him,” and my foes rejoice when I stumble.

וַאֲנִי בְּחַסְדְּךָ בָטַחְתִּי, יָגֵל לִבִּי בִּישׁוּעָתֶךָ, אָשִׁירָה לַיהֹוָה כִּי גָמַל עָלָי.

vah-ah-NEE beh-khahs-deh-KHAH vah-TAKH-tee, yah-GEL lee-BEE bee-shoo-ah-TEH-khah, ah-SHEE-rah lah-doh-NIGH kee gah-MAHL ah-LIGH.

But I trust in Your kindness; my heart will rejoice in Your salvation. I will sing to the Lord, for He has dealt kindly with me.

Psalm 16Also in Tikkun HaKlali→

Said for: Success in an Endeavor.

לַמְנַצֵּחַ מִזְמוֹר לְדָוִד.

lahm-nah-TZEH-ahkh meez-MOHR leh-dah-VEED.

For the conductor, a psalm of David.

הַשָּׁמַיִם מְסַפְּרִים כְּבוֹד אֵל, וּמַעֲשֵׂה יָדָיו מַגִּיד הָרָקִיעַ.

hah-shah-MAH-yeem meh-sahp-REEM keh-VOHD EL, oo-mah-ah-SEH yah-DAHV mah-GEED hah-rah-KEE-ah.

The heavens declare the glory of God, and the sky tells of the work of His hands.

יוֹם לְיוֹם יַבִּיעַ אֹמֶר, וְלַיְלָה לְּלַיְלָה יְחַוֶּה דָּעַת.

yohm leh-YOHM yah-BEE-ah OH-mer, veh-LIGH-lah leh-LIGH-lah yeh-khah-VEH DAH-aht.

Day to day pours forth speech, and night to night expresses knowledge.

אֵין אֹמֶר וְאֵין דְּבָרִים, בְּלִי נִשְׁמָע קוֹלָם.

AYN OH-mer veh-AYN deh-vah-REEM, beh-LEE neesh-MAH koh-LAHM.

There is no speech and there are no words; their voice is not heard.

בְּכָל הָאָרֶץ יָצָא קַוָּם, וּבִקְצֵה תֵבֵל מִלֵּיהֶם, לַשֶּׁמֶשׁ שָׂם אֹהֶל בָּהֶם.

beh-KHOHL hah-AH-retz yah-TZAH kah-VAHM, oo-veek-TZEH teh-VEL mee-lay-HEM, lah-SHEH-mesh SAHM OH-hel bah-HEM.

Their line goes out through all the earth and their words to the end of the world; in them He has set a tent for the sun.

וְהוּא כְּחָתָן יֹצֵא מֵחֻפָּתוֹ, יָשִׂישׂ כְּגִבּוֹר לָרוּץ אֹרַח.

veh-HOO keh-khah-TAHN yoh-TZEH meh-khoo-pah-TOH, yah-SEES keh-gee-BOHR lah-ROOTZ OH-rahkh.

It is like a bridegroom coming out of his chamber, rejoicing like a strong man to run his course.

מִקְצֵה הַשָּׁמַיִם מוֹצָאוֹ, וּתְקוּפָתוֹ עַל קְצוֹתָם, וְאֵין נִסְתָּר מֵחַמָּתוֹ.

meek-TZEH hah-shah-MAH-yeem moh-tzah-OH, oot-koo-fah-TOH ahl keh-tzoh-TAHM, veh-AYN nees-TAHR meh-khah-mah-TOH.

It rises at one end of the heavens and circles to the other end, and nothing is hidden from its heat.

תּוֹרַת יְהֹוָה תְּמִימָה מְשִׁיבַת נָפֶשׁ, עֵדוּת יְהֹוָה נֶאֱמָנָה מַחְכִּימַת פֶּתִי.

toh-RAHT ah-doh-NIGH teh-mee-MAH meh-shee-VAHT NAH-fesh, eh-DOOT ah-doh-NIGH neh-eh-mah-NAH mahkh-kee-MAHT PEH-tee.

The Torah of the Lord is perfect, restoring the soul; the testimony of the Lord is faithful, making the simple wise.

פִּקּוּדֵי יְהֹוָה יְשָׁרִים מְשַׂמְּחֵי לֵב, מִצְוַת יְהֹוָה בָּרָה מְאִירַת עֵינָיִם.

pee-koo-DAY ah-doh-NIGH yeh-shah-REEM meh-sahm-KHAY LEV, meetz-VAHT ah-doh-NIGH bah-RAH meh-ee-RAHT ay-NAH-yeem.

The precepts of the Lord are upright, gladdening the heart; the commandment of the Lord is clear, enlightening the eyes.

יִרְאַת יְהֹוָה טְהוֹרָה עוֹמֶדֶת לָעַד, מִשְׁפְּטֵי יְהֹוָה אֱמֶת צָדְקוּ יַחְדָּו.

yeer-AHT ah-doh-NIGH teh-hoh-RAH oh-MEH-det lah-AHD, meesh-peh-TAY ah-doh-NIGH eh-MET tzahd-KOO yahkh-DAHV.

The fear of the Lord is pure, enduring forever; the judgments of the Lord are true, altogether righteous.

הַנֶּחֱמָדִים מִזָּהָב וּמִפַּז רָב, וּמְתוּקִים מִדְּבַשׁ וְנֹפֶת צוּפִים.

hah-neh-kheh-mah-DEEM mee-zah-HAHV oo-mee-PAHZ RAHV, oom-too-KEEM mee-deh-VAHSH veh-NOH-fet tzoo-FEEM.

They are more desirable than gold, than much fine gold, and sweeter than honey from the honeycomb.

גַּם עַבְדְּךָ נִזְהָר בָּהֶם, בְּשָׁמְרָם עֵקֶב רָב.

gahm ahv-deh-KHAH neez-HAHR bah-HEM, beh-shohm-RAHM EH-kev RAHV.

Your servant, too, is careful with them; in keeping them there is great reward.

שְׁגִיאוֹת מִי יָבִין, מִנִּסְתָּרוֹת נַקֵּנִי.

sheh-gee-OHT mee yah-VEEN, mee-nees-tah-ROHT nah-KEH-nee.

Who can discern errors? Cleanse me from hidden faults.

גַּם מִזֵּדִים חֲשֹׂךְ עַבְדֶּךָ, אַל יִמְשְׁלוּ בִי אָז אֵיתָם, וְנִקֵּיתִי מִפֶּשַׁע רָב.

gahm mee-zeh-DEEM khah-SOHKH ahv-DEH-khah, ahl yeem-sheh-LOO VEE, AHZ ay-TAHM, veh-nee-KAY-tee mee-PEH-shah RAHV.

Keep Your servant back from willful sins; let them not rule over me. Then I will be blameless and clear of great transgression.

יִהְיוּ לְרָצוֹן אִמְרֵי פִי וְהֶגְיוֹן לִבִּי לְפָנֶיךָ, יְהֹוָה צוּרִי וְגֹאֲלִי.

yeeh-YOO leh-rah-TZOHN eem-RAY FEE veh-heg-YOHN lee-BEE leh-fah-NEH-khah, ah-doh-NIGH tzoo-REE veh-goh-ah-LEE.

May the words of my mouth and the meditation of my heart be pleasing before You, Lord, my Rock and my Redeemer.

Said for: Health and Healing; Safety and Protection; A Time of Trouble or a Life Challenge; Success in a Lawsuit.

לַמְנַצֵּחַ מִזְמוֹר לְדָוִד.

lahm-nah-TZEH-ahkh meez-MOHR leh-dah-VEED.

For the conductor, a psalm of David.

יַעַנְךָ יְהֹוָה בְּיוֹם צָרָה, יְשַׂגֶּבְךָ שֵׁם אֱלֹהֵי יַעֲקֹב.

yah-ahn-KHAH ah-doh-NIGH beh-YOHM tzah-RAH, yeh-sah-gev-KHAH SHEM eh-loh-HAY yah-ah-KOHV.

May the Lord answer you on the day of trouble; may the name of the God of Jacob protect you.

יִשְׁלַח עֶזְרְךָ מִקֹּדֶשׁ, וּמִצִּיּוֹן יִסְעָדֶךָּ.

yeesh-LAKH ez-reh-KHAH mee-KOH-desh, oo-mee-tzee-YOHN yees-ah-DEK-kah.

May He send you help from the sanctuary and support you from Zion.

יִזְכֹּר כָּל מִנְחֹתֶךָ, וְעוֹלָתְךָ יְדַשְּׁנֶה סֶלָה.

yeez-KOHR kohl meen-khoh-TEH-khah, veh-oh-laht-KHAH yeh-dahsh-NEH SEH-lah.

May He remember all your offerings and accept your burnt offering, Selah.

יִתֶּן לְךָ כִלְבָבֶךָ, וְכָל עֲצָתְךָ יְמַלֵּא.

yee-TEN leh-KHAH kheel-vah-VEH-khah, veh-khohl ah-tzaht-KHAH yeh-mah-LEH.

May He grant you your heart’s desire and fulfill all your plans.

נְרַנְּנָה בִּישׁוּעָתֶךָ, וּבְשֵׁם אֱלֹהֵינוּ נִדְגֹּל, יְמַלֵּא יְהֹוָה כָּל מִשְׁאֲלוֹתֶיךָ.

neh-rah-neh-NAH bee-shoo-ah-TEH-khah, oov-SHEM eh-loh-HAY-noo need-GOHL, yeh-mah-LEH ah-doh-NIGH kohl meesh-ah-loh-TEH-khah.

We will sing for joy at your salvation and raise our banners in the name of our God; may the Lord fulfill all your requests.

עַתָּה יָדַעְתִּי כִּי הוֹשִׁיעַ יְהֹוָה מְשִׁיחוֹ, יַעֲנֵהוּ מִשְּׁמֵי קָדְשׁוֹ, בִּגְבֻרוֹת יֵשַׁע יְמִינוֹ.

ah-TAH yah-DAH-tee kee hoh-SHEE-ah ah-doh-NIGH meh-shee-KHOH, yah-ah-NEH-hoo mee-sheh-MAY kohd-SHOH, beeg-voo-ROHT YEH-shah yeh-mee-NOH.

Now I know that the Lord saves His anointed; He answers him from His holy heavens with the saving might of His right hand.

אֵלֶּה בָרֶכֶב וְאֵלֶּה בַסּוּסִים, וַאֲנַחְנוּ בְּשֵׁם יְהֹוָה אֱלֹהֵינוּ נַזְכִּיר.

EH-leh vah-REH-khev veh-EH-leh vah-soo-SEEM, vah-ah-NAKH-noo beh-SHEM ah-doh-NIGH eh-loh-HAY-noo nahz-KEER.

Some trust in chariots and some in horses, but we call upon the name of the Lord our God.

הֵמָּה כָּרְעוּ וְנָפָלוּ, וַאֲנַחְנוּ קַּמְנוּ וַנִּתְעוֹדָד.

HEH-mah kahr-OO veh-nah-FAH-loo, vah-ah-NAKH-noo KAHM-noo vah-neet-oh-DAHD.

They collapse and fall, but we rise and stand firm.

יְהֹוָה הוֹשִׁיעָה, הַמֶּלֶךְ יַעֲנֵנוּ בְיוֹם קָרְאֵנוּ.

ah-doh-NIGH hoh-SHEE-ah, hah-MEH-lekh yah-ah-NEH-noo veh-YOHM kohr-EH-noo.

Lord, save! May the King answer us on the day we call.

Psalm 23 — Mizmor LeDavidAlso in Friday Night→

Said for: Success in an Endeavor.

The psalm of the day for Sunday; also recited on Rosh Hashanah and Yom Kippur.

לְדָוִד מִזְמוֹר, לַיהֹוָה הָאָרֶץ וּמְלוֹאָהּ, תֵּבֵל וְיֹשְׁבֵי בָהּ.

leh-dah-VEED meez-MOHR, lah-doh-NIGH hah-AH-retz oom-loh-AH, teh-VEL veh-yohsh-VAY VAH.

Of David, a psalm. The earth and all it holds are the Lord's, the world and those who dwell in it.

כִּי הוּא עַל יַמִּים יְסָדָהּ, וְעַל נְהָרוֹת יְכוֹנְנֶהָ.

kee HOO ahl yah-MEEM yeh-sah-DAH, veh-ahl neh-hah-ROHT yeh-khoh-neh-NEH-hah.

For He founded it upon the seas and established it upon the rivers.

מִי יַעֲלֶה בְהַר יְהֹוָה, וּמִי יָקוּם בִּמְקוֹם קָדְשׁוֹ.

mee yah-ah-LEH veh-HAR ah-doh-NIGH, oo-MEE yah-KOOM beem-KOHM kohd-SHOH.

Who may ascend the mountain of the Lord, and who may stand in His holy place?

נְקִי כַפַּיִם וּבַר לֵבָב, אֲשֶׁר לֹא נָשָׂא לַשָּׁוְא נַפְשִׁי, וְלֹא נִשְׁבַּע לְמִרְמָה.

neh-KEE khah-PAH-yeem oo-VAHR leh-VAHV, ah-SHER loh nah-SAH lah-SHAHV nahf-SHEE, veh-LOH neesh-BAH leh-meer-MAH.

One with clean hands and a pure heart, who has not taken My name in vain nor sworn deceitfully.

יִשָּׂא בְרָכָה מֵאֵת יְהֹוָה, וּצְדָקָה מֵאֱלֹהֵי יִשְׁעוֹ.

yee-SAH veh-rah-KHAH meh-ET ah-doh-NIGH, oo-tzeh-dah-KAH meh-eh-loh-HAY yeesh-OH.

He will receive a blessing from the Lord and righteousness from the God of his salvation.

זֶה דּוֹר דֹּרְשָׁיו, מְבַקְשֵׁי פָנֶיךָ יַעֲקֹב סֶלָה.

ZEH dohr dohr-SHAHV, meh-vahk-SHAY fah-NEH-khah yah-ah-KOHV, SEH-lah.

This is the generation of those who seek Him, who seek Your presence, Jacob, Selah.

שְׂאוּ שְׁעָרִים רָאשֵׁיכֶם, וְהִנָּשְׂאוּ פִּתְחֵי עוֹלָם, וְיָבוֹא מֶלֶךְ הַכָּבוֹד.

seh-OO sheh-ah-REEM rah-shay-KHEM, veh-hee-nahs-OO peet-KHAY oh-LAHM, veh-yah-VOH MEH-lekh hah-kah-VOHD.

Lift up your heads, O gates, and be lifted up, eternal doors, so the King of glory may enter.

מִי זֶה מֶלֶךְ הַכָּבוֹד, יְהֹוָה עִזּוּז וְגִבּוֹר, יְהֹוָה גִּבּוֹר מִלְחָמָה.

mee ZEH MEH-lekh hah-kah-VOHD, ah-doh-NIGH ee-ZOOZ veh-gee-BOHR, ah-doh-NIGH gee-BOHR meel-khah-MAH.

Who is the King of glory? The Lord, strong and mighty; the Lord, mighty in battle.

שְׂאוּ שְׁעָרִים רָאשֵׁיכֶם, וּשְׂאוּ פִּתְחֵי עוֹלָם, וְיָבֹא מֶלֶךְ הַכָּבוֹד.

seh-OO sheh-ah-REEM rah-shay-KHEM, oo-seh-OO peet-KHAY oh-LAHM, veh-yah-VOH MEH-lekh hah-kah-VOHD.

Lift up your heads, O gates; lift them up, eternal doors, so the King of glory may enter.

מִי הוּא זֶה מֶלֶךְ הַכָּבוֹד, יְהֹוָה צְבָאוֹת הוּא מֶלֶךְ הַכָּבוֹד סֶלָה.

mee HOO ZEH MEH-lekh hah-kah-VOHD, ah-doh-NIGH tzeh-vah-OHT, HOO MEH-lekh hah-kah-VOHD, SEH-lah.

Who is this King of glory? The Lord of hosts, He is the King of glory, Selah.

Said for: A Troubled or Wayward Child.

לְדָוִד, אֵלֶיךָ יְהֹוָה נַפְשִׁי אֶשָּׂא.

leh-dah-VEED, eh-LEH-khah ah-doh-NIGH nahf-SHEE eh-SAH.

Of David. To You, Lord, I lift up my soul.

אֱלֹהַי בְּךָ בָטַחְתִּי אַל אֵבוֹשָׁה, אַל יַעַלְצוּ אֹיְבַי לִי.

eh-loh-HIGH beh-KHAH vah-TAKH-tee ahl eh-VOH-shah, ahl yah-ahl-TZOO oy-VIGH LEE.

My God, in You I trust; let me not be ashamed; let not my enemies exult over me.

גַּם כָּל קֹוֶיךָ לֹא יֵבֹשׁוּ, יֵבֹשׁוּ הַבּוֹגְדִים רֵיקָם.

gahm kohl koh-VEH-khah loh yeh-VOH-shoo, yeh-VOH-shoo hah-boh-geh-DEEM ray-KAHM.

Indeed, none who hope in You will be ashamed; those who betray without cause will be ashamed.

דְּרָכֶיךָ יְהֹוָה הוֹדִיעֵנִי, אֹרְחוֹתֶיךָ לַמְּדֵנִי.

deh-rah-KHEH-khah ah-doh-NIGH hoh-dee-EH-nee, ohr-khoh-TEH-khah lahm-DEH-nee.

Make Your ways known to me, Lord; teach me Your paths.

הַדְרִיכֵנִי בַאֲמִתֶּךָ וְלַמְּדֵנִי, כִּי אַתָּה אֱלֹהֵי יִשְׁעִי, אוֹתְךָ קִוִּיתִי כָּל הַיּוֹם.

hahd-ree-KHEH-nee vah-ah-mee-TEH-khah veh-lahm-DEH-nee, kee ah-TAH eh-loh-HAY yeesh-EE, oht-KHAH kee-VEE-tee kohl hah-YOHM.

Guide me in Your truth and teach me, for You are the God of my salvation; I hope in You all day long.

זְכֹר רַחֲמֶיךָ יְהֹוָה וַחֲסָדֶיךָ, כִּי מֵעוֹלָם הֵמָּה.

zeh-KHOHR rah-khah-MEH-khah ah-doh-NIGH vah-khah-sah-DEH-khah, kee meh-oh-LAHM HEH-mah.

Remember Your mercies, Lord, and Your kindnesses, for they are from of old.

חַטֹּאות נְעוּרַי וּפְשָׁעַי אַל תִּזְכֹּר, כְּחַסְדְּךָ זְכָר לִי אַתָּה, לְמַעַן טוּבְךָ יְהֹוָה.
