khah-TOHT neh-oo-RIGH oo-feh-shah-IGH ahl teez-KOHR, keh-khahs-deh-KHAH zeh-khohr LEE ah-TAH, leh-MAH-ahn toov-KHAH ah-doh-NIGH.

Do not remember the sins of my youth or my transgressions; remember me according to Your kindness, for the sake of Your goodness, Lord.

טוֹב וְיָשָׁר יְהֹוָה, עַל כֵּן יוֹרֶה חַטָּאִים בַּדָּרֶךְ.

TOHV veh-yah-SHAHR ah-doh-NIGH, ahl KEN yoh-REH khah-tah-EEM bah-DAH-rekh.

Good and upright is the Lord; therefore He guides sinners in the way.

יַדְרֵךְ עֲנָוִים בַּמִּשְׁפָּט, וִילַמֵּד עֲנָוִים דַּרְכּוֹ.

yahd-REKH ah-nah-VEEM bah-meesh-PAHT, vee-lah-MED ah-nah-VEEM dahr-KOH.

He leads the humble in justice and teaches the humble His way.

כָּל אָרְחוֹת יְהֹוָה חֶסֶד וֶאֱמֶת, לְנֹצְרֵי בְרִיתוֹ וְעֵדֹתָיו.

kohl ohr-KHOHT ah-doh-NIGH KHEH-sed veh-eh-MET, leh-noh-tzeh-RAY veh-ree-TOH veh-eh-doh-TAHV.

All the paths of the Lord are kindness and truth for those who keep His covenant and His testimonies.

לְמַעַן שִׁמְךָ יְהֹוָה, וְסָלַחְתָּ לַעֲוֹנִי כִּי רַב הוּא.

leh-MAH-ahn sheem-KHAH ah-doh-NIGH, veh-sah-lahkh-TAH lah-ah-voh-NEE kee RAHV HOO.

For the sake of Your name, Lord, forgive my iniquity, for it is great.

מִי זֶה הָאִישׁ יְרֵא יְהֹוָה, יוֹרֶנּוּ בְּדֶרֶךְ יִבְחָר.

mee ZEH hah-EESH yeh-REH ah-doh-NIGH, yoh-REH-noo beh-DEH-rekh yeev-KHAHR.

Who is the man who fears the Lord? He will teach him the way he should choose.

נַפְשׁוֹ בְּטוֹב תָּלִין, וְזַרְעוֹ יִירַשׁ אָרֶץ.

nahf-SHOH beh-TOHV tah-LEEN, veh-zahr-OH yee-RAHSH AH-retz.

His soul will dwell in goodness, and his descendants will inherit the land.

סוֹד יְהֹוָה לִירֵאָיו, וּבְרִיתוֹ לְהוֹדִיעָם.

sohd ah-doh-NIGH lee-reh-AHV, oo-veh-ree-TOH leh-hoh-dee-AHM.

The counsel of the Lord is with those who fear Him, and He makes His covenant known to them.

עֵינַי תָּמִיד אֶל יְהֹוָה, כִּי הוּא יוֹצִיא מֵרֶשֶׁת רַגְלָי.

ay-NIGH tah-MEED el ah-doh-NIGH, kee HOO yoh-TZEE meh-REH-shet rahg-LIGH.

My eyes are always toward the Lord, for He will free my feet from the net.

פְּנֵה אֵלַי וְחָנֵּנִי, כִּי יָחִיד וְעָנִי אָנִי.

peh-NEH eh-LIGH veh-khah-NEH-nee, kee yah-KHEED veh-ah-NEE AH-nee.

Turn to me and be gracious to me, for I am alone and afflicted.

צָרוֹת לְבָבִי הִרְחִיבוּ, מִמְּצוּקוֹתַי הוֹצִיאֵנִי.

tzah-ROHT leh-vah-VEE heer-KHEE-voo, mee-meh-tzoo-koh-TIGH hoh-tzee-EH-nee.

The troubles of my heart have multiplied; bring me out of my distress.

רְאֵה עָנְיִי וַעֲמָלִי, וְשָׂא לְכָל חַטֹּאותָי.

reh-EH ohn-YEE vah-ah-mah-LEE, veh-SAH leh-KHOHL khah-toh-TIGH.

See my affliction and my toil, and forgive all my sins.

רְאֵה אוֹיְבַי כִּי רָבּוּ, וְשִׂנְאַת חָמָס שְׂנֵאוּנִי.

reh-EH oy-VIGH kee RAH-boo, veh-seen-AHT khah-MAHS seh-neh-OO-nee.

See how many are my enemies, and how they hate me with violent hatred.

שָׁמְרָה נַפְשִׁי וְהַצִּילֵנִי, אַל אֵבוֹשׁ כִּי חָסִיתִי בָךְ.

shohm-RAH nahf-SHEE veh-hah-tzee-LEH-nee, ahl eh-VOHSH kee khah-SEE-tee VAHKH.

Guard my soul and rescue me; let me not be ashamed, for I take refuge in You.

תֹּם וָיֹשֶׁר יִצְּרוּנִי, כִּי קִוִּיתִיךָ.

tohm vah-YOH-sher yee-tzeh-ROO-nee, kee kee-vee-TEE-khah.

May integrity and uprightness preserve me, for I hope in You.

פְּדֵה אֱלֹהִים אֶת יִשְׂרָאֵל, מִכֹּל צָרוֹתָיו.

peh-DEH eh-loh-HEEM eht yees-rah-EL, mee-KOHL tzah-roh-TAHV.

God, redeem Israel from all its troubles.

Said for: Annulling Harsh Decrees.

מִזְמוֹר שִׁיר חֲנֻכַּת הַבַּיִת לְדָוִד.

meez-MOHR sheer khah-noo-KAHT hah-BAH-yeet leh-dah-VEED.

A psalm, a song for the dedication of the House, of David.

אֲרוֹמִמְךָ יְהֹוָה כִּי דִלִּיתָנִי, וְלֹא שִׂמַּחְתָּ אֹיְבַי לִי.

ah-roh-meem-KHAH ah-doh-NIGH kee dee-lee-TAH-nee, veh-LOH see-MAKH-tah oy-VIGH LEE.

I will exalt You, Lord, for You have lifted me up and have not let my enemies rejoice over me.

יְהֹוָה אֱלֹהָי, שִׁוַּעְתִּי אֵלֶיךָ וַתִּרְפָּאֵנִי.

ah-doh-NIGH eh-loh-HIGH, shee-VAH-tee eh-LEH-khah vah-teer-pah-EH-nee.

Lord my God, I cried out to You and You healed me.

יְהֹוָה הֶעֱלִיתָ מִן שְׁאוֹל נַפְשִׁי, חִיִּיתַנִי מִיָּרְדִי בוֹר.

ah-doh-NIGH heh-eh-LEE-tah meen sheh-OHL nahf-SHEE, khee-yee-TAH-nee mee-yahr-DEE VOHR.

Lord, You brought my soul up from the grave; You kept me alive so that I did not go down to the pit.

זַמְּרוּ לַיהֹוָה חֲסִידָיו, וְהוֹדוּ לְזֵכֶר קָדְשׁוֹ.

zahm-ROO lah-doh-NIGH khah-see-DAHV, veh-hoh-DOO leh-ZEH-kher kohd-SHOH.

Sing praise to the Lord, you His devoted ones, and give thanks to His holy name.

כִּי רֶגַע בְּאַפּוֹ חַיִּים בִּרְצוֹנוֹ בָּעֶרֶב יָלִין בֶּכִי וְלַבֹּקֶר רִנָּה.

kee REH-gah beh-ah-POH khah-YEEM beer-tzoh-NOH, bah-EH-rev yah-LEEN BEH-khee veh-lah-BOH-ker ree-NAH.

For His anger lasts a moment, but His favor is for a lifetime; weeping may linger for the night, but joy comes in the morning.

וַאֲנִי אָמַרְתִּי בְשַׁלְוִי, בַּל אֶמּוֹט לְעוֹלָם.

vah-ah-NEE ah-MAHR-tee veh-shahl-VEE, bahl eh-MOHT leh-oh-LAHM.

In my ease I said, “I will never be shaken.”

יְהֹוָה בִּרְצוֹנְךָ הֶעֱמַדְתָּה לְהַרְרִי עֹז הִסְתַּרְתָּ פָנֶיךָ הָיִיתִי נִבְהָל.

ah-doh-NIGH beer-tzohn-KHAH heh-eh-MAHD-tah leh-hahr-REE OHZ, hees-TAHR-tah fah-NEH-khah hah-YEE-tee neev-HAHL.

Lord, by Your favor You made my mountain stand strong; You hid Your face, and I was terrified.

אֵלֶיךָ יְהֹוָה אֶקְרָא, וְאֶל אֲדֹנָי אֶתְחַנָּן.

eh-LEH-khah ah-doh-NIGH ek-RAH, veh-EL ah-doh-NIGH et-khah-NAHN.

To You, Lord, I call, and to the Lord I plead:

מַה בֶּצַע בְּדָמִי בְּרִדְתִּי אֶל שָׁחַת הֲיוֹדְךָ עָפָר, הֲיַגִּיד אֲמִתֶּךָ.

mah BEH-tzah beh-dah-MEE beh-reed-TEE el SHAH-khaht, hah-yoh-deh-KHAH ah-FAHR, hah-yah-GEED ah-mee-TEH-khah.

“What profit is there in my blood if I go down to the pit? Will the dust thank You? Will it declare Your truth?

שְׁמַע יְהֹוָה וְחָנֵּנִי, יְהֹוָה הֱיֵה עֹזֵר לִי.

sheh-MAH ah-doh-NIGH veh-khah-NEH-nee, ah-doh-NIGH heh-YEH oh-ZER LEE.

Hear, Lord, and be gracious to me; Lord, be my helper.”

הָפַכְתָּ מִסְפְּדִי לְמָחוֹל לִי פִּתַּחְתָּ שַׂקִּי, וַתְּאַזְּרֵנִי שִׂמְחָה.

hah-FAHKH-tah mees-peh-DEE leh-mah-KHOHL LEE, pee-TAKH-tah sah-KEE vah-teh-ahz-REH-nee seem-KHAH.

You turned my mourning into dancing; You loosened my sackcloth and girded me with joy,

לְמַעַן יְזַמֶּרְךָ כָבוֹד וְלֹא יִדֹּם, יְהֹוָה אֱלֹהַי לְעוֹלָם אוֹדֶךָּ.

leh-MAH-ahn yeh-zah-mer-KHAH khah-VOHD veh-LOH yee-DOHM, ah-doh-NIGH eh-loh-HIGH leh-oh-LAHM oh-DEK-kah.

so that my soul may sing praise to You and not be silent; Lord my God, I will thank You forever.

Said for: Protection from the Evil Eye.

לַמְנַצֵּחַ מִזְמוֹר לְדָוִד.

lahm-nah-TZEH-ahkh meez-MOHR leh-dah-VEED.

For the conductor, a psalm of David.

בְּךָ יְהֹוָה חָסִיתִי אַל אֵבוֹשָׁה לְעוֹלָם, בְּצִדְקָתְךָ פַלְּטֵנִי.

beh-KHAH ah-doh-NIGH khah-SEE-tee ahl eh-VOH-shah leh-oh-LAHM, beh-tzeed-kaht-KHAH fahl-TEH-nee.

In You, Lord, I take refuge; let me never be ashamed; deliver me in Your righteousness.

הַטֵּה אֵלַי אָזְנְךָ מְהֵרָה הַצִּילֵנִי הֱיֵה לִי לְצוּר מָעוֹז לְבֵית מְצוּדוֹת לְהוֹשִׁיעֵנִי.

hah-TEH eh-LIGH ohz-neh-KHAH meh-heh-RAH hah-tzee-LEH-nee, heh-YEH LEE leh-TZOOR mah-OHZ leh-VAYT meh-tzoo-DOHT leh-hoh-shee-EH-nee.

Incline Your ear to me and rescue me quickly; be for me a rock of strength, a fortress to save me.

כִּי סַלְעִי וּמְצוּדָתִי אָתָּה, וּלְמַעַן שִׁמְךָ תַּנְחֵנִי וּתְנַהֲלֵנִי.

kee sahl-EE oom-tzoo-dah-TEE AH-tah, ool-MAH-ahn sheem-KHAH tahn-KHEH-nee oot-nah-hah-LEH-nee.

For You are my rock and my fortress; for the sake of Your name, lead me and guide me.

תּוֹצִיאֵנִי מֵרֶשֶׁת זוּ טָמְנוּ לִי, כִּי אַתָּה מָעוּזִּי.

toh-tzee-EH-nee meh-REH-shet zoo tahm-NOO LEE, kee ah-TAH mah-oo-ZEE.

Free me from the net they have hidden for me, for You are my strength.

בְּיָדְךָ אַפְקִיד רוּחִי פָּדִיתָה אוֹתִי יְהֹוָה אֵל אֱמֶת.

beh-yahd-KHAH ahf-KEED roo-KHEE, pah-DEE-tah oh-TEE ah-doh-NIGH EL eh-MET.

Into Your hand I commit my spirit; You have redeemed me, Lord, God of truth.

שָׂנֵאתִי הַשֹּׁמְרִים הַבְלֵי שָׁוְא, וַאֲנִי אֶל יְהֹוָה בָּטָחְתִּי.

sah-NAY-tee hah-shohm-REEM hahv-LAY SHAHV, vah-ah-NEE el ah-doh-NIGH bah-TAKH-tee.

I hate those who cling to worthless idols; but I trust in the Lord.

אָגִילָה וְאֶשְׂמְחָה בְּחַסְדֶּךָ אֲשֶׁר רָאִיתָ אֶת עָנְיִי, יָדַעְתָּ בְּצָרוֹת נַפְשִׁי.

ah-GEE-lah veh-es-meh-KHAH beh-khahs-DEH-khah ah-SHER rah-EE-tah eht ohn-YEE, yah-DAHT-tah beh-tzah-ROHT nahf-SHEE.

I will rejoice and be glad in Your kindness, for You have seen my affliction and known the troubles of my soul.

וְלֹא הִסְגַּרְתַּנִי בְּיַד אוֹיֵב, הֶעֱמַדְתָּ בַמֶּרְחָב רַגְלָי.

veh-LOH hees-gahr-TAH-nee beh-YAHD oh-YEV, heh-eh-MAHD-tah vah-mer-KHAHV rahg-LIGH.

You have not handed me over to the enemy; You have set my feet in a broad place.

חָנֵּנִי יְהֹוָה כִּי צַר לִי עָשְׁשָׁה בְכַעַס עֵינִי נַפְשִׁי וּבִטְנִי.

khah-NEH-nee ah-doh-NIGH kee TZAHR LEE, ah-sheh-SHAH veh-KHAH-ahs ay-NEE nahf-SHEE oo-veet-NEE.

Be gracious to me, Lord, for I am in distress; my eye, my soul and my body waste away with grief.

כִּי כָלוּ בְיָגוֹן חַיַּי וּשְׁנוֹתַי בַּאֲנָחָה כָּשַׁל בַּעֲוֺנִי כֹחִי, וַעֲצָמַי עָשֵׁשׁוּ.

kee KHAH-loo veh-yah-GOHN khah-YIGH oosh-noh-TIGH bah-ah-nah-KHAH, kah-SHAHL bah-ah-voh-NEE koh-KHEE vah-ah-tzah-MIGH ah-SHEH-shoo.

For my life is spent with sorrow and my years with sighing; my strength fails because of my iniquity, and my bones waste away.

מִכָּל צֹרְרַי הָיִיתִי חֶרְפָּה וְלִשֲׁכֵנַי מְאֹד וּפַחַד לִמְיֻדָּעָי רֹאַי בַּחוּץ, נָדְדוּ מִמֶּנִּי.

mee-KOHL tzohr-RIGH hah-YEE-tee kher-PAH veh-leesh-kheh-NIGH meh-OHD oo-FAH-khahd leem-yoo-dah-IGH, roh-IGH bah-KHOOTZ nah-deh-DOO mee-MEH-nee.

Because of all my foes I have become a reproach, especially to my neighbors, and a dread to my acquaintances; those who see me outside flee from me.

נִשְׁכַּחְתִּי כְּמֵת מִלֵּב, הָיִיתִי כִּכְלִי אֹבֵד.

neesh-KAKH-tee keh-MET mee-LEV, hah-YEE-tee keekh-LEE oh-VED.

I am forgotten like the dead, out of mind; I have become like a broken vessel.

כִּי שָׁמַעְתִּי דִּבַּת רַבִּים מָגוֹר מִסָּבִיב בְּהִוָּסְדָם יַחַד עָלַי, לָקַחַת נַפְשִׁי זָמָמוּ.

kee shah-MAH-tee dee-BAHT rah-BEEM mah-GOHR mee-sah-VEEV, beh-hee-vah-seh-DAHM YAH-khahd ah-LIGH, lah-KAH-khaht nahf-SHEE zah-MAH-moo.

For I hear the whispering of many, terror on every side, as they plot together against me, scheming to take my life.

וַאֲנִי עָלֶיךָ בָטַחְתִּי יְהֹוָה, אָמַרְתִּי אֱלֹהַי אָתָּה.

vah-ah-NEE ah-LEH-khah vah-TAKH-tee ah-doh-NIGH, ah-MAHR-tee eh-loh-HIGH AH-tah.

But I trust in You, Lord; I say, “You are my God.”

בְּיָדְךָ עִתֹּתָי, הַצִּילֵנִי מִיַּד אוֹיְבַי וּמֵרֹדְפָי.

beh-yahd-KHAH ee-toh-TIGH, hah-tzee-LEH-nee mee-YAHD oy-VIGH oo-meh-rohd-FIGH.

My times are in Your hand; rescue me from the hand of my enemies and from my pursuers.

הָאִירָה פָנֶיךָ עַל עַבְדֶּךָ, הוֹשִׁיעֵנִי בְחַסְדֶּךָ.

hah-EE-rah fah-NEH-khah ahl ahv-DEH-khah, hoh-shee-EH-nee veh-khahs-DEH-khah.

Let Your face shine upon Your servant; save me in Your kindness.

יְהֹוָה אַל אֵבוֹשָׁה כִּי קְרָאתִיךָ, יֵבֹשׁוּ רְשָׁעִים יִדְּמוּ לִשְׁאוֹל.

ah-doh-NIGH ahl eh-VOH-shah kee keh-rah-TEE-khah, yeh-VOH-shoo reh-shah-EEM yee-deh-MOO leesh-OHL.

Lord, let me not be ashamed, for I have called upon You; let the wicked be ashamed and silenced in the grave.

תֵּאָלַמְנָה שִׂפְתֵי שָׁקֶר הַדֹּבְרוֹת עַל צַדִּיק עָתָק בְּגַאֲוָה וָבוּז.

teh-ah-LAHM-nah seef-TAY SHAH-ker, hah-dohv-ROHT ahl tzah-DEEK ah-TAHK beh-gah-ah-VAH vah-VOOZ.

Let lying lips be silenced, which speak arrogantly against the righteous with pride and contempt.

מָה רַב טוּבְךָ אֲשֶׁר צָפַנְתָּ לִּירֵאֶיךָ פָּעַלְתָּ לַחֹסִים בָּךְ, נֶגֶד בְּנֵי אָדָם.

mah RAHV toov-KHAH ah-SHER tzah-FAHN-tah lee-reh-EH-khah, pah-AHL-tah lah-khoh-SEEM BAHKH, NEH-ged beh-NAY ah-DAHM.

How great is Your goodness, which You have stored up for those who fear You and done for those who take refuge in You, before all people!

תַּסְתִּירֵם בְּסֵתֶר פָּנֶיךָ מֵרֻכְסֵי אִישׁ תִּצְפְּנֵם בְּסֻכָּה מֵרִיב לְשֹׁנוֹת.

tahs-tee-REM beh-SEH-ter pah-NEH-khah meh-rookh-SAY EESH, teetz-peh-NEM beh-soo-KAH meh-REEV leh-shoh-NOHT.

You hide them in the shelter of Your presence from the schemes of men; You keep them in a shelter from the strife of tongues.

בָּרוּךְ יְהֹוָה, כִּי הִפְלִיא חַסְדּוֹ לִי בְּעִיר מָצוֹר.

bah-ROOKH ah-doh-NIGH, kee heef-LEE khahs-DOH LEE beh-EER mah-TZOHR.

Blessed is the Lord, for He has shown me His wondrous kindness in a besieged city.

וַאֲנִי אָמַרְתִּי בְחָפְזִי נִגְרַזְתִּי מִנֶּגֶד עֵינֶיךָ אָכֵן שָׁמַעְתָּ קוֹל תַּחֲנוּנַי בְּשַׁוְּעִי אֵלֶיךָ.

vah-ah-NEE ah-MAHR-tee veh-khohf-ZEE neeg-RAHZ-tee mee-NEH-ged ay-NEH-khah, ah-KHEN shah-MAHT-tah kohl tah-khah-noo-NIGH beh-shah-veh-EE eh-LEH-khah.

In my alarm I said, “I am cut off from Your sight”; yet You heard the voice of my pleas when I cried out to You.

אֶהֱבוּ אֶת יְהֹוָה כָּל חֲסִידָיו אֱמוּנִים נֹצֵר יְהֹוָה, וּמְשַׁלֵּם עַל יֶתֶר עֹשֵׂה גַאֲוָה.

eh-heh-VOO eht ah-doh-NIGH kohl khah-see-DAHV, eh-moo-NEEM noh-TZER ah-doh-NIGH, oom-shah-LEM ahl YEH-ter oh-SEH gah-ah-VAH.

Love the Lord, all His devoted ones; the Lord guards the faithful and fully repays the one who acts in pride.

חִזְקוּ וְיַאֲמֵץ לְבַבְכֶם, כָּל הַמְיַחֲלִים לַיהֹוָה.

kheez-KOO veh-yah-ah-METZ leh-vahv-KHEM, kohl hahm-yah-khah-LEEM lah-doh-NIGH.

Be strong, and let your heart take courage, all who hope in the Lord.

Said for: Finding a Spouse.

Hebrew audio

לְדָוִד מַשְׂכִּיל, אַשְׁרֵי נְשׂוּי פֶּשַׁע כְּסוּי חֲטָאָה.

leh-dah-VEED mahs-KEEL, ahsh-RAY neh-SOOY PEH-shah keh-SOOY khah-tah-AH.

Of David, a maskil. Happy is the one whose transgression is forgiven, whose sin is covered.

אַשְׁרֵי אָדָם לֹא יַחְשֹׁב יְהֹוָה לוֹ עָוֹן, וְאֵין בְּרוּחוֹ רְמִיָּה.

ahsh-RAY ah-DAHM loh yahkh-SHOHV ah-doh-NIGH LOH ah-VOHN, veh-AYN beh-roo-KHOH reh-mee-YAH.

Happy is the man to whom the Lord counts no iniquity, and in whose spirit there is no deceit.

כִּי הֶחֱרַשְׁתִּי בָּלוּ עֲצָמָי, בְּשַׁאֲגָתִי כָּל הַיּוֹם.

kee heh-kheh-RAHSH-tee BAH-loo ah-tzah-MIGH, beh-shah-ah-gah-TEE kohl hah-YOHM.

When I kept silent, my bones wasted away through my groaning all day long.

כִּי יוֹמָם וָלַיְלָה תִּכְבַּד עָלַי יָדֶךָ, נֶהְפַּךְ לְשַׁדִּי בְּחַרְבֹנֵי קַיִץ סֶלָה.

kee yoh-MAHM vah-LIGH-lah teekh-BAHD ah-LIGH yah-DEH-khah, neh-PAHKH leh-shah-DEE beh-khahr-voh-NAY KAH-yeetz SEH-lah.

For day and night Your hand was heavy upon me; my vigor dried up as in the heat of summer, Selah.

חַטָּאתִי אוֹדִיעֲךָ וַעֲוֹנִי לֹא כִסִּיתִי, אָמַרְתִּי אוֹדֶה עֲלֵי פְשָׁעַי לַיהֹוָה, וְאַתָּה נָשָׂאתָ עֲוֹן חַטָּאתִי סֶלָה.

khah-tah-TEE oh-dee-ah-KHAH vah-ah-voh-NEE loh khee-SEE-tee, ah-MAHR-tee oh-DEH ah-LAY feh-shah-IGH lah-doh-NIGH, veh-ah-TAH nah-SAH-tah ah-VOHN khah-tah-TEE SEH-lah.

I acknowledged my sin to You and did not hide my iniquity; I said, “I will confess my transgressions to the Lord,” and You forgave the guilt of my sin, Selah.

עַל זֹאת יִתְפַּלֵּל כָּל חָסִיד אֵלֶיךָ לְעֵת מְצֹא, רַק לְשֵׁטֶף מַיִם רַבִּים, אֵלָיו לֹא יַגִּיעוּ.

ahl ZOHT yeet-pah-LEL kohl khah-SEED eh-LEH-khah leh-ET meh-TZOH, rahk leh-SHEH-tef MAH-yeem rah-BEEM, eh-LAHV loh yah-GEE-oo.

Therefore let every devoted one pray to You at a time when You may be found; when great waters overflow, they will not reach him.

אַתָּה סֵתֶר לִי מִצַּר תִּצְּרֵנִי, רָנֵּי פַלֵּט תְּסוֹבְבֵנִי סֶלָה.

ah-TAH SEH-ter LEE mee-TZAHR tee-tzeh-REH-nee, rah-NAY fah-LET teh-soh-veh-VEH-nee SEH-lah.

You are my hiding place; You protect me from trouble; You surround me with songs of deliverance, Selah.

אַשְׂכִּילְךָ וְאוֹרְךָ בְּדֶרֶךְ זוּ תֵלֵךְ, אִיעֲצָה עָלֶיךָ עֵינִי.

ahs-keel-KHAH veh-ohr-KHAH beh-DEH-rekh ZOO teh-LEKH, ee-ah-TZAH ah-LEH-khah ay-NEE.

I will instruct you and teach you the way you should go; I will counsel you with My eye upon you.

אַל תִּהְיוּ כְּסוּס כְּפֶרֶד אֵין הָבִין, בְּמֶתֶג וָרֶסֶן עֶדְיוֹ לִבְלוֹם, בַּל קְרֹב אֵלֶיךָ.

ahl teeh-YOO keh-SOOS keh-FEH-red AYN hah-VEEN, beh-MEH-teg vah-REH-sen ed-YOH leev-LOHM, bahl keh-ROHV eh-LEH-khah.

Do not be like a horse or a mule without understanding, whose mouth must be held with bit and bridle, or it will not come near you.

רַבִּים מַכְאוֹבִים לָרָשָׁע, וְהַבּוֹטֵחַ בַּיהֹוָה חֶסֶד יְסוֹבְבֶנּוּ.

rah-BEEM mahkh-oh-VEEM lah-rah-SHAH, veh-hah-boh-TEH-ahkh bah-doh-NIGH KHEH-sed yeh-soh-veh-VEH-noo.

Many are the sorrows of the wicked, but kindness surrounds the one who trusts in the Lord.

שִׂמְחוּ בַיהֹוָה וְגִילוּ צַדִּיקִים, וְהַרְנִינוּ כָּל יִשְׁרֵי לֵב.

seem-KHOO vah-doh-NIGH veh-GEE-loo tzah-dee-KEEM, veh-hahr-NEE-noo kohl yeesh-RAY LEV.

Rejoice in the Lord and be glad, you righteous; sing for joy, all you upright of heart.

Said for: Defeat of Enemies and Adversaries.

לְדָוִד רִיבָה יְהֹוָה אֶת יְרִיבַי, לְחַם אֶת לֹחֲמָי.

leh-dah-VEED, ree-VAH ah-doh-NIGH eht yeh-ree-VIGH, leh-KHAHM eht loh-khah-MIGH.

Of David. Contend, Lord, with those who contend with me; fight against those who fight against me.

הַחֲזֵק מָגֵן וְצִנָּה, וְקוּמָה בְּעֶזְרָתִי.

hah-khah-ZEK mah-GEN veh-tzee-NAH, veh-KOO-mah beh-ez-rah-TEE.

Take hold of shield and buckler, and rise up to help me.

וְהָרֵק חֲנִית וּסְגֹר לִקְרַאת רֹדְפָי, אֱמֹר לְנַפְשִׁי יְשֻׁעָתֵךְ אָנִי.

veh-hah-REK khah-NEET oos-GOHR leek-RAHT rohd-FIGH, eh-MOHR leh-nahf-SHEE yeh-shoo-ah-TEKH AH-nee.

Draw the spear and bar the way against my pursuers; say to my soul, “I am your salvation.”

יֵבֹשׁוּ וְיִכָּלְמוּ מְבַקְשֵׁי נַפְשִׁי יִסֹּגוּ אָחוֹר וְיַחְפְּרוּ, חֹשְׁבֵי רָעָתִי.

yeh-VOH-shoo veh-yee-kahl-MOO meh-vahk-SHAY nahf-SHEE, yee-SOH-goo ah-KHOHR veh-yahkh-peh-ROO khohsh-VAY rah-ah-TEE.

Let those who seek my life be ashamed and disgraced; let those who plot my harm be turned back and confounded.

יִהְיוּ כְּמֹץ לִפְנֵי רוּחַ, וּמַלְאַךְ יְהֹוָה דּוֹחֶה.

yeeh-YOO keh-MOHTZ leef-NAY ROO-ahkh, oo-mahl-AHKH ah-doh-NIGH doh-KHEH.

Let them be like chaff before the wind, with the angel of the Lord driving them on.

יְהִי דַרְכָּם חֹשֶׁךְ וַחֲלַקְלַקּוֹת, וּמַלְאַךְ יְהֹוָה רֹדְפָם.

yeh-HEE dahr-KAHM KHOH-shekh vah-khah-lahk-lah-KOHT, oo-mahl-AHKH ah-doh-NIGH rohd-FAHM.

Let their way be dark and slippery, with the angel of the Lord pursuing them.

כִּי חִנָּם טָמְנוּ לִי שַׁחַת רִשְׁתָּם, חִנָּם חָפְרוּ לְנַפְשִׁי.

kee khee-NAHM tahm-NOO LEE SHAH-khaht reesh-TAHM, khee-NAHM khahf-ROO leh-nahf-SHEE.

For without cause they hid their net for me; without cause they dug a pit for my soul.

תְּבוֹאֵהוּ שׁוֹאָה לֹא יֵדָע וְרִשְׁתּוֹ אֲשֶׁר טָמַן תִּלְכְּדוֹ, בְּשׁוֹאָה יִפָּל בָּהּ.

teh-voh-EH-hoo shoh-AH loh yeh-DAH, veh-reesh-TOH ah-SHER tah-MAHN teel-keh-DOH, beh-shoh-AH yee-POHL BAH.

Let ruin come upon him unawares; let the net he hid catch him; let him fall into it to his ruin.

וְנַפְשִׁי תָּגִיל בַּיהֹוָה, תָּשִׂישׂ בִּישׁוּעָתוֹ.

veh-nahf-SHEE tah-GEEL bah-doh-NIGH, tah-SEES bee-shoo-ah-TOH.

Then my soul will rejoice in the Lord and delight in His salvation.

כָּל עַצְמוֹתַי תֹּאמַרְנָה יְהֹוָה מִי כָמוֹךָ מַצִּיל עָנִי מֵחָזָק מִמֶּנּוּ, וְעָנִי וְאֶבְיוֹן מִגֹּזְלוֹ.

kohl ahtz-moh-TIGH toh-MAHR-nah ah-doh-NIGH mee khah-MOH-khah, mah-TZEEL ah-NEE meh-khah-ZAHK mee-MEH-noo, veh-ah-NEE veh-ev-YOHN mee-goh-zeh-LOH.

All my bones will say, “Lord, who is like You, who rescues the poor from one stronger than he, the poor and the needy from the one who robs him?”

יְקוּמוּן עֵדֵי חָמָס, אֲשֶׁר לֹא יָדַעְתִּי יִשְׁאָלוּנִי.

yeh-koo-MOON eh-DAY khah-MAHS, ah-SHER loh yah-DAH-tee yeesh-ah-LOO-nee.

Malicious witnesses rise up; they question me about things I do not know.

יְשַׁלְּמוּנִי רָעָה תַּחַת טוֹבָה שְׁכוֹל לְנַפְשִׁי.

yeh-shahl-MOO-nee rah-AH TAH-khaht toh-VAH, sheh-KHOHL leh-nahf-SHEE.

They repay me evil for good, bereavement for my soul.

וַאֲנִי בַּחֲלוֹתָם לְבוּשִׁי שָׂק עִנֵּיתִי בַצּוֹם נַפְשִׁי, וּתְפִלָּתִי עַל חֵיקִי תָשׁוּב.

vah-ah-NEE bah-khah-loh-TAHM leh-voo-SHEE SAHK, ee-NAY-tee vah-TZOHM nahf-SHEE, oot-fee-lah-TEE ahl khay-KEE tah-SHOOV.

But when they were ill, I wore sackcloth and afflicted my soul with fasting; may my prayer return to my own bosom.

כְּרֵעַ כְּאָח לִי הִתְהַלָּכְתִּי, כַּאֲבֶל אֵם קֹדֵר שַׁחוֹתִי.

keh-REH-ah keh-AHKH LEE heet-hah-LAHKH-tee, kah-ah-VEL EM koh-DER shah-KHOH-tee.

I went about as though for a friend or a brother; I bowed down in mourning, as one who mourns a mother.

וּבְצַלְעִי שָׂמְחוּ וְנֶאֱסָפוּ נֶאֶסְפוּ עָלַי נֵכִים וְלֹא יָדַעְתִּי, קָרְעוּ וְלֹא דָמּוּ.

oov-tzahl-EE sahm-KHOO veh-neh-eh-SAH-foo, neh-es-FOO ah-LIGH neh-KHEEM veh-LOH yah-DAH-tee, kahr-OO veh-LOH DAH-moo.

But when I stumbled they rejoiced and gathered; the lowly gathered against me, and I did not know why; they tore at me without ceasing.

בְּחַנְפֵי לַעֲגֵי מָעוֹג, חָרֹק עָלַי שִׁנֵּימוֹ.

beh-khahn-FAY lah-ah-GAY mah-OHG, khah-ROHK ah-LIGH shee-NAY-moh.

Like godless mockers at a feast, they gnashed their teeth at me.

אֲדֹנָי כַּמָּה תִּרְאֶה הָשִׁיבָה נַפְשִׁי מִשֹּׁאֵיהֶם, מִכְּפִירִים יְחִידָתִי.

ah-doh-NIGH kah-MAH teer-EH, hah-SHEE-vah nahf-SHEE mee-shoh-ay-HEM, mee-keh-fee-REEM yeh-khee-dah-TEE.

Lord, how long will You look on? Rescue my soul from their ravages, my only life from the lions.

אוֹדְךָ בְּקָהָל רָב, בְּעַם עָצוּם אֲהַלְלֶךָּ.

ohd-KHAH beh-kah-HAHL RAHV, beh-AHM ah-TZOOM ah-hah-leh-LEK-kah.

I will thank You in the great congregation; I will praise You among a mighty people.

אַל יִשְׂמְחוּ לִי אֹיְבַי שֶׁקֶר, שֹׂנְאַי חִנָּם יִקְרְצוּ עָיִן.

ahl yees-meh-KHOO LEE oy-VIGH SHEH-ker, sohn-IGH khee-NAHM yeek-reh-TZOO AH-yeen.

Let not those who are my enemies falsely rejoice over me, nor those who hate me without cause wink their eye.

כִּי לֹא שָׁלוֹם יְדַבֵּרוּ וְעַל רִגְעֵי אֶרֶץ, דִּבְרֵי מִרְמוֹת יַחֲשֹׁבוּן.

kee loh shah-LOHM yeh-dah-beh-ROO, veh-AHL reeg-AY EH-retz deev-RAY meer-MOHT yah-khah-shoh-VOON.

For they do not speak peace; they devise deceitful words against the quiet ones of the land.

וַיַּרְחִיבוּ עָלַי פִּיהֶם אָמְרוּ הֶאָח הֶאָח, רָאֲתָה עֵינֵינוּ.

vah-yahr-KHEE-voo ah-LIGH pee-HEM, ahm-ROO heh-AHKH heh-AHKH, rah-ah-TAH ay-NAY-noo.

They open their mouths wide against me and say, “Aha, aha, our eyes have seen it!”

רָאִיתָה יְהֹוָה אַל תֶּחֱרַשׁ, אֲדֹנָי אֲל תִּרְחַק מִמֶּנִּי.

rah-EE-tah ah-doh-NIGH ahl teh-kheh-RAHSH, ah-doh-NIGH ahl teer-KHAHK mee-MEH-nee.

You have seen, Lord; do not be silent; Lord, do not be far from me.

הָעִירָה וְהָקִיצָה לְמִשְׁפָּטִי, אֱלֹהַי וַאדֹנָי לְרִיבִי.

hah-EE-rah veh-hah-KEE-tzah leh-meesh-pah-TEE, eh-loh-HIGH vah-doh-NIGH leh-ree-VEE.

Awake and rouse Yourself for my judgment, my God and my Lord, for my cause.

שָׁפְטֵנִי כְצִדְקְךָ יְהֹוָה אֱלֹהָי וְאַל יִשְׂמְחוּ לִי.

shohf-TEH-nee kheh-tzeed-keh-KHAH ah-doh-NIGH eh-loh-HIGH, veh-AHL yees-meh-KHOO LEE.

Judge me according to Your righteousness, Lord my God, and let them not rejoice over me.

אַל יֹאמְרוּ בְלִבָּם הֶאָח נַפְשֵׁנוּ, אַל יֹאמְרוּ בִּלַּעֲנוּהוּ.

ahl yoh-meh-ROO veh-lee-BAHM heh-AHKH nahf-SHEH-noo, ahl yoh-meh-ROO bee-lah-ah-NOO-hoo.

Let them not say in their hearts, “Aha, just as we wished!” Let them not say, “We have swallowed him up.”

יֵבֹשׁוּ וְיַחְפְּרוּ יַחְדָּו שְׂמֵחֵי רָעָתִי יִלְבְּשׁוּ בֹשֶׁת וּכְלִמָּה, הַמַּגְדִּילִים עָלָי.

yeh-VOH-shoo veh-yahkh-peh-ROO yahkh-DAHV seh-meh-KHAY rah-ah-TEE, yeel-beh-SHOO VOH-shet ookh-lee-MAH hah-mahg-dee-LEEM ah-LIGH.

Let those who rejoice at my harm be ashamed and confounded together; let those who exalt themselves over me be clothed with shame and disgrace.

יָרֹנּוּ וְיִשְׂמְחוּ חֲפֵצֵי צִדְקִי וְיֹאמְרוּ תָמִיד יִגְדַּל יְהֹוָה, הֶחָפֵץ שְׁלוֹם עַבְדּוֹ.

yah-ROH-noo veh-yees-meh-KHOO khah-feh-TZAY tzeed-KEE, veh-yoh-meh-ROO tah-MEED yeeg-DAHL ah-doh-NIGH, heh-khah-FETZ sheh-LOHM ahv-DOH.

Let those who desire my vindication sing for joy and be glad; let them always say, “Great is the Lord, who desires the peace of His servant.”

וּלְשׁוֹנִי תֶּהְגֶּה צִדְקֶךָ, כָּל הַיּוֹם תְּהִלָּתֶךָ.

ool-shoh-NEE teh-GEH tzeed-KEH-khah, kohl hah-YOHM teh-hee-lah-TEH-khah.

My tongue will speak of Your righteousness and Your praise all day long.

Said for: Annulling Harsh Decrees.

לַמְנַצֵּחַ לְעֶבֶד יְהֹוָה לְדָוִד.

lahm-nah-TZEH-ahkh leh-EH-ved ah-doh-NIGH leh-dah-VEED.

For the conductor, of the servant of the Lord, of David.

נְאֻם פֶּשַׁע לָרָשָׁע בְּקֶרֶב לִבִּי, אֵין פַּחַד אֱלֹהִים לְנֶגֶד עֵינָיו.

neh-OOM PEH-shah lah-rah-SHAH beh-KEH-rev lee-BEE, AYN PAH-khahd eh-loh-HEEM leh-NEH-ged ay-NAHV.

Transgression speaks to the wicked within his heart; there is no fear of God before his eyes.

כִּי הֶחֱלִיק אֵלָיו בְּעֵינָיו, לִמְצֹא עֲוֺנוֹ לִשְׂנֹא.

kee heh-kheh-LEEK eh-LAHV beh-ay-NAHV, leem-TZOH ah-voh-NOH lees-NOH.

For he flatters himself in his own eyes that his iniquity will not be found out and hated.

דִּבְרֵי פִיו אָוֶן וּמִרְמָה, חָדַל לְהַשְׂכִּיל לְהֵיטִיב.

deev-RAY FEEV AH-ven oo-meer-MAH, khah-DAHL leh-hahs-KEEL leh-hay-TEEV.

The words of his mouth are iniquity and deceit; he has ceased to be wise and to do good.

אָוֶן יַחְשֹׁב עַל מִשְׁכָּבוֹ יִתְיַצֵּב עַל דֶּרֶךְ לֹא טוֹב, רָע לֹא יִמְאָס.

AH-ven yahkh-SHOHV ahl meesh-kah-VOH, yeet-yah-TZEV ahl DEH-rekh loh TOHV, RAH loh yeem-AHS.

He plots iniquity on his bed; he sets himself on a path that is not good; he does not reject evil.

יְהֹוָה בְּהַשָּׁמַיִם חַסְדֶּךָ, אֱמוּנָתְךָ עַד שְׁחָקִים.

ah-doh-NIGH beh-hah-shah-MAH-yeem khahs-DEH-khah, eh-moo-naht-KHAH ahd sheh-khah-KEEM.

Lord, Your kindness reaches to the heavens, Your faithfulness to the skies.

צִדְקָתְךָ כְּהַרְרֵי אֵל מִשְׁפָּטֶךָ תְּהוֹם רַבָּה, אָדָם וּבְהֵמָה תוֹשִׁיעַ יְהֹוָה.

tzeed-kaht-KHAH keh-hahr-RAY EL meesh-pah-TEH-khah teh-HOHM rah-BAH, ah-DAHM oo-veh-heh-MAH toh-SHEE-ah ah-doh-NIGH.

Your righteousness is like the mighty mountains, Your judgments like the great deep; man and beast You save, Lord.

מַה יָּקָר חַסְדְּךָ אֱלֹהִים וּבְנֵי אָדָם, בְּצֵל כְּנָפֶיךָ יֶחֱסָיוּן.

mah yah-KAHR khahs-deh-KHAH eh-loh-HEEM oov-NAY ah-DAHM, beh-TZEL keh-nah-feh-KHAH yeh-kheh-sah-YOON.

How precious is Your kindness, God! The children of man take refuge in the shadow of Your wings.

יִרְוְיֻן מִדֶּשֶׁן בֵּיתֶךָ, וְנַחַל עֲדָנֶיךָ תַשְׁקֵם.

yeer-veh-YOON mee-DEH-shen bay-teh-KHAH, veh-NAH-khahl ah-dah-neh-KHAH tahsh-KEM.

They are sated from the abundance of Your House, and You give them to drink from the stream of Your delights.

כִּי עִמְּךָ מְקוֹר חַיִּים, בְּאוֹרְךָ נִרְאֶה אוֹר.

kee ee-meh-KHAH meh-KOHR khah-YEEM, beh-oh-reh-KHAH neer-EH ohr.

For with You is the source of life; in Your light we see light.

מְשֹׁךְ חַסְדְּךָ לְיֹדְעֶיךָ, וְצִדְקָתְךָ לְיִשְׁרֵי לֵב.

meh-SHOHKH khahs-deh-KHAH leh-yohd-eh-KHAH, veh-tzeed-kaht-KHAH leh-yeesh-RAY layv.

Extend Your kindness to those who know You, and Your righteousness to the upright of heart.

אַל תְּבוֹאֵנִי רֶגֶל גַּאֲוָה, וְיַד רְשָׁעִים אַל תְּנִדֵנִי.

ahl teh-voh-EH-nee REH-gel gah-ah-VAH, veh-YAHD reh-shah-EEM ahl teh-nee-DEH-nee.

Let not the foot of the proud come against me, nor the hand of the wicked drive me away.

שָׁם נָפְלוּ פֹּעֲלֵי אָוֶן, דֹּחוּ וְלֹא יָכְלוּ קוּם.

SHAHM nahf-LOO poh-ah-LAY AH-ven, DOH-khoo veh-LOH yahkh-LOO KOOM.

There the evildoers have fallen; they are thrust down and cannot rise.

Said for: Finding a Spouse.

מִזְמוֹר לְדָוִד לְהַזְכִּיר.

meez-MOHR leh-dah-VEED leh-hahz-KEER.

A psalm of David, for remembrance.

יְהֹוָה אַל בְּקֶצְפְּךָ תוֹכִיחֵנִי, וּבַחֲמָתְךָ תְיַסְּרֵנִי.

ah-doh-NIGH ahl beh-ketz-peh-KHAH toh-khee-KHEH-nee, oo-vah-khah-maht-KHAH teh-yahs-REH-nee.

Lord, do not rebuke me in Your anger, nor chastise me in Your wrath.

כִּי חִצֶּיךָ נִחֲתוּ בִי, וַתִּנְחַת עָלַי יָדֶךָ.

kee khee-TZEH-khah nee-khah-TOO VEE, vah-teen-KHAHT ah-LIGH yah-DEH-khah.

For Your arrows have sunk into me, and Your hand has come down upon me.

אֵין מְתֹם בִּבְשָׂרִי מִפְּנֵי זַעְמֶךָ, אֵין שָׁלוֹם בַּעֲצָמַי מִפְּנֵי חַטָּאתִי.

AYN meh-TOHM beev-sah-REE mee-peh-NAY zah-MEH-khah, AYN shah-LOHM bah-ah-tzah-MIGH mee-peh-NAY khah-tah-TEE.

There is no soundness in my flesh because of Your anger; there is no peace in my bones because of my sin.

כִּי עֲוֹנֹתַי עָבְרוּ רֹאשִׁי, כְּמַשָּׂא כָבֵד יִכְבְּדוּ מִמֶּנִּי.

kee ah-voh-noh-TIGH ahv-ROO roh-SHEE, keh-mah-SAH khah-VED yeekh-beh-DOO mee-MEH-nee.

For my iniquities have gone over my head; like a heavy burden they are too heavy for me.

הִבְאִישׁוּ נָמַקּוּ חַבּוּרֹתָי, מִפְּנֵי אִוַּלְתִּי.

heev-EE-shoo nah-MAH-koo khah-boo-roh-TIGH, mee-peh-NAY ee-vahl-TEE.

My wounds fester and rot because of my folly.

נַעֲוֵיתִי שַׁחֹתִי עַד מְאֹד, כָּל הַיּוֹם קֹדֵר הִלָּכְתִּי.

nah-ah-VAY-tee shah-KHOH-tee ahd meh-OHD, kohl hah-YOHM koh-DER hee-LAHKH-tee.

I am bent and bowed down greatly; all day I go about in gloom.

כִּי כְסָלַי מָלְאוּ נִקְלֶה, וְאֵין מְתֹם בִּבְשָׂרִי.

kee kheh-sah-LIGH mahl-OO neek-LEH, veh-AYN meh-TOHM beev-sah-REE.

For my loins are filled with burning, and there is no soundness in my flesh.

נְפוּגוֹתִי וְנִדְכֵּיתִי עַד מְאֹד, שָׁאַגְתִּי מִנַּהֲמַת לִבִּי.

neh-foo-GOH-tee veh-need-KAY-tee ahd meh-OHD, shah-AHG-tee mee-nah-hah-MAHT lee-BEE.

I am numb and utterly crushed; I groan from the turmoil of my heart.

אֲדֹנָי נֶגְדְּךָ כָל תַּאֲוָתִי, וְאַנְחָתִי מִמְּךָ לֹא נִסְתָּרָה.

ah-doh-NIGH neg-deh-KHAH khohl tah-ah-vah-TEE, veh-ahn-khah-TEE mee-meh-KHAH loh nees-TAH-rah.

Lord, all my longing is before You, and my sighing is not hidden from You.

לִבִּי סְחַרְחַר עֲזָבַנִי כֹחִי, וְאוֹר עֵינַי גַּם הֵם אֵין אִתִּי.

lee-BEE seh-khahr-KHAHR ah-zah-VAH-nee khoh-KHEE, veh-OHR ay-NIGH gahm HEM AYN ee-TEE.

My heart pounds, my strength has left me, and even the light of my eyes is gone from me.

אֹהֲבַי וְרֵעַי מִנֶּגֶד נִגְעִי יַעֲמֹדוּ, וּקְרוֹבַי מֵרָחֹק עָמָדוּ.

oh-hah-VIGH veh-reh-IGH mee-NEH-ged neeg-EE yah-ah-MOH-doo, ook-roh-VIGH meh-rah-KHOHK ah-MAH-doo.

My loved ones and friends stand aloof from my affliction, and my relatives stand far off.

וַיְנַקְשׁוּ מְבַקְשֵׁי נַפְשִׁי, וְדֹרְשֵׁי רָעָתִי דִּבְּרוּ הַוּוֹת, וּמִרְמוֹת כָּל הַיּוֹם יֶהְגּוּ.

vah-yeh-nahk-SHOO meh-vahk-SHAY nahf-SHEE, veh-dohr-SHAY rah-ah-TEE dee-beh-ROO hah-VOHT, oo-meer-MOHT kohl hah-YOHM yeh-GOO.

Those who seek my life lay snares; those who seek my harm speak of ruin and plot deceit all day.

וַאֲנִי כְחֵרֵשׁ לֹא אֶשְׁמָע, וּכְאִלֵּם לֹא יִפְתַּח פִּיו.

vah-ah-NEE kheh-kheh-RESH loh esh-MAH, ookh-ee-LEM loh yeef-TAKH PEEV.

But I am like the deaf, I do not hear, like the mute who does not open his mouth.

וָאֱהִי כְּאִישׁ אֲשֶׁר לֹא שֹׁמֵעַ, וְאֵין בְּפִיו תּוֹכָחוֹת.

vah-eh-HEE keh-EESH ah-SHER loh shoh-MEH-ah, veh-AYN beh-FEEV toh-khah-KHOHT.

I have become like a man who does not hear, in whose mouth there are no rebukes.

כִּי לְךָ יְהֹוָה הוֹחָלְתִּי, אַתָּה תַעֲנֶה אֲדֹנָי אֱלֹהָי.

kee leh-KHAH ah-doh-NIGH hoh-KHAHL-tee, ah-TAH tah-ah-NEH ah-doh-NIGH eh-loh-HIGH.

For in You, Lord, I hope; You will answer, Lord my God.

כִּי אָמַרְתִּי פֶּן יִשְׂמְחוּ לִי, בְּמוֹט רַגְלִי עָלַי הִגְדִּילוּ.

kee ah-MAHR-tee pen yees-meh-KHOO LEE, beh-MOHT rahg-LEE ah-LIGH heeg-DEE-loo.

For I said, “Lest they rejoice over me; when my foot slips they boast against me.”

כִּי אֲנִי לְצֶלַע נָכוֹן, וּמַכְאוֹבִי נֶגְדִּי תָמִיד.

kee ah-NEE leh-TZEH-lah nah-KHOHN, oo-mahkh-oh-VEE neg-DEE tah-MEED.

For I am ready to fall, and my pain is always before me.

כִּי עֲוֹנִי אַגִּיד, אֶדְאַג מֵחַטָּאתִי.

kee ah-voh-NEE ah-GEED, ed-AHG meh-khah-tah-TEE.

For I confess my iniquity; I am troubled by my sin.

וְאֹיְבַי חַיִּים עָצֵמוּ, וְרַבּוּ שֹׂנְאַי שָׁקֶר.

veh-oy-VIGH khah-YEEM ah-TZEH-moo, veh-RAH-boo sohn-IGH SHAH-ker.

My enemies are vigorous and strong, and those who hate me without cause are many.

וּמְשַׁלְּמֵי רָעָה תַּחַת טוֹבָה, יִשְׂטְנוּנִי תַּחַת רָדְפִי טוֹב.

oom-shahl-MAY rah-AH TAH-khaht toh-VAH, yees-teh-NOO-nee TAH-khaht rohd-FEE TOHV.

Those who repay evil for good oppose me because I pursue what is good.

אַל תַּעַזְבֵנִי יְהֹוָה, אֱלֹהַי אַל תִּרְחַק מִמֶּנִּי.

ahl tah-ahz-VEH-nee ah-doh-NIGH, eh-loh-HIGH ahl teer-KHAHK mee-MEH-nee.

Do not forsake me, Lord; my God, do not be far from me.

חוּשָׁה לְעֶזְרָתִי, אֲדֹנָי תְּשׁוּעָתִי.

KHOO-shah leh-ez-rah-TEE, ah-doh-NIGH teh-shoo-ah-TEE.

Hurry to help me, Lord, my salvation.

Psalm 41Also in Tikkun HaKlali→
Psalm 42Also in Tikkun HaKlali→

Said for: Success in an Endeavor.

לַמְנַצֵּחַ אַל תַּשְׁחֵת לְדָוִד מִכְתָּם, בְּבָרְחוֹ מִפְּנֵי שָׁאוּל בַּמְּעָרָה.

lahm-nah-TZEH-ahkh ahl tahsh-KHET leh-dah-VEED meekh-TAHM, beh-vohr-KHOH mee-peh-NAY shah-OOL bah-meh-ah-RAH.

For the conductor, “Do not destroy”; of David, a michtam, when he fled from Saul into the cave.

חָנֵּנִי אֱלֹהִים חָנֵּנִי, כִּי בְךָ חָסָיָה נַפְשִׁי, וּבְצֵל כְּנָפֶיךָ אֶחְסֶה עַד יַעֲבֹר הַוּוֹת.

khah-NEH-nee eh-loh-HEEM khah-NEH-nee, kee veh-KHAH khah-SAH-yah nahf-SHEE, oov-TZEL keh-nah-FEH-khah ekh-SEH ahd yah-ah-VOHR hah-VOHT.

Be gracious to me, God, be gracious to me, for in You my soul takes refuge; in the shadow of Your wings I will take refuge until the storms pass.

אֶקְרָא לֵאלֹהִים עֶלְיוֹן, לָאֵל גֹּמֵר עָלָי.

ek-RAH leh-loh-HEEM el-YOHN, lah-EL goh-MER ah-LIGH.

I call to God Most High, to God who completes things for me.

יִשְׁלַח מִשָּׁמַיִם וְיוֹשִׁיעֵנִי, חֵרֵף שֹׁאֲפִי סֶלָה, יִשְׁלַח אֱלֹהִים חַסְדּוֹ וַאֲמִתּוֹ.

yeesh-LAKH mee-shah-MAH-yeem veh-yoh-shee-EH-nee, kheh-REF shoh-ah-FEE SEH-lah, yeesh-LAKH eh-loh-HEEM khahs-DOH vah-ah-mee-TOH.

He will send from heaven and save me, though the one who would swallow me taunts, Selah; God will send His kindness and His truth.

נַפְשִׁי בְּתוֹךְ לְבָאִם אֶשְׁכְּבָה לֹהֲטִים, בְּנֵי אָדָם שִׁנֵּיהֶם חֲנִית וְחִצִּים, וּלְשׁוֹנָם חֶרֶב חַדָּה.

nahf-SHEE beh-TOHKH leh-vah-EEM esh-keh-VAH loh-hah-TEEM, beh-NAY ah-DAHM shee-nay-HEM khah-NEET veh-khee-TZEEM, ool-shoh-NAHM KHEH-rev khah-DAH.

My soul is among lions; I lie among those who breathe fire, men whose teeth are spears and arrows and whose tongue is a sharp sword.

רוּמָה עַל הַשָּׁמַיִם אֱלֹהִים, עַל כָּל הָאָרֶץ כְּבוֹדֶךָ.

ROO-mah ahl hah-shah-MAH-yeem eh-loh-HEEM, ahl kohl hah-AH-retz keh-voh-DEH-khah.

Be exalted above the heavens, God; let Your glory be over all the earth.

רֶשֶׁת הֵכִינוּ לִפְעָמַי כָּפַף נַפְשִׁי, כָּרוּ לְפָנַי שִׁיחָה, נָפְלוּ בְתוֹכָהּ סֶלָה.

REH-shet heh-KHEE-noo leef-ah-MIGH kah-FAHF nahf-SHEE, KAH-roo leh-fah-NIGH shee-KHAH, nahf-LOO veh-toh-KHAH SEH-lah.

They set a net for my steps; my soul was bowed down; they dug a pit before me, but fell into it themselves, Selah.

נָכוֹן לִבִּי אֱלֹהִים נָכוֹן לִבִּי, אָשִׁירָה וַאֲזַמֵּרָה.

nah-KHOHN lee-BEE eh-loh-HEEM nah-KHOHN lee-BEE, ah-SHEE-rah vah-ah-zah-MEH-rah.

My heart is steadfast, God, my heart is steadfast; I will sing and make music.

עוּרָה כְבוֹדִי, עוּרָה הַנֵּבֶל וְכִנּוֹר, אָעִירָה שָּׁחַר.

OO-rah kheh-voh-DEE, OO-rah hah-NEH-vel veh-khee-NOHR, ah-EE-rah SHAH-khar.

Awake, my glory! Awake, harp and lyre! I will awaken the dawn.

אוֹדְךָ בָעַמִּים אֲדֹנָי, אֲזַמֶּרְךָ בַּל אֻמִּים.

ohd-KHAH vah-ah-MEEM ah-doh-NIGH, ah-zah-mer-KHAH bahl oo-MEEM.

I will thank You among the peoples, Lord; I will sing praise to You among the nations.

כִּי גָדֹל עַד שָׁמַיִם חַסְדֶּךָ, וְעַד שְׁחָקִים אֲמִתֶּךָ.

kee gah-DOHL ahd shah-MAH-yeem khahs-DEH-khah, veh-AHD sheh-khah-KEEM ah-mee-TEH-khah.

For Your kindness is great, reaching to the heavens, and Your truth to the skies.

רוּמָה עַל שָׁמַיִם אֱלֹהִים, עַל כָּל הָאָרֶץ כְּבוֹדֶךָ.

ROO-mah ahl shah-MAH-yeem eh-loh-HEEM, ahl kohl hah-AH-retz keh-voh-DEH-khah.

Be exalted above the heavens, God; let Your glory be over all the earth.

Psalm 59Also in Tikkun HaKlali→

Said for: Victory.

לַמְנַצֵּחַ עַל שׁוּשַׁן עֵדוּת, מִכְתָּם לְדָוִד לְלַמֵּד.

lahm-nah-TZEH-ahkh ahl shoo-SHAHN eh-DOOT, meekh-TAHM leh-dah-VEED leh-lah-MED.

For the conductor, on the shushan of testimony; a michtam of David, to teach,

בְּהַצּוֹתוֹ אֶת אֲרַם נַהֲרַיִם וְאֶת אֲרַם צוֹבָה וַיָּשָׁב יוֹאָב וַיַּךְ אֶת אֱדוֹם בְּגֵיא מֶלַח, שְׁנֵים עָשָׂר אָלֶף.

beh-hah-tzoh-TOH eht ah-RAHM nah-hah-RAH-yeem veh-ET ah-RAHM tzoh-VAH, vah-yah-SHAHV yoh-AHV vah-YAHKH eht eh-DOHM beh-GAY MEH-lahkh, sheh-NAYM ah-SAHR AH-lef.

when he fought Aram-naharaim and Aram-zobah, and Joab returned and struck down twelve thousand of Edom in the Valley of Salt.

אֱלֹהִים זְנַחְתָּנוּ פְרַצְתָּנוּ, אָנַפְתָּ תְּשׁוֹבֵב לָנוּ.

eh-loh-HEEM zeh-nahkh-TAH-noo feh-rahtz-TAH-noo, ah-NAHF-tah teh-shoh-VEV LAH-noo.

God, You rejected us and broke through our defenses; You were angry; now restore us.

הִרְעַשְׁתָּה אֶרֶץ פְּצַמְתָּהּ, רְפָה שְׁבָרֶיהָ כִי מָטָה.

heer-AHSH-tah EH-retz peh-tzahm-TAH, reh-FAH sheh-vah-REH-hah khee MAH-tah.

You made the land quake and split it open; heal its breaches, for it is tottering.

הִרְאִיתָה עַמְּךָ קָשָׁה, הִשְׁקִיתָנוּ יַיִן תַּרְעֵלָה.

heer-EE-tah ah-meh-KHAH kah-SHAH, heesh-kee-TAH-noo YAH-yeen tahr-ay-LAH.

You have shown Your people hardship; You have made us drink wine that makes us stagger.

נָתַתָּה לִּירֵאֶיךָ נֵּס לְהִתְנוֹסֵס, מִפְּנֵי קֹשֶׁט סֶלָה.

nah-TAH-tah lee-reh-EH-khah NES leh-heet-noh-SES, mee-peh-NAY KOH-shet SEH-lah.

You have given those who fear You a banner to rally around, for the sake of truth, Selah.

לְמַעַן יֵחָלְצוּן יְדִידֶיךָ, הוֹשִׁיעָה יְמִינְךָ וַעֲנֵנִי.

leh-MAH-ahn yeh-khahl-TZOON yeh-dee-DEH-khah, hoh-SHEE-ah yeh-meen-KHAH vah-ah-NEH-nee.

So that Your beloved ones may be rescued, save with Your right hand and answer me.

אֱלֹהִים דִּבֶּר בְּקָדְשׁוֹ אֶעְלֹזָה אֲחַלְּקָה שְׁכֶם, וְעֵמֶק סֻכּוֹת אֲמַדֵּד.

eh-loh-HEEM dee-BER beh-kohd-SHOH eh-eh-loh-ZAH ah-khahl-KAH sheh-KHEM, veh-EH-mek soo-KOHT ah-mah-DED.

God has spoken in His holiness: “I will exult; I will divide Shechem and measure out the Valley of Sukkot.

לִי גִלְעָד וְלִי מְנַשֶּׁה וְאֶפְרַיִם מָעוֹז רֹאשִׁי, יְהוּדָה מְחֹקְקִי.

LEE geel-AHD veh-LEE meh-nah-SHEH veh-ef-RAH-yeem mah-OHZ roh-SHEE, yeh-hoo-DAH meh-khoh-keh-KEE.

Gilead is Mine and Manasseh is Mine; Ephraim is the stronghold of My head; Judah is My scepter.

מוֹאָב סִיר רַחְצִי עַל אֱדוֹם אַשְׁלִיךְ נַעֲלִי, עָלַי פְּלֶשֶׁת הִתְרֹעָעִי.

moh-AHV SEER rahkh-TZEE ahl eh-DOHM ahsh-LEEKH nah-ah-LEE, ah-LIGH peh-LEH-shet heet-roh-ah-EE.

Moab is My washbasin; on Edom I cast My shoe; Philistia, cry out because of Me.”

מִי יֹבִלֵנִי עִיר מָצוֹר, מִי נָחַנִי עַד אֱדוֹם.

mee yoh-vee-LEH-nee EER mah-TZOHR, mee nah-KHAH-nee ahd eh-DOHM.

Who will bring me to the fortified city? Who will lead me to Edom?

הֲלֹא אַתָּה אֱלֹהִים זְנַחְתָּנוּ, וְלֹא תֵצֵא אֱלֹהִים בְּצִבְאוֹתֵינוּ.

hah-LOH ah-TAH eh-loh-HEEM zeh-nahkh-TAH-noo, veh-LOH teh-TZEH eh-loh-HEEM beh-tzeev-oh-TAY-noo.

Is it not You, God, who rejected us, and You, God, who no longer go out with our armies?

הָבָה לָּנוּ עֶזְרָת מִצָּר, וְשָׁוְא תְּשׁוּעַת אָדָם.

HAH-vah LAH-noo ez-RAHT mee-TZAHR, veh-SHAHV teh-shoo-AHT ah-DAHM.

Give us help against the foe, for the help of man is worthless.

בֵּאלֹהִים נַעֲשֶׂה חָיִל, וְהוּא יָבוּס צָרֵינוּ.

beh-loh-HEEM nah-ah-SEH KHAH-yeel, veh-HOO yah-VOOS tzah-RAY-noo.

With God we will do valiantly, and He will trample our foes.

Psalm 67Also in Friday Night (Birkat HaMazon)→

Said for: Finding a Spouse; Defeat of Enemies and Adversaries; Victory.

לַמְנַצֵּחַ לְדָוִד לְהַזְכִּיר.

lahm-nah-TZEH-ahkh leh-dah-VEED leh-hahz-KEER.

For the conductor, of David, for remembrance.

אֱלֹהִים לְהַצִּילֵנִי, יְהֹוָה לְעֶזְרָתִי חוּשָׁה.

eh-loh-HEEM leh-hah-tzee-LEH-nee, ah-doh-NIGH leh-ez-rah-TEE KHOO-shah.

God, rescue me; Lord, hurry to help me.

יֵבֹשׁוּ וְיַחְפְּרוּ מְבַקְשֵׁי נַפְשִׁי, יִסֹּגוּ אָחוֹר וְיִכָּלְמוּ חֲפֵצֵי רָעָתִי.

yeh-VOH-shoo veh-yahkh-peh-ROO meh-vahk-SHAY nahf-SHEE, yee-SOH-goo ah-KHOHR veh-yee-kahl-MOO khah-feh-TZAY rah-ah-TEE.

Let those who seek my life be ashamed and disgraced; let those who wish me harm be turned back and humiliated.

יָשׁוּבוּ עַל עֵקֶב בָּשְׁתָּם, הָאֹמְרִים הֶאָח הֶאָח.

yah-SHOO-voo ahl EH-kev bohsh-TAHM, hah-oh-meh-REEM heh-AHKH heh-AHKH.

Let those who say “Aha, aha!” turn back because of their shame.

יָשִׂישׂוּ וְיִשְׂמְחוּ בְּךָ כָּל מְבַקְשֶׁיךָ, וְיֹאמְרוּ תָמִיד יִגְדַּל אֱלֹהִים, אֹהֲבֵי יְשׁוּעָתֶךָ.

yah-SEE-soo veh-yees-meh-KHOO beh-KHAH kohl meh-vahk-SHEH-khah, veh-yoh-meh-ROO tah-MEED yeeg-DAHL eh-loh-HEEM, oh-hah-VAY yeh-shoo-ah-TEH-khah.

Let all who seek You rejoice and be glad in You; let those who love Your salvation always say, “God is great!”

וַאֲנִי עָנִי וְאֶבְיוֹן, אֱלֹהִים חוּשָׁה לִּי, עֶזְרִי וּמְפַלְטִי אַתָּה, יְהֹוָה אַל תְּאַחַר.

vah-ah-NEE ah-NEE veh-ev-YOHN, eh-loh-HEEM KHOO-shah LEE, ez-REE oom-fahl-TEE AH-tah, ah-doh-NIGH ahl teh-ah-KHAHR.

But I am poor and needy; God, hurry to me. You are my help and my deliverer; Lord, do not delay.

Said for: Finding a Spouse.

בְּךָ יְהֹוָה חָסִיתִי, אַל אֵבוֹשָׁה לְעוֹלָם.

beh-KHAH ah-doh-NIGH khah-SEE-tee, ahl eh-VOH-shah leh-oh-LAHM.

In You, Lord, I take refuge; let me never be ashamed.

בְּצִדְקָתְךָ תַּצִּילֵנִי וּתְפַלְּטֵנִי, הַטֵּה אֵלַי אָזְנְךָ וְהוֹשִׁיעֵנִי.

beh-tzeed-kaht-KHAH tah-tzee-LEH-nee oot-fahl-TEH-nee, hah-TEH eh-LIGH ohz-neh-KHAH veh-hoh-shee-EH-nee.

In Your righteousness rescue me and deliver me; incline Your ear to me and save me.

הֱיֵה לִי לְצוּר מָעוֹן לָבוֹא תָּמִיד, צִוִּיתָ לְהוֹשִׁיעֵנִי, כִּי סַלְעִי וּמְצוּדָתִי אָתָּה.

heh-YEH LEE leh-TZOOR mah-OHN lah-VOH tah-MEED, tzee-VEE-tah leh-hoh-shee-EH-nee, kee sahl-EE oom-tzoo-dah-TEE AH-tah.

Be for me a rock of refuge to which I can always come; You have commanded to save me, for You are my rock and my fortress.

אֱלֹהַי פַּלְּטֵנִי מִיַּד רָשָׁע, מִכַּף מְעַוֵּל וְחוֹמֵץ.

eh-loh-HIGH pahl-TEH-nee mee-YAHD rah-SHAH, mee-KAHF meh-ah-VEL veh-khoh-METZ.

My God, deliver me from the hand of the wicked, from the grasp of the unjust and the cruel.

כִּי אַתָּה תִקְוָתִי, אֲדֹנָי יֱהֹוִה מִבְטַחִי מִנְּעוּרָי.

kee ah-TAH teek-vah-TEE, ah-doh-NIGH eh-loh-HEEM meev-tah-KHEE mee-neh-oo-RIGH.

For You are my hope, Lord God, my trust from my youth.

עָלֶיךָ נִסְמַכְתִּי מִבֶּטֶן, מִמְּעֵי אִמִּי אַתָּה גוֹזִי, בְּךָ תְהִלָּתִי תָמִיד.

ah-LEH-khah nees-MAHKH-tee mee-BEH-ten, mee-meh-AY ee-MEE ah-TAH goh-ZEE, beh-KHAH teh-hee-lah-TEE tah-MEED.

On You I have leaned from birth; You drew me from my mother’s womb; my praise is always of You.

כְּמוֹפֵת הָיִיתִי לְרַבִּים, וְאַתָּה מַחֲסִי עֹז.

keh-moh-FET hah-YEE-tee leh-rah-BEEM, veh-ah-TAH mah-khah-SEE OHZ.

I have become a wonder to many, but You are my strong refuge.

יִמָּלֵא פִי תְּהִלָּתֶךָ, כָּל הַיּוֹם תִּפְאַרְתֶּךָ.

yee-mah-LEH FEE teh-hee-lah-TEH-khah, kohl hah-YOHM teef-ahr-TEH-khah.

My mouth is filled with Your praise, with Your glory all day long.

אַל תַּשְׁלִיכֵנִי לְעֵת זִקְנָה, כִּכְלוֹת כֹּחִי אַל תַּעַזְבֵנִי.

ahl tahsh-lee-KHEH-nee leh-ET zeek-NAH, keekh-LOHT koh-KHEE ahl tah-ahz-VEH-nee.

Do not cast me off in old age; when my strength fails, do not forsake me.

כִּי אָמְרוּ אוֹיְבַי לִי, וְשֹׁמְרֵי נַפְשִׁי נוֹעֲצוּ יַחְדָּו.

kee ahm-ROO oy-VIGH LEE, veh-shohm-RAY nahf-SHEE noh-ah-TZOO yahkh-DAHV.

For my enemies speak against me, and those who watch for my life take counsel together,

לֵאמֹר אֱלֹהִים עֲזָבוֹ, רִדְפוּ וְתִפְשׂוּהוּ כִּי אֵין מַצִּיל.

leh-MOHR eh-loh-HEEM ah-zah-VOH, reed-FOO veh-teef-SOO-hoo kee AYN mah-TZEEL.

saying, “God has forsaken him; pursue and seize him, for there is no one to rescue him.”

אֱלֹהִים אַל תִּרְחַק מִמֶּנִּי, אֱלֹהַי לְעֶזְרָתִי חוּשָׁה.

eh-loh-HEEM ahl teer-KHAHK mee-MEH-nee, eh-loh-HIGH leh-ez-rah-TEE KHOO-shah.

God, do not be far from me; my God, hurry to help me.

יֵבֹשׁוּ יִכְלוּ שֹׂטְנֵי נַפְשִׁי, יַעֲטוּ חֶרְפָּה וּכְלִמָּה מְבַקְשֵׁי רָעָתִי.

yeh-VOH-shoo yeekh-LOO soh-teh-NAY nahf-SHEE, yah-ah-TOO kher-PAH ookh-lee-MAH meh-vahk-SHAY rah-ah-TEE.

Let my accusers be ashamed and consumed; let those who seek my harm be covered with scorn and disgrace.

וַאֲנִי תָּמִיד אֲיַחֵל, וְהוֹסַפְתִּי עַל כָּל תְּהִלָּתֶךָ.

vah-ah-NEE tah-MEED ah-yah-KHEL, veh-hoh-SAHF-tee ahl kohl teh-hee-lah-TEH-khah.

But I will hope continually and add to all Your praise.

פִּי יְסַפֵּר צִדְקָתֶךָ, כָּל הַיּוֹם תְּשׁוּעָתֶךָ, כִּי לֹא יָדַעְתִּי סְפֹרוֹת.

PEE yeh-sah-PER tzeed-kah-TEH-khah, kohl hah-YOHM teh-shoo-ah-TEH-khah, kee loh yah-DAH-tee seh-foh-ROHT.

My mouth will tell of Your righteousness and Your salvation all day, though I cannot know their number.

אָבוֹא בִּגְבֻרוֹת אֲדֹנָי יֱהֹוִה, אַזְכִּיר צִדְקָתְךָ לְבַדֶּךָ.

ah-VOH beeg-voo-ROHT ah-doh-NIGH eh-loh-HEEM, ahz-KEER tzeed-kaht-KHAH leh-vah-DEH-khah.

I will come with the mighty deeds of the Lord God; I will speak of Your righteousness, Yours alone.

אֱלֹהִים לִמַּדְתַּנִי מִנְּעוּרָי, וְעַד הֵנָּה אַגִּיד נִפְלְאוֹתֶיךָ.

eh-loh-HEEM lee-mahd-TAH-nee mee-neh-oo-RIGH, veh-AHD HEH-nah ah-GEED neef-leh-oh-TEH-khah.

God, You have taught me from my youth, and to this day I declare Your wonders.

וְגַם עַד זִקְנָה וְשֵׂיבָה אֱלֹהִים אַל תַּעַזְבֵנִי, עַד אַגִּיד זְרוֹעֲךָ לְדוֹר, לְכָל יָבוֹא גְּבוּרָתֶךָ.

veh-GAHM ahd zeek-NAH veh-say-VAH eh-loh-HEEM ahl tah-ahz-VEH-nee, ahd ah-GEED zeh-roh-ah-KHAH leh-DOHR, leh-KHOHL yah-VOH geh-voo-rah-TEH-khah.

Even to old age and gray hair, God, do not forsake me, until I declare Your strength to this generation and Your might to all who are to come.

וְצִדְקָתְךָ אֱלֹהִים עַד מָרוֹם, אֲשֶׁר עָשִׂיתָ גְדֹלוֹת, אֱלֹהִים מִי כָמוֹךָ.

veh-tzeed-kaht-KHAH eh-loh-HEEM ahd mah-ROHM, ah-SHER ah-SEE-tah geh-doh-LOHT, eh-loh-HEEM mee khah-MOH-khah.

Your righteousness, God, reaches the heights; You have done great things; God, who is like You?

אֲשֶׁר הִרְאִיתַנִי צָרוֹת רַבּוֹת וְרָעוֹת, תָּשׁוּב תְּחַיֵּינִי, וּמִתְּהֹמוֹת הָאָרֶץ תָּשׁוּב תַּעֲלֵנִי.

ah-SHER heer-ee-TAH-nee tzah-ROHT rah-BOHT veh-rah-OHT, tah-SHOOV teh-khah-YAY-nee, oo-mee-teh-hoh-MOHT hah-AH-retz tah-SHOOV tah-ah-LEH-nee.

You who have shown me many troubles and hardships will revive me again and bring me up again from the depths of the earth.

תֶּרֶב גְּדֻלָּתִי, וְתִסֹּב תְּנַחֲמֵנִי.

TEH-rev geh-doo-lah-TEE, veh-tee-SOHV teh-nah-khah-MEH-nee.

Increase my greatness, and turn and comfort me.

גַּם אֲנִי אוֹדְךָ בִכְלִי נֶבֶל אֲמִתְּךָ אֱלֹהָי, אֲזַמְּרָה לְךָ בְכִנּוֹר, קְדוֹשׁ יִשְׂרָאֵל.

gahm ah-NEE ohd-KHAH veekh-LEE NEH-vel ah-meet-KHAH eh-loh-HIGH, ah-zahm-RAH leh-KHAH veh-khee-NOHR, keh-DOHSH yees-rah-EL.

I too will thank You with the harp for Your faithfulness, my God; I will sing praise to You with the lyre, Holy One of Israel.

תְּרַנֵּנָּה שְׂפָתַי כִּי אֲזַמְּרָה לָּךְ, וְנַפְשִׁי אֲשֶׁר פָּדִיתָ.

teh-rah-NEH-nah seh-fah-TIGH kee ah-zahm-rah LAHKH, veh-nahf-SHEE ah-SHER pah-DEE-tah.

My lips will sing for joy when I make music to You, and so will my soul, which You have redeemed.

גַּם לְשׁוֹנִי כָּל הַיּוֹם תֶּהְגֶּה צִדְקָתֶךָ, כִּי בֹשׁוּ כִי חָפְרוּ מְבַקְשֵׁי רָעָתִי.

gahm leh-shoh-NEE kohl hah-YOHM teh-GEH tzeed-kah-TEH-khah, kee VOH-shoo khee khahf-ROO meh-vahk-SHAY rah-ah-TEE.

My tongue too will speak of Your righteousness all day, for those who seek my harm are ashamed and disgraced.

Said for: Annulling Harsh Decrees.

Hebrew audio

לַמְנַצֵּחַ עַל יְדוּתוּן לְאָסָף מִזְמוֹר.

lahm-nah-TZEH-ahkh ahl yeh-doo-TOON leh-ah-SAHF meez-MOHR.

For the conductor, on Jeduthun; of Asaph, a psalm.

קוֹלִי אֶל אֱלֹהִים וְאֶצְעָקָה, קוֹלִי אֶל אֱלֹהִים וְהַאֲזִין אֵלָי.

koh-LEE el eh-loh-HEEM veh-etz-ah-KAH, koh-LEE el eh-loh-HEEM veh-hah-ah-ZEEN eh-LIGH.

My voice is to God, and I cry out; my voice is to God, that He may hear me.

בְּיוֹם צָרָתִי אֲדֹנָי דָּרָשְׁתִּי יָדִי לַיְלָה נִגְּרָה וְלֹא תָפוּג, מֵאֲנָה הִנָּחֵם נַפְשִׁי.

beh-YOHM tzah-rah-TEE ah-doh-NIGH dah-RAHSH-tee, yah-DEE LIGH-lah neeg-RAH veh-LOH tah-FOOG, meh-ah-NAH hee-nah-KHEM nahf-SHEE.

On the day of my trouble I seek the Lord; at night my hand is stretched out without rest; my soul refuses to be comforted.

אֶזְכְּרָה אֱלֹהִים וְאֶהֱמָיָה, אָשִׂיחָה וְתִתְעַטֵּף רוּחִי סֶלָה.

ez-keh-RAH eh-loh-HEEM veh-eh-heh-MAH-yah, ah-SEE-khah veh-teet-ah-TEF roo-KHEE SEH-lah.

I remember God and I moan; I meditate, and my spirit grows faint, Selah.

אָחַזְתָּ שְׁמֻרוֹת עֵינָי, נִפְעַמְתִּי וְלֹא אֲדַבֵּר.

ah-KHAHZ-tah sheh-moo-ROHT ay-NIGH, neef-AHM-tee veh-LOH ah-dah-BER.

You hold my eyelids open; I am troubled and cannot speak.

חִשַּׁבְתִּי יָמִים מִקֶּדֶם, שְׁנוֹת עוֹלָמִים.

khee-SHAHV-tee yah-MEEM mee-KEH-dem, sheh-NOHT oh-lah-MEEM.

I consider the days of old, the years long past.

אֶזְכְּרָה נְגִינָתִי בַּלָּיְלָה עִם לְבָבִי אָשִׂיחָה, וַיְחַפֵּשׂ רוּחִי.

ez-keh-RAH neh-gee-nah-TEE bah-LIGH-lah, eem leh-vah-VEE ah-SEE-khah, vah-yeh-khah-PES roo-KHEE.

I remember my song in the night; I meditate with my heart, and my spirit searches:

הַלְעוֹלָמִים יִזְנַח אֲדֹנָי, וְלֹא יֹסִיף לִרְצוֹת עוֹד.

hah-leh-oh-lah-MEEM yeez-NAKH ah-doh-NIGH, veh-LOH yoh-SEEF leer-TZOHT ohd.

Will the Lord reject forever and never again show favor?

הֶאָפֵס לָנֶצַח חַסְדּוֹ, גָּמַר אֹמֶר לְדֹר וָדֹר.

heh-ah-FES lah-NEH-tzahkh khahs-DOH, gah-MAHR OH-mer leh-DOHR vah-DOHR.

Has His kindness ceased forever? Has His promise come to an end for all generations?

הֲשָׁכַח חַנּוֹת אֵל, אִם קָפַץ בְּאַף רַחֲמָיו סֶלָה.

hah-shah-KHAKH khah-NOHT EL, eem kah-FAHTZ beh-AHF rah-khah-MAHV SEH-lah.

Has God forgotten to be gracious? Has He in anger shut off His compassion? Selah.

וָאֹמַר חַלּוֹתִי הִיא, שְׁנוֹת יְמִין עֶלְיוֹן.

vah-oh-MAHR khah-LOH-tee HEE, sheh-NOHT yeh-MEEN el-YOHN.

Then I said, “This is my weakness: the changing of the right hand of the Most High.”

אֶזְכּוֹר מַעַלְלֵי יָהּ, כִּי אֶזְכְּרָה מִקֶּדֶם פִּלְאֶךָ.

ez-KOHR mah-ahl-LAY YAH, kee ez-keh-RAH mee-KEH-dem peel-EH-khah.

I will remember the deeds of the Lord; I will remember Your wonders of old.

וְהָגִיתִי בְכָל פָּעֳלֶךָ, וּבַעֲלִילוֹתֶיךָ אָשִׂיחָה.

veh-hah-GEE-tee veh-KHOHL pah-oh-LEH-khah, oo-vah-ah-lee-loh-TEH-khah ah-SEE-khah.

I will meditate on all Your works and consider all Your deeds.

אֱלֹהִים בַּקֹּדֶשׁ דַּרְכֶּךָ, מִי אֵל גָּדוֹל כֵּאלֹהִים.

eh-loh-HEEM bah-KOH-desh dahr-KEH-khah, mee EL gah-DOHL keh-loh-HEEM.

God, Your way is in holiness; what god is as great as God?

אַתָּה הָאֵל עֹשֵׂה פֶלֶא, הוֹדַעְתָּ בָעַמִּים עֻזֶּךָ.

ah-TAH hah-EL oh-SEH FEH-leh, hoh-DAHT-tah vah-ah-MEEM oo-ZEH-khah.

You are the God who works wonders; You have made Your strength known among the peoples.

גָּאַלְתָּ בִּזְרוֹעַ עַמֶּךָ, בְּנֵי יַעֲקֹב וְיוֹסֵף סֶלָה.

gah-AHL-tah beez-ROH-ah ah-MEH-khah, beh-NAY yah-ah-KOHV veh-yoh-SEF SEH-lah.

With Your arm You redeemed Your people, the children of Jacob and Joseph, Selah.

רָאוּךָ מַּיִם אֱלֹהִים רָאוּךָ מַּיִם יָחִילוּ, אַף יִרְגְּזוּ תְהֹמוֹת.

rah-OO-khah MAH-yeem eh-loh-HEEM rah-OO-khah MAH-yeem yah-KHEE-loo, ahf yeer-geh-ZOO teh-hoh-MOHT.

The waters saw You, God; the waters saw You and trembled; the depths too were shaken.

זֹרְמוּ מַיִם עָבוֹת קוֹל נָתְנוּ שְׁחָקִים, אַף חֲצָצֶיךָ יִתְהַלָּכוּ.

zohr-MOO MAH-yeem ah-VOHT kohl nah-teh-NOO sheh-khah-KEEM, ahf khah-tzah-TZEH-khah yeet-hah-LAH-khoo.

The clouds poured down water, the skies thundered, and Your arrows flashed back and forth.

קוֹל רַעַמְךָ בַּגַּלְגַּל הֵאִירוּ בְרָקִים תֵּבֵל, רָגְזָה וַתִּרְעַשׁ הָאָרֶץ.

kohl rah-ahm-KHAH bah-gahl-GAHL heh-EE-roo veh-rah-KEEM teh-VEL, rahg-ZAH vah-teer-AHSH hah-AH-retz.

The sound of Your thunder was in the whirlwind; lightning lit up the world; the earth trembled and shook.

בַּיָּם דַּרְכֶּךָ וּשְׁבִילְךָ בְּמַיִם רַבִּים, וְעִקְּבוֹתֶיךָ לֹא נֹדָעוּ.

bah-YAHM dahr-KEH-khah oosh-veel-KHAH beh-MAH-yeem rah-BEEM, veh-ee-keh-voh-TEH-khah loh noh-DAH-oo.

Your way was through the sea, Your path through mighty waters, and Your footsteps were not seen.

נָחִיתָ כַצֹּאן עַמֶּךָ, בְּיַד מֹשֶׁה וְאַהֲרֹן.

nah-KHEE-tah khah-TZOHN ah-MEH-khah, beh-YAHD moh-SHEH veh-ah-hah-ROHN.

You led Your people like a flock by the hand of Moses and Aaron.

Said for: Defeat of Enemies and Adversaries; Victory.

שִׁיר מִזְמוֹר לְאָסָף.

sheer meez-MOHR leh-ah-SAHF.

A song, a psalm of Asaph.

אֱלֹהִים אַל דֳּמִי לָךְ, אַל תֶּחֱרַשׁ וְאַל תִּשְׁקֹט אֵל.

eh-loh-HEEM ahl doh-MEE LAHKH, ahl teh-kheh-RAHSH veh-AHL teesh-KOHT EL.

God, do not keep silent; do not be still, and do not be quiet, God.

כִּי הִנֵּה אוֹיְבֶיךָ יֶהֱמָיוּן, וּמְשַׂנְאֶיךָ נָשְׂאוּ רֹאשׁ.

kee hee-NEH oy-veh-KHAH yeh-heh-mah-YOON, oom-sahn-EH-khah nahs-OO ROHSH.

For behold, Your enemies are in uproar, and those who hate You have raised their heads.

עַל עַמְּךָ יַעֲרִימוּ סוֹד, וְיִתְיָעֲצוּ עַל צְפוּנֶיךָ.

ahl ah-meh-KHAH yah-ah-REE-moo SOHD, veh-yeet-yah-ah-TZOO ahl tzeh-foo-NEH-khah.

They plot craftily against Your people and conspire against Your treasured ones.

אָמְרוּ לְכוּ וְנַכְחִידֵם מִגּוֹי, וְלֹא יִזָּכֵר שֵׁם יִשְׂרָאֵל עוֹד.

ahm-ROO leh-KHOO veh-nahkh-khee-DEM mee-GOY, veh-LOH yee-zah-KHER SHEM yees-rah-EL ohd.

They say, “Come, let us wipe them out as a nation, so that the name of Israel will be remembered no more.”

כִּי נוֹעֲצוּ לֵב יַחְדָּו, עָלֶיךָ בְּרִית יִכְרֹתוּ.

kee noh-ah-TZOO LEV yahkh-DAHV, ah-LEH-khah beh-REET yeekh-ROH-too.

For they conspire together with one mind; against You they make a covenant:

אָהֳלֵי אֱדוֹם וְיִשְׁמְעֵאלִים מוֹאָב וְהַגְרִים.

oh-hoh-LAY eh-DOHM veh-yeesh-meh-eh-LEEM, moh-AHV veh-hahg-REEM.

the tents of Edom and the Ishmaelites, Moab and the Hagrites,

גְּבָל וְעַמּוֹן וַעֲמָלֵק, פְּלֶשֶׁת עִם יֹשְׁבֵי צוֹר.

geh-VAHL veh-ah-MOHN vah-ah-mah-LEK, peh-LEH-shet eem yohsh-VAY TZOHR.

Gebal, Ammon and Amalek, Philistia with the inhabitants of Tyre.

גַּם אַשּׁוּר נִלְוָה עִמָּם, הָיוּ זְרוֹעַ לִבְנֵי לוֹט סֶלָה.

gahm ah-SHOOR neel-VAH ee-MAHM, HAH-yoo zeh-ROH-ah leev-NAY LOHT SEH-lah.

Assyria too has joined them; they have become an arm for the children of Lot, Selah.

עֲשֵׂה לָהֶם כְּמִדְיָן, כְּסִיסְרָא כְיָבִין בְּנַחַל קִישׁוֹן.

ah-SEH lah-HEM keh-meed-YAHN, keh-sees-RAH kheh-yah-VEEN beh-NAH-khahl kee-SHOHN.

Do to them as You did to Midian, to Sisera and Jabin at the Wadi Kishon,

נִשְׁמְדוּ בְעֵין דֹּאר, הָיוּ דֹּמֶן לָאֲדָמָה.

neesh-meh-DOO veh-AYN DOHR, HAH-yoo DOH-men lah-ah-dah-MAH.

who were destroyed at Ein-dor and became dung for the ground.

שִׁיתֵמוֹ נְדִיבֵמוֹ כְּעֹרֵב וְכִזְאֵב, וּכְזֶבַח וּכְצַלְמֻנָּע כָּל נְסִיכֵמוֹ.

shee-TAY-moh neh-dee-VAY-moh keh-oh-REV veh-kheez-EV, ookh-ZEH-vahkh ookh-tzahl-moo-NAH kohl neh-see-KHAY-moh.

Make their nobles like Oreb and Zeeb, and all their princes like Zebah and Zalmunna,

אֲשֶׁר אָמְרוּ נִירֲשָׁה לָּנוּ, אֵת נְאוֹת אֱלֹהִים.

ah-SHER ahm-ROO nee-reh-SHAH LAH-noo, ET neh-OHT eh-loh-HEEM.

who said, “Let us take possession of the pastures of God for ourselves.”

אֱלֹהַי שִׁיתֵמוֹ כַגַּלְגַּל, כְּקַשׁ לִפְנֵי רוּחַ.

eh-loh-HIGH shee-TAY-moh khah-gahl-GAHL, keh-KAHSH leef-NAY ROO-ahkh.

My God, make them like whirling dust, like chaff before the wind.

כְּאֵשׁ תִּבְעַר יָעַר, וּכְלֶהָבָה תְּלַהֵט הָרִים.

keh-ESH teev-AHR YAH-ahr, ookh-leh-hah-VAH teh-lah-HET hah-REEM.

As fire burns a forest, as a flame sets the mountains ablaze,

כֵּן תִּרְדְּפֵם בְּסַעֲרֶךָ, וּבְסוּפָתְךָ תְבַהֲלֵם.

KEN teer-deh-FEM beh-sah-ah-REH-khah, oov-soo-faht-KHAH teh-vah-hah-LEM.

so pursue them with Your storm and terrify them with Your tempest.

מַלֵּא פְנֵיהֶם קָלוֹן, וִיבַקְשׁוּ שִׁמְךָ יְהֹוָה.

mah-LEH feh-nay-HEM kah-LOHN, vee-vahk-SHOO sheem-KHAH ah-doh-NIGH.

Fill their faces with shame, so that they will seek Your name, Lord.

יֵבֹשׁוּ וְיִבָּהֲלוּ עֲדֵי עַד וְיַחְפְּרוּ וְיֹאבֵדוּ.

yeh-VOH-shoo veh-yee-bah-hah-LOO ah-DAY AHD, veh-yahkh-peh-ROO veh-yoh-VEH-doo.

Let them be ashamed and terrified forever; let them be disgraced and perish,

וְיֵדְעוּ כִּי אַתָּה שִׁמְךָ יְהֹוָה לְבַדֶּךָ, עֶלְיוֹן עַל כָּל הָאָרֶץ.

veh-yed-OO kee ah-TAH sheem-KHAH ah-doh-NIGH leh-vah-DEH-khah, el-YOHN ahl kohl hah-AH-retz.

so that they may know that You alone, whose name is the Lord, are Most High over all the earth.

Said for: Success in an Endeavor.

The prayer of Moses. Recited on Shabbat and festival mornings and customarily at a funeral or house of mourning.

תְּפִלָּה לְמֹשֶׁה אִישׁ הָאֱלֹהִים, אֲדֹנָי מָעוֹן אַתָּה הָיִיתָ לָּנוּ בְּדֹר וָדֹר.

teh-fee-LAH leh-moh-SHEH eesh hah-eh-loh-HEEM, ah-doh-NIGH mah-OHN ah-TAH hah-YEE-tah LAH-noo beh-DOHR vah-DOHR.

A prayer of Moses, the man of God. Lord, You have been our dwelling place in every generation.

בְּטֶרֶם הָרִים יֻלָּדוּ, וַתְּחוֹלֵל אֶרֶץ וְתֵבֵל, וּמֵעוֹלָם עַד עוֹלָם אַתָּה אֵל.

beh-TEH-rem hah-REEM yoo-LAH-doo, vah-teh-khoh-LEL EH-retz veh-teh-VEL, oo-meh-oh-LAHM ahd oh-LAHM ah-TAH EL.

Before the mountains were born, before You formed the earth and the world, from eternity to eternity You are God.

תָּשֵׁב אֱנוֹשׁ עַד דַּכָּא, וַתֹּאמֶר שׁוּבוּ בְנֵי אָדָם.

tah-SHEV eh-NOHSH ahd dah-KAH, vah-TOH-mer SHOO-voo veh-NAY ah-DAHM.

You return man to dust, and say: Return, children of man.

כִּי אֶלֶף שָׁנִים בְּעֵינֶיךָ כְּיוֹם אֶתְמוֹל כִּי יַעֲבֹר, וְאַשְׁמוּרָה בַלָּיְלָה.

kee EH-lef shah-NEEM beh-ay-NEH-khah keh-YOHM et-MOHL kee yah-ah-VOHR, veh-ahsh-moo-RAH vah-LIGH-lah.

For a thousand years in Your eyes are like yesterday when it has passed, like a watch in the night.

זְרַמְתָּם שֵׁנָה יִהְיוּ, בַּבֹּקֶר כֶּחָצִיר יַחֲלֹף.

zeh-rahm-TAHM sheh-NAH yeeh-YOO, bah-BOH-ker keh-khah-TZEER yah-khah-LOHF.

You sweep them away; they are like a sleep; in the morning they are like grass that passes.

בַּבֹּקֶר יָצִיץ וְחָלָף, לָעֶרֶב יְמוֹלֵל וְיָבֵשׁ.

bah-BOH-ker yah-TZEETZ veh-khah-LAHF, lah-EH-rev yeh-moh-LEL veh-yah-VESH.

In the morning it flourishes and sprouts; by evening it withers and dries.

כִּי כָלִינוּ בְאַפֶּךָ, וּבַחֲמָתְךָ נִבְהָלְנוּ.

kee khah-LEE-noo veh-ah-PEH-khah, oo-vah-khah-mah-teh-KHAH neev-HAHL-noo.

For we are consumed by Your anger, and terrified by Your wrath.

שַׁתָּה עֲוֹנֹתֵינוּ לְנֶגְדֶּךָ, עֲלֻמֵנוּ לִמְאוֹר פָּנֶיךָ.

shah-TAH ah-voh-noh-TAY-noo leh-neg-DEH-khah, ah-loo-MEH-noo leem-OHR pah-NEH-khah.

You have set our iniquities before You, our hidden sins in the light of Your face.

כִּי כָל יָמֵינוּ פָּנוּ בְעֶבְרָתֶךָ, כִּלִּינוּ שָׁנֵינוּ כְמוֹ הֶגֶה.

kee khohl yah-MAY-noo pah-NOO veh-ev-rah-TEH-khah, kee-LEE-noo shah-NAY-noo kheh-MOH HEH-geh.

For all our days pass away in Your fury; we finish our years like a sigh.

יְמֵי שְׁנוֹתֵינוּ בָהֶם שִׁבְעִים שָׁנָה, וְאִם בִּגְבוּרֹת שְׁמוֹנִים שָׁנָה, וְרָהְבָּם עָמָל וָאָוֶן, כִּי גָז חִישׁ וַנָּעֻפָה.

yeh-MAY sheh-noh-TAY-noo vah-HEM sheev-EEM shah-NAH, veh-EEM beeg-voo-ROHT sheh-moh-NEEM shah-NAH, veh-roh-BAHM ah-MAHL vah-AH-ven, kee GAHZ KHEESH vah-nah-OO-fah.

The days of our years are seventy, or with strength eighty; yet their pride is toil and sorrow, for it is soon cut off and we fly away.

מִי יוֹדֵעַ עֹז אַפֶּךָ, וּכְיִרְאָתְךָ עֶבְרָתֶךָ.

mee yoh-DEH-ah ohz ah-PEH-khah, ookh-yeer-ah-teh-KHAH ev-rah-TEH-khah.

Who knows the power of Your anger? Your fury matches the fear due You.

לִמְנוֹת יָמֵינוּ כֵּן הוֹדַע, וְנָבִא לְבַב חָכְמָה.

leem-NOHT yah-MAY-noo KEN hoh-DAH, veh-nah-VEE leh-VAHV khokh-MAH.

Teach us to number our days, that we may gain a heart of wisdom.

שׁוּבָה יְהֹוָה עַד מָתָי, וְהִנָּחֵם עַל עֲבָדֶיךָ.

SHOO-vah ah-doh-NIGH ahd mah-TIGH, veh-hee-nah-KHEM ahl ah-vah-DEH-khah.

Return, Lord; how long? Have compassion on Your servants.

שַׂבְּעֵנוּ בַבֹּקֶר חַסְדֶּךָ, וּנְרַנְּנָה וְנִשְׂמְחָה בְּכָל יָמֵינוּ.

sah-beh-EH-noo vah-BOH-ker khahs-DEH-khah, oo-neh-rah-neh-NAH veh-nees-meh-KHAH beh-KHOHL yah-MAY-noo.

Satisfy us in the morning with Your kindness, that we may sing and rejoice all our days.

שַׂמְּחֵנוּ כִּימוֹת עִנִּיתָנוּ, שְׁנוֹת רָאִינוּ רָעָה.

sah-meh-KHEH-noo kee-MOHT ee-nee-TAH-noo, sheh-NOHT rah-EE-noo rah-AH.

Gladden us for as many days as You afflicted us, for the years we saw trouble.

יֵרָאֶה אֶל עֲבָדֶיךָ פָעֳלֶךָ, וַהֲדָרְךָ עַל בְּנֵיהֶם.

yeh-rah-EH el ah-vah-DEH-khah fah-oh-LEH-khah, vah-hah-dah-reh-KHAH ahl beh-nay-HEM.

Let Your work be seen by Your servants, and Your splendor upon their children.

וִיהִי נֹעַם אֲדֹנָי אֱלֹהֵינוּ עָלֵינוּ, וּמַעֲשֵׂה יָדֵינוּ כּוֹנְנָה עָלֵינוּ, וּמַעֲשֵׂה יָדֵינוּ כּוֹנְנֵהוּ.

vee-HEE NOH-ahm ah-doh-NIGH eh-loh-HAY-noo ah-LAY-noo, oo-mah-ah-SEH yah-DAY-noo koh-neh-NAH ah-LAY-noo, oo-mah-ah-SEH yah-DAY-noo koh-neh-NEH-hoo.

May the pleasantness of the Lord our God be upon us; establish for us the work of our hands; establish the work of our hands.

Said for: Safety and Protection; Annulling Harsh Decrees; Protection from the Evil Eye.

The last verse is customarily said twice.

יֹשֵׁב בְּסֵתֶר עֶלְיוֹן, בְּצֵל שַׁדַּי יִתְלוֹנָן.

yoh-SHEV beh-SEH-ter el-YOHN, beh-TZEL shah-DIGH yeet-loh-NAHN.

One who dwells in the shelter of the Most High abides in the shadow of the Almighty.

אֹמַר לַיהֹוָה מַחְסִי וּמְצוּדָתִי, אֱלֹהַי אֶבְטַח בּוֹ.

oh-MAHR lah-doh-NIGH mahkh-SEE oom-tzoo-dah-TEE, eh-loh-HIGH ev-TAKH BOH.

I say of the Lord, “He is my refuge and my fortress, my God in whom I trust.”

כִּי הוּא יַצִּילְךָ מִפַּח יָקוּשׁ, מִדֶּבֶר הַוּוֹת.

kee HOO yah-tzeel-KHAH mee-PAHKH yah-KOOSH, mee-DEH-ver hah-VOHT.

For He will save you from the fowler’s snare and from the destructive plague.

בְּאֶבְרָתוֹ יָסֶךְ לָךְ, וְתַחַת כְּנָפָיו תֶּחְסֶה, צִנָּה וְסֹחֵרָה אֲמִתּוֹ.

beh-ev-rah-TOH YAH-sekh LAHKH, veh-TAH-khaht keh-nah-FAHV tekh-SEH, tzee-NAH veh-soh-kheh-RAH ah-mee-TOH.

He will cover you with His feathers, and under His wings you will find refuge; His truth is a shield and armor.

לֹא תִירָא מִפַּחַד לָיְלָה, מֵחֵץ יָעוּף יוֹמָם.

loh tee-RAH mee-PAH-khahd LIGH-lah, meh-KHETZ yah-OOF yoh-MAHM.

You will not fear the terror of the night, nor the arrow that flies by day,

מִדֶּבֶר בָּאֹפֶל יַהֲלֹךְ, מִקֶּטֶב יָשׁוּד צָהֳרָיִם.

mee-DEH-ver bah-OH-fel yah-hah-LOHKH, mee-KEH-tev yah-SHOOD tzoh-hoh-RAH-yeem.

nor the plague that stalks in darkness, nor the destruction that ravages at noon.

יִפֹּל מִצִּדְּךָ אֶלֶף, וּרְבָבָה מִימִינֶךָ, אֵלֶיךָ לֹא יִגָּשׁ.

yee-POHL mee-tzee-deh-KHAH EH-lef, oor-vah-VAH mee-mee-NEH-khah, eh-LEH-khah loh yee-GAHSH.

A thousand may fall at your side and ten thousand at your right hand, but it will not come near you.

רַק בְּעֵינֶיךָ תַבִּיט, וְשִׁלֻּמַת רְשָׁעִים תִּרְאֶה.

rahk beh-ay-NEH-khah tah-BEET, veh-shee-loo-MAHT reh-shah-EEM teer-EH.

You will only look with your eyes and see the punishment of the wicked.

כִּי אַתָּה יְהֹוָה מַחְסִי, עֶלְיוֹן שַׂמְתָּ מְעוֹנֶךָ.

kee ah-TAH ah-doh-NIGH mahkh-SEE, el-YOHN sahm-TAH meh-oh-NEH-khah.

For You, Lord, are my refuge; you have made the Most High your dwelling place.

לֹא תְאֻנֶּה אֵלֶיךָ רָעָה, וְנֶגַע לֹא יִקְרַב בְּאָהֳלֶךָ.

loh teh-oo-NEH eh-LEH-khah rah-AH, veh-NEH-gah loh yeek-RAHV beh-oh-hoh-LEH-khah.

No evil will befall you, and no plague will come near your tent.

כִּי מַלְאָכָיו יְצַוֶּה לָּךְ, לִשְׁמָרְךָ בְּכָל דְּרָכֶיךָ.

kee mahl-ah-KHAHV yeh-tzah-VEH LAHKH, leesh-mohr-KHAH beh-KHOHL deh-rah-KHEH-khah.

For He will command His angels concerning you, to guard you in all your ways.

עַל כַּפַּיִם יִשָּׂאוּנְךָ, פֶּן תִּגֹּף בָּאֶבֶן רַגְלֶךָ.

ahl kah-PAH-yeem yee-sah-oon-KHAH, pen tee-GOHF bah-EH-ven rahg-LEH-khah.

They will carry you in their hands, lest you strike your foot against a stone.

עַל שַׁחַל וָפֶתֶן תִּדְרֹךְ, תִּרְמֹס כְּפִיר וְתַנִּין.

ahl SHAH-khahl vah-FEH-ten teed-ROHKH, teer-MOHS keh-FEER veh-tah-NEEN.

You will tread on the lion and the cobra; you will trample the young lion and the serpent.

כִּי בִי חָשַׁק וַאֲפַלְּטֵהוּ, אֲשַׂגְּבֵהוּ כִּי יָדַע שְׁמִי.

kee VEE khah-SHAHK vah-ah-fahl-TEH-hoo, ah-sahg-VEH-hoo kee yah-DAH sheh-MEE.

“Because he loves Me, I will rescue him; I will protect him, for he knows My name.

יִקְרָאֵנִי וְאֶעֱנֵהוּ, עִמּוֹ אָנֹכִי בְצָרָה, אֲחַלְּצֵהוּ וַאֲכַבְּדֵהוּ.

yeek-rah-EH-nee veh-eh-eh-NEH-hoo, ee-MOH ah-noh-KHEE veh-tzah-RAH, ah-khahl-TZEH-hoo vah-ah-khah-beh-DEH-hoo.

He will call upon Me and I will answer him; I will be with him in trouble; I will rescue him and honor him.

אֹרֶךְ יָמִים אַשְׂבִּיעֵהוּ, וְאַרְאֵהוּ בִּישׁוּעָתִי.

OH-rekh yah-MEEM ahs-bee-EH-hoo, veh-ahr-EH-hoo bee-shoo-ah-TEE.

With length of days I will satisfy him and show him My salvation.”

Said for: Success in a Lawsuit.

יְהֹוָה מָלָךְ גֵּאוּת לָבֵשׁ לָבֵשׁ יְהֹוָה עֹז הִתְאַזָּר, אַף תִּכּוֹן תֵּבֵל בַּל תִּמּוֹט.

ah-doh-NIGH mah-LAHKH geh-OOT lah-VESH, lah-VESH ah-doh-NIGH OHZ heet-ah-ZAHR, ahf tee-KOHN teh-VEL bahl tee-MOHT.

The Lord reigns; He is clothed in majesty; the Lord is clothed and girded with strength; the world stands firm and cannot be moved.

נָכוֹן כִּסְאֲךָ מֵאָז, מֵעוֹלָם אָתָּה.

nah-KHOHN kees-ah-KHAH meh-AHZ, meh-oh-LAHM AH-tah.

Your throne is established from of old; You are from eternity.

נָשְׂאוּ נְהָרוֹת יְהֹוָה נָשְׂאוּ נְהָרוֹת קוֹלָם, יִשְׂאוּ נְהָרוֹת דָּכְיָם.

nahs-OO neh-hah-ROHT ah-doh-NIGH nahs-OO neh-hah-ROHT koh-LAHM, yees-OO neh-hah-ROHT dokh-YAHM.

The rivers have lifted up, Lord, the rivers have lifted up their voice; the rivers lift up their roaring waves.

מִקֹּלוֹת מַיִם רַבִּים אַדִּירִים מִשְׁבְּרֵי יָם, אַדִּיר בַּמָּרוֹם יְהֹוָה.

mee-koh-LOHT MAH-yeem rah-BEEM ah-dee-REEM meesh-beh-RAY YAHM, ah-DEER bah-mah-ROHM ah-doh-NIGH.

Mightier than the sound of many waters, mightier than the breakers of the sea, is the Lord on high.

עֵדֹתֶיךָ נֶאֶמְנוּ מְאֹד לְבֵיתְךָ נַאֲוָה קֹדֶשׁ, יְהֹוָה לְאֹרֶךְ יָמִים.

eh-doh-TEH-khah neh-em-NOO meh-OHD leh-vayt-KHAH nah-ah-VAH KOH-desh, ah-doh-NIGH leh-OH-rekh yah-MEEM.

Your testimonies are very sure; holiness befits Your House, Lord, for all time.

Said for: Victory; Gratitude.

מִזְמוֹר לְתוֹדָה, הָרִיעוּ לַיהֹוָה כָּל הָאָרֶץ.

meez-MOHR leh-toh-DAH, hah-REE-oo lah-doh-NIGH kohl hah-AH-retz.

A psalm of thanksgiving. Shout for joy to the Lord, all the earth.

עִבְדוּ אֶת יְהֹוָה בְּשִׂמְחָה, בֹּאוּ לְפָנָיו בִּרְנָנָה.

eev-DOO eht ah-doh-NIGH beh-seem-KHAH, BOH-oo leh-fah-NAHV beer-nah-NAH.

Serve the Lord with gladness; come before Him with joyful song.

דְּעוּ כִּי יְהֹוָה הוּא אֱלֹהִים, הוּא עָשָׂנוּ וְלוֹ אֲנַחְנוּ, עַמּוֹ וְצֹאן מַרְעִיתוֹ.

deh-OO kee ah-doh-NIGH HOO eh-loh-HEEM, HOO ah-SAH-noo veh-LOH ah-NAKH-noo, ah-MOH veh-TZOHN mahr-ee-TOH.

Know that the Lord is God; He made us and we are His, His people and the flock of His pasture.

בֹּאוּ שְׁעָרָיו בְּתוֹדָה, חֲצֵרֹתָיו בִּתְהִלָּה, הוֹדוּ לוֹ בָּרְכוּ שְׁמוֹ.

BOH-oo sheh-ah-RAHV beh-toh-DAH, khah-tzeh-roh-TAHV beet-hee-LAH, hoh-DOO LOH bahr-KHOO sheh-MOH.

Enter His gates with thanksgiving and His courts with praise; give thanks to Him and bless His name.

כִּי טוֹב יְהֹוָה, לְעוֹלָם חַסְדּוֹ, וְעַד דֹּר וָדֹר אֱמוּנָתוֹ.

kee TOHV ah-doh-NIGH, leh-oh-LAHM khahs-DOH, veh-AHD dohr vah-DOHR eh-moo-nah-TOH.

For the Lord is good; His kindness is forever, and His faithfulness through all generations.

Said for: Success in an Endeavor.

לְדָוִד מִזְמוֹר, חֶסֶד וּמִשְׁפָּט אָשִׁירָה, לְךָ יְהֹוָה אֲזַמֵּרָה.

leh-dah-VEED meez-MOHR, KHEH-sed oo-meesh-PAHT ah-SHEE-rah, leh-KHAH ah-doh-NIGH ah-zah-MEH-rah.

Of David, a psalm. I will sing of kindness and justice; to You, Lord, I will make music.

אַשְׂכִּילָה בְּדֶרֶךְ תָּמִים, מָתַי תָּבוֹא אֵלָי, אֶתְהַלֵּךְ בְּתָם לְבָבִי בְּקֶרֶב בֵּיתִי.

ahs-KEE-lah beh-DEH-rekh tah-MEEM, mah-TIGH tah-VOH eh-LIGH, et-hah-LEKH beh-TAHM leh-vah-VEE beh-KEH-rev bay-TEE.

I will be wise in the way of integrity; when will You come to me? I will walk with a whole heart within my house.

לֹא אָשִׁית לְנֶגֶד עֵינַי דְּבַר בְּלִיָּעַל, עֲשֹׂה סֵטִים שָׂנֵאתִי, לֹא יִדְבַּק בִּי.

loh ah-SHEET leh-NEH-ged ay-NIGH deh-VAHR beh-lee-YAH-ahl, ah-SOH seh-TEEM sah-NAY-tee, loh yeed-BAHK BEE.

I will not set anything base before my eyes; I hate the deeds of those who turn aside; it will not cling to me.

לֵבָב עִקֵּשׁ יָסוּר מִמֶּנִּי, רָע לֹא אֵדָע.

leh-VAHV ee-KESH yah-SOOR mee-MEH-nee, RAH loh eh-DAH.

A crooked heart will depart from me; I will know no evil.

מְלָשְׁנִי בַסֵּתֶר רֵעֵהוּ אוֹתוֹ אַצְמִית, גְּבַהּ עֵינַיִם וּרְחַב לֵבָב אֹתוֹ לֹא אוּכָל.

meh-lohsh-NEE vah-SEH-ter reh-EH-hoo oh-TOH ahtz-MEET, geh-VAH ay-NAH-yeem oor-KHAHV leh-VAHV oh-TOH loh oo-KHAHL.

Whoever slanders his neighbor in secret I will silence; the haughty of eye and proud of heart I will not tolerate.

עֵינַי בְּנֶאֶמְנֵי אֶרֶץ לָשֶׁבֶת עִמָּדִי, הֹלֵךְ בְּדֶרֶךְ תָּמִים הוּא יְשָׁרְתֵנִי.

ay-NIGH beh-neh-em-NAY EH-retz lah-SHEH-vet ee-mah-DEE, hoh-LEKH beh-DEH-rekh tah-MEEM HOO yeh-shahr-TEH-nee.

My eyes are on the faithful of the land, that they may dwell with me; one who walks in the way of integrity will serve me.

לֹא יֵשֵׁב בְּקֶרֶב בֵּיתִי עֹשֵׂה רְמִיָּה, דֹּבֵר שְׁקָרִים לֹא יִכּוֹן לְנֶגֶד עֵינָי.

loh yeh-SHEV beh-KEH-rev bay-TEE oh-SEH reh-mee-YAH, doh-VER sheh-kah-REEM loh yee-KOHN leh-NEH-ged ay-NIGH.

No one who practices deceit will dwell in my house; no one who speaks falsehood will stand before my eyes.

לַבְּקָרִים אַצְמִית כָּל רִשְׁעֵי אָרֶץ, לְהַכְרִית מֵעִיר יְהֹוָה כָּל פֹּעֲלֵי אָוֶן.

lah-beh-kah-REEM ahtz-MEET kohl reesh-AY AH-retz, leh-hahkh-REET meh-EER ah-doh-NIGH kohl poh-ah-LAY AH-ven.

Every morning I will silence all the wicked of the land, to cut off all evildoers from the city of the Lord.

Said for: Health and Healing.

תְּפִלָּה לְעָנִי כִי יַעֲטֹף, וְלִפְנֵי יְהֹוָה יִשְׁפֹּךְ שִׂיחוֹ.

teh-fee-LAH leh-ah-NEE khee yah-ah-TOHF, veh-leef-NAY ah-doh-NIGH yeesh-POHKH see-KHOH.

A prayer of the afflicted when he is faint and pours out his complaint before the Lord.

יְהֹוָה שִׁמְעָה תְפִלָּתִי, וְשַׁוְעָתִי אֵלֶיךָ תָבוֹא.

ah-doh-NIGH sheem-AH teh-fee-lah-TEE, veh-shahv-ah-TEE eh-LEH-khah tah-VOH.

Lord, hear my prayer, and let my cry come to You.

אַל תַּסְתֵּר פָּנֶיךָ מִמֶּנִּי בְּיוֹם צַר לִי, הַטֵּה אֵלַי אָזְנֶךָ, בְּיוֹם אֶקְרָא מַהֵר עֲנֵנִי.

ahl tahs-TER pah-NEH-khah mee-MEH-nee beh-YOHM TZAHR LEE, hah-TEH eh-LIGH ohz-NEH-khah, beh-YOHM ek-RAH mah-HER ah-NEH-nee.

Do not hide Your face from me on the day of my distress; incline Your ear to me; on the day I call, answer me quickly.

כִּי כָלוּ בְעָשָׁן יָמָי, וְעַצְמוֹתַי כְּמוֹ קֵד נִחָרוּ.

kee KHAH-loo veh-ah-SHAHN yah-MIGH, veh-ahtz-moh-TIGH keh-MOH KED nee-KHAH-roo.

For my days vanish like smoke, and my bones are scorched like a hearth.

הוּכָּה כָעֵשֶׂב וַיִּבַשׁ לִבִּי, כִּי שָׁכַחְתִּי מֵאֲכֹל לַחְמִי.

hoo-KAH khah-EH-sev vah-yee-VAHSH lee-BEE, kee shah-KHAKH-tee meh-ah-KHOHL lahkh-MEE.

My heart is struck and withered like grass, for I forget to eat my bread.

מִקּוֹל אַנְחָתִי, דָּבְקָה עַצְמִי לִבְשָׂרִי.

mee-KOHL ahn-khah-TEE, dahv-KAH ahtz-MEE leev-sah-REE.

Because of the sound of my groaning, my bones cling to my flesh.

דָּמִיתִי לִקְאַת מִדְבָּר, הָיִיתִי כְּכוֹס חֳרָבוֹת.

dah-MEE-tee leek-AHT meed-BAHR, hah-YEE-tee keh-KHOHS khoh-rah-VOHT.

I am like a pelican of the wilderness; I have become like an owl among the ruins.

שָׁקַדְתִּי וָאֶהְיֶה, כְּצִפּוֹר בּוֹדֵד עַל גָּג.

shah-KAHD-tee vah-eh-YEH, keh-tzee-POHR boh-DED ahl GAHG.

I lie awake; I am like a lonely bird on a rooftop.

כָּל הַיּוֹם חֵרְפוּנִי אוֹיְבָי, מְהוֹלָלַי בִּי נִשְׁבָּעוּ.

kohl hah-YOHM kher-FOO-nee oy-VIGH, meh-hoh-lah-LIGH BEE neesh-BAH-oo.

All day my enemies taunt me; those who mock me curse by my name.

כִּי אֵפֶר כַּלֶּחֶם אָכָלְתִּי, וְשִׁקֻּוַי בִּבְכִי מָסָכְתִּי.

kee EH-fer kah-LEH-khem ah-KHAHL-tee, veh-shee-koo-VIGH beev-KHEE mah-SAHKH-tee.

For I eat ashes like bread and mingle my drink with tears,

מִפְּנֵי זַעַמְךָ וְקִצְפֶּךָ, כִּי נְשָׂאתַנִי וַתַּשְׁלִיכֵנִי.

mee-peh-NAY zah-ahm-KHAH veh-keetz-PEH-khah, kee neh-sah-TAH-nee vah-tahsh-lee-KHEH-nee.

because of Your indignation and anger, for You have lifted me up and cast me down.

יָמַי כְּצֵל נָטוּי, וַאֲנִי כָּעֵשֶׂב אִיבָשׁ.

yah-MIGH keh-TZEL nah-TOOY, vah-ah-NEE kah-EH-sev ee-VAHSH.

My days are like a lengthening shadow, and I wither like grass.

וְאַתָּה יְהֹוָה לְעוֹלָם תֵּשֵׁב, וְזִכְרְךָ לְדֹר וָדֹר.

veh-ah-TAH ah-doh-NIGH leh-oh-LAHM teh-SHEV, veh-zeekh-reh-KHAH leh-DOHR vah-DOHR.

But You, Lord, are enthroned forever, and Your name endures through all generations.

אַתָּה תָקוּם תְּרַחֵם צִיּוֹן, כִּי עֵת לְחֶנְנָהּ כִּי בָא מוֹעֵד.

ah-TAH tah-KOOM teh-rah-KHEM tzee-YOHN, kee ET leh-khen-NAH kee VAH moh-ED.

You will arise and have compassion on Zion, for it is time to be gracious to her; the appointed time has come.

כִּי רָצוּ עֲבָדֶיךָ אֶת אֲבָנֶיהָ, וְאֶת עֲפָרָהּ יְחֹנֵנוּ.

kee rah-TZOO ah-vah-DEH-khah eht ah-vah-NEH-hah, veh-ET ah-fah-RAH yeh-khoh-NEH-noo.

For Your servants cherish her stones and favor her dust.

וְיִירְאוּ גוֹיִם אֶת שֵׁם יְהֹוָה, וְכָל מַלְכֵי הָאָרֶץ אֶת כְּבוֹדֶךָ.

veh-yeer-OO goh-YEEM eht SHEM ah-doh-NIGH, veh-khohl mahl-KHAY hah-AH-retz eht keh-voh-DEH-khah.

The nations will revere the name of the Lord, and all the kings of the earth Your glory.

כִּי בָנָה יְהֹוָה צִיּוֹן, נִרְאָה בִּכְבוֹדוֹ.

kee vah-NAH ah-doh-NIGH tzee-YOHN, neer-AH beekh-voh-DOH.

For the Lord has built Zion; He has appeared in His glory.

פָּנָה אֶל תְּפִלַּת הָעַרְעָר, וְלֹא בָזָה אֶת תְּפִלָּתָם.

pah-NAH el teh-fee-LAHT hah-ahr-AHR, veh-LOH vah-ZAH eht teh-fee-lah-TAHM.

He has turned to the prayer of the destitute and has not despised their prayer.

תִּכָּתֶב זֹאת לְדוֹר אַחֲרוֹן, וְעַם נִבְרָא יְהַלֶּל יָהּ.

tee-KAH-tev ZOHT leh-DOHR ah-khah-ROHN, veh-AHM neev-RAH yeh-hah-LEL YAH.

Let this be written for a generation to come, so that a people yet to be created will praise the Lord.

כִּי הִשְׁקִיף מִמְּרוֹם קָדְשׁוֹ, יְהֹוָה מִשָּׁמַיִם אֶל אֶרֶץ הִבִּיט.

kee heesh-KEEF mee-meh-ROHM kohd-SHOH, ah-doh-NIGH mee-shah-MAH-yeem el EH-retz hee-BEET.

For He looked down from His holy height; from heaven the Lord gazed upon the earth,

לִשְׁמֹעַ אֶנְקַת אָסִיר, לְפַתֵּחַ בְּנֵי תְמוּתָה.

leesh-MOH-ah en-KAHT ah-SEER, leh-fah-TEH-ahkh beh-NAY teh-moo-TAH.

to hear the groaning of the prisoner, to set free those condemned to death,

לְסַפֵּר בְּצִיּוֹן שֵׁם יְהֹוָה, וּתְהִלָּתוֹ בִּירוּשָׁלָיִם.

leh-sah-PER beh-tzee-YOHN SHEM ah-doh-NIGH, oot-hee-lah-TOH bee-roo-shah-LAH-yeem.

so that the name of the Lord may be declared in Zion and His praise in Jerusalem,

בְּהִקָּבֵץ עַמִּים יַחְדָּו, וּמַמְלָכוֹת לַעֲבֹד אֶת יְהֹוָה.

beh-hee-kah-VETZ ah-MEEM yahkh-DAHV, oo-mahm-lah-KHOHT lah-ah-VOHD eht ah-doh-NIGH.

when peoples and kingdoms gather together to serve the Lord.

עִנָּה בַדֶּרֶךְ כֹּחִי, קִצַּר יָמָי.

ee-NAH vah-DEH-rekh koh-KHEE, kee-TZAHR yah-MIGH.

He has weakened my strength along the way; He has shortened my days.

אֹמַר אֵלִי אַל תַּעֲלֵנִי בַּחֲצִי יָמָי, בְּדוֹר דּוֹרִים שְׁנוֹתֶיךָ.

oh-MAHR eh-LEE ahl tah-ah-LEH-nee bah-khah-TZEE yah-MIGH, beh-DOHR doh-REEM sheh-noh-TEH-khah.

I say, “My God, do not take me away in the middle of my days; Your years endure through all generations.

לְפָנִים הָאָרֶץ יָסַדְתָּ, וּמַעֲשֵׂה יָדֶיךָ שָׁמָיִם.

leh-fah-NEEM hah-AH-retz yah-SAHD-tah, oo-mah-ah-SEH yah-DEH-khah shah-MAH-yeem.

Long ago You founded the earth, and the heavens are the work of Your hands.

הֵמָּה יֹאבֵדוּ וְאַתָּה תַעֲמֹד, וְכֻלָּם כַּבֶּגֶד יִבְלוּ, כַּלְּבוּשׁ תַּחֲלִיפֵם וְיַחֲלֹפוּ.

HEH-mah yoh-VEH-doo veh-ah-TAH tah-ah-MOHD, veh-khoo-LAHM kah-BEH-ged yeev-LOO, kah-leh-VOOSH tah-khah-lee-FEM veh-yah-khah-LOH-foo.

They will perish, but You will remain; they will all wear out like a garment; like clothing You will change them and they will pass away.

וְאַתָּה הוּא, וּשְׁנוֹתֶיךָ לֹא יִתָּמּוּ.

veh-ah-TAH HOO, oosh-noh-TEH-khah loh yee-TAH-moo.

But You are the same, and Your years will never end.

בְּנֵי עֲבָדֶיךָ יִשְׁכּוֹנוּ, וְזַרְעָם לְפָנֶיךָ יִכּוֹן.

beh-NAY ah-vah-DEH-khah yeesh-KOH-noo, veh-zahr-AHM leh-fah-NEH-khah yee-KOHN.

The children of Your servants will dwell secure, and their descendants will be established before You.”

Said for: Livelihood.

בָּרְכִי נַפְשִׁי אֶת יְהֹוָה, יְהֹוָה אֱלֹהַי גָּדַלְתָּ מְּאֹד, הוֹד וְהָדָר לָבָשְׁתָּ.

bahr-KHEE nahf-SHEE eht ah-doh-NIGH, ah-doh-NIGH eh-loh-HIGH gah-DAHL-tah meh-OHD, hohd veh-hah-DAHR lah-VAHSH-tah.

Bless the Lord, my soul. Lord my God, You are very great; You are clothed in splendor and majesty.

עֹטֶה אוֹר כַּשַּׂלְמָה, נוֹטֶה שָׁמַיִם כַּיְרִיעָה.

oh-TEH ohr kah-sahl-MAH, noh-TEH shah-MAH-yeem kah-yeh-ree-AH.

He wraps Himself in light as in a garment; He stretches out the heavens like a curtain.

הַמְקָרֶה בַמַּיִם עֲלִיּוֹתָיו, הַשָּׂם עָבִים רְכוּבוֹ, הַמְהַלֵּךְ עַל כַּנְפֵי רוּחַ.

hahm-kah-REH vah-MAH-yeem ah-lee-yoh-TAHV, hah-SAHM ah-VEEM reh-khoo-VOH, hahm-hah-LEKH ahl kahn-FAY ROO-ahkh.

He lays the beams of His upper chambers in the waters; He makes the clouds His chariot and rides on the wings of the wind.

עֹשֶׂה מַלְאָכָיו רוּחוֹת, מְשָׁרְתָיו אֵשׁ לֹהֵט.

oh-SEH mahl-ah-KHAHV roo-KHOHT, meh-shahr-TAHV ESH loh-HET.

He makes the winds His messengers, flames of fire His servants.

יָסַד אֶרֶץ עַל מְכוֹנֶיהָ, בַּל תִּמּוֹט עוֹלָם וָעֶד.

yah-SAHD EH-retz ahl meh-khoh-NEH-hah, bahl tee-MOHT oh-LAHM vah-ED.

He set the earth on its foundations, so that it will never be moved.

תְּהוֹם כַּלְּבוּשׁ כִּסִּיתוֹ, עַל הָרִים יַעַמְדוּ מָיִם.

teh-HOHM kah-leh-VOOSH kee-see-TOH, ahl hah-REEM yah-ahm-DOO MAH-yeem.

You covered it with the deep as with a garment; the waters stood above the mountains.

מִן גַּעֲרָתְךָ יְנוּסוּן, מִן קוֹל רַעַמְךָ יֵחָפֵזוּן.

meen gah-ah-raht-KHAH yeh-noo-SOON, meen kohl rah-ahm-KHAH yeh-khah-feh-ZOON.

At Your rebuke they fled; at the sound of Your thunder they hurried away.

יַעֲלוּ הָרִים יֵרְדוּ בְקָעוֹת, אֶל מְקוֹם זֶה יָסַדְתָּ לָהֶם.

yah-ah-LOO hah-REEM yer-DOO veh-kah-OHT, el meh-KOHM ZEH yah-SAHD-tah lah-HEM.

Mountains rose and valleys sank down to the place You established for them.

גְּבוּל שַׂמְתָּ בַּל יַעֲבֹרוּן, בַּל יְשׁוּבוּן לְכַסּוֹת הָאָרֶץ.

geh-VOOL sahm-TAH bahl yah-ah-voh-ROON, bahl yeh-shoo-VOON leh-khah-SOHT hah-AH-retz.

You set a boundary they may not pass, so they will not again cover the earth.

הַמְשַׁלֵּחַ מַעְיָנִים בַּנְּחָלִים, בֵּין הָרִים יְהַלֵּכוּן.

hahm-shah-LEH-ahkh mah-yah-NEEM bah-neh-khah-LEEM, bayn hah-REEM yeh-hah-leh-KHOON.

He sends springs into the valleys; they flow between the mountains.

יַשְׁקוּ כָּל חַיְתוֹ שָׂדָי, יִשְׁבְּרוּ פְרָאִים צְמָאָם.

yahsh-KOO kohl khai-TOH sah-DIGH, yeesh-beh-ROO feh-rah-EEM tzeh-mah-AHM.

They give drink to every beast of the field; the wild donkeys quench their thirst.

עֲלֵיהֶם עוֹף הַשָּׁמַיִם יִשְׁכּוֹן, מִבֵּין עֳפָאיִם יִתְּנוּ קוֹל.

ah-lay-HEM OHF hah-shah-MAH-yeem yeesh-KOHN, mee-BAYN oh-fah-EEM yee-teh-NOO KOHL.

Beside them dwell the birds of the sky; they sing among the branches.

מַשְׁקֶה הָרִים מֵעֲלִיּוֹתָיו, מִפְּרִי מַעֲשֶׂיךָ תִּשְׂבַּע הָאָרֶץ.

mahsh-KEH hah-REEM meh-ah-lee-yoh-TAHV, mee-peh-REE mah-ah-SEH-khah tees-BAH hah-AH-retz.

He waters the mountains from His upper chambers; the earth is satisfied with the fruit of Your works.

מַצְמִיחַ חָצִיר לַבְּהֵמָה, וְעֵשֶׂב לַעֲבֹדַת הָאָדָם, לְהוֹצִיא לֶחֶם מִן הָאָרֶץ.

mahtz-MEE-ahkh khah-TZEER lah-beh-heh-MAH, veh-EH-sev lah-ah-voh-DAHT hah-ah-DAHM, leh-hoh-TZEE LEH-khem meen hah-AH-retz.

He makes grass grow for the cattle and plants for people to cultivate, bringing forth bread from the earth,

וְיַיִן יְשַׂמַּח לְבַב אֱנוֹשׁ, לְהַצְהִיל פָּנִים מִשָּׁמֶן, וְלֶחֶם לְבַב אֱנוֹשׁ יִסְעָד.

veh-YAH-yeen yeh-sah-MAKH leh-VAHV eh-NOHSH, leh-hahtz-HEEL pah-NEEM mee-SHAH-men, veh-LEH-khem leh-VAHV eh-NOHSH yees-AHD.

and wine that gladdens the human heart, oil that makes the face shine, and bread that sustains the human heart.

יִשְׂבְּעוּ עֲצֵי יְהֹוָה, אַרְזֵי לְבָנוֹן אֲשֶׁר נָטָע.

yees-beh-OO ah-tzay ah-doh-NIGH, ahr-ZAY leh-vah-NOHN ah-SHER nah-TAH.

The trees of the Lord are well watered, the cedars of Lebanon that He planted,

אֲשֶׁר שָׁם צִפֳּרִים יְקַנֵּנוּ, חֲסִידָה בְּרוֹשִׁים בֵּיתָהּ.

ah-SHER SHAHM tzee-poh-REEM yeh-kah-NEH-noo, khah-see-DAH beh-roh-SHEEM bay-TAH.

where birds build their nests; the stork makes its home in the cypresses.

הָרִים הַגְּבֹהִים לַיְּעֵלִים, סְלָעִים מַחְסֶה לַשְׁפַנִּים.

hah-REEM hah-geh-voh-HEEM lah-yeh-eh-LEEM, seh-lah-EEM makh-SEH lahsh-fah-NEEM.

The high mountains are for the wild goats; the rocks are a refuge for the rock badgers.

עָשָׂה יָרֵחַ לְמוֹעֲדִים, שֶׁמֶשׁ יָדַע מְבוֹאוֹ.

ah-SAH yah-REH-ahkh leh-moh-ah-DEEM, SHEH-mesh yah-DAH meh-voh-OH.

He made the moon to mark the seasons; the sun knows when to set.

תָּשֶׁת חֹשֶׁךְ וִיהִי לָיְלָה, בּוֹ תִרְמֹשׂ כָּל חַיְתוֹ יָעַר.

TAH-shet KHOH-shekh vee-HEE LIGH-lah, BOH teer-MOHS kohl khai-TOH YAH-ahr.
