You bring darkness and it becomes night, when all the beasts of the forest stir.

הַכְּפִירִים שֹׁאֲגִים לַטָּרֶף, וּלְבַקֵּשׁ מֵאֵל אָכְלָם.

hah-keh-fee-REEM shoh-ah-GEEM lah-TAH-ref, ool-vah-KESH meh-EL ohkh-LAHM.

The young lions roar for prey and seek their food from God.

תִּזְרַח הַשֶּׁמֶשׁ יֵאָסֵפוּן, וְאֶל מְעוֹנֹתָם יִרְבָּצוּן.

teez-RAKH hah-SHEH-mesh yeh-ah-seh-FOON, veh-EL meh-oh-noh-TAHM yeer-bah-TZOON.

When the sun rises they withdraw and lie down in their dens.

יֵצֵא אָדָם לְפָעֳלוֹ, וְלַעֲבֹדָתוֹ עֲדֵי עָרֶב.

yeh-TZEH ah-DAHM leh-fah-oh-LOH, veh-lah-ah-voh-dah-TOH ah-DAY AH-rev.

Man goes out to his work and to his labor until evening.

מָה רַבּוּ מַעֲשֶׂיךָ יְהֹוָה, כֻּלָּם בְּחָכְמָה עָשִׂיתָ, מָלְאָה הָאָרֶץ קִנְיָנֶךָ.

mah RAH-boo mah-ah-SEH-khah ah-doh-NIGH, koo-LAHM beh-khokh-MAH ah-SEE-tah, mahl-AH hah-AH-retz keen-yah-NEH-khah.

How many are Your works, Lord! In wisdom You made them all; the earth is full of Your creations.

זֶה הַיָּם גָּדוֹל וּרְחַב יָדָיִם, שָׁם רֶמֶשׂ וְאֵין מִסְפָּר, חַיּוֹת קְטַנּוֹת עִם גְּדֹלוֹת.

ZEH hah-YAHM gah-DOHL oor-KHAHV yah-DAH-yeem, SHAHM REH-mes veh-AYN mees-PAHR, khah-YOHT keh-tah-NOHT eem geh-doh-LOHT.

There is the sea, great and wide, teeming with creatures beyond number, living things both small and great.

שָׁם אֳנִיּוֹת יְהַלֵּכוּן, לִוְיָתָן זֶה יָצַרְתָּ לְשַׂחֶק בּוֹ.

SHAHM oh-nee-YOHT yeh-hah-leh-KHOON, leev-yah-TAHN ZEH yah-TZAHR-tah leh-sah-KHEK BOH.

There the ships sail, and Leviathan, which You formed to play there.

כֻּלָּם אֵלֶיךָ יְשַׂבֵּרוּן, לָתֵת אָכְלָם בְּעִתּוֹ.

koo-LAHM eh-LEH-khah yeh-sah-beh-ROON, lah-TET ohkh-LAHM beh-ee-TOH.

All of them look to You to give them their food in its season.

תִּתֵּן לָהֶם יִלְקֹטוּן, תִּפְתַּח יָדְךָ יִשְׂבְּעוּן טוֹב.

tee-TEN lah-HEM yeel-koh-TOON, teef-TAKH yahd-KHAH yees-beh-OON TOHV.

When You give to them they gather it up; when You open Your hand they are satisfied with good.

תַּסְתִּיר פָּנֶיךָ יִבָּהֵלוּן, תֹּסֵף רוּחָם יִגְוָעוּן, וְאֶל עֲפָרָם יְשׁוּבוּן.

tahs-TEER pah-NEH-khah yee-bah-heh-LOON, toh-SEF roo-KHAHM yeeg-vah-OON, veh-EL ah-fah-RAHM yeh-shoo-VOON.

When You hide Your face they are dismayed; when You take away their breath they die and return to their dust.

תְּשַׁלַּח רוּחֲךָ יִבָּרֵאוּן, וּתְחַדֵּשׁ פְּנֵי אֲדָמָה.

teh-shah-LAKH roo-khah-KHAH yee-bah-reh-OON, oot-khah-DESH peh-NAY ah-dah-MAH.

When You send forth Your spirit they are created, and You renew the face of the earth.

יְהִי כְבוֹד יְהֹוָה לְעוֹלָם, יִשְׂמַח יְהֹוָה בְּמַעֲשָׂיו.

yeh-HEE kheh-VOHD ah-doh-NIGH leh-oh-LAHM, yees-MAKH ah-doh-NIGH beh-mah-ah-SAHV.

May the glory of the Lord endure forever; may the Lord rejoice in His works.

הַמַּבִּיט לָאָרֶץ וַתִּרְעָד, יִגַּע בֶּהָרִים וְיֶעֱשָׁנוּ.

hah-mah-BEET lah-AH-retz vah-teer-AHD, yee-GAH beh-hah-REEM veh-yeh-eh-SHAH-noo.

He looks at the earth and it trembles; He touches the mountains and they smoke.

אָשִׁירָה לַיהֹוָה בְּחַיָּי, אֲזַמְּרָה לֵאלֹהַי בְּעוֹדִי.

ah-SHEE-rah lah-doh-NIGH beh-khah-YIGH, ah-zahm-RAH leh-loh-HIGH beh-oh-DEE.

I will sing to the Lord all my life; I will sing praise to my God as long as I live.

יֶעֱרַב עָלָיו שִׂיחִי, אָנֹכִי אֶשְׂמַח בַּיהֹוָה.

yeh-eh-RAHV ah-LAHV see-KHEE, ah-noh-KHEE es-MAKH bah-doh-NIGH.

May my meditation be pleasing to Him; I will rejoice in the Lord.

יִתַּמּוּ חַטָּאִים מִן הָאָרֶץ, וּרְשָׁעִים עוֹד אֵינָם, בָּרְכִי נַפְשִׁי אֶת יְהֹוָה, הַלְלוּיָהּ.

yee-TAH-moo khah-tah-EEM meen hah-AH-retz, oor-shah-EEM ohd ay-NAHM, bahr-KHEE nahf-SHEE eht ah-doh-NIGH, hahl-loo-YAH.

May sinners vanish from the earth and the wicked be no more. Bless the Lord, my soul. Halleluyah.

Psalm 105Also in Tikkun HaKlali→

Said for: Success in a Lawsuit.

Also said as part of Hallel.

הוֹדוּ לַיהֹוָה כִּי טוֹב, כִּי לְעוֹלָם חַסְדּוֹ.

hoh-DOO lah-doh-NIGH kee TOHV, kee leh-oh-LAHM khahs-DOH.

Give thanks to the Lord, for He is good; His kindness endures forever.

יֹאמַר נָא יִשְׂרָאֵל, כִּי לְעוֹלָם חַסְדּוֹ.

yoh-mahr NAH yees-rah-EL, kee leh-oh-LAHM khahs-DOH.

Let Israel say: His kindness endures forever.

יֹאמְרוּ נָא בֵית אַהֲרֹן, כִּי לְעוֹלָם חַסְדּוֹ.

yoh-meh-ROO NAH vayt ah-hah-ROHN, kee leh-oh-LAHM khahs-DOH.

Let the house of Aaron say: His kindness endures forever.

יֹאמְרוּ נָא יִרְאֵי יְהֹוָה, כִּי לְעוֹלָם חַסְדּוֹ.

yoh-meh-ROO NAH yeer-AY ah-doh-NIGH, kee leh-oh-LAHM khahs-DOH.

Let those who fear the Lord say: His kindness endures forever.

מִן הַמֵּצַר קָרָאתִי יָּהּ, עָנָנִי בַמֶּרְחָב יָהּ.

meen hah-meh-TZAHR kah-RAH-tee YAH, ah-NAH-nee vah-mer-KHAHV YAH.

From the straits I called to the Lord; the Lord answered me with abundant relief.

יְהֹוָה לִי לֹא אִירָא, מַה יַּעֲשֶׂה לִי אָדָם.

ah-doh-NIGH LEE loh ee-RAH, mah yah-ah-SEH LEE ah-DAHM.

The Lord is with me; I will not fear; what can man do to me?

יְהֹוָה לִי בְּעֹזְרָי, וַאֲנִי אֶרְאֶה בְשֹׂנְאָי.

ah-doh-NIGH LEE beh-ohz-RIGH, vah-ah-NEE er-EH veh-sohn-IGH.

The Lord is with me among my helpers; I will see the downfall of those who hate me.

טוֹב לַחֲסוֹת בַּיהֹוָה, מִבְּטֹחַ בָּאָדָם.

TOHV lah-khah-SOHT bah-doh-NIGH, mee-beh-TOH-ahkh bah-ah-DAHM.

It is better to take refuge in the Lord than to trust in man.

טוֹב לַחֲסוֹת בַּיהֹוָה, מִבְּטֹחַ בִּנְדִיבִים.

TOHV lah-khah-SOHT bah-doh-NIGH, mee-beh-TOH-ahkh been-dee-VEEM.

It is better to take refuge in the Lord than to trust in princes.

כָּל גּוֹיִם סְבָבוּנִי, בְּשֵׁם יְהֹוָה כִּי אֲמִילַם.

kohl goh-YEEM seh-vah-VOO-nee, beh-SHEM ah-doh-NIGH kee ah-mee-LAHM.

All the nations surrounded me; in the name of the Lord I cut them down.

סַבּוּנִי גַם סְבָבוּנִי, בְּשֵׁם יְהֹוָה כִּי אֲמִילַם.

SAH-boo-nee gahm seh-vah-VOO-nee, beh-SHEM ah-doh-NIGH kee ah-mee-LAHM.

They surrounded me, yes, they surrounded me; in the name of the Lord I cut them down.

סַבּוּנִי כִדְבוֹרִים דֹּעֲכוּ כְּאֵשׁ קוֹצִים, בְּשֵׁם יְהֹוָה כִּי אֲמִילַם.

SAH-boo-nee khee-deh-voh-REEM doh-ah-KHOO keh-ESH koh-TZEEM, beh-SHEM ah-doh-NIGH kee ah-mee-LAHM.

They surrounded me like bees, but they were put out like a fire of thorns; in the name of the Lord I cut them down.

דַּחֹה דְחִיתַנִי לִנְפֹּל, וַיהֹוָה עֲזָרָנִי.

dah-KHOH deh-khee-TAH-nee leen-POHL, vah-doh-NIGH ah-zah-RAH-nee.

You pushed me hard to make me fall, but the Lord helped me.

עָזִּי וְזִמְרָת יָהּ, וַיְהִי לִי לִישׁוּעָה.

oh-ZEE veh-zeem-RAHT YAH, vah-yeh-HEE LEE lee-shoo-AH.

The Lord is my strength and my song, and He has become my salvation.

קוֹל רִנָּה וִישׁוּעָה בְּאָהֳלֵי צַדִּיקִים, יְמִין יְהֹוָה עֹשָׂה חָיִל.

kohl ree-NAH vee-shoo-AH beh-oh-hoh-LAY tzah-dee-KEEM, yeh-MEEN ah-doh-NIGH OH-sah KHAH-yeel.

A sound of joy and salvation is in the tents of the righteous: the right hand of the Lord does valiantly.

יְמִין יְהֹוָה רוֹמֵמָה, יְמִין יְהֹוָה עֹשָׂה חָיִל.

yeh-MEEN ah-doh-NIGH roh-meh-MAH, yeh-MEEN ah-doh-NIGH OH-sah KHAH-yeel.

The right hand of the Lord is exalted; the right hand of the Lord does valiantly.

לֹא אָמוּת כִּי אֶחְיֶה, וַאֲסַפֵּר מַעֲשֵׂי יָהּ.

loh ah-MOOT kee ekh-YEH, vah-ah-sah-PER mah-ah-SAY YAH.

I will not die but live, and tell of the deeds of the Lord.

יַסֹּר יִסְּרַנִּי יָּהּ, וְלַמָּוֶת לֹא נְתָנָנִי.

yah-SOHR yee-seh-RAH-nee YAH, veh-lah-MAH-vet loh neh-tah-NAH-nee.

The Lord has chastened me severely, but He has not given me over to death.

פִּתְחוּ לִי שַׁעֲרֵי צֶדֶק, אָבֹא בָם אוֹדֶה יָהּ.

peet-KHOO LEE shah-ah-RAY TZEH-dek, ah-VOH VAHM oh-DEH YAH.

Open for me the gates of righteousness; I will enter them and thank the Lord.

זֶה הַשַּׁעַר לַיהֹוָה, צַדִּיקִים יָבֹאוּ בוֹ.

ZEH hah-SHAH-ahr lah-doh-NIGH, tzah-dee-KEEM yah-VOH-oo VOH.

This is the gate of the Lord; the righteous will enter through it.

אוֹדְךָ כִּי עֲנִיתָנִי, וַתְּהִי לִי לִישׁוּעָה.

ohd-KHAH kee ah-nee-TAH-nee, vah-teh-HEE LEE lee-shoo-AH.

I thank You, for You answered me and became my salvation.

אֶבֶן מָאֲסוּ הַבּוֹנִים, הָיְתָה לְרֹאשׁ פִּנָּה.

EH-ven mah-ah-SOO hah-boh-NEEM, hah-yeh-TAH leh-ROHSH pee-NAH.

The stone the builders rejected has become the cornerstone.

מֵאֵת יְהֹוָה הָיְתָה זֹּאת, הִיא נִפְלָאת בְּעֵינֵינוּ.

meh-ET ah-doh-NIGH hah-yeh-TAH ZOHT, HEE neef-LAHT beh-ay-NAY-noo.

This was from the Lord; it is wondrous in our eyes.

זֶה הַיּוֹם עָשָׂה יְהֹוָה, נָגִילָה וְנִשְׂמְחָה בוֹ.

ZEH hah-YOHM ah-SAH ah-doh-NIGH, nah-GEE-lah veh-nees-meh-KHAH VOH.

This is the day the Lord has made; let us rejoice and be glad in it.

אָנָּא יְהֹוָה הוֹשִׁיעָה נָּא, אָנָּא יְהֹוָה הַצְלִיחָה נָּא.

AH-nah ah-doh-NIGH hoh-SHEE-ah NAH, AH-nah ah-doh-NIGH hahtz-LEE-khah NAH.

Please, Lord, save us! Please, Lord, grant us success!

בָּרוּךְ הַבָּא בְּשֵׁם יְהֹוָה, בֵּרַכְנוּכֶם מִבֵּית יְהֹוָה.

bah-ROOKH hah-BAH beh-SHEM ah-doh-NIGH, beh-rahkh-NOO-khem mee-BAYT ah-doh-NIGH.

Blessed is the one who comes in the name of the Lord; we bless you from the House of the Lord.

אֵל יְהֹוָה וַיָּאֶר לָנוּ אִסְרוּ חַג בַּעֲבֹתִים, עַד קַרְנוֹת הַמִּזְבֵּחַ.

EL ah-doh-NIGH vah-YAH-er LAH-noo, eez-ROO KHAHG bah-ah-voh-TEEM, ahd kahr-NOHT hah-meez-BAY-ahkh.

The Lord is God, and He has given us light; bind the festival offering with cords to the horns of the altar.

אֵלִי אַתָּה וְאוֹדֶךָּ, אֱלֹהַי אֲרוֹמְמֶךָּ.

ay-LEE ah-TAH veh-oh-DEK-kah, eh-loh-HIGH ah-roh-meh-MEK-kah.

You are my God and I will thank You; my God, I will exalt You.

הוֹדוּ לַיהֹוָה כִּי טוֹב, כִּי לְעוֹלָם חַסְדּוֹ.

hoh-DOO lah-doh-NIGH kee TOHV, kee leh-oh-LAHM khahs-DOH.

Give thanks to the Lord, for He is good; His kindness endures forever.

Said for: Peace in the Home.

Only the seven sections whose opening letters spell “Shalom Bayit” (ש ל ו ם ב י ת) are given here, in that order, as Hidabroot prints them for peace in the home. The full psalm has 22 sections.

Shin ש (verses 161–168)

שָׂרִים רְדָפוּנִי חִנָּם, וּמִדְּבָרְךָ פָּחַד לִבִּי.

sah-REEM reh-dah-FOO-nee khee-NAHM, oo-mee-deh-vahr-KHAH pah-KHAHD lee-BEE.

Princes have pursued me without cause, but my heart stands in awe of Your word.

שָׂשׂ אָנֹכִי עַל אִמְרָתֶךָ, כְּמוֹצֵא שָׁלָל רָב.

SAHS ah-noh-KHEE ahl eem-rah-TEH-khah, keh-moh-TZEH shah-LAHL RAHV.

I rejoice over Your word like one who finds great spoil.

שֶׁקֶר שָׂנֵאתִי וַאֲתַעֵבָה, תּוֹרָתְךָ אָהָבְתִּי.

SHEH-ker sah-NAY-tee vah-ah-tah-ah-VAH, toh-raht-KHAH ah-HAHV-tee.

I hate and abhor falsehood; Your Torah I love.

שֶׁבַע בַּיּוֹם הִלַּלְתִּיךָ, עַל מִשְׁפְּטֵי צִדְקֶךָ.

SHEH-vah bah-YOHM hee-lahl-TEE-khah, ahl meesh-peh-TAY tzeed-KEH-khah.

Seven times a day I praise You for Your righteous judgments.

שָׁלוֹם רָב לְאֹהֲבֵי תוֹרָתֶךָ, וְאֵין לָמוֹ מִכְשׁוֹל.

shah-LOHM RAHV leh-oh-hah-VAY toh-rah-TEH-khah, veh-AYN LAH-moh meekh-SHOHL.

Great peace have those who love Your Torah, and nothing makes them stumble.

שִׂבַּרְתִּי לִישׁוּעָתְךָ יְהֹוָה, וּמִצְוֹתֶיךָ עָשִׂיתִי.

see-BAHR-tee lee-shoo-aht-KHAH ah-doh-NIGH, oo-meetz-voh-TEH-khah ah-SEE-tee.

I hope for Your salvation, Lord, and I have done Your commandments.

שָׁמְרָה נַפְשִׁי עֵדֹתֶיךָ, וָאֹהֲבֵם מְאֹד.

shahm-RAH nahf-SHEE eh-doh-TEH-khah, vah-oh-hah-VEM meh-OHD.

My soul keeps Your testimonies, and I love them greatly.

שָׁמַרְתִּי פִקּוּדֶיךָ וְעֵדֹתֶיךָ, כִּי כָל דְּרָכַי נֶגְדֶּךָ.

shah-MAHR-tee fee-koo-DEH-khah veh-eh-doh-TEH-khah, kee khohl deh-rah-KHIGH neg-DEH-khah.

I keep Your precepts and Your testimonies, for all my ways are before You.

Lamed ל (verses 89–96)

לְעוֹלָם יְהֹוָה, דְּבָרְךָ נִצָּב בַּשָּׁמָיִם.

leh-oh-LAHM ah-doh-NIGH, deh-vahr-KHAH nee-TZAHV bah-shah-MAH-yeem.

Forever, Lord, Your word stands firm in the heavens.

לְדֹר וָדֹר אֱמוּנָתֶךָ, כּוֹנַנְתָּ אֶרֶץ וַתַּעֲמֹד.

leh-DOHR vah-DOHR eh-moo-nah-TEH-khah, koh-NAHN-tah EH-retz vah-tah-ah-MOHD.

Your faithfulness endures for all generations; You established the earth and it stands.

לְמִשְׁפָּטֶיךָ עָמְדוּ הַיּוֹם, כִּי הַכֹּל עֲבָדֶיךָ.

leh-meesh-pah-TEH-khah ahm-DOO hah-YOHM, kee hah-KOHL ah-vah-DEH-khah.

By Your decrees they stand to this day, for all things are Your servants.

לוּלֵי תוֹרָתְךָ שַׁעֲשֻׁעָי, אָז אָבַדְתִּי בְעָנְיִי.

LOO-lay toh-raht-KHAH shah-ah-shoo-IGH, AHZ ah-VAHD-tee veh-ohn-YEE.

Had Your Torah not been my delight, I would have perished in my affliction.

לְעוֹלָם לֹא אֶשְׁכַּח פִּקּוּדֶיךָ, כִּי בָם חִיִּיתָנִי.

leh-oh-LAHM loh esh-KAKH pee-koo-DEH-khah, kee VAHM khee-yee-TAH-nee.

I will never forget Your precepts, for through them You have given me life.

לְךָ אֲנִי הוֹשִׁיעֵנִי, כִּי פִקּוּדֶיךָ דָרָשְׁתִּי.

leh-KHAH ah-NEE hoh-shee-EH-nee, kee fee-koo-DEH-khah dah-RAHSH-tee.

I am Yours; save me, for I have sought Your precepts.

לִי קִוּוּ רְשָׁעִים לְאַבְּדֵנִי, עֵדֹתֶיךָ אֶתְבּוֹנָן.

LEE kee-VOO reh-shah-EEM leh-ah-beh-DEH-nee, eh-doh-TEH-khah et-boh-NAHN.

The wicked lie in wait to destroy me, but I ponder Your testimonies.

לְכָל תִּכְלָה רָאִיתִי קֵץ, רְחָבָה מִצְוָתְךָ מְאֹד.

leh-KHOHL teekh-LAH rah-EE-tee KETZ, reh-khah-VAH meetz-vaht-KHAH meh-OHD.

To every perfection I have seen a limit, but Your commandment is exceedingly broad.

Vav ו (verses 41–48)

וִיבֹאֻנִי חֲסָדֶךָ יְהֹוָה, תְּשׁוּעָתְךָ כְּאִמְרָתֶךָ.

vee-voh-OO-nee khah-sah-DEH-khah ah-doh-NIGH, teh-shoo-aht-KHAH keh-eem-rah-TEH-khah.

May Your kindnesses come to me, Lord, Your salvation according to Your word.

וְאֶעֱנֶה חֹרְפִי דָבָר, כִּי בָטַחְתִּי בִּדְבָרֶךָ.

veh-eh-eh-NEH khohr-FEE dah-VAHR, kee vah-TAKH-tee beed-vah-REH-khah.

Then I will have an answer for the one who taunts me, for I trust in Your word.

וְאַל תַּצֵּל מִפִּי דְבַר אֱמֶת עַד מְאֹד, כִּי לְמִשְׁפָּטֶךָ יִחָלְתִּי.

veh-AHL tah-TZEL mee-PEE deh-VAHR eh-MET ahd meh-OHD, kee leh-meesh-pah-TEH-khah yee-KHAHL-tee.

Do not take the word of truth utterly out of my mouth, for I hope in Your judgments.

וְאֶשְׁמְרָה תוֹרָתְךָ תָמִיד, לְעוֹלָם וָעֶד.

veh-esh-meh-RAH toh-raht-KHAH tah-MEED, leh-oh-LAHM vah-ED.

I will keep Your Torah always, forever and ever.

וְאֶתְהַלְּכָה בָרְחָבָה, כִּי פִקֻּדֶיךָ דָרָשְׁתִּי.

veh-et-hahl-KHAH vah-reh-khah-VAH, kee fee-koo-DEH-khah dah-RAHSH-tee.

I will walk in freedom, for I have sought Your precepts.

וַאֲדַבְּרָה בְעֵדֹתֶיךָ נֶגֶד מְלָכִים, וְלֹא אֵבוֹשׁ.

vah-ah-dah-beh-RAH veh-eh-doh-TEH-khah NEH-ged meh-lah-KHEEM, veh-LOH eh-VOHSH.

I will speak of Your testimonies before kings and will not be ashamed.

וְאֶשְׁתַּעֲשַׁע בְּמִצְוֹתֶיךָ, אֲשֶׁר אָהָבְתִּי.

veh-esh-tah-ah-SHAH beh-meetz-voh-TEH-khah, ah-SHER ah-HAHV-tee.

I will delight in Your commandments, which I love.

וְאֶשָּׂא כַפַּי אֶל מִצְוֹתֶיךָ אֲשֶׁר אָהָבְתִּי, וְאָשִׂיחָה בְחֻקֶּיךָ.

veh-eh-SAH khah-PIGH el meetz-voh-TEH-khah ah-SHER ah-HAHV-tee, veh-ah-SEE-khah veh-khoo-KEH-khah.

I will lift up my hands to Your commandments, which I love, and I will meditate on Your statutes.

Mem מ (verses 97–104)

מָה אָהַבְתִּי תוֹרָתֶךָ, כָּל הַיּוֹם הִיא שִׂיחָתִי.

mah ah-HAHV-tee toh-rah-TEH-khah, kohl hah-YOHM HEE see-khah-TEE.

How I love Your Torah! It is my meditation all day.

מֵאֹיְבַי תְּחַכְּמֵנִי מִצְוֹתֶךָ, כִּי לְעוֹלָם הִיא לִי.

meh-oy-VIGH teh-khahk-MEH-nee meetz-voh-TEH-khah, kee leh-oh-LAHM HEE LEE.

Your commandment makes me wiser than my enemies, for it is always with me.

מִכָּל מְלַמְּדַי הִשְׂכַּלְתִּי, כִּי עֵדְוֹתֶיךָ שִׂיחָה לִי.

mee-KOHL meh-lahm-DIGH hees-KAHL-tee, kee eh-deh-voh-TEH-khah see-KHAH LEE.

I have more understanding than all my teachers, for Your testimonies are my meditation.

מִזְּקֵנִים אֶתְבּוֹנָן, כִּי פִקּוּדֶיךָ נָצָרְתִּי.

mee-zeh-keh-NEEM et-boh-NAHN, kee fee-koo-DEH-khah nah-TZAHR-tee.

I understand more than the elders, for I have kept Your precepts.

מִכָּל אֹרַח רָע כָּלִאתִי רַגְלָי, לְמַעַן אֶשְׁמֹר דְּבָרֶךָ.

mee-KOHL OH-rahkh RAH kah-LEE-tee rahg-LIGH, leh-MAH-ahn esh-MOHR deh-vah-REH-khah.

I have held back my feet from every evil path, so that I may keep Your word.

מִמִּשְׁפָּטֶיךָ לֹא סָרְתִּי, כִּי אַתָּה הוֹרֵתָנִי.

mee-meesh-pah-TEH-khah loh SAHR-tee, kee ah-TAH hoh-reh-TAH-nee.

I have not turned from Your judgments, for You have taught me.

מַה נִּמְלְצוּ לְחִכִּי אִמְרָתֶךָ, מִדְּבַשׁ לְפִי.

mah neem-leh-TZOO leh-khee-KEE eem-rah-TEH-khah, mee-deh-VAHSH leh-FEE.

How sweet are Your words to my palate, sweeter than honey to my mouth!

מִפִּקּוּדֶיךָ אֶתְבּוֹנָן, עַל כֵּן שָׂנֵאתִי כָּל אֹרַח שָׁקֶר.

mee-pee-koo-DEH-khah et-boh-NAHN, ahl KEN sah-NAY-tee kohl OH-rahkh SHAH-ker.

From Your precepts I gain understanding; therefore I hate every false way.

Bet ב (verses 9–16)

בַּמֶּה יְזַכֶּה נַּעַר אֶת אָרְחוֹ, לִשְׁמֹר כִּדְבָרֶךָ.

bah-MEH yeh-zah-KEH NAH-ahr eht ohr-KHOH, leesh-MOHR keed-vah-REH-khah.

How can a young man keep his way pure? By guarding it according to Your word.

בְּכָל לִבִּי דְרַשְׁתִּיךָ, אַל תַּשְׁגֵּנִי מִמִּצְוֹתֶיךָ.

beh-KHOHL lee-BEE deh-rahsh-TEE-khah, ahl tahsh-GEH-nee mee-meetz-voh-TEH-khah.

With all my heart I seek You; do not let me stray from Your commandments.

בְּלִבִּי צָפַנְתִּי אִמְרָתֶךָ, לְמַעַן לֹא אֶחֱטָא לָךְ.

beh-lee-BEE tzah-FAHN-tee eem-rah-TEH-khah, leh-MAH-ahn loh eh-kheh-TAH LAHKH.

I have treasured Your word in my heart, so that I may not sin against You.

בָּרוּךְ אַתָּה יְהֹוָה, לַמְּדֵנִי חֻקֶּיךָ.

bah-ROOKH ah-TAH ah-doh-NIGH, lahm-DEH-nee khoo-KEH-khah.

Blessed are You, Lord; teach me Your statutes.

בִּשְׂפָתַי סִפַּרְתִּי, כֹּל מִשְׁפְּטֵי פִיךָ.

bees-fah-TIGH see-PAHR-tee, kohl meesh-peh-TAY FEE-khah.

With my lips I recount all the judgments of Your mouth.

בְּדֶרֶךְ עֵדְוֹתֶיךָ שַׂשְׂתִּי, כְּעַל כָּל הוֹן.

beh-DEH-rekh eh-deh-voh-TEH-khah SAHS-tee, keh-AHL kohl HOHN.

I rejoice in the way of Your testimonies as much as in all riches.

בְּפִקֻּדֶיךָ אָשִׂיחָה, וְאַבִּיטָה אֹרְחֹתֶיךָ.

beh-fee-koo-DEH-khah ah-SEE-khah, veh-ah-BEE-tah ohr-khoh-TEH-khah.

I meditate on Your precepts and look to Your paths.

בְּחֻקֹּתֶיךָ אֶשְׁתַּעֲשָׁע, לֹא אֶשְׁכַּח דְּבָרֶךָ.

beh-khoo-koh-TEH-khah esh-tah-ah-SHAH, loh esh-KAKH deh-vah-REH-khah.

I delight in Your statutes; I will not forget Your word.

Yod י (verses 73–80)

יָדֶיךָ עָשׂוּנִי וַיְכוֹנְנוּנִי, הֲבִינֵנִי וְאֶלְמְדָה מִצְוֹתֶיךָ.

yah-DEH-khah ah-SOO-nee vah-yeh-khoh-neh-NOO-nee, hah-vee-NEH-nee veh-el-meh-DAH meetz-voh-TEH-khah.

Your hands made me and formed me; give me understanding, that I may learn Your commandments.

יְרֵאֶיךָ יִרְאוּנִי וְיִשְׂמָחוּ, כִּי לִדְבָרְךָ יִחָלְתִּי.

yeh-reh-EH-khah yeer-OO-nee veh-yees-MAH-khoo, kee leed-vahr-KHAH yee-KHAHL-tee.

Those who fear You will see me and rejoice, for I hope in Your word.

יָדַעְתִּי יְהֹוָה כִּי צֶדֶק מִשְׁפָּטֶיךָ, וֶאֱמוּנָה עִנִּיתָנִי.

yah-DAH-tee ah-doh-NIGH kee TZEH-dek meesh-pah-TEH-khah, veh-eh-moo-NAH ee-nee-TAH-nee.

I know, Lord, that Your judgments are righteous, and that in faithfulness You afflicted me.

יְהִי נָא חַסְדְּךָ לְנַחֲמֵנִי, כְּאִמְרָתְךָ לְעַבְדֶּךָ.

yeh-HEE NAH khahs-deh-KHAH leh-nah-khah-MEH-nee, keh-eem-raht-KHAH leh-ahv-DEH-khah.

Let Your kindness comfort me, according to Your word to Your servant.

יְבֹאוּנִי רַחֲמֶיךָ וְאֶחְיֶה, כִּי תוֹרָתְךָ שַׁעֲשֻׁעָי.

yeh-voh-OO-nee rah-khah-MEH-khah veh-ekh-YEH, kee toh-raht-KHAH shah-ah-shoo-IGH.

Let Your mercies come to me that I may live, for Your Torah is my delight.

יֵבֹשׁוּ זֵדִים כִּי שֶׁקֶר עִוְּתוּנִי, אֲנִי אָשִׂיחַ בְּפִקּוּדֶיךָ.

yeh-VOH-shoo zeh-DEEM kee SHEH-ker ee-veh-TOO-nee, ah-NEE ah-SEE-ahkh beh-fee-koo-DEH-khah.

Let the arrogant be ashamed, for they have wronged me with lies; I will meditate on Your precepts.

יָשׁוּבוּ לִי יְרֵאֶיךָ, וְיֹדְעֵי עֵדֹתֶיךָ.

yah-SHOO-voo LEE yeh-reh-EH-khah, veh-yohd-AY eh-doh-TEH-khah.

Let those who fear You turn to me, and those who know Your testimonies.

יְהִי לִבִּי תָמִים בְּחֻקֶּיךָ, לְמַעַן לֹא אֵבוֹשׁ.

yeh-HEE lee-BEE tah-MEEM beh-khoo-KEH-khah, leh-MAH-ahn loh eh-VOHSH.

May my heart be whole in Your statutes, so that I will not be ashamed.

Tav ת (verses 169–176)

תִּקְרַב רִנָּתִי לְפָנֶיךָ יְהֹוָה, כִּדְבָרְךָ הֲבִינֵנִי.

teek-RAHV ree-nah-TEE leh-fah-NEH-khah ah-doh-NIGH, keed-vahr-KHAH hah-vee-NEH-nee.

Let my cry come near before You, Lord; give me understanding according to Your word.

תָּבוֹא תְּחִנָּתִי לְפָנֶיךָ, כְּאִמְרָתְךָ הַצִּילֵנִי.

tah-VOH teh-khee-nah-TEE leh-fah-NEH-khah, keh-eem-raht-KHAH hah-tzee-LEH-nee.

Let my plea come before You; rescue me according to Your word.

תַּבַּעְנָה שְׂפָתַי תְּהִלָּה, כִּי תְלַמְּדֵנִי חֻקֶּיךָ.

tah-BAH-nah seh-fah-TIGH teh-hee-LAH, kee teh-lahm-DEH-nee khoo-KEH-khah.

My lips will pour forth praise, for You teach me Your statutes.

תַּעַן לְשׁוֹנִי אִמְרָתֶךָ, כִּי כָל מִצְוֹתֶיךָ צֶּדֶק.

TAH-ahn leh-shoh-NEE eem-rah-TEH-khah, kee khohl meetz-voh-TEH-khah TZEH-dek.

My tongue will sing of Your word, for all Your commandments are righteous.

תְּהִי יָדְךָ לְעָזְרֵנִי, כִּי פִקּוּדֶיךָ בָחָרְתִּי.

teh-HEE yahd-KHAH leh-ohz-REH-nee, kee fee-koo-DEH-khah vah-KHAHR-tee.

Let Your hand be ready to help me, for I have chosen Your precepts.

תָּאַבְתִּי לִישׁוּעָתְךָ יְהֹוָה, וְתוֹרָתְךָ שַׁעֲשֻׁעָי.

tah-AHV-tee lee-shoo-aht-KHAH ah-doh-NIGH, veh-toh-raht-KHAH shah-ah-shoo-IGH.

I long for Your salvation, Lord, and Your Torah is my delight.

תְּחִי נַפְשִׁי וּתְהַלְלֶךָּ, וּמִשְׁפָּטֶךָ יַעֲזְרֻנִי.

teh-KHEE nahf-SHEE oot-hah-leh-LEK-kah, oo-meesh-pah-TEH-khah yah-zeh-ROO-nee.

Let my soul live and praise You, and let Your judgments help me.

תָּעִיתִי כְּשֶׂה אֹבֵד, בַּקֵּשׁ עַבְדֶּךָ, כִּי מִצְוֹתֶיךָ לֹא שָׁכָחְתִּי.

tah-EE-tee keh-SEH oh-VED, bah-KESH ahv-DEH-khah, kee meetz-voh-TEH-khah loh shah-KHAKH-tee.

I have strayed like a lost sheep; seek Your servant, for I have not forgotten Your commandments.

Said for: Safety and Protection; A Time of Trouble or a Life Challenge.

A song of ascents for protection; customarily recited for travelers and for the sick.

שִׁיר לַמַּעֲלוֹת, אֶשָּׂא עֵינַי אֶל הֶהָרִים, מֵאַיִן יָבֹא עֶזְרִי.

sheer lah-mah-ah-LOHT, eh-SAH ay-NIGH el heh-hah-REEM, meh-AH-yeen yah-VOH ez-REE.

A song of ascents. I lift my eyes to the mountains; from where will my help come?

עֶזְרִי מֵעִם יְהֹוָה, עֹשֵׂה שָׁמַיִם וָאָרֶץ.

ez-REE meh-EEM ah-doh-NIGH, oh-SEH shah-MAH-yeem vah-AH-retz.

My help comes from the Lord, Maker of heaven and earth.

אַל יִתֵּן לַמּוֹט רַגְלֶךָ, אַל יָנוּם שֹׁמְרֶךָ.

ahl yee-TEN lah-MOHT rahg-LEH-khah, ahl yah-NOOM shoh-meh-REH-khah.

He will not let your foot slip; your Guardian will not slumber.

הִנֵּה לֹא יָנוּם וְלֹא יִישָׁן, שׁוֹמֵר יִשְׂרָאֵל.

hee-NEH loh yah-NOOM veh-LOH yee-SHAHN, shoh-MER yees-rah-EL.

Behold, the Guardian of Israel neither slumbers nor sleeps.

יְהֹוָה שֹׁמְרֶךָ, יְהֹוָה צִלְּךָ עַל יַד יְמִינֶךָ.

ah-doh-NIGH shoh-meh-REH-khah, ah-doh-NIGH tzee-leh-KHAH ahl yahd yeh-mee-NEH-khah.

The Lord is your Guardian; the Lord is your shade at your right hand.

יוֹמָם הַשֶּׁמֶשׁ לֹא יַכֶּכָּה, וְיָרֵחַ בַּלָּיְלָה.

yoh-MAHM hah-SHEH-mesh loh yah-KEH-kah, veh-yah-REH-ahkh bah-LIGH-lah.

The sun will not strike you by day, nor the moon by night.

יְהֹוָה יִשְׁמָרְךָ מִכָּל רָע, יִשְׁמֹר אֶת נַפְשֶׁךָ.

ah-doh-NIGH yeesh-mohr-KHAH mee-KOHL RAH, yeesh-MOHR eht nahf-SHEH-khah.

The Lord will guard you from all harm; He will guard your soul.

יְהֹוָה יִשְׁמָר צֵאתְךָ וּבוֹאֶךָ, מֵעַתָּה וְעַד עוֹלָם.

ah-doh-NIGH yeesh-mohr tzeht-KHAH oo-voh-EH-khah, meh-ah-TAH veh-AHD oh-LAHM.

The Lord will guard your going out and your coming in, from now and forever.

Said for: Finding a Spouse.

שִׁיר הַמַּעֲלוֹת לְדָוִד, לוּלֵי יְהֹוָה שֶׁהָיָה לָנוּ, יֹאמַר נָא יִשְׂרָאֵל.

sheer hah-mah-ah-LOHT leh-dah-VEED, LOO-lay ah-doh-NIGH sheh-hah-YAH LAH-noo, yoh-mahr NAH yees-rah-EL.

A song of ascents, of David. Were it not for the Lord who was on our side, let Israel now say,

לוּלֵי יְהֹוָה שֶׁהָיָה לָנוּ, בְּקוּם עָלֵינוּ אָדָם.

LOO-lay ah-doh-NIGH sheh-hah-YAH LAH-noo, beh-KOOM ah-LAY-noo ah-DAHM.

were it not for the Lord who was on our side when people rose up against us,

אֲזַי חַיִּים בְּלָעוּנוּ, בַּחֲרוֹת אַפָּם בָּנוּ.

ah-ZIGH khah-YEEM beh-lah-OO-noo, bah-khah-ROHT ah-PAHM BAH-noo.

then they would have swallowed us alive when their anger burned against us;

אֲזַי הַמַּיִם שְׁטָפוּנוּ, נַחְלָה עָבַר עַל נַפְשֵׁנוּ.

ah-ZIGH hah-MAH-yeem sheh-tah-FOO-noo, NAHKH-lah ah-VAHR ahl nahf-SHEH-noo.

then the waters would have swept us away; the torrent would have gone over our soul;

אֲזַי עָבַר עַל נַפְשֵׁנוּ, הַמַּיִם הַזֵּידוֹנִים.

ah-ZIGH ah-VAHR ahl nahf-SHEH-noo, hah-MAH-yeem hah-zay-doh-NEEM.

then the raging waters would have gone over our soul.

בָּרוּךְ יְהֹוָה, שֶׁלֹּא נְתָנָנוּ טֶרֶף לְשִׁנֵּיהֶם.

bah-ROOKH ah-doh-NIGH, sheh-LOH neh-tah-NAH-noo TEH-ref leh-shee-nay-HEM.

Blessed is the Lord, who did not give us as prey to their teeth.

נַפְשֵׁנוּ כְּצִפּוֹר נִמְלְטָה מִפַּח יוֹקְשִׁים, הַפַּח נִשְׁבָּר וַאֲנַחְנוּ נִמְלָטְנוּ.

nahf-SHEH-noo keh-tzee-POHR neem-leh-TAH mee-PAHKH yohk-SHEEM, hah-PAHKH neesh-BAHR vah-ah-NAKH-noo neem-LAHT-noo.

Our soul escaped like a bird from the fowler’s snare; the snare is broken and we have escaped.

עֶזְרֵנוּ בְּשֵׁם יְהֹוָה, עֹשֵׂה שָׁמַיִם וָאָרֶץ.

ez-REH-noo beh-SHEM ah-doh-NIGH, oh-SEH shah-MAH-yeem vah-AH-retz.

Our help is in the name of the Lord, Maker of heaven and earth.

“When the Lord returned the captives of Zion.” Said before Birkat HaMazon on Shabbat and festivals in many communities, in place of Psalm 137.

שִׁיר הַמַּעֲלוֹת, בְּשׁוּב יְהֹוָה אֶת שִׁיבַת צִיּוֹן, הָיִינוּ כְּחֹלְמִים.

sheer hah-mah-ah-LOHT, beh-SHOOV ah-doh-NIGH eht shee-VAHT tzee-YOHN, hah-YEE-noo keh-khoh-leh-MEEM.

A song of ascents. When the Lord returned the captives of Zion, we were like dreamers.

אָז יִמָּלֵא שְׂחוֹק פִּינוּ וּלְשׁוֹנֵנוּ רִנָּה, אָז יֹאמְרוּ בַגּוֹיִם, הִגְדִּיל יְהֹוָה לַעֲשׂוֹת עִם אֵלֶּה.

AHZ yee-mah-LEH seh-KHOHK PEE-noo ool-shoh-NEH-noo ree-NAH, AHZ yoh-meh-ROO vah-goh-YEEM, heeg-DEEL ah-doh-NIGH lah-ah-SOHT eem EH-leh.

Then our mouths were filled with laughter and our tongues with song; then they said among the nations: The Lord has done great things for them.

הִגְדִּיל יְהֹוָה לַעֲשׂוֹת עִמָּנוּ, הָיִינוּ שְׂמֵחִים.

heeg-DEEL ah-doh-NIGH lah-ah-SOHT ee-MAH-noo, hah-YEE-noo seh-meh-KHEEM.

The Lord has done great things for us; we rejoiced.

שׁוּבָה יְהֹוָה אֶת שְׁבִיתֵנוּ, כַּאֲפִיקִים בַּנֶּגֶב.

SHOO-vah ah-doh-NIGH eht sheh-vee-TEH-noo, kah-ah-fee-KEEM bah-NEH-gev.

Return our captives, Lord, like streams in the Negev.

הַזֹּרְעִים בְּדִמְעָה, בְּרִנָּה יִקְצֹרוּ.

hah-zoh-reh-EEM beed-MAH, beh-ree-NAH yeek-TZOH-roo.

Those who sow in tears will reap in song.

הָלוֹךְ יֵלֵךְ וּבָכֹה נֹשֵׂא מֶשֶׁךְ הַזָּרַע, בֹּא יָבוֹא בְרִנָּה, נֹשֵׂא אֲלֻמֹּתָיו.

hah-LOHKH yeh-LEKH oo-vah-KHOH noh-SEH MEH-shekh hah-ZAH-rah, BOH yah-VOH veh-ree-NAH, noh-SEH ah-loo-moh-TAHV.

He goes out weeping, carrying the bag of seed; he will come back with song, carrying his sheaves.

Said for: Peace in the Home; Livelihood.

שִׁיר הַמַּעֲלוֹת, אַשְׁרֵי כָּל יְרֵא יְהֹוָה, הַהֹלֵךְ בִּדְרָכָיו.

sheer hah-mah-ah-LOHT, ahsh-RAY kohl yeh-REH ah-doh-NIGH, hah-hoh-LEKH beed-rah-KHAHV.

A song of ascents. Happy is everyone who fears the Lord, who walks in His ways.

יְגִיעַ כַּפֶּיךָ כִּי תֹאכֵל, אַשְׁרֶיךָ וְטוֹב לָךְ.

yeh-GEE-ah kah-PEH-khah kee toh-KHEL, ahsh-REH-khah veh-TOHV LAHKH.

When you eat from the labor of your hands, you will be happy and it will be well with you.

אֶשְׁתְּךָ כְּגֶפֶן פֹּרִיָּה בְּיַרְכְּתֵי בֵיתֶךָ, בָּנֶיךָ כִּשְׁתִלֵי זֵיתִים סָבִיב לְשֻׁלְחָנֶךָ.

esh-teh-KHAH keh-GEH-fen poh-ree-YAH beh-yahr-keh-TAY vay-TEH-khah, bah-NEH-khah keesh-tee-LAY zay-TEEM sah-VEEV leh-shool-khah-NEH-khah.

Your wife will be like a fruitful vine within your house, your children like olive shoots around your table.

הִנֵּה כִי כֵן יְבֹרַךְ גָּבֶר, יְרֵא יְהֹוָה.

hee-NEH khee KHEN yeh-VOH-rahkh GAH-ver, yeh-REH ah-doh-NIGH.

Behold, so will the man be blessed who fears the Lord.

יְבָרֶכְךָ יְהֹוָה מִצִּיּוֹן, וּרְאֵה בְּטוּב יְרוּשָׁלָיִם, כֹּל יְמֵי חַיֶּיךָ.

yeh-vah-reh-kheh-KHAH ah-doh-NIGH mee-tzee-YOHN, oor-EH beh-TOOV yeh-roo-shah-LAH-yeem, kohl yeh-MAY khah-YEH-khah.

May the Lord bless you from Zion, and may you see the good of Jerusalem all the days of your life,

וּרְאֵה בָנִים לְבָנֶיךָ, שָׁלוֹם עַל יִשְׂרָאֵל.

oor-EH vah-NEEM leh-vah-NEH-khah, shah-LOHM ahl yees-rah-EL.

and may you see your children’s children. Peace upon Israel.

Said for: Health and Healing; A Time of Trouble or a Life Challenge.

“From the depths.” Recited in times of distress and during the Ten Days of Repentance.

שִׁיר הַמַּעֲלוֹת, מִמַּעֲמַקִּים קְרָאתִיךָ יְהֹוָה.

sheer hah-mah-ah-LOHT, mee-mah-ah-mah-KEEM keh-rah-TEE-khah ah-doh-NIGH.

A song of ascents. From the depths I have called You, Lord.

אֲדֹנָי שִׁמְעָה בְקוֹלִי, תִּהְיֶינָה אָזְנֶיךָ קַשֻּׁבוֹת לְקוֹל תַּחֲנוּנָי.

ah-doh-NIGH sheem-AH veh-koh-LEE, teeh-YEH-nah ohz-NEH-khah kah-shoo-VOHT leh-KOHL tah-khah-noo-NIGH.

Lord, hear my voice; let Your ears be attentive to the sound of my pleas.

אִם עֲוֹנוֹת תִּשְׁמָר יָהּ, אֲדֹנָי מִי יַעֲמֹד.

eem ah-voh-NOHT teesh-mohr YAH, ah-doh-NIGH mee yah-ah-MOHD.

If You kept count of sins, O Lord, Lord, who could stand?

כִּי עִמְּךָ הַסְּלִיחָה, לְמַעַן תִּוָּרֵא.

kee ee-meh-KHAH hah-seh-lee-KHAH, leh-MAH-ahn tee-vah-REH.

But forgiveness is with You, so that You may be revered.

קִוִּיתִי יְהֹוָה קִוְּתָה נַפְשִׁי, וְלִדְבָרוֹ הוֹחָלְתִּי.

kee-VEE-tee ah-doh-NIGH, kee-veh-TAH nahf-SHEE, veh-leed-vah-ROH hoh-KHAHL-tee.

I hope in the Lord, my soul hopes, and I wait for His word.

נַפְשִׁי לַאדֹנָי מִשֹּׁמְרִים לַבֹּקֶר, שֹׁמְרִים לַבֹּקֶר.

nahf-SHEE lah-doh-NIGH mee-shoh-meh-REEM lah-BOH-ker, shoh-meh-REEM lah-BOH-ker.

My soul waits for the Lord more than watchmen for the morning, watchmen for the morning.

יַחֵל יִשְׂרָאֵל אֶל יְהֹוָה, כִּי עִם יְהֹוָה הַחֶסֶד, וְהַרְבֵּה עִמּוֹ פְדוּת.

yah-KHEL yees-rah-EL el ah-doh-NIGH, kee eem ah-doh-NIGH hah-KHEH-sed, veh-hahr-BEH ee-MOH feh-DOOT.

Israel, hope in the Lord, for with the Lord is kindness and abundant redemption.

וְהוּא יִפְדֶּה אֶת יִשְׂרָאֵל מִכֹּל עֲוֹנֹתָיו.

veh-HOO yeef-DEH eht yees-rah-EL mee-KOHL ah-voh-noh-TAHV.

He will redeem Israel from all its sins.

“By the rivers of Babylon.” Recited before Birkat HaMazon on weekdays; on Shabbat it is replaced by Psalm 126.

עַל נַהֲרוֹת בָּבֶל שָׁם יָשַׁבְנוּ גַּם בָּכִינוּ, בְּזָכְרֵנוּ אֶת צִיּוֹן.

ahl nah-hah-ROHT bah-VEL, shahm yah-SHAHV-noo gahm bah-KHEE-noo, beh-zohkh-REH-noo eht tzee-YOHN.

By the rivers of Babylon, there we sat and wept as we remembered Zion.

עַל עֲרָבִים בְּתוֹכָהּ, תָּלִינוּ כִּנֹּרוֹתֵינוּ.

ahl ah-rah-VEEM beh-toh-KHAH, tah-LEE-noo kee-noh-roh-TAY-noo.

On the willows in its midst we hung our harps.

כִּי שָׁם שְׁאֵלוּנוּ שׁוֹבֵינוּ דִּבְרֵי שִׁיר, וְתוֹלָלֵינוּ שִׂמְחָה, שִׁירוּ לָנוּ מִשִּׁיר צִיּוֹן.

kee SHAHM sheh-eh-LOO-noo shoh-VAY-noo deev-RAY sheer, veh-toh-lah-LAY-noo seem-KHAH, SHEE-roo LAH-noo mee-SHEER tzee-YOHN.

For there our captors asked us for songs, our tormentors for joy: Sing us one of the songs of Zion.

אֵיךְ נָשִׁיר אֶת שִׁיר יְהֹוָה, עַל אַדְמַת נֵכָר.

AYKH nah-SHEER eht sheer ah-doh-NIGH, ahl ahd-MAHT neh-KHAHR.

How can we sing the Lord's song on foreign soil?

אִם אֶשְׁכָּחֵךְ יְרוּשָׁלָיִם, תִּשְׁכַּח יְמִינִי.

eem esh-kah-KHEKH yeh-roo-shah-LAH-yeem, teesh-KAHKH yeh-mee-NEE.

If I forget you, Jerusalem, let my right hand forget its skill.

תִּדְבַּק לְשׁוֹנִי לְחִכִּי אִם לֹא אֶזְכְּרֵכִי, אִם לֹא אַעֲלֶה אֶת יְרוּשָׁלַיִם עַל רֹאשׁ שִׂמְחָתִי.

teed-BAHK leh-shoh-NEE leh-khee-KEE, eem loh ez-keh-REH-khee, eem loh ah-ah-LEH eht yeh-roo-shah-LAH-yeem ahl rohsh seem-khah-TEE.

Let my tongue cling to my palate if I do not remember you, if I do not set Jerusalem above my highest joy.

זְכֹר יְהֹוָה לִבְנֵי אֱדוֹם אֵת יוֹם יְרוּשָׁלָיִם, הָאֹמְרִים עָרוּ עָרוּ עַד הַיְסוֹד בָּהּ.

zeh-KHOHR ah-doh-NIGH leev-NAY eh-DOHM eht yohm yeh-roo-shah-LAH-yeem, hah-oh-meh-REEM AH-roo AH-roo, ahd hah-yeh-SOHD BAH.

Remember, Lord, against the Edomites the day of Jerusalem, when they said: Tear it down, tear it down to its foundation.

בַּת בָּבֶל הַשְּׁדוּדָה, אַשְׁרֵי שֶׁיְשַׁלֶּם לָךְ, אֶת גְּמוּלֵךְ שֶׁגָּמַלְתְּ לָנוּ.

baht bah-VEL hah-sheh-doo-DAH, ahsh-RAY sheh-yeh-shah-LEM LAHKH, eht geh-moo-LEKH sheh-gah-MAHLT LAH-noo.

Daughter of Babylon, destined for ruin, happy is the one who repays you for what you did to us.

אַשְׁרֵי שֶׁיֹּאחֵז וְנִפֵּץ, אֶת עֹלָלַיִךְ אֶל הַסָּלַע.

ahsh-RAY sheh-yoh-KHEZ veh-nee-PETZ, eht oh-lah-LAH-yeekh el hah-SAH-lah.

Happy is the one who seizes and dashes your infants against the rock.

Said for: Peace in the Home.

לַמְנַצֵּחַ לְדָוִד מִזְמוֹר, יְהֹוָה חֲקַרְתַּנִי וַתֵּדָע.

lahm-nah-TZEH-ahkh leh-dah-VEED meez-MOHR, ah-doh-NIGH khah-kahr-TAH-nee vah-teh-DAH.

For the conductor, of David, a psalm. Lord, You have searched me and You know me.

אַתָּה יָדַעְתָּ שִׁבְתִּי וְקוּמִי, בַּנְתָּה לְרֵעִי מֵרָחוֹק.

ah-TAH yah-DAHT-tah sheev-TEE veh-koo-MEE, BAHN-tah leh-reh-EE meh-rah-KHOHK.

You know when I sit and when I rise; You understand my thoughts from afar.

אָרְחִי וְרִבְעִי זֵרִיתָ, וְכָל דְּרָכַי הִסְכַּנְתָּה.

ohr-KHEE veh-reev-EE zeh-REE-tah, veh-khohl deh-rah-KHIGH hees-KAHN-tah.

You observe my going and my lying down, and You know all my ways.

כִּי אֵין מִלָּה בִּלְשׁוֹנִי, הֵן יְהֹוָה יָדַעְתָּ כֻלָּהּ.

kee AYN mee-LAH beel-shoh-NEE, HEN ah-doh-NIGH yah-DAHT-tah khoo-LAH.

Before a word is on my tongue, You, Lord, know it all.

אָחוֹר וָקֶדֶם צַרְתָּנִי, וַתָּשֶׁת עָלַי כַּפֶּכָה.

ah-KHOHR vah-KEH-dem tzahr-TAH-nee, vah-TAH-shet ah-LIGH kah-PEH-khah.

Behind and before You have formed me, and You have laid Your hand upon me.

פְּלִיאָה דַעַת מִמֶּנִּי, נִשְׂגְּבָה לֹא אוּכַל לָהּ.

peh-lee-AH DAH-aht mee-MEH-nee, nees-geh-VAH loh OO-khahl LAH.

Such knowledge is too wonderful for me; it is too high, and I cannot reach it.

אָנָה אֵלֵךְ מֵרוּחֶךָ, וְאָנָה מִפָּנֶיךָ אֶבְרָח.

AH-nah eh-LEKH meh-roo-KHEH-khah, veh-AH-nah mee-pah-NEH-khah ev-RAHKH.

Where can I go from Your spirit? Where can I flee from Your presence?

אִם אֶסַּק שָׁמַיִם שָׁם אָתָּה, וְאַצִּיעָה שְּׁאוֹל הִנֶּךָּ.

eem eh-SAHK shah-MAH-yeem SHAHM AH-tah, veh-ah-TZEE-ah sheh-OHL hee-NEK-kah.

If I ascend to heaven, You are there; if I make my bed in the depths, You are there.

אֶשָּׂא כַנְפֵי שָׁחַר, אֶשְׁכְּנָה בְּאַחֲרִית יָם.

eh-SAH khahn-FAY SHAH-khar, esh-keh-NAH beh-ah-khah-REET YAHM.

If I take the wings of the dawn and dwell at the far end of the sea,

גַּם שָׁם יָדְךָ תַנְחֵנִי, וְתֹאחֲזֵנִי יְמִינֶךָ.

gahm SHAHM yahd-KHAH tahn-KHEH-nee, veh-toh-khah-ZEH-nee yeh-mee-NEH-khah.

even there Your hand will guide me, and Your right hand will hold me.

וָאֹמַר אַךְ חֹשֶׁךְ יְשׁוּפֵנִי, וְלַיְלָה אוֹר בַּעֲדֵנִי.

vah-oh-MAHR ahkh KHOH-shekh yeh-shoo-FEH-nee, veh-LIGH-lah OHR bah-ah-DEH-nee.

If I say, “Surely darkness will cover me,” the night becomes light around me.

גַּם חֹשֶׁךְ לֹא יַחְשִׁיךְ מִמֶּךָ, וְלַיְלָה כַּיּוֹם יָאִיר, כַּחֲשֵׁיכָה כָּאוֹרָה.

gahm KHOH-shekh loh yahkh-SHEEKH mee-MEH-khah, veh-LIGH-lah kah-YOHM yah-EER, kah-khah-shay-KHAH kah-oh-RAH.

Even darkness is not dark to You; the night shines like the day, and darkness is like light.

כִּי אַתָּה קָנִיתָ כִלְיֹתָי, תְּסֻכֵּנִי בְּבֶטֶן אִמִּי.

kee ah-TAH kah-NEE-tah kheel-yoh-TIGH, teh-soo-KEH-nee beh-VEH-ten ee-MEE.

For You formed my inner being; You wove me together in my mother’s womb.

אוֹדְךָ עַל כִּי נוֹרָאוֹת נִפְלֵיתִי, נִפְלָאִים מַעֲשֶׂיךָ, וְנַפְשִׁי יֹדַעַת מְאֹד.

ohd-KHAH ahl kee noh-rah-OHT neef-LAY-tee, neef-lah-EEM mah-ah-SEH-khah, veh-nahf-SHEE yoh-DAH-aht meh-OHD.

I thank You, for I am awesomely and wonderfully made; Your works are wonderful, and my soul knows it well.

לֹא נִכְחַד עָצְמִי מִמֶּךָּ, אֲשֶׁר עֻשֵּׂיתִי בַסֵּתֶר, רֻקַּמְתִּי בְּתַחְתִּיּוֹת אָרֶץ.

loh neekh-KHAHD ohtz-MEE mee-MEK-kah, ah-SHER oo-SAY-tee vah-SEH-ter, roo-KAHM-tee beh-takh-tee-YOHT AH-retz.

My frame was not hidden from You when I was made in secret, intricately formed in the depths of the earth.

גָּלְמִי רָאוּ עֵינֶיךָ, וְעַל סִפְרְךָ כֻּלָּם יִכָּתֵבוּ, יָמִים יֻצָּרוּ וְלוֹ אֶחָד בָּהֶם.

gohl-MEE RAH-oo ay-NEH-khah, veh-AHL seef-reh-KHAH koo-LAHM yee-kah-TEH-voo, yah-MEEM yoo-TZAH-roo veh-LOH eh-KHAHD bah-HEM.

Your eyes saw my unformed body; all my days were written in Your book, formed before one of them came to be.

וְלִי מַה יָּקְרוּ רֵעֶיךָ אֵל, מֶה עָצְמוּ רָאשֵׁיהֶם.

veh-LEE mah yahk-ROO reh-EH-khah EL, meh ahtz-MOO rah-shay-HEM.

How precious to me are Your thoughts, God; how vast is their sum!

אֶסְפְּרֵם מֵחוֹל יִרְבּוּן, הֱקִיצֹתִי וְעוֹדִי עִמָּךְ.

es-peh-REM meh-KHOHL yeer-BOON, heh-kee-TZOH-tee veh-oh-DEE ee-MAHKH.

Were I to count them, they would outnumber the sand; when I awake, I am still with You.

אִם תִּקְטֹל אֱלוֹהַּ רָשָׁע, וְאַנְשֵׁי דָמִים סוּרוּ מֶנִּי.

eem teek-TOHL eh-LOH-ah rah-SHAH, veh-ahn-SHAY dah-MEEM SOO-roo MEH-nee.

If only You would slay the wicked, God! Men of bloodshed, depart from me,

אֲשֶׁר יֹאמְרֻךָ לִמְזִמָּה, נָשֻׂא לַשָּׁוְא עָרֶיךָ.

ah-SHER yoh-meh-ROO-khah leem-zee-MAH, nah-SOO lah-SHAHV ah-REH-khah.

those who speak of You with evil intent; Your enemies take Your name in vain.

הֲלוֹא מְשַׂנְאֶיךָ יְהֹוָה אֶשְׂנָא, וּבִתְקוֹמְמֶיךָ אֶתְקוֹטָט.

hah-LOH meh-sahn-EH-khah ah-doh-NIGH es-NAH, oo-veet-koh-meh-MEH-khah et-koh-TAHT.

Do I not hate those who hate You, Lord, and loathe those who rise against You?

תַּכְלִית שִׂנְאָה שְׂנֵאתִים, לְאוֹיְבִים הָיוּ לִי.

tahkh-LEET seen-AH seh-neh-TEEM, leh-oy-VEEM HAH-yoo LEE.

I hate them with utter hatred; they have become my enemies.

חָקְרֵנִי אֵל וְדַע לְבָבִי, בְּחָנֵנִי וְדַע שַׂרְעַפָּי.

khohk-REH-nee EL veh-DAH leh-vah-VEE, beh-khah-NEH-nee veh-DAH sahr-ah-PIGH.

Search me, God, and know my heart; test me and know my thoughts.

וּרְאֵה אִם דֶּרֶךְ עֹצֶב בִּי, וּנְחֵנִי בְּדֶרֶךְ עוֹלָם.

oor-EH eem DEH-rekh OH-tzev BEE, oon-KHEH-nee beh-DEH-rekh oh-LAHM.

See if there is any hurtful way in me, and lead me in the everlasting way.

Said for: A Time of Trouble or a Life Challenge.

מַשְׂכִּיל לְדָוִד, בִּהְיוֹתוֹ בַמְּעָרָה תְפִלָּה.

mahs-KEEL leh-dah-VEED, beeh-yoh-TOH vah-meh-ah-RAH teh-fee-LAH.

A maskil of David, when he was in the cave; a prayer.

קוֹלִי אֶל יְהֹוָה אֶזְעָק, קוֹלִי אֶל יְהֹוָה אֶתְחַנָּן.

koh-LEE el ah-doh-NIGH ez-AHK, koh-LEE el ah-doh-NIGH et-khah-NAHN.

With my voice I cry out to the Lord; with my voice I plead with the Lord.

אֶשְׁפֹּךְ לְפָנָיו שִׂיחִי, צָרָתִי לְפָנָיו אַגִּיד.

esh-POHKH leh-fah-NAHV see-KHEE, tzah-rah-TEE leh-fah-NAHV ah-GEED.

I pour out my complaint before Him; I tell my trouble before Him.

בְּהִתְעַטֵּף עָלַי רוּחִי וְאַתָּה יָדַעְתָּ נְתִיבָתִי, בְּאֹרַח זוּ אֲהַלֵּךְ טָמְנוּ פַח לִי.

beh-heet-ah-TEF ah-LIGH roo-KHEE veh-ah-TAH yah-DAHT-tah neh-tee-vah-TEE, beh-OH-rahkh ZOO ah-hah-LEKH tahm-NOO FAHKH LEE.

When my spirit grows faint within me, You know my path; in the way I walk they have hidden a snare for me.

הַבֵּיט יָמִין וּרְאֵה וְאֵין לִי מַכִּיר, אָבַד מָנוֹס מִמֶּנִּי, אֵין דּוֹרֵשׁ לְנַפְשִׁי.

hah-BAYT yah-MEEN oor-EH veh-AYN LEE mah-KEER, ah-VAHD mah-NOHS mee-MEH-nee, AYN doh-RESH leh-nahf-SHEE.

Look to my right and see: no one acknowledges me; refuge has vanished from me; no one cares for my soul.

זָעַקְתִּי אֵלֶיךָ יְהֹוָה, אָמַרְתִּי אַתָּה מַחְסִי, חֶלְקִי בְּאֶרֶץ הַחַיִּים.

zah-AHK-tee eh-LEH-khah ah-doh-NIGH, ah-MAHR-tee ah-TAH mahkh-SEE, khel-KEE beh-EH-retz hah-khah-YEEM.

I cry out to You, Lord; I say, “You are my refuge, my portion in the land of the living.”

הַקְשִׁיבָה אֶל רִנָּתִי כִּי דַלּוֹתִי מְאֹד, הַצִּילֵנִי מֵרֹדְפַי כִּי אָמְצוּ מִמֶּנִּי.

hahk-SHEE-vah el ree-nah-TEE kee dah-LOH-tee meh-OHD, hah-tzee-LEH-nee meh-rohd-FIGH kee ahm-TZOO mee-MEH-nee.

Listen to my cry, for I am brought very low; rescue me from my pursuers, for they are too strong for me.

הוֹצִיאָה מִמַּסְגֵּר נַפְשִׁי לְהוֹדוֹת אֶת שְׁמֶךָ, בִּי יַכְתִּרוּ צַדִּיקִים כִּי תִגְמֹל עָלָי.

hoh-TZEE-ah mee-mahs-GER nahf-SHEE leh-hoh-DOHT eht sheh-MEH-khah, BEE yahkh-TEE-roo tzah-dee-KEEM kee teeg-MOHL ah-LIGH.

Bring my soul out of prison so that I may thank Your name; the righteous will surround me because You deal kindly with me.

שַׁבַּת שָׁלוֹם

Shabbat shalom
